## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [1] [You Don't Need Strong Assumptions: Visual Representation Learning via Temporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15956)
*Ninad Daithankar, Alexi Gladstone, Yann LeCun, Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 高优先级 (95.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Temporal Difference in Vision (TDV)，一种从视频中自监督学习视觉表征的新范式。TDV 避免使用数据增强、掩码等强归纳偏置，仅依赖"过去导致未来"的因果假设，通过联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，使当前帧表征加上运动编码等于下一帧表征。实验表明，随着数据量增长，弱归纳偏置方法表现更好，TDV 在密集空间任务上匹配了 SOTA 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 的进步趋势表明，随着计算和数据增长，弱归纳偏置的方法通常优于强归纳偏置的方法。然而，现有的自监督学习方法仍然依赖数据增强、掩码、裁剪等强归纳偏置。作者假设这些剩余偏置在大规模数据下会成为瓶颈，并通过实验验证了这一假设。因此，他们寻求一种依赖更少假设的视觉表征学习方法。

Method: 提出 Temporal Difference in Vision (TDV)，核心思想是联合训练一个图像编码器 f 和一个运动编码器 g，使得 f(x_t) + g(x_t, x_{t+1}) = f(x_{t+1})，即当前帧的视觉表征加上编码的运动信息等于下一帧的视觉表征。该方法仅依赖"过去导致未来"的因果假设，不使用数据增强、掩码或裁剪等强归纳偏置。训练时从视频中采样帧对，通过最小化预测表征与目标表征之间的差异来学习。

Result: TDV 在密集空间任务（如语义分割、深度估计）上匹配了使用强归纳偏置的 SOTA 自监督方法。实验验证了随着数据量增加，最优归纳偏置强度逐渐减弱的趋势，支持了作者的假设。

Conclusion: TDV 证明了在不依赖强归纳偏置的情况下进行有效的视觉表征学习是可行的，为未来更大规模数据下的表征学习奠定了基础。该方法遵循了 AI 发展中"假设更少"的趋势，有望在数据规模进一步增长时展现出更大的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TDV，通过联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，在连续潜空间中对视觉表征进行学习（当前帧表征+运动编码=下一帧表征），属于连续视觉表征学习与自编码器范式，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器" 高度吻合。

Importance reason: Proposes a weak-inductive-bias visual representation learning paradigm from video, directly aligning with the user's priority on continuous visual tokenizers and representation autoencoders. Signals: continuous visual representation, temporal difference learning, weak inductive bias, video self-supervised learning, Yann LeCun co-author

Abstract: Progress in AI has largely been driven by methods that assume less. As compute and data increase, approaches with weaker inductive biases generally outperform those with stronger assumptions. This is particularly characteristic of the field of Visual Representation Learning, where approaches have gone from being dominated by Supervised Learning, to Weakly Supervised Learning, to the now widespread success of Self-Supervised Learning without human labels. Yet, even modern Self-Supervised Learning approaches still depend on strong inductive biases such as augmentations, masking, or cropping. If this trend holds, even these remaining biases should become bottlenecks at scale -- and our experiments confirm this: the optimal strength of inductive biases decreases as data grows. This motivates the search for approaches that rely on fewer assumptions. To this end, we introduce Temporal Difference in Vision (TDV), a new paradigm for self-supervised learning from video that avoids existing inductive biases, relying instead on a causal assumption that the past causes the future. TDV functions by jointly training an image encoder and a motion encoder so that the current frame's representation plus the encoded motion equals the next frame's representation. Despite not leveraging any strong inductive biases, TDV matches state-of-the-art recipes on dense spatial tasks, laying the foundation for representation learning without strong assumptions.

</details>

### [2] [Simplifying the Modeling of Arbitrary Conditionals in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14943)
*Yinhan Lu, Eric Elmoznino, Léo Gagnon, Sarthak Mittal, Tejas Kasetty, Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (76.92) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AC-GPT 提出一种简单修改，使标准因果 Transformer 能够在单次前向传播中评估和采样任意条件（包括过去、未来和混合上下文），同时保持从左到右的排序和 next-token 预测目标，兼容现有 LLM 的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果 Transformer 只能进行从左到右的自回归分解，无法高效处理任意条件（如给定过去和未来 token 预测中间块）。现有方法通过修改架构来解决，但往往导致条件建模次优和生成质量下降。本文旨在提出一种简单、兼容现有 LLM 的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: AC-GPT 对标准因果 Transformer 进行简单修改：在注意力掩码中引入特殊标记（如 [MASK] 和 [PRED]），使得模型可以在单次前向传播中同时处理过去、未来和混合上下文的条件。该方法保持标准的从左到右排序和 next-token 预测目标，因此可以直接对现有 LLM 进行微调。

Result: 实验表明，AC-GPT 在建模任意条件分布上优于基线方法，且不降低标准的从左到右生成性能。

Conclusion: AC-GPT 提供了一种简单有效的方案，使因果 Transformer 能够处理任意条件建模，同时保持与现有 LLM 架构和训练目标的兼容性，为语言模型的条件生成和推理提供了更灵活的工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AC-GPT，通过修改标准因果 Transformer 实现对任意条件（past、future、mixed contexts）的评估和采样，属于连续语言建模中关于条件分布建模的扩展。虽然不直接涉及连续 latent/embedding 空间，但其核心关注语言模型的条件分布建模能力，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 主题相关（该主题涵盖语言建模的各类创新范式）。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model capabilities by enabling efficient arbitrary conditional sampling, a core need for multimodal latent reasoning and flow matching. Signals: continuous language model, arbitrary conditional sampling, causal transformer modification, fine-tuning compatible, next-token prediction preserved

Abstract: Causal Transformers model sequences through an autoregressive factorization of the joint distribution, which enables efficient left-to-right decoding and conditional likelihood computation. However, they cannot tractably sample from or evaluate arbitrary conditionals -- e.g., a block of text conditioned on past and future tokens. Recent work aims to solve this problem through novel architectures, but they often lead to sub-optimal modeling of such conditionals and degraded generations. We propose Arbitrary Conditionals GPT (AC-GPT) which introduces a simple modification to standard causal Transformers to enable evaluating and sampling from arbitrary conditionals -- including past, future, and mixed contexts -- within a single forward pass. Unlike prior approaches, our method preserves the standard left-to-right ordering and next-token prediction objective essential for both strong performance and efficient training on natural language. Crucially, this compatibility allows existing LLMs to be fine-tuned for arbitrary conditioning. Our empirical results indicate that our method outperforms baselines on modeling arbitrary conditionals, without degrading standard left-to-right performance.

</details>


### [3] [Semantic DLM+: Improving Diffusion Language Models through Bias-variance Trade-off in Transition Kernel Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15327)
*Keyue Jiang, Yuxiang Wang, Yanan Zhao, Xiang Yu, Qifang Zhao, Bohan Tang, Baojian Zhou, Yanghua Xiao, Lin Qu, Xiaoxiao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过偏差-方差权衡分析扩散语言模型中 transition kernel 的设计，指出掩码扩散与均匀扩散各自的优劣，并改进语义扩散语言模型（SemDLM），提出 SemDLM+ 通过全局转移和语义频率惩罚解决语义盆地问题，在 LM1B 和 OpenWebText 上取得有竞争力的生成质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）的性能对 transition kernel 的选择高度敏感，设计不当会导致训练不稳定、收敛慢和采样偏差。现有研究缺乏对 kernel 设计原则的系统性理解，需要从理论角度分析不同 kernel 的优劣并寻找更好的折中方案。

Method: 首先通过泛化误差分析识别出三个关键因素：渐近偏差（后验近似难度）、暴露偏差（采样误差传播）和优化方差（kernel 分散度）。对比掩码扩散（后验易近似但修复能力弱）和均匀扩散（修复能力强但后验难近似）后，重新审视语义扩散（SemDLM），其 kernel 将 token 转移到语义相似邻域。针对 SemDLM 的"语义盆地"问题（采样陷入低多样性语义区域），提出 SemDLM+，在采样过程中加入全局转移和语义频率惩罚。

Result: 在 LM1B 和 OpenWebText 数据集上，SemDLM+ 改善了训练动态，在语言建模和文本生成质量上达到有竞争力的水平，同时保持了满意的多样性。

Conclusion: 通过偏差-方差权衡视角系统分析了扩散语言模型的 kernel 设计，验证了语义扩散作为掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间折中方案的可行性，并通过全局转移和频率惩罚有效缓解了语义盆地问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的 transition kernel 设计，提出 SemDLM+ 改进语义扩散语言模型。这属于连续语言模型范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖扩散语言模型和 flow matching language model。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model design via bias-variance trade-off, a core priority subtopic. Signals: diffusion language model, continuous language model, transition kernel design, bias-variance trade-off, semantic diffusion

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have demonstrated strong scaling capacity as alternatives to autoregressive language models. However, their performance is highly sensitive to the choice of transition kernels, and poorly designed kernels can lead to issues like training instability, slow convergence, and biased sampling. In this paper, we study this sensitivity through a principled analysis of generalization error and identify three critical factors: asymptotic bias (difficulty in approximating the posterior distribution), exposure bias (error propagation during sampling), and optimization variance induced by kernel dispersion. We further compare different transition kernels: masking diffusion yields sparse and easier posterior-approximation targets, while uniform diffusion provides stronger sampling-side repair but induces harder approximation. Motivated by this trade-off, we revisit a previously overlooked variant, semantic DLM (SemDLM), where the transition kernel corrupts tokens to neighborhoods that are semantically similar. Our theory suggests that SemDLM can serve as a plausible middle ground by reducing the posterior approximation difficulty of uniform diffusion while retaining repair ability. However, we find that SemDLM suffers from a semantic basin problem, where sampling repeatedly stays within a semantic region and produces low-diversity text. To address this, we propose SemDLM+, which adds a global transition and a semantic-frequency penalty during sampling. Experiments on LM1B and OpenWebText show that SemDLM+ improves training dynamics and achieves competitive language modeling and generation quality with satisfactory diversity.

</details>


### [4] [Who Should Lead Decoding Now? Tracking Reliable Trajectories for Ensembling Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16281)
*Heecheol Yun, Joonhyung Park, Joowon Kim, Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (89.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TIE（Trajectory-based Iterative Ensembling），一种针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的知识融合框架。通过追踪解码过程中答案相关位置的置信度动态，识别可靠轨迹并在不同模型间传递部分去噪序列，实现多模型互补集成。实验表明该方法在多种推理任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在能力和知识覆盖上日益多样化，如何有效融合多个MDLM的知识成为一个重要但尚未充分探索的问题。

Method: 提出 TIE 框架：首先分析 MDLM 的解码动态，发现成功生成在答案相关位置上表现出稳定的置信度动态，而不可靠轨迹可通过注入其他模型的中间状态得到纠正。TIE 迭代地追踪各模型在答案相关位置上的置信度动态，判断哪个模型当前遵循更可靠的轨迹，并选择性地将部分去噪序列从一个模型传递到另一个模型，使不同模型在不同解码阶段贡献互补优势。

Result: 在多种推理任务上取得了强性能，验证了 TIE 作为 MDLM 集成方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TIE 提供了一种实用的 MDLM 集成方法，通过追踪和传递可靠解码轨迹实现多模型知识融合，为扩散语言模型的集成学习开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) 的集成解码方法，属于连续潜空间中的语言建模，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding with a novel ensembling method for masked diffusion models. Signals: continuous language model, masked diffusion language model, knowledge fusion, decoding dynamics, reasoning tasks

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) have emerged as a distinct paradigm for sequence generation. As MDLMs become diverse in capabilities and knowledge coverage, an important question is how to combine their knowledge. Toward this, we first investigate the unique decoding dynamics of MDLMs. We find that successful generations exhibit stable confidence dynamics over answer-relevant positions, while unreliable trajectories can often be corrected by injecting promising intermediate states from other models. Guided by this observation, we propose $\textbf{TIE}$ ($\textbf{T}$rajectory-based $\textbf{I}$terative $\textbf{E}$nsembling), a knowledge fusion framework in which MDLMs iteratively identify reliable decoding trajectories and relay them across models. TIE tracks confidence dynamics over answer-relevant positions to determine which model currently follows a more reliable trajectory and selectively transfers partially denoised sequences across models. As the model on the more promising trajectory often changes across denoising steps, TIE allows different models to contribute complementary strengths at different stages of generation. Strong performance across diverse reasoning tasks, along with our analyses, suggests that TIE offers a practical approach to the underexplored problem of MDLM ensembling.

</details>


### [5] [LESS Is More: Mutual-Stability Sampling for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16908)
*Amr Mohamed, Guokan Shang, Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LESS 提出一种无需训练、模型无关的自适应采样方法，通过互稳定性规则（联合置信度、token 持久性和分布稳定性）动态决定扩散语言模型的去噪步数，在 Dream-7B、LLaDA-8B 等模型上平均提升准确率的同时减少 72.1% 的反向步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLM）在推理时通常使用固定步数的去噪过程，导致计算浪费在已经稳定的位置上，同时可能过早提交不稳定的预测。现有自适应采样方法仍不够高效，需要一种更智能的采样策略来动态决定何时提交 token。

Method: LESS 将 token 提交视为在线停止问题，提出互稳定性采样（mutual-stability sampling）：一个被掩码的位置只有当其 top-1 预测置信度高、top-1 token 在最近几步中持续不变、且预测分布在 top-K 跨步 JS 散度下稳定时，才被允许解除掩码。该方法无需训练，可即插即用于任意扩散语言模型。

Result: 在 Dream-7B、LLaDA-8B 和 LLaDA-1.5-8B 上，覆盖全序列扩散和半自回归块状采样两种范式，在 7 个基准（通用知识、数学、代码）上，LESS 相比强基线自适应采样器提升了平均准确率，同时使用比固定预算解码少 72.1% 的反向步数，从而降低了延迟和推理计算量。

Conclusion: LESS 是一种高效、训练无关的自适应采样方法，能显著减少扩散语言模型的推理步数而不牺牲性能，为扩散语言模型的实际部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（dLLM）的自适应采样方法，属于连续潜空间中的语言建模，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model efficiency via training-free adaptive sampling, aligning with top-priority subtopic and boost keywords. Signals: diffusion language model, training-free adaptive sampling, mutual-stability rule, step reduction 72.1%, continuous_language_multimodal direction

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive decoding by iteratively refining masked sequences, enabling parallel token updates and bidirectional conditioning. Their practical efficiency, however, is limited by sampling procedures that execute a fixed number of reverse denoising steps selected before decoding, spending computation on already-stable positions and sometimes committing unstable ones too early. We present \textsc{LESS}, a training-free, model-agnostic adaptive sampler that treats token commitment as an online stopping problem. \textsc{LESS} implements mutual-stability sampling through a joint stability rule that makes a masked position eligible for unmasking only when its top-1 prediction has high confidence, its top-1 token persists across recent reverse steps, and its predictive distribution is stable under top-$K$ inter-step Jensen--Shannon divergence. We evaluate \textsc{LESS} on Dream-7B, LLaDA-8B, and LLaDA-1.5-8B, covering full-sequence diffusion and semi-autoregressive blockwise sampling regimes, across seven benchmarks spanning general knowledge, math, and code. \textsc{LESS} improves average accuracy over strong training-free adaptive samplers while using $72.1\%$ fewer reverse steps than fixed-budget decoding. Since each reverse step requires a Transformer forward pass, these step-count reductions translate into fewer forward evaluations, lower measured wall-clock latency, and lower estimated inference compute.

</details>

### [6] [VANDERER: Map-Free Exploration using Future-Aware and Visual-Curiosity-Guided Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14879)
*Venkata Naren Devarakonda, Raktim Gautam Goswami, Prashanth Krishnamurthy, Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: VANDERER 是一个面向单目相机移动机器人的无地图探索框架，通过视觉好奇心模块（VCM）评估动作的好奇心代价，并引导预训练扩散策略生成最大化探索的动作，在多种模拟环境中平均比 NoMaD 多探索 13.4% 的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动机器人探索方法依赖生成占用地图并优化未探索区域的访问顺序，但在仅限单目相机等传感器受限场景下，生成准确占用地图非常困难。因此需要一种不依赖显式地图、仅利用单目图像数据的高效探索方法。

Method: 提出 VANDERER 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 视觉好奇心模块（VCM），包含一个导航世界模型用于预测动作的未来观测结果，并通过好奇心代价函数评估动作的探索价值；(2) 预训练的扩散策略，在好奇心代价引导下生成探索动作。整个流程仅使用单目图像数据，无需显式建图。

Result: 在多种模拟环境中，VANDERER 平均比 NoMaD 多探索 13.4% 的区域，持续优于多个基线方法。实验还揭示了室外环境中视觉好奇心与几何好奇心之间的直接相关性。

Conclusion: VANDERER 证明了在传感器受限条件下，通过好奇心引导的扩散策略可以有效实现无地图探索，为移动机器人在未知环境中的自主探索提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出移动机器人探索框架 VANDERER，利用视觉好奇心模块（VCM）引导扩散策略生成探索动作，其中包含一个导航世界模型（navigation world model）来预测动作结果。这属于具身智能中机器人探索与规划控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied exploration with diffusion policy, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, diffusion policy, visual curiosity, map-free exploration, monocular navigation

Abstract: Mobile agents require efficient exploration strategies to map unseen environments and autonomously plan tasks. Traditional methods rely on generating occupancy maps and optimizing the sequence in which unexplored regions are visited. However, in sensor-constrained settings, such as those limited to monocular cameras, generating accurate occupancy maps is challenging. To address this, we propose VANDERER, an exploration framework that leverages a Visual Curiosity Module (VCM) to guide pre-trained diffusion policies using only monocular image data. This curiosity module predicts the outcomes of proposed actions via a navigation world model and evaluates them through a curiosity cost. The cost then guides the diffusion process toward generating actions that maximize exploration. Evaluated across diverse simulated environments, VANDERER consistently outperforms established baselines, exploring an average of 13.4% more area than NoMaD. Our results reveal a direct correlation between visual and geometric curiosity in outdoor environments, demonstrating that VANDERER can effectively leverage this relationship for efficient exploration using sensor-constrained agents.

</details>


### [7] [Inference-time Policy Steering via Vision and Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14981)
*Yilin Wu, Zilin Si, Zeynep Temel, Oliver Kroemer, Andrea Bajcsy*

TL;DR: ViTaL 提出了一种视觉-触觉推理时引导框架，通过高层视觉采样验证进行长程行为模式选择，低层触觉引导扩散编辑满足局部接触需求，并学习视觉-触觉潜空间世界模型和文本条件触觉奖励函数，在接触丰富的机器人操作任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法仅依赖视觉观测验证候选动作，但对于接触丰富的操作任务，仅靠视觉不足以判断成功与否——成功既取决于全局任务进展，也依赖于接触力等细微局部交互。因此需要引入触觉模态来弥补视觉的不足。

Method: ViTaL 将多模态引导建模为双层优化问题：高层通过视觉采样与验证进行长程模式选择（决定机器人应执行什么行为）；低层通过触觉引导的扩散编辑在更短时域内精炼所选动作序列以满足局部接触要求。同时学习一个视觉-触觉潜空间世界模型，并采用语义对齐的视觉和触觉验证器，包括一个新颖的文本条件触觉奖励函数，直接在潜空间中对预测的触觉未来进行评分。

Result: 在三个真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ViTaL 相比基础策略整体成功率提升 51%，优于单模态引导至少 33%，超过朴素多模态融合至少 20%。

Conclusion: ViTaL 证明了在接触丰富的机器人操作中，融合视觉和触觉模态进行推理时引导能够显著提升策略的鲁棒性和成功率，为多模态感知在具身智能中的应用提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ViTaL，一个视觉-触觉推理时引导框架，通过双层级优化（高层视觉采样验证做长程模式选择，低层触觉引导扩散编辑做短程接触优化）来引导预训练机器人策略。论文核心涉及具身智能中的世界模型（visuo-tactile latent world model）、动作规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong world model and multimodal reasoning for contact-rich manipulation, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, visuo-tactile steering, bi-level optimization, diffusion editing, contact-rich manipulation

Abstract: Inference-time steering adapts pre-trained generative robot policies during deployment by verifying candidate actions before execution. While prior methods typically perform this verification only with visual observations, vision alone is often insufficient for contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on both global task progress and subtle local interactions such as contact force. We introduce ViTaL, a visuo-tactile inference-time steering framework that formulates multimodal guidance as a bi-level optimization problem. At the high level, visual sampling-and-verification performs long-horizon mode selection, deciding what behavior the robot should execute. At the low level, tactile-guided diffusion editing refines the selected action sequence over a shorter horizon to satisfy local contact requirements. To support outcome-based steering, ViTaL learns a visuo-tactile latent world model and employs semantically aligned visual and tactile verifiers, including a novel text-conditioned tactile reward that scores predicted tactile futures directly in latent space. Across three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ViTaL improves overall success by 51% over the base policy, outperforms unimodal steering by at least 33%, and exceeds naive multimodal fusion by at least 20%. Website: .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Token Steering (TS)，一种在推理时通过直接干预自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的动作 token 空间来动态引导轨迹生成的方法。TS 将低维用户输入注入模型的原生动作 token 表示，无需额外训练或微调，即可在保持 VLA 模型灵巧性和任务先验的同时引导机器人动作。在两个家庭操作任务上，TS 将成功率从 10.0% 提升至 72.5%，从 16.7% 提升至 93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型虽然能够学习丰富的任务先验和灵巧操作能力，但缺乏在推理时让用户动态干预和引导轨迹生成的能力。用户希望能在不重新训练模型的前提下，对机器人行为进行轻量级、直观的引导，以提升人机交互的灵活性和可访问性。

Method: Token Steering (TS) 方法在推理时通过向自回归 VLA 模型的动作 token 序列中注入低维用户输入（如操纵杆信号），直接干预动作 token 空间。用户输入被映射到模型的原生动作 token 表示空间，以引导而非覆盖预训练策略的生成轨迹。该方法完全在推理时运行，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在两个家庭操作任务上：1) 放置物体后关闭抽屉任务，成功率从 10.0% 提升至 72.5%；2) 状态感知物体交换任务，成功率从 16.7% 提升至 93.8%。实验表明 TS 能有效提升任务成功率，同时保持 VLA 模型的灵巧性和任务先验。

Conclusion: Token Steering 提供了一种轻量级、直观的机器人基础模型操控接口，通过推理时动作 token 干预实现用户引导的轨迹生成，显著提升了人机交互效果，并有望扩大机器人技术在消费环境中的可访问性，尤其适用于身体控制能力有限的用户。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Token Steering 方法，通过干预自回归 VLA 模型的动作 token 空间来动态引导机器人轨迹生成，属于机器人具身策略控制与人类交互的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot control steering, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Autoregressive VLA, action token intervention, inference-time steering, household manipulation, no training required

Abstract: We present Token Steering (TS), a method for dynamically steering trajectories generated by an autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) model through direct intervention in the action-token space. TS injects low-dimensional user inputs into the model's native action-token representation, allowing users to influence trajectory generation without modifying the underlying vision-language model (VLM) architecture. Because TS operates entirely at inference time, it requires no additional training or finetuning. User inputs guide rather than override the pretrained policy, allowing users to influence robot actions while preserving the dexterity, smoothness, and task priors learned by the VLA. We evaluate TS on two household manipulation tasks -- drawer closing after object placement and state-aware object swapping -- and improve success rates from 10.0% to 72.5% and from 16.7% to 93.8%, respectively. By enabling lightweight, intuitive steering over robot foundation models, our interface has the potential to improve human-robot interaction in consumer environments and broaden accessibility for individuals with limited physical control. Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

TL;DR: DragMesh-2 是一个面向灵巧手与铰接物体交互的接触驱动框架，通过物理信息感知训练机制 PICA 在无触觉/力反馈条件下提升策略鲁棒性，在多种阻尼条件下实现更强的接触负载鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体（如抽屉、门）的灵巧操作不同于静态物体操作：目标部件无法直接驱动，其运动必须通过手与手柄的持续物理接触产生。现有方法多采用几何轨迹回放或开环执行，无法建模接触动力学；且策略在固定动力学下训练容易过拟合标称接触负载，在接触负载变化时性能下降。

Method: 提出 DragMesh-2 框架，将铰接交互从物体中心生成扩展到手驱动的灵巧手-物体交互，使铰接运动必须通过物理接触产生。进一步提出 PICA（Physically Informed Contact-Aware）训练机制，在无触觉/力反馈条件下将物理信号注入策略学习，提升接触负载变化下的鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Result: 在 7 个 GAPartNet 物体上，DragMesh-2 在接触负载变化下比对比方法具有更强的鲁棒性，同时在多种阻尼条件下保持高任务成功率。

Conclusion: DragMesh-2 有效解决了从物体中心铰接生成到手驱动灵巧交互的过渡问题，PICA 机制在无触觉反馈下提升了策略对接触负载变化的鲁棒性，为未来人形机器人手-物体交互研究提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究灵巧手与铰接物体的交互，涉及物理接触动力学、策略学习与鲁棒控制，属于具身智能中机器人操作与规划控制的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous hand-object interaction with articulated objects, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world_model, dexterous manipulation, articulated objects, contact dynamics, PICA

Abstract: Dexterous interaction with articulated objects is important for household, assistive, and humanoid manipulation, where multi-finger hands can provide compliant contact patterns beyond parallel-jaw grasping. However, articulated-object manipulation differs from static-object manipulation: the target part cannot be directly actuated, and its motion must emerge through sustained physical hand--handle contact. This makes the transition from object-centric articulated generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction non-trivial, since geometric trajectory replay or open-loop execution does not model the contact dynamics required to move the articulated part. Moreover, policies trained only for task completion under fixed dynamics can overfit nominal contact loads, especially without tactile or force feedback, and may degrade when the contact load changes. To address these challenges, we present DragMesh-2, a contact-driven framework for dexterous interaction with articulated objects that extends articulated interaction from object-centric generation to hand-driven dexterous hand--object interaction, where articulated motion must arise through physical contact. We further propose PICA, a physically informed contact-aware training mechanism that injects physical signals into policy learning without tactile or force feedback, improving robustness and task success under changing contact loads. Finally, we conduct systematic evaluation across multiple damping conditions and articulated-object categories to study robustness under contact-load variation, and provide a pure-geometry dexterous interaction resource to support future loco-manipulation and humanoid hand--object interaction research. Across seven GAPartNet objects, DragMesh-2 achieves stronger robustness under contact-load variation than the compared methods while maintaining high task success across damping conditions.

</details>

*Bharawee Phoompho, Gokul Puthumanaillam, Yan Miao, Ruben Hernandez, Tim Bretl, Sayan Mitra, Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoDA，一种基于屏蔽条件扩散模型的任务感知环境增强方法，通过在有预算限制的条件下智能放置视觉地标，提升机器人在部分可观测环境中的轨迹执行可靠性和完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，机器人轨迹规划的可靠性不仅取决于几何路径的可行性，还取决于执行过程中能否获得足够的信息观测。现有方法通常固定环境、通过信念空间规划或主动定位来适应机器人，在观测匮乏区域表现脆弱且计算成本高。本文提出反转思路：通过任务感知的环境增强（在环境中智能放置视觉地标）来提升轨迹执行可靠性。

Method: 提出SCoDA（Shielded Conditional Diffusion for Environment Augmentation），学习一个条件扩散模型，以环境地图、规划轨迹、扰动上下文和期望执行剖面为条件，生成高性能的地标布局。其屏蔽采样器（shielded sampler）推理沿轨迹应在何处进行位姿修正，并将生成分布引导至任务相关且符合有限预算的增强方案。

Result: 在模拟基准测试和硬件部署实验中，SCoDA在轨迹执行可靠性和完成时间上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 任务感知的环境增强是一种有效的替代思路，通过少量精心放置的视觉地标即可显著提升机器人在部分可观测环境中的轨迹执行可靠性，SCoDA证明了条件扩散模型在该问题上的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究在部分可观测环境下，通过条件扩散模型学习地标布局来增强环境，以提升机器人轨迹规划的可靠性。这属于机器人规划与控制中的环境建模与不确定性管理问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Applies diffusion for robot navigation augmentation, but is far from core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model application, shielded conditional diffusion, task-aware augmentation, robotics navigation, partial observability

Abstract: Reliable trajectory planning under partial observability depends not only on computing a feasible geometric path, but also on whether the robot receives informative observations while executing that trajectory. Existing approaches usually keep the environment fixed and adapt the robot through belief-space planning, active localization, or added sensing, often incurring costly uncertainty propagation and brittle behavior in observation-poor regions. We flip this perspective and address the largely open problem of \emph{task-aware environment augmentation}: given a mapped environment, a planned task trajectory, and a small budget of visual fiducial markers, where should the environment be augmented so that the planned trajectory can be executed reliably under uncertainty? Our key observation is that useful marker layouts are defined by the localization support they provide along the task trajectory: a small number of well-timed observations can be sufficient to prevent uncertainty from accumulating in regions where state-estimation error would otherwise compromise control. Building on this observation, we present \tbp{SCoDA}, $\textbf{S}$hielded $\textbf{Co}$nditional $\textbf{D}$iffusion for Environment $\textbf{A}$ugmentation. \tbp{SCoDA} learns a conditional distribution over high-performing fiducial layouts from data, using the environment, planned trajectory, disturbance context, and desired execution profile as conditioning. Its shielded sampler reasons over where along the planned execution pose corrections should occur, and steers this distribution toward task-relevant, finite-budget augmentations. Across simulated benchmarks and hardware deployments, we show that \tbp{SCoDA} improves trajectory execution reliability and completion time over strong baselines. Code, models and dataset available at: \hyperlink{ }{ }

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管spatial softmax pooling已被广泛用于视觉运动策略，但其有效性和底层机制尚未被充分理解。本文旨在系统研究这种隐式空间表征在机器人操作中的作用机制及其局限性。

Result: PRISM在多个任务和策略骨干上取得一致改进。在低分辨率高精度的ToolHang任务上，平均成功率从5.0%提升至13.4%，参数量仅增加15.4%。

Conclusion: 多尺度隐式空间表征是机器人操作中有效且高效的设计原则，PRISM验证了通过保留多尺度空间信息可以缓解视觉编码器的表征瓶颈。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic visuomotor policy, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: spatial softmax pooling, implicit spatial representation, visuomotor policy, multiscale fusion, low-resolution manipulation

Abstract: Generative model-based imitation learning has become a widely adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation, where policy performance depends critically on the conditioned visual representations. Although spatial softmax-based representations have been adopted in prior visuomotor policies, their effectiveness and underlying mechanisms remain insufficiently understood. This work rethinks the use of spatial softmax pooling: do such implicit spatial representations provide effective and stable visual features for robotic manipulation? Through systematic studies of different pooling methods in visual encoders, we find that this pooling operation produces compact and stable spatial representations, which outperform feature-value representations, despite using substantially fewer dimensions. Complementary saliency analysis further suggests that these spatial representations guide the encoder to focus more consistently on task-relevant regions. However, this advantage is limited by a representation bottleneck in current visual encoders: repeated downsampling operations weaken fine-grained spatial information before the action-generation module can use it, especially under low-resolution observations. Motivated by these findings, we propose PRISM, a visual encoder that preserves multiscale implicit spatial information through top-down cross-attention fusion. Experiments across multiple tasks and policy backbones show consistent improvements. In particular, on the low-resolution, high-precision ToolHang task, PRISM shows clear gains, improving the average success rate from 5.0% to 13.4% while increasing parameters by only 15.4%. These results support the use of multiscale implicit spatial representations as an effective and efficient design principle for robotic manipulation.

</details>

Signals: robotics VLA, real-time control, asynchronous decoupling, semantic-action interface, high-frequency action module

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have demonstrated strong task understanding and generalization in robotic manipulation, yet the high computational cost of full-model inference limits their deployment in low-latency, high-frequency closed-loop control. We propose an asynchronous semantic-action decoupling framework that separates semantic understanding from action generation along the internal semantic-action interface of existing VLAs, without redesigning the vision-language backbone or introducing an external planner. A low-frequency understanding module asynchronously updates reusable semantic conditions, while a high-frequency action module continuously outputs control actions without repeatedly invoking the full model. To mitigate the temporal mismatch between stale semantics and the current execution state, we further introduce historical action conditioning and time-misalignment training, which provide short-horizon execution context and improve feedback control robustness under stale semantic conditions. Experiments on LIBERO with $\pi_{0.5}$ and UniVLA, together with real-robot deployment using UniVLA, show that the proposed framework achieves up to 35.6 Hz server-side action-module inference throughput and offers a low-intrusion path to high-frequency closed-loop control without running full VLA inference at control rate.

</details>

Conclusion: 基于神经ODE的上下文感知动力学模型能够有效推断环境变化并自适应调整控制策略，为机器人在不确定动态环境中的可靠控制提供了一种可行的解决方案，且具有良好的跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic systems deployed in uncertain and dynamically changing environments often face variations in contact conditions, aerodynamic effects, and external disturbances that challenge reliable control. To remain effective under model-based control, these systems require dynamics models that can adapt to such changes, especially when direct access to complete environmental information is limited. To enable adaptability and facilitate integration with model predictive control, we propose a context-aware dynamics model based on neural ordinary differential equations, which infers environmental factors from state-action histories using a two-phase training procedure. We validate the approach across diverse robotic platforms, including a quadrotor in simulation, as well as a Sphero BOLT robot and a Fanuc manipulator in real-world experiments. The results demonstrate that our method effectively adapts to temporally and spatially varying environmental changes across different tasks. Videos are available at , and the source code is available at .

Importance reason: Novel token-based multi-robot coordination via diffusion transformer, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: multi-robot trajectory generation, diffusion transformer, token-based representation, coordinated planning, conditional generation

Abstract: The success of generative models in language and visual generation has inspired extensive applications to generative robot planning. However, most existing works either focus on single-robot planning, or generate multi-robot trajectories in a sequential manner with iterative post-processing to resolve inter-robot conflicts. In this work, we investigate whether coordinated multi-robot trajectories, as a special spatiotemporal distribution, can be learned and generated with a generative model in a feed-forward manner. We propose Robots as Tokens (Roken), a unified diffusion transformer that directly generates multi-robot trajectories that satisfy both (individual) safety and (global) connectivity constraints. The core design of Roken is to represent each robot as a discrete token, allowing them to naturally interact with each other through self-attention, and cross-attend to map tokens for environment layouts. We further introduce several auxiliary tasks based on Bayes' theorem to provide multi-scale spatial-temporal supervision for efficient learning of the conditional distribution. In training, Roken absorbs diverse expert trajectories from different team sizes. During inference, Roken behaves as a versatile multi-robot planner that can handle single-robot planning, coordinated multi-robot trajectory generation, and conditional trajectory generation by fixing some robot tokens as conditions. Experiments in diverse cluttered environments show that Roken can generate coordinated multi-robot trajectories to perform connectivity-constrained goal navigation tasks with high success rates, outperforming the baseline method used to generate the training dataset. Roken also demonstrates good scalability after training with mixed team sizes, and shows generalization to unseen or partially observed environments, verifying its potential to learn from diverse data and perform versatile tasks.

</details>

Conclusion: SLS^2框架通过将共形预测与鲁棒MPC结合，有效弥合了潜空间世界模型预测与真实系统行为之间的差距，为基于视觉的安全控制提供了概率可证的安全保证。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SLS^2框架，利用学习的潜空间世界模型进行基于视觉的安全反馈运动规划，结合鲁棒模型预测控制（MPC）和共形预测来保证安全性。这直接属于世界模型方向中的"具身世界模型与规划控制"（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题，该子主题涵盖面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型。

Importance reason: Strong world model safety contribution but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: World model safety, Conformal prediction, Robust MPC, Latent space planning, Vision-based control

Abstract: We present SLS^2, a framework for safe feedback motion planning from pixels using robust model predictive control (MPC) in learned latent world models. Our approach trains an action-conditioned joint-embedding world model with compact Markovian latent states, enabling efficient gradient-based trajectory optimization through learned latent dynamics. To enforce safety for the true system despite imperfect latent predictions, we inform a GPU-accelerated system level synthesis (SLS) robust MPC scheme with conformal prediction to obtain calibrated latent error bounds and robust latent-space constraint sets. We further learn and conformalize a latent constraint checker, allowing the SLS planner to impose probabilistic safety constraints during closed-loop execution. We evaluate our method on vision-based control tasks, where it improves both goal-reaching performance and safety over latent world-model and safe-planning baselines.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA策略扩展到新任务需要任务特定的遥操作示教数据和逐任务微调，数据采集和计算成本高昂。本文旨在用检索替代目标侧逐任务适配成本，实现一次训练、冻结模型、通过索引新数据即可吸收新任务。

Method: 提出检索增强策略Cosmos Policy：1）在目标具身（query）和低成本具身（pool，如人手视频）的配对示教数据上训练一次后冻结模型；2）新任务通过向检索池添加pool侧示教数据来添加，无需更新参数；3）冻结策略在每个控制步条件化于检索到的轨迹；4）利用视频生成式世界动作模型（WAM）的未来图像目标提供额外的视觉一致性信号，增强检索条件化动作。

Result: 在PushT上验证了检索提供可复用的高层运动先验，实现跨具身泛化到未见目标角度；在RoboTwin 2.0上超越跨具身基线方法；并在真实机器人上进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: 检索可以替代逐任务微调，使VLA策略高效扩展到新任务；结合视频生成式世界模型的视觉一致性信号，检索增强策略在跨具身泛化上表现尤为突出。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于检索增强的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，用于机器人具身任务的新任务泛化，核心是具身智能中的动作规划与控制，属于[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题。

Signals: retrieval-augmented VLA, zero-shot task generalization, world-action model, cross-embodiment, efficient adaptation

Abstract: Extending a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to a new task typically requires task-specific teleoperated demonstrations and per-task fine-tuning, making adaptation costly in both data collection and compute. In this paper, we show that this target-side per-task adaptation cost can be replaced by retrieval. Our retrieval-augmented policy is trained once on paired demonstrations from the target embodiment (query) and a cheaper embodiment (pool, e.g., human-hand video), then frozen. New tasks are added at deployment by appending pool-side demonstrations to a retrieval pool. The frozen policy conditions on retrieved trajectories at every control step, so new tasks are absorbed by indexing data rather than updating parameters. Fine-tuning is needed only to take on a new, unseen embodiment, not for each new task. We show that retrieval improves policies beyond a specific backbone, including standard VLA policies, but its effect is especially pronounced in Cosmos Policy, a video-generation-based world-action model (WAM). In this setting, retrieval supplies coarse task progression, while the WAM's future-image objective provides an additional visual consistency signal that strengthens the retrieval-conditioned actions. On PushT, we study how retrieval provides a reusable high-level motion prior for cross-embodiment generalization to unseen goal angles, while on RoboTwin 2.0 our method outperforms cross-embodiment baselines on unseen tasks, and we additionally demonstrate the method on a real robot.

</details>

Importance reason: Strong robotics planning paper but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative video/world models. Signals: Symbolic POMDP, Robot planning under uncertainty, Visual demonstration learning, PDDL extension, Real-world manipulation

Abstract: Real-world robot task planning must operate under both stochastic action execution and partial observability, yet constructing Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) models for real robotics domains remains difficult and labor-intensive. We introduce PO-PDDL, a symbolic formulation of POMDPs that preserves the relational structure and LLM-friendly syntax of the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL), while explicitly modeling partial observability, stochasticity, and beliefs. Building on this formulation, we propose a demonstration-driven pipeline for learning PO-PDDL models. The proposed method reconstructs latent symbolic state trajectories from real-robot execution videos, identifies partial observability via inconsistencies between inferred states and visual observations, and learns stochastic transition and observation models accordingly. The resulting PO-PDDL domains are reusable across tasks and enable online belief-space planning under both perception and execution uncertainty. Experiments on real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks show that our method consistently outperforms existing PDDL and POMDP model-learning approaches, achieving robust task planning under uncertainty with significantly lower planning cost.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身持续学习（ECL）中，机器人在闭环控制下持续学习新操作任务时，特征漂移会通过顺序决策逐步累积，导致已有行为严重遗忘。现有方法缺乏对技能的显式组织和结构化复用，难以实现连贯的任务执行。

Method: 提出 SCE 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Compositional Skill Grounding (CSG)，将任务演示分解为可复用的技能基元；(2) Dual Execution-and-Transition Experts (DETE)，一个分支负责技能执行，另一个分支负责技能间转换，通过技能组合实现新任务学习。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准和真实机器人操作任务上，SCE 在保留旧任务性能和新任务学习方面均优于基线方法，特征漂移分析和消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: SCE 通过显式的技能分解与组合专家机制，有效缓解了具身持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，为机器人在动态环境中持续积累操作技能提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身持续学习（Embodied Continual Learning），面向机器人操作任务的技能复用与组合，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied continual learning and skill composition, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and generative video/image models. Signals: Embodied continual learning, Skill composition, Catastrophic forgetting, Robotics manipulation, LIBERO benchmark

Abstract: Embodied Continual Learning (ECL) aims to enable robots to continually acquire new manipulation tasks while retaining previously learned behaviors under closed-loop control. Compared with conventional continual learning, ECL suffers from more severe catastrophic forgetting. Feature drift accumulated under closed-loop control progressively propagates through sequential decision-making, leading to degradation of previously learned behaviors. A key challenge in ECL lies in structured skill reuse across continually evolving tasks, since existing methods primarily focus on skill learning without explicitly organizing them for coherent task execution. To address this issue, we propose SCE, a Skill-Compositional Experts framework for ECL. SCE builds a skill base via Compositional Skill Grounding (CSG), which decomposes task demonstrations into reusable skills. Based on this, Dual Execution-and-Transition Experts (DETE) enable new task learning through skill composition, where one branch ensures skill execution and the other supports transitions between skills for coherent behavior. Experiments on LIBERO benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SCE consistently improves retention and overall task performance. Further feature drift analyses and ablation studies verify the effectiveness of our method. Project website: .

</details>


### [19] [LaWAM: Latent World Action Models for Efficient Dynamics-Aware Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15768)
*Jialei Chen, Kai Wang, Kang Chen, Shuaihang Chen, Feng Gao, Wenhao Tang, Zhiyuan Li, Weilin Liu, Zhuyu Yao, Boxun Li, Yuanbo Xu, Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LaWAM 提出了一种潜空间世界动作模型，利用预训练视觉基础模型的潜空间训练潜世界模型（LaWM），通过预测潜视觉子目标而非重建未来视频来为机器人策略提供动力学感知，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 和真实世界操作任务上达到 SOTA 或竞争性成功率，同时推理延迟低至 187ms，比像素空间 WAM 快 24 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）虽然利用大规模视觉-语言预训练实现语义控制，但缺乏对动作如何改变场景的显式预测能力。世界动作模型（WAM）通过条件化策略于预测的未来状态来解决此问题，但现有方法依赖计算昂贵的像素级视频生成，存在大量冗余。因此，作者希望设计一种更高效的潜空间世界模型，在保持动力学感知能力的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Method: LaWAM 的核心是一个潜动作条件潜世界模型（LaWM）。首先在预训练视觉基础模型（如 DINOv2）的潜空间中训练一个潜动作模型，然后利用其前向解码器预测未来观测特征（潜视觉子目标）。最后，LaWAM 将这些预测的潜视觉子目标作为条件输入到动作生成策略中，实现动力学感知的机器人控制。整个流程在潜空间中进行，避免了像素级视频重建。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上达到 98.6% 的成功率，在 RoboTwin 上达到 91.22% 的成功率，并在真实世界操作任务中取得竞争性表现。推理延迟为 187ms 每动作块预测，相比像素空间 WAM 实现了最高 24 倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: LaWAM 证明了在潜空间中进行世界建模和动力学感知策略学习是高效且有效的，能够在保持高成功率的同时大幅降低计算开销，为具身智能中的世界模型应用提供了更实用的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LaWAM，一种潜空间世界动作模型，通过预测潜视觉子目标实现具身机器人策略的动力学感知控制，属于面向机器人动作规划与控制的世界模型，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Latent world model for efficient robot policy aligns with user's priority on world models and continuous latent representations. Signals: latent world model, efficient inference 187ms, 24x faster than pixel-space, world model for robot policy, pretrained vision foundation model latent space

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for semantic robot control, but often lack explicit foresight into how robot actions change the scene. World-Action Models (WAMs) address this limitation by conditioning policies on predicted futures, yet existing approaches typically rely on computationally expensive video generation with substantial pixel-level redundancy. We present LaWAM, a Latent World Action Model that exposes predictive dynamics to robot policies through compact latent visual subgoals instead of reconstructed future video. At the core of LaWAM is a latent-action-conditioned Latent World Model (LaWM). We obtain LaWM by training a latent action model in the latent space of a pretrained vision foundation model and repurposing its forward decoder to predict future observation features for scene evolution. LaWAM then conditions action generation on these predicted latent visual subgoals to enable dynamics-aware robot control. LaWAM achieves state-of-the-art or competitive success rates (SRs) across LIBERO (98.6% SR), RoboTwin (91.22% SR), and real-world manipulation tasks while retaining low-latency inference. LaWAM runs in 187 ms per action-chunk prediction and achieves up to 24x lower wall-clock latency than pixel-space WAMs.

</details>


### [20] [VL2Spike: Spike-driven Distillation from VLMs for Low-Power Visual Perception in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15898)
*Zinan Liu, Eric Zheng, Soumyaratna Debnath, Hao Shi, Ling Xiao, Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VL2Spike提出了一种基于脉冲的知识蒸馏框架，将VLM的多模态知识迁移到紧凑的Spikformer模型中，通过时空视觉脉冲蒸馏和脉冲原型引导的语言蒸馏策略，在仅消耗15.7%能量的情况下，在静态数据集上提升6.81%，在机器人视觉位置识别任务上提升6.63%，为具身智能的低功耗视觉感知提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络(SNN)具有低功耗优势，但受限于脉冲架构和优化困难，性能不如传统深度网络。视觉语言模型(VLM)具备丰富的多模态知识表征能力，但计算开销大。如何将VLM的知识高效迁移到SNN中，在保持低功耗优势的同时提升感知能力，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出VL2Spike框架，包含两个关键技术：(1) 时空视觉脉冲蒸馏(SVS)，通过共享流形对齐对齐VLM图像特征与脉冲token，并利用暖启动机制保持膜电位和脉冲率的时间一致性；(2) 脉冲原型引导的语言蒸馏(SPL)，将Spikformer的类别原型和logits与可提示的VLM文本嵌入对齐。

Result: 在三个静态数据集上平均提升6.81%，仅消耗15.7%的能量；在机器人视觉位置识别(VPR)任务上提升6.63%，展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VL2Spike成功将VLM的多模态知识蒸馏到Spikformer中，在显著降低能耗的同时提升了视觉感知性能，为资源受限的具身智能系统提供了低功耗视觉感知的实用方案。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于面向具身智能的低功耗视觉感知，通过脉冲神经网络蒸馏VLM知识提升Spikformer在机器人视觉感知中的性能，属于具身智能环境感知与模型优化范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on spike-based distillation for embodied AI, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Spike-driven distillation, low-power embodied perception, Spikformer, VLM knowledge transfer, robotic visual place recognition

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are brain-inspired, event-driven models that compute with sparse spikes, which enables highly efficient visual perception in resource-constrained embodied AI models. The emergence of Spiking-Transformer models with spike self-attention has substantially improved the learning capacity of pure SNNs. Although SNNs are energy efficient, their performance is still limited by the spike-based architecture and optimization challenges, as standard gradient descent rules cannot be directly applied. Recently, vision-language models (VLMs) have shown rich multi-modal knowledge representation capabilities for visual perception. Thus, it is promising to leverage VLMs for better Spikformer training. To this end, we present VL2Spike, a novel spike-based knowledge distillation (KD) framework that bridges multi-modal knowledge from VLMs with compact Spikformer models. This design enhances the learning capacity of Spikformer models while preserving their energy-efficiency merits, thereby offering a practical pathway toward low-power robotic perception. Our VL2Spike brings two key technical contributions. To align with spiking dynamics, we first propose spatial-temporal visual spike (SVS) distillation, which achieves (1) shared manifold alignment between VLM image features and spike tokens, and (2) warm-started temporal consistency on membrane potentials and spike rates. We then design a novel spike prototype-guided linguistic (SPL) distillation strategy that aligns Spikformer's class prototypes and logits with promptable VLM text embeddings. Extensive experiments show that VL2Spike achieves 6.81% gain across three static datasets with only 15.7% energy consumption. It also exhibits strong generalization capacity on robotic visual place recognition (VPR) with a gain of 6.63%, highlighting its potential for low-power perception in embodied AI.

</details>


### [21] [QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801)
*Yifan Ruan, Chenyang Cao, Andreas Burger, Ali Pesaranghader, Kaveh Kamali, Jaehong Kim, Nandita Vijaykumar, Alan Aspuru-Guzik, Igor Gilitschenski, Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (65.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: QPILOTS提出了一种测试时Q值引导的流策略（flow policy）推理方法，通过将去噪中间状态投影到最终干净动作估计上计算critic梯度，在不修改原始策略的情况下实现高效的动作引导，在50个任务的离线到在线RL基准上达到90%平均成功率，并成功应用于冻结的VLA基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配和扩散策略是强大的动作生成器，但使用时序差分RL优化它们很困难。现有方法要么丢弃梯度信息、要么将策略蒸馏为单步actor、要么反复微调去噪策略，这些方法存在数值不稳定或效率低的问题。作者希望在不修改原始策略的前提下，在推理时利用critic梯度引导去噪过程。

Method: QPILOTS在去噪的每一步，不直接在噪声中间动作上评估critic（因为critic预测不可靠），而是先将中间状态投影到最终干净动作的估计上，再在该估计上计算critic梯度来引导去噪方向。提出两种变体：QPILOTS-U使用快速单点近似，QPILOTS-M通过学习的辅助网络生成可微的后验样本。

Result: 在标准离线到在线RL基准上，QPILOTS在50个任务中达到90%的平均成功率，取得最佳综合性能。在冻结的VLA基础模型上，QPILOTS在6个模拟操作任务中优于或持平于现有的推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: QPILOTS提供了一种高效、无需修改原始策略的测试时Q值引导方法，能够有效提升流策略的性能，并适用于大规模预训练VLA模型，为机器人策略的推理时优化提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出QPILOTS方法，在推理时通过critic梯度引导流匹配/扩散策略（flow policy）的生成过程，用于机器人动作生成与规划。论文在离线到在线RL基准和VLA基础模型上验证，属于具身智能中动作策略优化的范畴，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Novel test-time Q-steering for flow policies, but focuses on robotics/VLA rather than core continuous language or video generation priorities. Signals: flow policy steering, test-time inference, critic gradient projection, offline-to-online RL, VLA foundation model

Abstract: Flow-matching and diffusion policies are expressive action generators, but optimizing them with temporal-difference reinforcement learning (RL) remains difficult. Effective policy extraction requires exploiting the critic's action gradient, yet directly backpropagating this signal through a multi-step denoising process can be numerically unstable. Existing methods work around this either by discarding gradient information, distilling the policy into a simpler one-step actor, or repeatedly fine-tuning the denoising policy as the critic improves. We propose QPILOTS, a method that leaves the original policy unmodified and steers the denoising process at inference time. At each denoising step, instead of evaluating the critic on the noisy intermediate action where critic predictions are unreliable, we first project that intermediate state to an estimate of the final clean action and compute the critic gradient there. We introduce two variants: QPILOTS-U uses a fast single-point approximation, while QPILOTS-M draws differentiable posterior samples via a learned auxiliary network. On a standard offline-to-online RL benchmark, QPILOTS achieves the best aggregate performance, reaching an average success rate of 90% across 50 tasks. We also apply QPILOTS to steer a large, frozen, pretrained Vision-Language Action (VLA) foundation model, outperforming or matching prior inference-time approaches across six manipulation tasks in simulation.

</details>


### [22] [PolyMerge: Compressing 3D Gaussian Splats with Polytope Coverings for Provably Safe Resource-Constrained Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16232)
*Jihoon Hong, Chih-Yuan Chiu, Sara Fridovich-Keil, Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: PolyMerge 提出将大规模3DGS场景模型压缩为凸多面体覆盖表示，保证对障碍物的保守逼近，并与控制障碍函数（CBF）结合，在计算资源受限的无人机上实现实时安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于辐射场重建的场景模型（如3DGS）虽然能高保真地建模障碍物，但其内存和计算开销过大，难以在机载计算资源受限的机器人平台上用于实时路径规划。需要一种轻量级且能保证安全性的场景表示方法。

Method: PolyMerge 将3DGS场景模型中的高斯椭球体转换为凸多面体覆盖，通过调整多面体数量来权衡保守性与计算成本；然后将该表示集成到控制障碍函数（CBF）中，用于规划无碰撞路径。在Crazyflie无人机上进行了仿真和硬件实验验证。

Result: 在Crazyflie无人机上，PolyMerge能够在严重受限的机载计算资源下实时计算并跟踪安全轨迹，在保证安全性的前提下速度优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PolyMerge 提供了一种将高保真3DGS场景模型转化为轻量级、可证明安全的障碍物表示的方法，使得资源受限的机器人能够利用神经辐射场重建进行实时安全导航。

Classification reason: 该论文将3DGS场景模型压缩为凸多面体覆盖表示，用于无人机（Crazyflie）的实时避障路径规划，属于机器人具身智能中的安全导航与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS compression for safe navigation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS compression, convex polytope covering, control barrier functions, drone navigation, safety guarantee

Abstract: Obstacle avoidance is essential for safe navigation and motion planning. Recent radiance field reconstruction methods enable object detection and modeling with high fidelity, but remain too memory- and compute-intensive for on-board perception-based path planning. To address these limitations, we propose PolyMerge to convert a large, photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model of a scene into a lightweight representation of convex polytopes whose union provably over-approximates all obstacles in the original 3DGS model. PolyMerge tunes the polytope count to trade off conservativeness and compute cost, and integrates with control barrier functions (CBFs) to plan collision-free paths. We showcase PolyMerge in simulation and hardware experiments on a Crazyflie drone, which uses PolyMerge to compute and follow safe trajectories in real time under severe onboard compute constraints, outperforming baselines in speed while guaranteeing safety. For our code and videos, visit .

</details>

*Yu Liu, Zongyang Chen, Yan Guo, Chao Liu, Xianfei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: SemGeoNav提出一种分层视觉导航框架，将端到端语义推理与几何规划器紧密结合，并引入时序轨迹平滑机制，在真实四足机器人上实现了比ViNT和NoMaD更高的成功率和更短的导航时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯端到端视觉导航模型缺乏显式几何约束，在开放环境中避障不可预测且不可靠；而传统几何规划器虽能保证安全，但难以处理高维视觉目标。如何将两者的优势结合是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出SemGeoNav分层架构：高层使用端到端语义推理模型进行目标理解和全局导航决策，底层使用基于几何方法的局部规划器进行安全避障，并通过时序轨迹平滑机制保证机器人运动的连续性和稳定性。

Result: 在宇树Go2四足机器人上的真实环境实验中，SemGeoNav在成功率和导航时间上均优于ViNT和NoMaD等代表性方法。

Conclusion: 将语义推理与几何规划相结合的分层导航策略能够有效提升视觉导航的安全性和效率，为具身机器人在开放环境中的可靠导航提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SemGeoNav，一种面向四足机器人的分层视觉导航方法，结合高层语义推理与几何局部规划实现安全避障。这属于机器人具身导航与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics navigation safety, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robotics navigation, semantic reasoning, geometric planning, quadruped robot, safety

Abstract: Learning-based visual navigation has enhanced semantic goal-reaching capabilities. However, due to their black-box nature, purely end-to-end models often lack explicit geometric constraints, leading to unpredictable and unreliable obstacle avoidance in open environments. Conversely, traditional geometric planners ensure safety but struggle with high-dimensional visual targets. To address these limitations, we propose SemGeoNav, a novel hierarchical visual navigation tightly integrates the high-level semantic reasoning of end-to-end models with the reliable local planning ability of geometry-based methods, achieving robust image-based navigation while significantly improving obstacle avoidance. Furthermore, we introduce a temporal trajectory smoothing mechanism to ensure continuous and stable robot motion. We evaluated SemGeoNav on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot in real-world environments. The results demonstrate that SemGeoNav outperforms existing representative methods, including ViNT and NoMaD, achieving higher success rates and shorter navigation times.

</details>


### [24] [V2P-Manip: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Monocular Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16436)
*Kaihan Chen, Yanming Shao, Haifeng Ji, Xiaokang Yang, Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (53.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: V2P-Manip 提出一个高效框架，从单目人类视频中提取具有视觉保真度和物理合理性的灵巧操作轨迹，通过两阶段精炼流程实现空间对齐和物理一致性，在 TACO 和 OakInk 基准上显著优于先前方法，平均成功率超过 75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作数据采集成本高昂且难以规模化，而互联网上存在大量人类演示视频。如何从单目视频中提取兼具视觉保真度和物理合理性的操作轨迹，并将其迁移到灵巧机器人手上，是一个具有前景但尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: V2P-Manip 构建了一个包含3D资产获取、轨迹估计和灵巧策略学习的端到端流水线。核心创新在于引入两阶段精炼流程：第一阶段进行空间对齐（spatial alignment），第二阶段施加物理一致性约束（physical consistency），从而弥合视觉感知与物理约束之间的差距。

Result: 在 TACO 和 OakInk 基准上，V2P-Manip 在姿态精度、非结构化环境适应性和训练效率方面显著优于先前方法。在多个合成灵巧操作任务上平均成功率超过 75%，且提取的操作先验可迁移到不同的灵巧手形态。

Conclusion: V2P-Manip 验证了从单目人类视频中学习灵巧操作策略的可行性，为降低机器人数据采集成本、利用大规模人类视频数据提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目人类视频中提取灵巧操作轨迹并学习机器人操作策略，属于具身智能中从人类演示学习策略的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous manipulation from human videos, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Robotics manipulation, Monocular video learning, Dexterous policy, Physical plausibility, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Achieving autonomous robotic dexterous manipulation requires precise, human-like action sequences at scale. As a scalable supplement to costly teleoperation data, extracting trajectories with both visual fidelity and physical plausibility from monocular videos represents a promising frontier in embodied AI. To this end, we introduce V2P-Manip, an efficient framework designed to learn dexterous manipulation policies directly from human demonstration videos. We establish an efficient, integrated pipeline encompassing 3D asset acquisition, trajectory estimation, and dexterous policy learning. To bridge the gap between visual perception and physical constraints, we introduce a two-stage refinement process to enforce spatial alignment and physical consistency. Evaluations on the TACO and OakInk benchmarks demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms previous methods in pose accuracy, adaptability to unstructured environments, and training efficiency. Ultimately, experimental results confirm an average success rate of over 75% across multiple synthetic manipulation tasks and validate the adaptability of the extracted manipulation priors across diverse dexterous hand embodiments.

</details>


### [25] [HOLO-MPPI: Multi-Scenario Motion Planning via Hierarchical Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16480)
*Youngjae Min, Jovin D'sa, Faizan M. Tariq, David Isele, Navid Azizan, Sangjae Bae*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, eess.SY

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (77.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HOLO-MPPI 提出一种分层运动规划框架，离线学习高层策略作为 MPPI 的采样先验，在线通过 MPPI 进行实时优化，在自动驾驶多场景下优于纯 MPPI 和端到端 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在真实世界部署时需要在多种场景下规划运动，而无需针对每个场景重新调参。端到端 RL 在分布偏移、奖励误指定和随机交互下容易失效；MPPI 控制虽能实时优化但依赖精心设计的采样先验，手动设计先验无法扩展到多场景部署。

Method: 提出 HOLO-MPPI 分层框架：离线阶段学习一个高层策略，在抽象动作空间中生成场景鲁棒的规划，并使用学习到的世界模型进行在线 rollout；在线阶段，该策略作为数据驱动的先验生成器，根据当前观测和目标参数化 MPPI 的采样分布，MPPI 在此先验附近实时优化底层控制序列以适应局部扰动。在自动驾驶场景中设计了有效的高层动作空间和定制模型架构。

Result: 在多种驾驶场景下的评估表明，HOLO-MPPI 在保持实时控制的同时，优于纯 MPPI 和端到端 RL 基线方法。

Conclusion: HOLO-MPPI 通过将离线学习的策略先验与在线 MPPI 优化相结合，有效解决了多场景运动规划中的泛化与实时性挑战，为自动驾驶等机器人应用提供了可行的分层规划框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HOLO-MPPI 框架，结合高层策略学习与低层随机最优控制（MPPI），用于多场景运动规划。其中离线学习的高层策略使用学习到的世界模型进行 rollout，属于面向机器人运动规划与控制的世界模型方法，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Combines offline policy learning with online MPPI for robust motion planning, but focuses on autonomous driving rather than the user's priority subtopics. Signals: hierarchical motion planning, MPPI with learned prior, autonomous driving, world model for rollout, offline-online framework

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world must plan motions across diverse scenarios without per-scenario retuning. End-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) can generalize across scenarios but often becomes brittle under distribution shift, reward misspecification, and stochastic interactions. Model predictive path integral (MPPI) control enables strong real-time refinement without gradients, but its performance depends on a well-shaped sampling prior, while manually designing the priors does not scale to multi-scenario deployment. We present HOLO-MPPI (High-level Offline, Low-level Online MPPI), a multi-scenario motion planning framework that combines high-level policy learning with low-level stochastic optimal control. Offline, we learn a high-level policy that proposes scenario-robust plans in an abstract action space, with a learned world model for online rollout. Online, the policy serves as a data-driven prior generator that parameterizes MPPI's sampling distribution conditioned on the current observation and goal. MPPI then optimizes low-level control sequences around this prior in real time to adapt to local disturbances. We instantiate HOLO-MPPI in autonomous driving by designing an effective high-level action space and tailored model architectures. Our evaluation across diverse driving scenarios shows that HOLO-MPPI improves upon MPPI and end-to-end RL baselines while maintaining real-time control.

</details>

*Yincong Chen, Ranpeng Qiu, Zihao Li, Yanan Zhou, Guoqiang Ren, Weiming Zhi*

TL;DR: 提出 Sequential Asymmetric Imitation (SAI)，一种单操作员课程式模仿学习方法，用于训练双移动机械臂在物理耦合（刚体/柔体物体）下的协作策略，无需同步双人示教或显式通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作移动操作中，失败往往源于时机不当（等待、让步、拉拽、释放、重新定位等），而非局部技能不足。现有方法需要同步双人示教或显式通信，成本高且不灵活。

Method: SAI 采用三阶段课程：1) 从单侧示教（配合人类伙伴）训练机器人A；2) 固定A策略，训练机器人B与之交互；3) 在协作失败点附近用稀疏干预微调A。整个过程仅需一名操作员，无需同步双人示教或机器人间显式通信。

Result: 在真实双机器人操作任务中，SAI 在任务成功率、相位同步和伙伴条件让步等指标上优于独立模仿学习和课程消融基线。

Conclusion: 物理耦合的协作行为可以通过模仿课程的结构来学习，而非依赖同步多操作员示教或显式协调机制。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双机器人协作操作，通过顺序非对称模仿学习耦合的机器人策略，涉及机器人动作规划、协调控制与具身智能，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot collaboration via imitation learning, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robot collaboration, imitation learning, mobile manipulation, curriculum learning, no explicit communication

Abstract: Collaborative mobile manipulation requires robots to coordinate with a partially observed partner while physically interacting through shared objects. This is difficult because failures often arise not from poor local skills, but from mistimed waiting, yielding, pulling, releasing, or repositioning. We study this problem with two bimanual mobile manipulators coupled through rigid and deformable objects. We propose Sequential Asymmetric Imitation (SAI), a single-teleoperator curriculum for learning coupled multi-robot behaviors without synchronized dual-operator demonstrations or explicit inter-robot communication. SAI trains Robot A from unilateral demonstrations with a compliant human partner, trains Robot B against the deployed Robot A policy, and then refines Robot A using sparse interventions near coordination failures. This staged process exposes the policies to increasingly realistic partner behaviors, including delay, phase mismatch,insufficient yielding, and interaction conflict. Across real-world dual-robot manipulation tasks, SAI improves task success, phase synchronization, and partner-contingent yielding over independent imitation and curriculum-ablation baselines. These results suggest that physically coupled collaboration can be learned through the structure of the imitation curriculum, rather than through synchronized multi-operator demonstrations or explicit coordination page:

</details>

*Wenhao Lu, Zhengqiu Zhu, Xiaofeng Wang, Xiaoran Zhang, Yatai Ji, Yong Zhao, Yue Hu, Yingzhen Nie, Jinlong Zhu, Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: ScoutVLA 提出了一种面向无人机室外具身问答（EQA）的视觉-语言-动作模型，采用解耦的双专家架构（视觉语言专家+动作专家），通过高自由度流匹配生成连续视角调整轨迹，并设计了知识隔离机制防止动作梯度破坏多模态推理能力。在提出的细粒度主动感知基准 FG-EQA 上，严格成功率提升 10.48 倍，QA 正确率提升 7.72 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外 EQA 系统通常在目标进入视野后即停止，缺乏细粒度视角调整能力，无法回答需要寻找证据的细粒度问题。受蜜蜂"摇摆舞"迭代调整飞行路径以验证目标信息的启发，作者希望构建一个能主动探索、证据驱动的无人机 EQA 系统。

Method: 提出 ScoutVLA，包含解耦的双专家架构：(1) 视觉语言专家（VLM）推断语义意图，识别缺失证据；(2) 独立动作专家使用高自由度流匹配（high-DoF flow matching）生成连续视角调整轨迹。采用解耦训练策略与知识隔离机制，防止动作梯度擦除模型的多模态推理能力。同时构建了 FG-EQA 基准，包含 4 万+模拟轨迹和 1 千+真实轨迹。

Result: 在模拟实验中，ScoutVLA 相比 SOTA 基线严格成功率平均提升 10.48 倍，QA 正确率平均提升 7.72 倍。真实世界实地研究也验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: ScoutVLA 通过解耦双专家架构和知识隔离机制，有效解决了无人机细粒度主动感知 EQA 中连续控制与语义推理的竞争需求，在模拟和真实场景中均显著优于现有方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无人机（UAV）在开放世界中的具身问答（EQA），涉及主动感知、动作规划与连续控制，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。同时，论文涉及视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和多模态推理，也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal generation priorities via flow matching for UAV active perception. Signals: world model, continuous trajectory generation, flow matching, embodied question answering, dual-expert VLA

Abstract: Aerial Embodied Question Answering (EQA) requires Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to actively perceive the environment and answer natural language questions. Existing outdoor EQA systems usually stop once the target enters the UAV's field of view, leaving the fine-grained viewpoint adjustment needed for evidence-seeking questions largely unresolved. To address this issue, we introduce FG-EQA, a fine-grained active perception EQA benchmark with more than 40K simulated trajectories and 1K real-world trajectories. Drawing inspiration from the ``waggle dance'' of scout bees, which iteratively adjust their flight paths to verify target information, we propose ScoutVLA, an evidence-driven Vision-Language-Action model for outdoor EQA. To emulate this active exploration behavior, ScoutVLA features a decoupled dual-expert architecture: a vision-language expert infers the semantic intent to identify missing evidence, while an independent action expert employs high-DoF flow matching to generate continuous viewpoint-refinement trajectories. To balance the competing demands of continuous control and semantic reasoning, we devise a decoupled training strategy with a knowledge insulation mechanism that prevents the action gradients from erasing the model's multimodal reasoning ability. Extensive simulated experiments and a qualitative real-world field study both verify the superiority of ScoutVLA over the state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a 10.48$\boldsymbol{\times}$ higher average strict success rate and a 7.72$\boldsymbol{\times}$ higher average QA correctness.

</details>


### [28] [Steering Generative Reinforcement Learning into Stable Robotic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16572)
*Yixuan Wang, Shutong Ding, Ke Hu, Tianxiang Gui, Jingya Wang, Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 提出SteerGenPO框架，通过潜在空间强化学习将训练好的生成式扩散/流策略转化为稳定的确定性机器人控制器，在Isaac Lab基准和Unitree G1运动任务上优于经典RL和生成式RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流式生成策略通过迭代动作生成提供丰富的随机探索，但其随机性不适合高维机器人系统的稳定精确控制，微小动作变化会累积为不一致的运动并降低鲁棒性。

Method: 将训练好的生成式策略中的随机潜在采样替换为学习到的潜在actor，该actor预测状态相关的潜在输入。训练时使用随机生成采样提供多样化动作提案，部署时使用确定性潜在导向提供稳定自适应控制，实现探索与控制的分离。

Result: 在六个Isaac Lab基准和一个Unitree G1运动任务上，SteerGenPO优于经典RL和生成式RL基线，其确定性潜在导向在推理时产生更稳定的行为和更可靠的命令响应。

Conclusion: SteerGenPO通过潜在空间中的确定性导向有效解决了生成式策略随机性带来的控制不稳定问题，为机器人控制中生成式RL的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SteerGenPO框架，将生成式强化学习（扩散/流策略）转化为稳定的机器人控制器，核心是机器人控制与策略学习，属于具身智能/机器人领域，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics control stability, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: generative reinforcement learning, diffusion policy, latent-space steering, deterministic controller, Isaac Lab benchmark

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative policies provide a powerful policy class for reinforcement learning by inducing rich stochastic exploration through iterative action generation. However, the stochasticity of diffusion policies is not suitable for stable and precise control in high-dimensional robotic systems, where small action variations can accumulate into inconsistent motion and reduced robustness. To address this issue, we propose SteerGenPO, a latent-space reinforcement learning framework that steers a trained generative policy into a robust deterministic robotic controller. The key idea is to replace stochastic latent sampling of the trained generative policy with a learned latent actor that predicts a state-dependent latent input for the generative policies. This separates exploration and control: stochastic generative sampling provides diverse action proposals during policy learning, while deterministic latent steering provides stable and adaptive control at deployment. We evaluate SteerGenPO on six Isaac Lab benchmarks and a Unitree G1 locomotion task. The results show SteerGenPO improves over both classical RL and generative RL baselines, while its deterministic latent steering produces more stable inference-time behaviors and more reliable command responses.

</details>


### [29] [PATCH: Action-Chunk-Conditioned Latent Patch Innovation Monitoring for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16690)
*Yanan Zhou, Ranpeng Qiu, Yincong Chen, Jiajie Cui, Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PATCH 提出一种动作块条件化的潜空间补丁创新监测方法，用于机器人操作部署时的运行时干预。通过定义执行走廊、预测潜空间补丁演化并累积残差，PATCH 能区分任务相关执行风险与良性视觉变化，在检测到局部扰动时暂停执行、选择恢复源，并在扰动消退后恢复策略。实验表明该方法比现有运行时监测器产生更稳定、上下文相关的触发信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的操作策略在短视界动作生成上取得了显著进展，但在开放工作空间中部署时，面对意外的局部场景动态（如移动物体、瞬态遮挡、干扰）仍然脆弱。现有运行时监测器依赖全局观测异常、策略不确定性或帧级视觉变化，难以区分任务相关的执行风险与良性的视觉变化。因此需要一种能够感知局部、任务相关的运行时监测方法。

Method: PATCH 包含三个核心组件：(1) 给定当前动作块，定义投影执行走廊（projected execution corridor）；(2) 预测该走廊内潜空间补丁的演化，并累积机器人自身运动无法解释的持久残差；(3) 这些残差形成局部化干预信号，PATCH-Router 据此暂停执行、选择可用恢复源，并在局部创新消退后恢复原始策略。

Result: 在真实机器人 rollout 数据上的实验表明，PATCH 产生的触发信号比现有运行时监测器更稳定且上下文相关。真实机器人部署进一步验证了监测驱动的干预和策略恢复能力，实现了对扰动感知的操作。

Conclusion: PATCH 通过动作块条件化的潜空间补丁创新监测，有效解决了机器人操作中局部场景扰动检测与干预的问题，为开放世界中的鲁棒机器人部署提供了一种实用的运行时监控方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向机器人操作的运行时监控方法 PATCH，利用动作块条件化的潜空间补丁创新监测来检测局部场景扰动并触发干预。这属于具身智能中机器人操作与规划控制的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot manipulation runtime monitoring, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics of continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: world model, robot manipulation, runtime monitoring, latent patch innovation, action-chunk-conditioned

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies have made substantial progress in real-world robot manipulation, particularly for short-horizon action generation. However, deployment in open workspaces remains fragile under unexpected local scene dynamics, such as moving objects, transient occlusions, or disturbances near the intended motion. Existing runtime monitors often rely on global observation anomalies, policy uncertainty, or frame-level visual changes, and struggle to distinguish task-relevant execution risk from benign visual variation. We introduce PATCH, an action-chunk-conditioned latent patch innovation monitor for deployment-time intervention. Given the active action chunk, PATCH defines a projected execution corridor, predicts latent patch evolution inside it, and accumulates persistent residuals unexplained by the robot's own motion. These residuals form a localized intervention signal that allows PATCH-Router to pause execution, select an available recovery source, and resume the original policy once localized innovation subsides. Experiments on real robot rollout data show that PATCH produces more stable and context-relevant triggers than competing runtime monitors. Real-robot deployment further demonstrates monitor-driven intervention and policy resumption for disturbance-aware manipulation. Project Page: .

</details>


### [30] [DIFF-IPPO: Diffusion-Based Informative Path Planning with Open-Vocabulary Belief Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16780)
*Sausar Karaf, Oleg Sautenkov, Mikhail Martynov, Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 提出 DIFF-IPPO 框架，将开放词汇置信地图生成器与基于扩散模型的轨迹规划器结合，用于无人机在搜索救援场景中的信息路径规划，在模拟场景中达到 81.49%-86.55% 的归一化检测分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息路径规划（IPP）方法多依赖高斯过程置信模型，难以处理开放词汇语义感知产生的复杂多模态置信图。基于扩散的规划器虽能建模复杂分布，但在 IPP 中的应用尚不充分。

Method: 提出 DIFF-IPPO 流水线，包含两个核心组件：(1) 开放词汇置信地图生成器，利用视觉语言模型生成非高斯、多模态的置信图；(2) 基于扩散模型的全局轨迹规划器，以置信图为条件生成轨迹，使传感器覆盖集中在高置信区域。

Result: 在不同数据集场景下达到 81.49%-86.55% 的归一化检测分数。在模拟搜索救援场景中，五架无人机编队使用批处理置信图条件轨迹生成，首次检测到燃烧建筑的时间为 3.5 分钟。

Conclusion: DIFF-IPPO 成功将扩散模型引入信息路径规划，结合开放词汇感知，为复杂环境下的机器人探索和目标搜索提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于扩散模型的全局轨迹规划器，结合开放词汇置信地图进行信息路径规划（IPP），用于无人机搜索与救援场景。核心是机器人规划与控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "embodied_world_model"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics path planning with diffusion, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: diffusion-based planner, open-vocabulary belief maps, informative path planning, drone search and rescue, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Exploration and object search require robots to perceive their environment, identify regions of interest, and plan trajectories that improve target-detection likelihood or maximize information gain. Many IPP methods, especially in continuous environmental monitoring, rely on Gaussian-process belief models, while object-search settings often produce complex, multimodal belief maps from semantic or open-vocabulary perception. Global trajectory generation directly conditioned on such non-Gaussian belief maps remains comparatively underexplored. Although diffusion-based planners offer strong capabilities for modeling such distributions, their use in informative path planning remains limited. In this work, we propose DIFF-IPPO, a pipeline that integrates an open-vocabulary belief map generator with a diffusion-based planner for global trajectory generation over belief maps. The method generates trajectories that concentrate sensor coverage over high-belief regions, achieving normalized detection scores between 81.49% and 86.55% across different dataset scenarios. We validate the system in a simulated search-and-rescue scenario where the planner searches candidate building regions to locate a burning building. In this setting, a team of five drones using batched belief-map-conditioned trajectory generation achieves first detections in 3.5 minutes.

</details>

TL;DR: ATOM-Bench 是一个面向机器人操作策略的原子技能与组合泛化能力的真实世界基准，包含30个原子任务和24个组合任务，通过2700次物理 rollout 评估了五种代表性策略，发现当前策略在细粒度电机原子、计数和逻辑过滤上仍存在困难，且强原子性能不能可靠迁移到组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用操作策略在真实世界中的泛化能力难以诊断——策略可能在演示任务上成功，但在细粒度原子技能或技能重组上失败。需要一个系统性的基准来区分失败源于弱原子技能还是有限的组合复用能力。

Method: 将桌面操作分解为电机原子和指令原子，构建30个原子任务和24个保留组合任务，涵盖单臂和双臂机器人轨道。收集3000个人类演示用于原子微调，引入原子分数(AS)和组合失败份额(CFS)指标来区分两类失败原因。

Result: 在2700次物理 rollout 上评估五种代表性操作策略，发现当前策略能获取简单的指令接地技能，但在细粒度电机原子、计数和逻辑过滤上表现不佳；强原子性能不能可靠迁移到保留组合任务。

Conclusion: ATOM-Bench 为诊断操作策略失败来源（弱电机执行、弱指令接地或有限组合复用）提供了有效的测试平台，揭示了当前策略在组合泛化方面的关键瓶颈。

Classification reason: 该论文提出机器人操作策略的原子技能与组合泛化基准，属于具身智能/机器人策略评估，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for world model and manipulation generalization, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Real-world robot manipulation benchmark, Atomic skill and compositional generalization evaluation, 2700 physical rollouts, Weak transfer from atomic to compositional tasks, Diagnostic metrics (AS, CFS)

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies are increasingly presented as foundation models for robotic control, but their real-world generalization remains difficult to diagnose. A policy may succeed on demonstrated tasks while still failing to execute fine-grained atomic skills or recombine learned skills in new task structures. We introduce \textbf{ATOM-Bench}, a real-world benchmark for evaluating both atomic skills and compositional generalization in manipulation policies. ATOM-Bench factorizes tabletop manipulation into motor atoms and instruction atoms, and contains 30 atomic tasks and 24 held-out compositional tasks across paired single-arm and dual-arm robot tracks. We collect 3,000 human demonstrations for atomic fine-tuning and release both the demonstration data and evaluation rollout data to support reproducible real-world evaluation. Policies are fine-tuned on atomic tasks and evaluated on both atomic skill acquisition and held-out compositional tasks. We further introduce Atomic Score (AS) and Compositional Failure Share (CFS) to distinguish failures caused by weak atomic skills from failures caused by limited compositional reuse. Through 2,700 physical rollouts on five representative manipulation policies, we find that current policies can acquire simple instruction-grounding skills, but still struggle with fine-grained motor atoms, counting, and logical filtering. More importantly, strong atomic performance does not reliably transfer to held-out compositional tasks. ATOM-Bench provides a diagnostic testbed for studying whether failures arise from weak motor execution, poor instruction grounding, or limited compositional reuse.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
