Method: CrossMaps 采用双记忆架构：短期记忆（STM）利用几何、语义和时间置信度线索聚合噪声视觉观测；当 STM 中的单元达到置信度阈值后，被提升至长期记忆（LTM）作为持久语义地标。系统集成多尺度 CLIP 嵌入进行开放词汇语义查询，部署在 Jetson Orin 驱动的 UGV 上，结合 SLAM 实时运行。

Result: 在真实机器人平台上实现了实时运行，能够生成可被自然语言查询的语义热力图，有效指导导航决策。置信度感知机制提升了地图的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: CrossMaps 证明了将传感器置信度信息融入语义建图对于部分可观测环境下机器人导航的重要性，双记忆架构和置信度融合策略为具身智能中的环境建模提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建用于机器人导航的置信度感知开放词汇语义地图，属于具身智能中环境建模与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on rover navigation and open-vocabulary mapping, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language models and generative video/image generation. Signals: Robotics and SLAM, open-vocabulary mapping, confidence-aware fusion, dual-memory architecture, real-time deployment on Jetson Orin

Abstract: Rovers rely on perception to maintain spatial maps that encode both objects and sensor quality (e.g., range reliability, lighting artifacts, data density), guiding data fusion, embedding updates, and navigation under partial observability. To study these coupled perception-navigation processes, we present CrossMaps, a real-time confidence-aware open-vocabulary semantic mapping pipeline that constructs language-queryable maps from RGB-D data. Building on VLMaps-style approaches, CrossMaps integrates multi-scale CLIP embeddings with confidence-aware fusion and a dual-memory architecture consisting of Short-Term Memory (STM) and Long-Term Memory (LTM). The STM aggregates noisy visual observations using geometric, semantic, and temporal confidence cues, while confident and coherent cells are promoted to the LTM as persistent semantic landmarks. Designed for deployment with a Jetson Orin-powered UGV alongside SLAM, CrossMaps runs in real time and produces semantic heatmaps that can be queried with natural language to guide rover navigation.

</details>

Conclusion: SidewalkBench 为城市人行道视觉导航提供了首个统一基准，揭示了现有模型在行人交互和长时域导航方面的不足，并指出合成数据训练是提升性能的有效方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot navigation benchmarking, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Robotics benchmark, world model application, no direct link to continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation, negative keywords absent but direction is peripheral

Abstract: Urban sidewalk navigation presents significant challenges due to complex structural layouts, dynamic pedestrian behaviors, and long distances. While recent visual navigation models offer a promising solution, the lack of a unified benchmark hinders quantitative and reproducible evaluation. To bridge this gap, we propose SidewalkBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed for visual navigation on urban sidewalks. Built upon NVIDIA Isaac Sim, SidewalkBench brings GPU-accelerated simulation of diverse, high-fidelity sidewalk environments, including both procedurally generated and real-world scanned scenes. We further populate the scenes with rich, reactive event-based pedestrian behaviors and flexible, efficient animation, enabling standardized model evaluation under realistic real-world settings. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 9 visual navigation models on 330 unit-test scenarios, 800 pedestrian-reactive scenarios, and 105 long-horizon scenarios. Our findings highlight that pedestrian interaction and long-horizon robustness remain critical bottlenecks for existing models, and scaling up sidewalk training with synthetic data emerges as a promising solution.

</details>


### [34] [When Should a Robot Replan? Regret-Guided Update Scheduling in Time-Varying MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16972)
*Negin Musavi, Gokul Puthumanaillam, Ruben Hernandez, William Schafer, Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO, eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人在非平稳环境中何时应进行重规划的问题，提出了一种基于动态遗憾的在线自适应更新调度规则，在时变MDP框架下通过skip-update方案在有限预算内优化重规划时机，并在火星车导航和Crazyflie四旋翼飞行器上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非平稳环境中需要不断适应漂移的动态变化，但机载能量和计算预算限制了状态估计和重规划的频率。现有方法通常假设固定频率的重规划，缺乏对"何时应该重规划"这一核心问题的理论指导。

Method: 将问题建模为时变MDP（TVMDP），假设转移漂移率有已知上界。提出skip-update方案：在选定的更新时间点通过最大似然估计转移核并计算有限时域策略，在更新间隔间复用策略并传播状态估计。推导了动态遗憾的理论界，并据此设计了一种在线、遗憾引导的自适应更新规则来分配有限预算。

Result: 在模拟的火星车导航任务（时变滑移动力学）和Crazyflie四旋翼室内障碍场实验中，自适应分配策略优于其他预算受限的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于动态遗憾分析的自适应重规划调度规则能够有效利用有限预算，在非平稳环境中取得更好的性能，为机器人在线决策中的计算资源分配提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人在时变MDP中的重规划调度问题，属于具身智能中的规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical contribution to robot replanning in non-stationary environments but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics. Signals: time-varying MDP, dynamic regret, skip-update scheme, adaptive replanning, Mars rover

Abstract: Robots operating in non-stationary environments must continually adapt their policies as the dynamics drift, but onboard energy and compute budgets cap how often a full state estimation and re-planning step can be performed. This raises a question: \emph{when}, along a horizon, should a robot spend its limited budget? We formulate this problem in time-varying Markov decision processes (TVMDPs) with a known bound on the rate of transition drift. We model execution as a \emph{skip-update} scheme in which, at chosen update times, the agent estimates the transition kernel by maximum likelihood and computes a finite-horizon policy, and between updates reuses this policy under a propagated state estimate. We analyze the dynamic regret of this scheme and show how it grows during skip intervals in terms of the properties of the TVMDP and the skip lengths; the resulting bound answers the opening question via an online, regret-guided update rule that allocates the budget adaptively. We evaluate the rule in a simulated Mars-rover navigation task with time-varying slip dynamics and on a Crazyflie quadrotor in indoor obstacle fields. Adaptive allocation outperforms other budgeted baselines.

</details>


### [35] [Hierarchical Advantage Weighting for Online RL Fine-Tuning of VLAs from Sparse Episode Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17043)
*Tongyan Fang, Siyuan Huang, Naiyu Fang, Ganlong Zhao, Zhongjin Luo, Jianbo Liu, Xiaogang Wang, Ying Dong, Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HABC方法，通过分层优势加权行为克隆，解决VLA策略在线RL微调中稀疏二元结果（成功/失败）导致的信用分配问题，在双臂机器人操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将稀疏的回合级二元结果简化为单一标量奖励或优势信号，混淆了"可行性"和"效率"两个不同目标，且无法处理自主与干预混合轨迹中的信用分配问题，导致基本任务成功后缺乏梯度区分。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC)：1) 训练两个独立的critic头分别建模可行性和效率目标，在不同数据子集上训练；2) 使用状态自适应门控机制融合两个优势信号，在成功不确定时优先可行性，成功确定时转向效率；3) 引入干预感知的信用分配，将回合标签限制在当前策略执行的片段上。

Result: 在三个接触丰富的双臂机器人真实任务上，HABC将SFT基线的成功率从36%、44%、12%分别提升至92%、88%、38%。

Conclusion: HABC通过分层优势加权有效解决了稀疏二元结果下的信用分配问题，为VLA策略的在线RL微调提供了更精细的逐帧监督信号，在真实机器人任务上取得了显著提升。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于VLA策略的在线强化学习微调，涉及机器人操作任务中的动作规划与控制，属于具身智能/机器人领域，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses sparse reward credit assignment in VLA RL fine-tuning, but focuses on robotics rather than core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: Hierarchical advantage weighting, Online RL fine-tuning, Sparse binary outcomes, VLA policy, Bimanual manipulation

Abstract: When pretrained VLA policies are fine-tuned through online RL, each rollout episode produces only a single binary outcome (success or failure), yet the actor update requires per-transition supervision. Existing approaches commonly reduce this sparse outcome to a single scalar reward or advantage signal, which conflates distinct forms of transition-level feedback and provides limited guidance once basic task success becomes achievable. First, a single scalar signal conflates the two objectives of viability and efficiency; once basic success is achieved, the binary label provides no gradient to distinguish efficient completions from slow ones. Second, real-world rollouts mix autonomous and intervention segments; naively assigning episode outcomes across these boundaries introduces incorrect credit assignment. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC), which trains separate critic heads for these two objectives on different data subsets and combines their outputs with a state-adaptive balance. A state-adaptive gate $g_t$ merges their one-step advantages, prioritizing viability when success is uncertain and shifting to efficiency only when viability is high, and converts the result into per-transition weights on the actor loss. Intervention-aware credit assignment further restricts outcome labels to segments executed by the current policy, preventing supervision from leaking across intervention boundaries. In real-robot experiments on three contact-rich bimanual tasks, HABC raises success from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines of 36%, 44%, and 12% to 92%, 88%, and 38%.

</details>


### [36] [Think Less, Act Early: Reinforced Latent Reasoning with Early Exit in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15099)
*Dianqiao Lei, Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (73.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出AVA-VLA框架，通过强化学习优化的潜空间推理（Latent Reasoning）替代显式CoT推理，并引入早退策略（Early-Exit）动态控制推理深度，在LIBERO基准上实现6倍加速和98.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖显式CoT推理，计算成本高且在多步任务中易产生错误传播。作者希望用潜空间推理替代显式文本生成，同时解决潜轨迹易受噪声干扰和与下游目标不对齐的问题。

Method: 提出AVA-VLA框架：(1) 将推理建模为不可观测潜变量的序列，绕过显式文本生成；(2) 引入基于强化学习的去噪机制，将潜状态生成视为序列决策过程，通过任务级奖励优化推理轨迹；(3) 引入早退策略，基于状态置信度自适应终止推理，实现深度与效率的动态权衡。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，AVA-VLA相比显式CoT方法实现6倍推理加速，平均成功率达98.3%，在效率和长程稳定性上均优于全推理基线。

Conclusion: 潜空间推理结合强化学习优化和早退策略，可以有效替代显式CoT推理，在保持高成功率的同时大幅提升推理效率，为VLA模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出AVA-VLA，一种面向具身决策的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型，使用强化学习优化的潜空间推理与早退策略来提升效率。核心场景是机器人具身决策与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes latent reasoning with early exit for VLA, directly aligning with user's priority on continuous latent models and efficient inference. Signals: latent reasoning, early exit, reinforcement learning denoising, vision-language-action, embodied decision

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models predominantly rely on explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to bridge perception and action. While effective, this paradigm suffers from high computational costs and error propagation in multi-step tasks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Variable Alignment VLA (AVA-VLA), a novel Latent Reasoning VLA framework that models reasoning as a sequence of unobservable latent variables, bypassing the need for explicit text generation. However, latent trajectories are inherently susceptible to noise interference and misalignment with downstream objectives. To address this, we introduce a Reinforcement Learning-based Denoising mechanism that treats latent state generation as a sequential decision process, optimizing reasoning trajectories via task-level rewards. Furthermore, we incorporate an Early-Exit Strategy that adaptively terminates reasoning based on state confidence, enabling a dynamic trade-off between depth and efficiency. Extensive experiments on embodied decision benchmarks demonstrate that AVA-VLA achieves a 6x inference speedup over explicit CoT methods while attaining a 98.3% average success rate on LIBERO, improving both efficiency and long-horizon stability over full-reasoning baselines.

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance in enhancing healthcare efficiency across a wide range of medical applications. Nevertheless, their limited ability to perceive, understand, and interact with the physical world significantly constrains their effectiveness in real-world clinical workflows, where safety-critical decision-making and physical execution are tightly coupled. Recently, embodied artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a promising physical-interactive paradigm for intelligent healthcare, enabling agents to operate in complex medical environments. As research in this area rapidly expands, understanding how intelligent agents function as integrated, end-to-end systems in clinical environments becomes increasingly critical. However, existing surveys on medical embodied AI largely emphasize individual aspects or functional components, lacking a unified system-level organization of the field. To support and consolidate recent advances, we systematically survey the core components of medical embodied AI, with a particular emphasis on the coordinated integration of perception, decision-making, and action. We further review representative medical applications and relevant datasets, and we analyze the major challenges encountered in real-world clinical practice. Finally, we discuss key directions for future research in this rapidly evolving field. The associated project can be found at .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练/评估框架缺乏共享且可控的潜结构，无法衡量智能体是否以及为何在跨任务序列中改进。为了研究持续学习智能体如何从相关任务序列中推断隐藏结构并改进未来决策，需要一个能够分离探索与利用、并提供可控潜变量的标准化测试平台。

Method: 提出 LatentGym 可控测试套件，每个环境围绕一个控制任务间共享结构的真实潜变量组织。通过设计指标将探索（智能体行为是否收集潜变量信息）与利用（智能体是否使用已收集信息）分离。在多个前沿 LLM 上进行了实证研究，包括评估跨任务适应能力、后训练效果分析以及设计选择（如任务间反馈）的影响。

Result: 揭示了前沿模型在跨相关任务适应中的失败模式；证明了在相关任务序列上进行后训练可以改善通用跨任务适应能力，并分析了改进来源；展示了任务间反馈等设计选择如何塑造训练动态和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LatentGym 为研究 LLM 智能体如何从跨任务经验中学习提供了受控基础，有助于设计在序列化、个性化和交互式场景中更可靠适应的智能体系统。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM智能体在序列化任务中跨任务经验学习（cross-task experiential learning），通过推断隐藏的潜变量结构来改进未来决策，这与具身世界模型中智能体通过与环境交互学习动力学和规划控制的核心思想高度一致。虽然论文不直接涉及机器人，但其关注智能体在连续任务中的适应、探索与利用，属于具身世界模型与规划控制的广义范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent cross-task learning, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, controllable latent structure, cross-task adaptation, LLM agents, exploration vs exploitation

Abstract: We envision continually learning agentic systems that become more useful over time: as they encounter sequences of related tasks, they should infer the hidden structure shared across those tasks and use it to improve future decisions. This cross-task experiential learning capability is pivotal in domains such as personalization and interactive assistance, but existing training/evaluation frameworks do not provide shared, controllable latent structures and cannot measure whether or why agents improve. We introduce LatentGym: a controllable suite in which each environment is organized around a ground-truth latent variable governing the structure across tasks. Our construction yields metrics that separate exploration (whether the agent's actions gather information about the latent) from exploitation (whether the agent uses what it has gathered). We demonstrate our suite on empirical studies addressing three questions: how and why frontier models fail to adapt across related tasks; whether post-training on related task sequences improves general cross-task adaptation, and where those gains come from; and how design choices such as inter-task feedback shape training dynamics and generalization. Together, these results establish a controlled foundation for studying how LLM agents learn from experience across tasks, and for designing agents that adapt more reliably in sequential, personalized, and interactive settings.

</details>

*Paolo Giaretta, Zeyang Li, Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: DiRecT 是一种无需训练的扩散模型约束采样算法，受模型预测控制启发，通过滚动时域去噪与终端约束，在最终干净轨迹上施加约束而非中间噪声样本，从而在不扭曲扩散动力学的前提下实现安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在规划控制中展现出强大能力，但推理时的安全约束施加仍是一大挑战。现有方法对每一步去噪中间样本施加约束，导致采样动力学被过度约束，样本质量严重下降。需要一种仅在最终干净轨迹上施加约束的算法。

Method: 将约束扩散采样建模为随机最优控制（SOC）问题，受模型预测控制（MPC）启发，提出滚动时域去噪与终端约束（Receding-Horizon Denoising with Terminal Constraints），将原本难以求解的约束SOC分解为可高效求解的滚动优化，分离随机去噪与约束满足过程。

Result: 在多个安全规划基准上，DiRecT 显著提升了部署安全性和任务性能，优于现有的扩散规划基线方法。

Conclusion: DiRecT 提供了一种训练免费、灵活高效的扩散模型约束采样方案，可集成现成或领域专用优化器、环境动力学先验及软奖励，为安全关键任务中的扩散规划部署提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于扩散模型的规划控制方法（DiRecT），通过滚动时域去噪实现安全约束下的轨迹规划，属于具身智能/机器人领域的规划与控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel training-free constrained sampling for diffusion planning, highly relevant to safe world models and efficient inference. Signals: diffusion model planning, safe control, receding-horizon denoising, training-free constraint, world model safety

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for planning and control by learning multimodal distributions over actions and trajectories. Yet reliable inference-time safety enforcement remains a key barrier to their deployment in safety-critical tasks. Existing approaches typically project each denoising iterate onto the feasible set, even though constraints are defined only on the final clean trajectory. Enforcing feasibility on noisy intermediate samples can therefore overconstrain the sampling dynamics, substantially degrading sample quality. To address this limitation, we introduce DiRecT (Diffusion-based planning via Receding-horizon denoising with Terminal constraints), a training-free algorithm for constrained sampling from diffusion models via stochastic optimal control (SOC). DiRecT enforces constraints only on the final clean sample, avoiding unnecessary restrictions on the intermediate denoising dynamics. Inspired by model predictive control, we derive a principled receding-horizon surrogate for the otherwise intractable constrained SOC formulation, yielding an efficient algorithm that cleanly separates stochastic denoising from constraint satisfaction, progressively steering samples toward feasible final trajectories without distorting the learned diffusion dynamics. Furthermore, DiRecT is highly flexible: it can leverage off-the-shelf or domain-specific optimizers, incorporate priors over environment dynamics, and optimize additional soft rewards. Extensive experiments on safe planning benchmarks demonstrate that DiRecT substantially improves deployment safety and task performance over existing diffusion-based planning baselines.

</details>

*Jisang Han, Seonghu Jeon, Jaewoo Jung, René Zurbrügg, Honggyu An, Tifanny Portela, Marco Hutter, Marc Pollefeys, Seungryong Kim, Sunghwan Hong*

TL;DR: GAM 提出一种几何动作模型，通过复用预训练几何基础模型（GFM）作为共享骨干，在中间层插入因果未来预测器，实现语言条件化的时序世界建模与机器人操作策略学习，在仿真和真实操作任务上超越现有基础模型规模的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和视频世界-动作模型（WAM）虽然继承了大规模基础模型的语义或时序先验，但主要在 2D 图像或 2D 导出的潜空间上操作，缺乏接触丰富操作所需的 3D 几何信息。作者希望在不丢失几何先验的前提下，赋予基础模型语言条件化的时序世界建模能力。

Method: GAM 将预训练的几何基础模型（GFM）在中间层拆分为两部分：浅层作为观测编码器，在拆分点插入一个因果未来预测器（causal future predictor），该预测器以语言指令、本体感知和动作历史为条件预测未来的潜空间 token；预测的 token 再通过剩余的 GFM 模块进行特征传播和解码，最终同时输出未来几何和动作。通过极小的架构修改，使 GFM 具备语言条件化的时序世界建模能力。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实机器人操作基准上，GAM 在精度、鲁棒性、推理速度和模型轻量化方面均优于当前基础模型规模的基线方法。

Conclusion: GAM 证明了通过最小化架构修改，可以直接复用几何基础模型作为机器人策略学习的共享骨干，同时保留其丰富的几何先验，为语言条件化的机器人操作提供了一种高效、准确且鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Geometric Action Model (GAM)，将预训练的几何基础模型改造为语言条件化的机器人操作策略，实现感知、时序预测与动作解码的统一。核心是面向机器人操作的世界建模与策略学习，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 embodied_world_model。

Importance reason: Strong world model approach for robot policy, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: world model, robot policy, geometric foundation model, causal future prediction, language-conditioned manipulation

Abstract: Generalist robot policies must follow user instructions while reasoning about how objects, cameras, and robot actions interact in the 3D physical world. Recent vision-language-action models (VLAs) and video world-action models (WAMs) inherit strong semantic or temporal priors from large-scale foundation models, but they still operate primarily on 2D image frames or 2D-derived latent spaces, leaving implicit the 3D geometry required for contact-rich manipulation. We propose the Geometric Action Model (GAM), a language-conditioned manipulation policy that directly repurposes a pretrained geometric foundation model (GFM) as a shared substrate for perception, temporal prediction, and action decoding. GAM splits the GFM at an intermediate layer: the shallow layers serve as an observation encoder, and a causal future predictor inserted at the split layer forecasts future latent tokens conditioned on language, proprioception, and action history. The predicted future tokens are then routed through the remaining GFM blocks for feature propagation and decoding, allowing a single backbone to produce both future geometry and actions. This design equips the GFM with language-conditioned temporal world modeling through minimal architectural modification while preserving its rich geometric priors. Across a broad suite of simulation and real-robot manipulation benchmarks, GAM is more accurate, more robust, faster, and lighter than current foundation-model-scale baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Self-Driving Negotiator，一个基于文本的多轮程序化生成环境，用于评估自动驾驶中基于隐藏意图的隐式社交协商能力。代理需生成具体驾驶动作，奖励由特权模拟器状态计算。实验表明当前模型远不及脚本专家，最佳平均成功率仅0.68。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶语言基准多聚焦于感知、VQA或开环规划，而语言代理协商基准通常将协商显式化为文本。两者之间存在空白：缺乏衡量驾驶场景中隐式社交协调能力的基准。

Method: 设计了一个文本仅、多轮、程序化生成的驾驶协商环境，包含三种场景（无信号交叉口、有信号交叉口、有争议并道）。代理需生成具体驾驶动作（如加速、减速、等待），奖励函数基于特权模拟器状态计算，并设计了反博弈不变量防止代理通过非自然行为刷分。

Result: 对六个模型进行推理排行榜评估，最佳平均成功率仅0.68；有争议并道场景在所有模型上统计平坦；难度层级区分了线索跟随行为与真正的等待承诺行为。

Conclusion: 当前模型在隐式社交协商任务上远不及脚本专家，尤其在需要推断隐藏意图并做出承诺性等待的场景中表现薄弱，表明该方向仍有巨大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶场景下的社交协商与意图推理，属于具身智能中的规划控制与交互决策问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on social negotiation in driving, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative video/world models. Signals: world model evaluation, social negotiation, hidden intent, autonomous driving, text-only environment

Abstract: Autonomous driving is full of tiny social negotiations: a driver presses forward, another yields, a pedestrian fakes toward the curb, or a lane vehicle chooses whether to open a merge gap. Such interactions require inferring hidden intent from behavior under partial observability and then acting safely and efficiently. Existing autonomous-driving language benchmarks mostly focus on perception, visual question answering, or open-loop planning, while existing language-agent negotiation benchmarks typically make the negotiation explicit in text. Self-Driving Negotiator bridges the gap between the two: a text-only, multi-turn, procedurally generated environment for measuring implicit social coordination in driving. Agents generate specific driving actions. Reward and diagnostics are computed from the privileged simulator state, not from the explanation of the model. This report covers task design, reward and anti-gaming invariants, validated scenarios, non-LLM baselines, and a six-model inference leaderboard. Current models are far removed from the scripted expert. The best average success rate across three scenarios is 0.68; contested merge is statistically flat across models; and difficulty tiers separate cue-following from true wait-for-commitment behavior.

</details>


### [42] [FlowMPC: Improving Flow Matching policies with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16286)
*Chandon Hamel*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (88.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowMPC 将 Flow Matching 模仿学习策略与 TD-MPC2 世界模型结合，通过 MPPI 规划在 ManiSkill 操作任务中提升策略性能，在 PickCube 和 PickSingleYCB 上取得明显改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 在模仿学习的多模态动作空间中表现优异，但其训练目标不直接最大化期望回报，测试时仍有改进空间。作者希望利用学习到的世界模型在测试时对 Flow Matching 策略输出的候选动作序列进行规划优化，以提升任务成功率。

Method: 基于 TD-MPC2 学习一个世界模型（包括潜空间编码器、动力学模型、奖励/价值预测器），在测试时使用 Flow Matching 策略生成候选动作序列，通过 MPPI（Model Predictive Path Integral）在世界模型中进行规划，选择最优动作序列执行。Flow Matching 策略本身不做额外训练，仅用于提供候选动作。

Result: 在 ManiSkill 的 PickCube 和 PickSingleYCB 操作任务上，FlowMPC 相比纯 Flow Matching 策略在成功率上有明显提升，尤其在 episode 末端的成功率增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的测试时规划可以有效补充基于流的模仿学习策略，无需修改 FM 训练目标即可提升性能，为结合模仿学习与世界模型提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FlowMPC，将模仿学习的 Flow Matching 策略与学习到的世界模型（TD-MPC2）结合，在 ManiSkill 操作任务中进行测试时规划（MPPI）。核心贡献是利用世界模型提升机器人操作策略的性能，属于具身智能中的规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Combines flow matching policies with world models for test-time planning, directly relevant to world model and continuous language model priorities. Signals: world model, flow matching, test-time planning, MPPI, manipulation tasks

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) is a powerful approach for behavior cloning in multimodal action spaces [Jiang et al., 2025], but because it is not trained to directly maximize expected return, there is still room to improve how FM policies act at test time. This work investigates whether a learned world model can improve FM policies by enabling Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planning over candidate action sequences proposed by the policy. Building on TD-MPC2 [Hansen et al., 2024], I introduce FlowMPC, a framework that combines an imitation-learned FM policy with a learned world model for test-time planning in ManiSkill manipulation tasks [Tao et al., 2025]. Across PickCube and PickSingleYCB, adding the world model improved performance over the FM policy alone, with especially clear gains in end-of-episode success. These results suggest that world-model-based planning can effectively complement flow-based imitation policies without modifying the FM training objective.

</details>


### [43] [Direction-Conditioned Policies via Compositional Subgoal Scoring for Online Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16515)
*Swaminathan S K, Damiya Gondha, Theyanesh Eswaramoorthy Rajahkrishnan, Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出方向条件策略（DCP），一种完全在线的目标条件强化学习方法。DCP将目标达成分解为两个共享InfoNCE表征ψ的组件：子目标评分步骤选择与最终目标对齐的已访问状态，以及方向条件actor消耗从当前状态到子目标的单位方向和距离。理论证明了方向充分性、训练与部署一致性以及可控子空间表征。在9个环境中，DCP在大多数指标上优于Contrastive RL，尤其在操作和障碍交互任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线GCRL中，actor直接以原始目标为条件，但当目标远离数据分布时，该信号在几何上信息不足。HJB理论表明最优目标条件动作仅通过目标到达距离的梯度依赖于目标，因此需要一种利用几何信息更丰富的方向信号的方法。

Method: 提出DCP框架，包含：(1) 子目标评分模块，选择与最终目标g在ψ空间中对齐的已访问状态z_t；(2) 方向条件actor，以从ψ(s_t)到ψ(z_t)的单位方向d_t和幅度r_t为条件。两个组件共享InfoNCE表征ψ，联合训练。部署时移除子目标评分，方向条件直接使用g替代z_t。理论证明了方向充分性、训练-部署一致性边界以及方向条件失效的可控子空间刻画。

Result: 在9个环境中，DCP在大多数最终指标上优于Contrastive RL，在操作和障碍交互任务上提升最大。定性分析显示对比表征ψ表现为在线拟度量，编码了环境拓扑结构。唯一的失败案例（AntSoccer）定位到学习梯度病理，与理论预期一致。

Conclusion: DCP通过方向条件策略有效解决了在线GCRL中远距离目标几何信息不足的问题，理论分析与实验验证一致，为在线目标条件强化学习提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出方向条件策略（DCP），通过子目标评分和方向条件actor实现在线目标条件强化学习，属于机器人/具身智能中的规划控制方法，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical grounding in goal-conditioned RL but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Goal-conditioned RL, InfoNCE representation, subgoal scoring, direction-conditioned actor, online learning

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman theory implies that the optimal goal-conditioned action depends on the goal only through the gradient of the goal-reaching distance at the current state, yet standard online GCRL still conditions the actor on the raw goal -- a signal that is geometrically uninformative when the goal is far from the data distribution. We propose Direction-Conditioned Policies (DCP), a fully online method that decomposes goal-reaching into two components sharing one InfoNCE representation $\psi$: a subgoal-scoring step that selects a visited state $z_t$ aligned with the final goal $g$ in $\psi_g$, and a direction-conditioned actor that consumes the unit direction $d_t$ and magnitude $r_t$ from $\psi(s_t)$ to $\psi(z_t)$. The two components train jointly, factor cleanly at deployment (subgoal scoring is removed, while direction conditioning remains with $g$ in place of $z_t$), and admit independent modification at the same $(d_t,r_t)$ interface. We prove three results. First, direction sufficiency under HJB: the optimal action under control-affine dynamics depends on the goal only through the value gradient. Second, a quantitative bound showing that, under mild conditions on the learned representation and assuming the scoring rule returns an on-path $z_t$, the actor's conditioning input at training and at deployment coincide up to representation error and geodesic slack. Third, a controllable-subspace characterization of when directional conditioning fails. Across nine environments, DCP improves over Contrastive RL on most final metrics, with the largest gains on manipulation and obstacle-interaction tasks; a qualitative analysis of the learned $\psi$-distance landscape shows the contrastive representation behaves as an online quasimetric encoding environment topology, and the single failure case (AntSoccer) localizes to a learned-gradient pathology that the theory anticipates.

</details>

*Lai Jiang, Cheng Qian, Zhenhailong Wang, Pan Lu, Heng Ji, Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

TL;DR: ACCORD 是一个无需额外训练的 LLM agent 框架，通过在每次动作前主动探测环境缺失信息并整合轨迹上下文，显著提升了在数字环境（AppWorld）和具身环境（AlfWorld）中的任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户指令往往因隐含的环境假设而欠指定，现有 LLM agent 常基于假设而非观察到的具体信息行动，忽略可获取的信息，且未能利用已返回的证据。需要一种方法让 agent 在执行前主动识别并补全缺失的上下文。

Method: 提出 ACCORD 框架，核心思想是在每次动作前进行"动作条件上下文接地"：agent 主动探测环境以获取缺失信息，并从自身轨迹中整合相关上下文。该框架无需额外训练或任务成功信号，是一种轻量级的推理时策略。

Result: 在 AppWorld 上，ACCORD 将 GPT-5-mini 的任务完成率从 42.0% 提升至 62.6%（+20.6 点）；Claude-4.5-sonnet 上提升 +10.8 点；Qwen3.5-27B-FP8 上提升 +10.1 点。在具身环境 AlfWorld 上，GPT-5-mini 的成功率提升 +7.4 点。

Conclusion: ACCORD 是一种简单有效的 agent 框架，通过主动上下文接地显著提升了 LLM agent 在数字和物理环境中的任务执行能力，且在不同模型和环境中均表现出一致的改进效果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ACCORD 框架，使 LLM agent 在数字环境（AppWorld）和具身环境（AlfWorld）中通过动作条件上下文接地来改进任务执行。AlfWorld 是经典的具身智能基准，论文在具身环境中验证了效果，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Strong LLM agent grounding method but outside user's priority subtopics and negative for world model focus. Signals: LLM agent framework, action-conditioned grounding, no extra training, AppWorld +20.6, AlfWorld +7.4

Abstract: User instructions are often underspecified because humans rely on implicit assumptions about the surrounding environment. For large language model (LLM) agents operating in information-rich digital and physical environments, these assumptions cannot be inferred from the instruction alone; they must be recovered from the current state of tools, data, interfaces, and observations. Effective execution therefore requires agents to identify missing context, ground it in observed evidence, and carry it forward into subsequent actions. We show that current agents often fail to do so. They act from assumed rather than observed specifics, overlook information they could have gathered, and fail to incorporate evidence that has already been returned. Building on this insight, we propose ACCORD (Action-Conditioned Contextual Grounding), a simple and effective agent framework for adaptive grounding. Before each action, ACCORD actively probes the environment for missing information and integrates relevant context from the agent's trajectory that would otherwise be overlooked. Requiring no additional training or task-success signals, ACCORD improves task-goal completion on AppWorld by up to +20.6 points with GPT-5-mini, from 42.0% to 62.6%, compared to strong baselines. These gains persist with a substantially stronger base model (+10.8 with Claude-4.5-sonnet), an open-weight model (+10.1 with Qwen3.5-27B-FP8), and on the embodied AlfWorld benchmark (+7.4 success rate with GPT-5-mini).

</details>


### [45] [Can LLM Agents Infer World Models? Evidence from Agentic Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16576)
*Reef Menaged, Gili Lior, Shauli Ravfogel, Roee Aharoni, Gabriel Stanovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体自动机学习"框架，评估LLM智能体能否通过与oracle交互（成员查询和等价查询）来推断隐藏的确定性有限自动机（DFA）。实验发现，随着DFA规模增大，LLM性能急剧下降；推理模型优于非推理模型，但轨迹分析显示智能体在查询规划、证据整合和假设构建方面存在系统性失败。当前LLM智能体在交互式环境发现方面远不如经典算法鲁棒和高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在交互式任务中表现出一定能力，但缺乏系统性的基准来评估它们能否通过交互发现隐藏的环境结构（即世界模型）。作者希望构建一个可控、可量化、有强基线的测试平台，来评估LLM智能体在交互式环境发现中的能力与局限性。

Method: 提出"智能体自动机学习"框架：LLM智能体需要通过与oracle交互来发现隐藏的DFA。交互包括两种查询：(1)成员查询——询问某字符串是否属于目标语言；(2)等价查询——询问当前假设的DFA是否等于目标DFA。使用经典自动机学习算法（如L*算法）作为强基线。评估多种LLM（包括推理模型和非推理模型），分析其在不同规模DFA上的表现，并通过轨迹分析研究失败模式。

Result: 随着DFA规模增大，LLM性能急剧下降。推理模型（如o1等）显著强于非推理模型，但整体仍远不如经典算法。轨迹分析揭示了三种典型失败模式：查询规划失败（无法选择信息量大的查询）、证据整合失败（无法从历史查询中正确推断）、假设构建失败（无法形成正确的DFA假设）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在某些情况下能够进行非平凡的交互式环境发现，但在鲁棒性和效率上远不如经典算法。这揭示了LLM智能体在系统化推理、证据整合和假设构建方面的根本性局限，为未来改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 论文研究LLM智能体通过与环境的交互（成员查询和等价查询）来推断隐藏的确定性有限自动机（DFA），这本质上是在学习一个隐藏的"世界模型"（即DFA所定义的语言/环境）。这与世界模型方向中的"具身世界模型与规划控制"子主题相关，因为该子主题关注智能体通过交互发现环境模型并进行规划控制。虽然论文使用DFA作为抽象环境而非物理世界，但其核心问题——智能体通过交互推断环境结构——与世界模型的核心思想一致。

Importance reason: Evaluates LLM agents' world model inference via automata learning, but focuses on symbolic DFA rather than continuous multimodal world models. Signals: world model inference, LLM agent interaction, automata learning, reasoning model advantage, performance scaling failure

Abstract: We propose agentic automata learning to evaluate the extent to which tool-calling LLM agents can uncover hidden environments through interaction. In our setup, an agent should uncover a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA) by interacting with an oracle through (1) membership queries ("Does this string belong to the target language?") and (2) equivalence queries ("Is this the target DFA?"). This yields a scalable testbed with controlled task complexity, measurable interaction efficiency, and strong baselines (classic automata-learning algorithms). Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that performance drops sharply as DFA size increases. Reasoning models are markedly stronger than non-reasoning models, yet trajectory analyses reveal recurring failures in query planning, evidence integration, and hypothesis construction. Overall, our results show that current LLM agents can sometimes perform non-trivial interactive discovery, but remain far less robust and efficient than classic algorithms for the task.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人等安全关键场景中广泛应用，但现有评估缺乏统一的对抗鲁棒性基准来测试策略、价值和潜动力学层面的威胁。

Method: 提出ARB4WM框架，定义五个白盒损失目标（策略级、价值级、潜动力学级），结合单步/多步扰动策略和全帧、半序列、稀疏帧三种时序攻击模式，在MetaWorld和DeepMind Control Suite的20个任务上评估四个Dreamer风格智能体。

Result: 价值估计、潜表征和RSSM动力学攻击与直接策略破坏同样有害；早期或频繁扰动危害更大；输入级防御在自适应攻击下恢复能力有限。

Conclusion: 世界模型的安全评估应覆盖多组件导向的攻击目标和时序暴露协议，而非仅依赖动作空间鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究世界模型在连续控制任务中的对抗鲁棒性，评估Dreamer系列智能体在MetaWorld和DeepMind Control Suite上的鲁棒性，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial robustness for world models in continuous control, which is a niche subtopic not aligned with user's priority on video generation and continuous multimodal models. Signals: world model robustness, continuous control, adversarial attack, Dreamer agents, safety-critical deployment

Abstract: World models are widely used in robotic and agentic engineering control systems due to their ability to learn latent dynamics for planning and decision-making. As these systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical settings, understanding their robustness under adversarial conditions has become essential. However, existing evaluations lack a unified benchmark for testing adversarial threats across the policy, value, and latent-dynamics levels of world-model agents. To fill this gap, we present ARB4WM, a unified evaluation framework for pre-deployment robustness and risk assessment of world-model agents under visual perturbations. ARB4WM defines five white-box loss objectives across these three levels and studies their effects when combined with single-step or multi-step perturbation strategies and temporal attack modes, including full-frame, half-sequence, and sparse-frame exposure. Specifically, we evaluate four Dreamer-style agents across 20 tasks from MetaWorld and the DeepMind Control Suite under different loss objectives, perturbation strategies, and temporal attack modes. Results show that attacks targeting value estimation, latent representations, and RSSM dynamics can be as damaging as direct policy disruption, and that early or frequent perturbations are especially harmful, while input-level defenses provide limited recovery under adaptive attacks. These findings suggest that safety, risk, and reliability assessment for world models should cover multiple component-oriented attack objectives and temporal exposure protocols rather than relying solely on action-space robustness. Source code is available at .

</details>


### [47] [Metis: A Generalizable and Efficient World-Action Model for Autonomous Driving and Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15869)
*Jingyu Li, Zhe Liu, Dongnan Hu, Junjie Wu, Zipei Ma, Wenxiao Wu, Chao Han, Zhihui Hao, Zhikang Liu, Kun Zhan, Jiankang Deng, Xiatian Zhu, Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: Metis 提出了一种解耦视频生成与动作预测的世界-动作模型框架，采用 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构和不对称注意力掩码，在自动驾驶导航任务上实现 SOTA 性能并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）在自动驾驶中存在两大问题：一是推理时需先生成未来观测视频导致高延迟，二是视频与动作建模紧密耦合导致表征不匹配和泛化能力下降。本文旨在解决这两个关键瓶颈。

Method: Metis 采用 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，包含视频生成专家和动作预测专家两个专用模块，分别保持各自任务的分布特性。引入不对称注意力掩码机制，使两个专家可以联合训练，同时允许动作模型在推理时跳过显式视频生成，实现训练-推理一致性并大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navhard/navtest 基准和 CityWalker 导航基准上取得 SOTA 性能，并在真实机器人部署中验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: Metis 通过解耦视频生成与动作预测，有效解决了现有 WAM 的高延迟和表征不匹配问题，在自动驾驶导航任务上展现了优越的泛化能力和效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Metis，一种用于自动驾驶和城市导航的世界-动作模型（WAM），将视频生成与动作预测解耦，属于面向具身智能（自动驾驶）的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Decouples video generation and action prediction for efficient autonomous driving, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world-action model, Mixture-of-Transformers, asymmetric attention mask, decoupled video and action, SOTA on NAVSIM

Abstract: World action models~(WAMs) have shown great promise for autonomous driving and urban navigation. Built upon Vision-Language-Action models or video generation models, existing approaches suffer key limitations: (1) High inference latency due to future observation prediction at test time, and (2) tightly coupled video and action modeling leading to representational mismatch and degraded generalization. To address both issues, we propose Metis, an end-to-end WAM framework that decouples video generation and action prediction. Specifically, Metis employs a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture with dedicated experts for video generation and action prediction, preserving the intrinsic distributional properties of each task. To enhance efficiency, we introduce an asymmetric attention mask that enables joint training of both experts while allowing the action model to bypass explicit video generation during inference. This design ensures training-inference consistency and significantly reduces computational costs without compromising planning performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the NAVSIM navhard and navtest benchmarks and the CityWalker navigation benchmark, validating both the generalizability and efficiency across diverse tasks. Real-robot deployments further confirm the practical feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [48] [BRICKS-WM: Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16489)
*Shaowei Zhang, Jiahan Cao, Xunlan Zhou, Shenghua Wan, De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (78.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BRICKS-WM 提出一种模块化结构世界模型框架，将潜状态分解为 Agent 和 Background 两个独立动力学模块，通过可学习的潜接口进行组合。该方法在保持与单块基线相当的控制性能的同时，实现了背景动力学的跨智能体复用，无需重新训练整个世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜世界模型的 MBRL 方法通常采用单块（monolithic）潜动力学，将环境动力学耦合在一起，导致可复用性极差：更换智能体时必须从头重新训练整个世界模型，即使环境保持不变。受物理世界由独立实体组成的启发，作者希望将全局动力学建模为不同动力学模块通过潜接口组合的方式，实现模块化复用。

Method: BRICKS-WM 将潜状态空间分解为两个模块：Agent 模块（受动作影响的智能体动力学）和 Background 模块（不受智能体动作影响的环境动力学），两者通过一个可学习的潜接口（latent interface）桥接。潜接口负责在 Agent 和 Background 之间传递信息，使得 Background 动力学对 Agent 的动力学保持不可知（agnostic）。训练时采用标准的 RSSM 架构进行潜动力学学习和策略优化，推理时可将冻结的 Background 模块与新的 Agent 模块组合使用。

Result: 在连续控制任务上，BRICKS-WM 从头训练时取得了与 DreamerV3 等强单块基线相当的控制性能。在跨智能体复用实验中，冻结的 Background 模块可与不同 Agent 模块组合，显著减少了重新训练所需的数据量，验证了模块化结构世界模型的可复用性。

Conclusion: BRICKS-WM 通过功能解耦的模块化潜动力学设计，在保持控制性能的同时实现了世界模型的可复用性。该工作为构建可组合、可复用的结构化世界模型提供了新思路，未来可扩展到更多模块的分解与组合。

Classification reason: 该论文提出模块化结构世界模型 BRICKS-WM，将潜状态分解为 Agent 和 Background 模块，通过潜接口组合动力学，实现背景动力学跨智能体复用。这属于面向机器人/具身智能控制的世界模型方法，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes modular world model for agent-background dynamics separation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, modular dynamics, model-based RL, latent interface, reusability

Abstract: Model-based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable success in continuous control by leveraging latent world models. However, prevailing approaches typically rely on monolithic latent dynamics, entangling environment dynamics into a coupled process. This coupling severely limits reusability: altering the agent necessitates retraining the entire world from scratch, even if the environment remains constant. To address this, we introduce BRICKS-WM (Building Reusability via Interface Composition Kinetics for Structured World Models), a framework for the modular assembly of structured world models. Driven by the insight that the physical world is composed of independent entities, we posit that global dynamics can be modeled as a composition of distinct dynamical modules interacting via latent interfaces. As a minimal instantiation, we factorize the latent state space into an actuated Agent module and an external Background module, bridged by a learned latent interface. Unlike prior object-centric methods that prioritize visual segmentation, BRICKS-WM enforces a functional separation in transition dynamics, ensuring that background dynamics remains agnostic to the agent's dynamics. Empirically, BRICKS-WM achieves control performance comparable to strong monolithic baselines when trained from scratch, and enables the reuse of frozen background dynamics across agents.

</details>

*Reza T Batley, Andrew Kichline, Sourav Saha*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 本文提出可分离神经架构(SNA)，将神经网络近似与张量分解结合，作为物理世界模型求解偏微分方程。在变分SNA框架下满足经典变分保证，在高维时空参数化PDE中以代数而非指数级缩放缓解维度灾难。在7D参数化制造仿真和热-属性反演管道中，实现百万次蒙特卡洛查询仅需102秒（比有限元基线快150,000倍），实时生成式逆重建低于100ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法（如有限元）求解高维偏微分方程时面临维度灾难，计算成本随维度指数增长。现有神经方法虽有一定进展，但缺乏严格的数学保证和高效的可扩展性。本文旨在设计一种兼具理论保证、高效计算和物理世界建模能力的神经架构。

Method: 提出可分离神经架构(SNA)，将函数表示为局部坐标函数（原子）与稀疏低秩交互对象的解耦组合。在变分SNA(VSNA)框架下将其视为Galerkin试验空间，满足Lax-Milgram引理下的适定性、拟最优性、收敛性和稳定性。采用完全因子化的张量原生交替最小二乘(ALS)优化，使计算成本随维度线性增长。

Result: 在椭圆、双曲和抛物型PDE系统上验证了预测的代数与谱缩放率。在7D参数化制造仿真中，VSNA在标准笔记本CPU上102秒完成百万次蒙特卡洛查询，比A100 GPU上的全网格有限元基线快150,000倍。在Inconel 718热-属性反演中实现低于100ms的实时生成式逆重建。

Conclusion: SNA作为一种紧凑的数学基元，能够对连续参数流形进行建模，实现实时反演、优化循环和快速不确定性传播，是一种"一次求解，随处查询"的物理世界模型，在工程仿真和逆问题中具有广阔应用前景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出可分离神经架构(SNA)作为物理世界模型，用于求解偏微分方程(PDE)并实现物理仿真，属于世界模型方向下的"可交互与物理世界建模"([CANONICAL] interactive_physical_world)子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, offering a novel neural architecture for physical simulation with massive speedups. Signals: world model, efficient inference, neural simulator, continuous parameter manifolds, high-dimensional PDE solving

Abstract: This work introduces the Separable Neural Architecture (SNA), a function representational class combining neural approximation with tensor decomposition. The SNA decouples localized coordinate functions (atoms) from global interactions governed by a sparse, low-rank interaction object. This architecture possesses a compact and smooth inductive bias well-suited for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). When viewed as a Galerkin trial space under the variational SNA (VSNA) framework, the formulation satisfies classical variational guarantees under Lax-Milgram: well-posedness, quasi-optimality, convergence, and stability. In high-dimensional spatiotemporal--parametric PDEs, the VSNA mitigates the curse of dimensionality by scaling algebraically rather than exponentially. Exploiting an entirely factorized, tensor-native alternating least squares (ALS) optimization framework reduces this cost to linear in dimension. The VSNA is validated across elliptic, hyperbolic, and parabolic systems, demonstrating close alignment with predicted algebraic and spectral scaling rates. We showcase the SNA as a "solve once, query anywhere" physical world model via two engineering case studies: a 7D parametric manufacturing simulation and an experimental thermal-to-property inversion pipeline for Inconel 718. The VSNA executes a 1,000,000-query Monte Carlo sweep in 102s on a standard laptop CPU, yielding a 150,000x speedup over a full-grid finite element baseline hosted on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. It further enables real-time generative inverse-mode reconstructions under 100ms. These results demonstrate that the SNA serves as a compact mathematical substrate for continuous parameter manifolds to enable real-time inversion, optimization loops, and rapid uncertainty propagation.

</details>

Conclusion: 世界模型的评测应与其声称的用途相匹配。对于面向具身决策的世界模型，核心评测不应停留在视觉质量，而应聚焦于反事实推理、策略评估、规划和策略优化等决策相关能力。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦世界模型的评测问题，核心关注世界模型在具身决策中的用途（反事实推理、策略评估、规划、策略优化），属于世界模型方向。论文提出的L0-L7评测阶梯涵盖从视觉真实性到策略优化效用的多个层次，核心关注可交互与物理世界建模中的评测协议，因此映射到[可交互与物理世界建模]这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model evaluation for decision-making, aligning with user's high-priority world_model direction and boosting key signals. Signals: world model evaluation, decision-making-centric, counterfactual reasoning, policy optimization, L0-L7 ladder

Abstract: World models have rapidly become one of the central abstractions in modern AI. Yet the term now refers to several different objects: action-conditioned environment models, latent imagination models, future-video predictors, interactive neural simulators, latent predictive representations, and synthetic-data engines. Evaluation has broadened with the term. Recent papers measure video realism, perceptual similarity, instruction following, physical plausibility, policy ranking, executability, planning success, and downstream policy improvement. The result is not only metric diversity but also a recurring problem of claim/evidence mismatch: papers frequently make a stronger claim about what their model is useful for than their evaluation can actually establish. This paper surveys the recent literature and argues that the central question is use-dependent. When a model is presented as a world model for embodied decision-making, a more decisive issue is not whether it generates visually compelling videos, but whether it supports reliable counterfactual reasoning, policy evaluation, planning, and policy optimization under intervention, policy-induced distribution shift, and long-horizon rollout. We organize the literature using an L0--L7 ladder that ranges from visual plausibility to policy optimization utility. In our interpretation, L0--L3 are most naturally read as diagnostics of generated artifacts, L4 is often the first genuinely interventional test, and L5--L7 provide the most direct evidence of decision usefulness. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a decision-making-centric evaluation framework and a benchmark protocol that foreground counterfactual action fidelity, closed-loop rollout validity, reward/value prediction, policy-ranking agreement, optimization lift, model exploitability, and uncertainty calibration.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轨迹的方法通常孤立建模物理效应，难以在接触丰富的3D场景中组合保守力（如重力、弹性形变）与非保守力（如阻尼、碰撞能量损失），导致长期物理一致性不足。

Method: 将每个物体表示为结构图，构建动态物体-物体和物体-环境接触图；受哈密顿神经网络启发，通过标量能量项和耗散项（Rayleigh型耗散）建模运动，而非直接预测状态或加速度；保守效应作为可加性能量项组合，非保守效应通过学习耗散函数建模；通过能量和耗散函数的梯度导出力，并使用多子步半隐式积分器进行 rollout。

Result: 在受控轨迹基准上，NEXUS 在多种力学属性和物理效应组合下，长期预测精度优于代表性学习方法和物理结构化动力学基线；NEXUS 轨迹可为接触丰富的视频生成提供有效引导，提升物理合理性同时保持视觉质量。

Conclusion: NEXUS 通过神经能量场框架有效组合保守与非保守动力学，实现了物理一致的接触丰富3D物体动力学建模，并展示了其在引导视频生成方面的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于神经能量场的3D物体动力学框架，关注物理一致的接触丰富场景建模，属于物理世界建模与可交互模拟器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation with physically consistent dynamics, aligning with top user priorities. Signals: world model, neural energy field, contact-rich dynamics, video generation guidance, Hamiltonian neural networks

Abstract: Physics-grounded video generation requires controllable 3D object dynamics that remain physically consistent under contact, deformation, and external forcing. Existing trajectory-based methods often model isolated physical effects, making it difficult to compose conservative and non-conservative dynamics in contact-rich 3D scenes. We present NEXUS, a neural energy-field framework for contact-rich 3D object dynamics. NEXUS represents each object as a structural graph and constructs dynamic object-object and object-environment contact graphs. Inspired by Hamiltonian Neural Networks, NEXUS formulates motion through scalar energy and dissipation terms rather than directly predicting states or accelerations. Conservative effects, including gravity and elastic deformation, are composed as additive energy terms, while non-conservative effects such as damping and impact-induced energy loss are modeled with learned Rayleigh-style dissipation. Forces are derived by differentiating the energy and dissipation functions and rolled out with a multi-substep semi-implicit integrator. Across controlled trajectory benchmarks, NEXUS improves long-horizon accuracy over representative learned and physics-structured dynamics baselines under varying mechanical properties and physical-effect compositions. We further show that NEXUS trajectories provide effective guidance for contact-rich video generation, improving physical plausibility while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>

Conclusion: EgoPhys 证明了从第一人称RGB观测构建可变形物体物理数字孪生的可行性，为真实到仿真（real-to-sim）管线提供了一条可扩展的路径，展示了第一人称视觉作为世界模型数据源的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文从第一人称RGB视频构建可变形物体的物理数字孪生，预测弹性材料动力学，属于物理世界建模与可交互模拟器范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous representation priorities via deformable physics digital twins from egocentric video. Signals: world model, deformable object physics, egocentric video, zero-shot generalization, real-to-sim

Abstract: Humans naturally understand object physics through everyday interactions, but faithfully predicting complex deformable dynamics, such as elastic materials and fabrics, remains a major challenge for computer vision and robotics. We present EgoPhys, a framework that constructs deformable physical digital twins from egocentric RGB-only video using generalizable priors. EgoPhys overcomes the limitations of existing methods to enable controllable deformable digital twin generation from egocentric videos by distilling per-object inverse-physics solutions into a compact codebook, enabling prediction of dense spring stiffness fields for unseen objects without per-spring test-time optimization. Trained with generalizable priors from diverse egocentric interactions, EgoPhys outperforms baselines in reconstruction, future prediction, and zero-shot generalization. To support training and evaluation, we curate an egocentric interaction dataset covering diverse deformable objects, scenes, and manipulation styles. We deploy EgoPhys on a real xArm6 robot, demonstrating that a digital twin initialized from a single egocentric human play video can serve as an internal world representation to aid in deformable-object planning, highlighting egocentric RGB observations as a scalable path toward real-to-sim pipelines.

</details>

Signals: world model synthesis, executable pygame model, K-step lookahead fidelity, partial observability, Montezuma's Revenge

Abstract: World-model synthesis aims to turn interaction experience into an internal model of environment dynamics. Existing symbolic approaches often fit observed transitions or mixtures of local rules, but they do not produce a complete executable program that can run independently of the real environment. We present Mind-Studio, a framework that synthesizes executable pygame-style world models from state-action-next-state trajectories using large language models. Mind-Studio combines entropy-selected traces with a lightweight game skill file containing object, action, and static scene information extracted from screenshots. We evaluate synthesis quality with a K-step lookahead fidelity protocol that compares generated world-model rollouts against Real-ALE rollouts from the same state. On Montezuma's Revenge, Mind-Studio improves chosen-action next-state prediction from 0.3% for PoE-World to 48.7% while verifying 5 of 8 subgoals; across Alien, Assault, and Skiing, it achieves stronger branch-level fidelity than prior learned lookahead sources.

</details>


### [54] [Phys-JEPA: Physics-Informed Latent World Models for Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16076)
*Weizhi Nie, Weichao Liu, Honglin Guo, Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.GT, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: Phys-JEPA 是一种物理信息驱动的联合嵌入预测架构，将多变量时间序列预测中的物理一致性约束从解码输出层迁移到潜状态和潜状态转移上，通过将潜状态分解为物理分量和残差分量，在保持物理可解释性的同时保留对未解析动力学的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度预测模型能够拟合时序相关性，物理信息模型也能用科学约束正则化预测，但这些方法通常仅在解码输出层连接物理约束，导致生成未来轨迹的隐状态在统计上有用但物理上无结构。作者希望将物理先验直接引入潜预测状态空间，使潜世界模型既保持物理一致性又具备对未解析动力学的建模弹性。

Method: Phys-JEPA 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），学习一个潜世界模型。其核心创新是将潜状态分解为物理分量（由已知物理变量组织）和残差分量（捕获未解析动力学），并在潜状态和潜状态转移上施加物理一致性约束，而非仅在解码输出上施加。模型在多个真实世界多变量时间序列数据集（Jena Climate、Traffic、Electricity）上进行评估。

Result: 在 Jena Climate 数据集上，H=24 时聚合 MSE 从 0.12482 降至 0.12273，温度 MSE 从 0.01892 降至 0.01831。在 Traffic 数据集上，完整 Phys-JEPA 在所有预测范围内均优于监督基线，H=192 时 MSE 从 0.800784 降至 0.773873。在 Electricity 数据集上，最佳变体取决于预测范围：静态潜一致性在短范围最优，完整 Phys-JEPA 在 H=192 时给出最佳聚合和目标变量 MSE。

Conclusion: 将物理信息学习从输出空间迁移到潜预测状态空间是构建可解释时序世界模型的一个有前景的方向。Phys-JEPA 通过潜状态分解和潜空间物理约束，在保持预测性能的同时提升了模型的可解释性和物理一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出物理信息驱动的潜世界模型（Phys-JEPA），在潜空间中施加物理一致性约束进行多变量时间序列预测，属于物理世界建模与动力学建模范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong physics-informed latent world model for time-series, but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: world model, latent state decomposition, physics-informed, multivariate forecasting, JEPA

Abstract: Multivariate forecasting in physical systems requires models that predict coupled temporal variables while preserving meaningful state evolution. Deep forecasters can fit temporal correlations, and physics-informed models can regularize predictions with scientific constraints, but these directions are often connected only at the decoded-output level. As a result, the hidden predictive state that generates future trajectories may remain statistically useful but physically unstructured. We introduce Phys-JEPA, a physics-informed joint-embedding predictive architecture for multivariate time-series forecasting. Phys-JEPA learns a latent world model in which predictive states are decomposed into physical and residual components, and physical consistency is imposed directly on latent states and latent transitions rather than only on decoded forecasts. This formulation uses known physical variables to organize the representation space while retaining residual capacity for unresolved dynamics. On Jena Climate 2009--2016, Phys-JEPA reduces aggregate MSE from 0.12482 to 0.12273 and temperature MSE from 0.01892 to 0.01831 at H=24. On Traffic, full Phys-JEPA improves aggregate MSE over the supervised baseline across all tested horizons, reducing H=192 MSE from 0.800784 to 0.773873. On Electricity, the best variant depends on horizon: static latent consistency is strongest at H=24 and H=48, while full Phys-JEPA gives the best aggregate and target-variable MSE at H=192. These initial results suggest that moving physics-informed learning from output space to latent predictive state space is a promising direction for interpretable temporal world models.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出"orchestrated reality"框架，将 LLM 驱动的游戏世界模拟形式化为参数化动作 POMDP。世界状态由规范 JSON 实体树表示，动作分解为离散意图类型加结构化 JSON 参数，LLM 驱动的 PDVA（Plan-Diff-Validate-Apply）管线提交经过模式验证和内容哈希的 JSON 增量来更新世界状态。该框架使 LLM 能够维持持久化、一致的游戏世界，类似于桌游中的 Game Master。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏在紧密编排的叙事与深度模拟的世界之间难以兼顾，沙盒/开放世界游戏尤其昂贵。LLM 驱动的世界提供了一条新路径，但当前部署的系统无法维持持久化世界状态（谁在哪、刚发生了什么、当前什么为真），因为叙事声音以自由散文断言状态而缺乏可验证的表征。本文将此视为架构选择而非语言模型限制，旨在构建一个使世界成为规范对象的框架。

Method: 将 LLM 驱动的游戏世界形式化为参数化动作 POMDP：状态为规范 JSON 实体树；动作分解为 a=(k, x_k)，其中 k 是离散意图类型，x_k 是结构化 JSON 参数；智能体仅观测到状态的叙事投影 o=O(s)；转移核 F 是 LLM 驱动的 Plan-Diff-Validate-Apply (PDVA) 管线，提交经过模式验证和内容哈希的 JSON 增量。框架由单例编排代理（类似桌游 GM）拥有世界规范对象。

Result: 给出了形式化模型、JSON 状态示例、单轮交互示例，以及从实际部署中提取的 15 个说明性事件，展示了框架的实际运行情况。实证验证（人类玩家研究、多 NPC 并发代理、作为 RL 环境部署）定位为未来工作。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的游戏世界模拟可以通过将世界状态形式化为规范 JSON 对象并采用 PDVA 管线来实现持久化、一致的世界维护。该框架为构建完全自主的游戏引擎提供了可行的架构基础，未来工作包括人类玩家研究、多 NPC 并发代理以及作为强化学习环境部署。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LLM 驱动的游戏世界模拟框架（orchestrated reality），将世界建模为参数化动作 POMDP，核心是 LLM 驱动的 Plan-Diff-Validate-Apply 管线来维护持久化世界状态。这属于可交互世界建模与模拟器范畴，与 canonical subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel formalization of LLM-driven world simulation as POMDP, but lacks empirical validation and is tangential to core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: world model, LLM-driven simulation, POMDP formalization, game world, no empirical results

Abstract: Many games rely on storytelling combined with systems that track levelling, NPC behaviour, and consequence simulation; bridging tightly-authored narrative with deeply-simulated worlds -- most acute in sandbox and open-world settings -- has been prohibitively expensive. LLM-driven worlds open a new path: a single harness can coordinate numerical state, narrative voice, storytelling pacing, and rule logic together. Realising this requires the LLM system to sustain a persistent world (who is where, what has just happened, what is currently true), which today's deployed systems do not: the narrative voice asserts state in free prose without any validated representation, so a fully autonomous game engine remains infeasible. We treat this as an architectural choice, not a limitation of language models, and report work in progress on a framework -- orchestrated reality -- that makes the world a canonical object owned by a singleton orchestration agent analogous to the tabletop-RPG Game Master (GM). We formalise an LLM-driven game world for a human player as a Parameterized-Action POMDP: state is a tree of canonical JSON entities, actions decompose as $a=(k, x_k)$ (a discrete intent kind plus structured JSON parameters), the agent observes only a narrative projection $o=O(s)$ of state, and the transition kernel $F$ is an LLM-driven Plan-Diff-Validate-Apply (PDVA) pipeline that commits schema-validated, content-hashed JSON deltas. We give the formal model, a JSON-state example, a worked single-turn example, and a catalogue of 15 illustrative incidents drawn from a real deployment showing the framework in action. Empirical validation through a planned human player study -- together with multi-NPC concurrent agency and deployment as an RL environment -- is situated as future work.

</details>

*Zongren Zou, Théo Bourdais, Ricardo Baptista, Houman Owhadi*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图形化条件生成建模框架，通过识别影响目标变量完整条件分布的输入变量（而非仅条件均值），从观测数据中发现简约随机替代模型，用于数字孪生建模、控制与数据同化。该方法结合条件生成模型与基于高斯过程的方差分析（核模态分解），迭代剪枝非影响变量，在随机动力系统、PDE控制、强化学习等任务中生成可解释的随机替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生建模面临"开放性保真度问题"：增加变量、数据流和时间尺度会无限增加模型复杂度，导致系统难以维护、验证、解释和用于压力/安全测试。因此需要一种方法，仅基于描述相关感兴趣量所需的变量构建简约随机替代模型。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 条件生成建模——学习目标变量在给定候选输入下的条件分布；(2) 基于高斯过程的方差分析（核模态分解）——迭代剪枝非影响输入变量，发现可解释的结构。在控制场景中，该方法可解释为学习马尔可夫决策过程，自动识别使动力学有效马尔可夫化所需的状态、动作和记忆变量。

Result: 在随机动力系统、缺失变量、PDE控制、强化学习和经济数据等多个示例中，该方法发现的结构能生成可解释的随机替代模型，其下游性能与在全变量集上训练的模型相当。

Conclusion: 该框架为数字孪生建模提供了一种系统性的变量选择与结构发现方法，能够在保持下游任务性能的同时大幅简化模型复杂度，提升模型的可解释性和可维护性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于条件生成建模与核模态分解的框架，用于从观测数据中发现随机替代模型的简约变量结构，构建可解释的马尔可夫决策过程替代模型。这属于物理世界建模与动力学建模范畴，与[可交互与物理世界建模]方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on digital twin modeling with conditional generative models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: conditional generative modeling, digital twin, variable selection, kernel mode decomposition, stochastic surrogate models

Abstract: Digital twin modeling, including control and data assimilation under model uncertainty, often faces an open-ended fidelity problem: adding variables, data streams, and time scales can indefinitely increase model complexity, ultimately producing systems that are difficult to maintain, validate, interpret, and use for stress or safety testing. As an alternative, one can seek parsimonious stochastic surrogate models built only on the variables needed to describe the relevant quantities of interest. We introduce a framework for discovering such variables from observational data by identifying which candidate inputs influence the full conditional law of a target quantity, rather than only its conditional mean. This distinction is essential in stochastic, coarse-grained, or partially observed systems, where dependencies may appear through changes in variability, tail behavior, multimodality, or uncertainty rather than through deterministic functional relationships. The framework couples conditional generative modeling, which learns the conditional distribution of the target given candidate inputs, with Gaussian-process-based analysis of variance (through kernel mode decomposition), which enables iterative pruning of non-influential inputs and interpretable structure discovery. In control settings, the resulting surrogate can be interpreted as a learned Markov decision process: the method identifies not only a transition model, but also the state, action, and memory variables needed to make the learned dynamics effectively Markovian. Across examples involving stochastic dynamical systems, missing variables, PDE control, reinforcement learning, and economic data, the discovered structures yield interpretable stochastic surrogates whose downstream performance is comparable to models trained on the full variable set.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督单目视频深度估计方法通常独立处理视频帧或仅依赖弱时序正则化，缺乏对底层3D场景的整体感知，导致几何不一致的预测和严重的跨帧漂移。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 将视频深度估计重铸为多视图3D重建问题，提出3D一致性优化框架，包含三个约束：图像级光度渲染损失、显式世界坐标几何对齐损失、多尺度时序梯度一致性损失，利用3D基础模型（如DUSt3R）的几何先验进行全局优化。

Result: 在自监督训练和零样本临床内窥镜场景中均取得SOTA空间精度，优于基于帧的、基于视频的深度估计器以及多视图3D重建基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将视频深度估计转化为全局3D重建问题并引入3D基础模型的几何先验，可以有效解决跨帧几何不一致问题，为内窥镜导航等下游3D推理和具身智能应用提供可靠的深度估计。

Classification reason: 该论文提出将自监督单目视频深度估计重铸为多视图3D重建问题，利用3D基础模型的几何先验进行全局3D一致性优化，属于3D场景建模与空间世界模型范畴，因此映射到 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "空间 3D/4D 世界模型"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D consistency for depth estimation, which is tangentially related to world models but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D consistency optimization, self-supervised monocular depth, world-coordinate geometric alignment, zero-shot clinical, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Reliable monocular video depth estimation is crucial for downstream 3D reasoning and embodied AI in endoscopic navigation. However, existing self-supervised approaches typically treat video frames independently or rely on weak temporal regularization. These methods, lacking a holistic perception of the underlying 3D scene, inevitably suffer from geometrically inconsistent predictions and severe cross-frame drift. To address these limitations, we introduce a new paradigm that recasts sequential video depth estimation as an unconstrained multi-view 3D reconstruction problem, enabling full exploitation of the powerful geometric priors embedded in recent 3D foundation models. The core of our approach is a 3D consistency optimization framework driven by three constraints: image-level photometric rendering, explicit world-coordinate geometric alignment, and multi-scale temporal gradient consistency. Such unified optimization elegantly anchors isolated frames to a globally coherent 3D structure. Our method has been validated in both the self-supervised training scenarios and challenging zero-shot clinical environments. Results show that the proposed approach achieves state-of-the-art spatial accuracy, outperforming the frame-based, video-based depth estimators and the multi-view 3D reconstruction baselines.

</details>

### [58] [Momentum-Guided Semantic Forecasting (MoFore) for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14765)
*Qinwu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (81.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MoFore 是一种动量引导的语义预测框架，通过从远距离上下文片段预测未来潜嵌入来学习自监督视频表示，结合对比正则化防止表示坍塌，在 UCF101 上展示了良好的时序一致性和类别结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督视频表示学习方法主要依赖像素级重建（如 MAE/VideoMAE）或对比学习对齐，前者计算成本高且不直接学习语义，后者需要精心设计正负样本。本文希望探索一种更高效的方式——通过预测未来潜空间嵌入来学习具有时序一致性和语义意义的视频表示。

Method: MoFore 框架包含一个动量更新的编码器，从远距离上下文片段提取潜嵌入，并训练一个预测器来预测未来片段的潜嵌入。引入随机时间间隔预测（randomized temporal-gap forecasting）增强跨时间尺度的鲁棒性，同时结合对比正则化损失来保持时序一致性并防止表示坍塌。

Result: 在 UCF101 数据集上的实验表明，MoFore 学习到的嵌入空间具有强时序稳定性和涌现的类别级结构。定性检索实验显示运动感知的组织结构，且无需使用动作标签进行训练。

Conclusion: 长距离潜空间预测是一种有效且计算高效的自监督视频表示学习方法，无需依赖重建目标，能够学习到具有时序一致性和语义结构的视频表征。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MoFore 框架，通过预测未来潜空间嵌入进行自监督视频表示学习，核心是时序预测与未来潜空间建模，与视频世界模型（video_world_model）中未来预测、长时序视频建模的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Proposes momentum-guided latent forecasting for video representation, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or world model generation. Signals: Self-supervised video representation, latent forecasting, contrastive regularization, UCF101 evaluation, no reconstruction objective

Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has recently advanced through contrastive learning, masked reconstruction, and predictive representation learning. Reconstruction-based approaches such as MAE and VideoMAE learn representations by recovering masked visual content \cite{he2022mae,tong2022videomae}, while contrastive methods such as CLIP learn semantically meaningful embedding spaces through representation alignment \cite{radford2021clip}. In this work, we introduce a Momentum-Guided Semantic Forecasting framework (MoFore) for self-supervised video representation learning. Instead of optimizing for pixel-level reconstruction or task-specific semantic alignment, the proposed method learns temporally predictive video representations by forecasting future latent embeddings from temporally distant context clips. To improve robustness across temporal scales, we further introduce randomized temporal-gap forecasting during training. The framework combines predictive latent forecasting with contrastive regularization to encourage temporal consistency while preventing representation collapse. Experiments on the UCF101 dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework learns temporally consistent and semantically meaningful video representations without using action labels during training. Quantitative analysis shows strong temporal stability and emergent category-level structure in the learned embedding space, while qualitative retrieval experiments reveal motion-aware organization across related activities. Overall, the results suggest that long-range latent forecasting provides an effective and computationally efficient approach for self-supervised video representation learning without relying on reconstruction-based objectives.

</details>


### [59] [GeoStream: Toward Precise Camera Controlled Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15162)
*Yizhou Zhao, Yifan Wang, Xiaoyuan Wang, Yushu Wu, Hao Zhang, Moayed Haji-Ali, Rameen Abdal, Ashkan Mirzaei, Yanyu Li, Willi Menapace, Laszlo Jeni, Sergey Tulyakov, Peter Wonka, Chaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoStream 提出了一种支持精确度量级摄像机控制的流式自回归视频生成框架。通过维护一个自刷新的 3D 缓存（从模型自身生成帧中估计深度并反投影到 3D，再重投影到目标视角），结合在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation），解决了现有方法在摄像机轨迹超出初始视锥时控制失效的问题，显著提升了摄像机可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型大多隐式学习摄像机运动，导致在分布外轨迹下控制不精确。显式几何条件方法虽提升了可控性，但非自回归且依赖从初始帧构建的静态 3D 缓存，一旦视角移出原始视锥便失效。因此需要一种能够持续更新几何条件、支持长序列自回归生成的精确摄像机控制方法。

Method: GeoStream 采用自回归流式视频生成架构，维护一个自刷新 3D 缓存：从最新生成帧估计深度图，反投影到 3D 点云，再根据目标摄像机参数重投影到新视角，生成点重投影（point reprojections）作为后续帧生成的几何条件。训练时采用 on-policy 蒸馏，即条件信号也从模型自身生成的帧中渲染，使训练与推理的条件分布自然对齐，缓解自回归漂移和几何反馈回路问题。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，GeoStream 在摄像机控制精度上显著优于现有方法，能够处理超出初始视锥的复杂摄像机轨迹，生成具有精确几何一致性的长视频序列。

Conclusion: GeoStream 通过自刷新 3D 缓存和 on-policy 蒸馏，有效解决了自回归视频生成中摄像机控制不精确和分布外轨迹失效的问题，为视频世界模型提供了更可靠的摄像机控制能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GeoStream，一种支持精确摄像机控制的流式视频生成框架，用于视频世界模型。核心贡献在于通过自刷新 3D 缓存实现度量级摄像机控制，属于动作/摄像机条件视频生成，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型 (video_world_model) 高度匹配。同时，该方法涉及视频生成技术，也可匹配 multimodal_generation 下的 text_to_video_diffusion，但主要贡献在世界模型方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances autoregressive video generation with precise camera control, a core need for video world models and a top-weighted user priority. Signals: autoregressive video generation, world model, camera control, on-policy distillation, 3D cache

Abstract: Accurate interactive camera control is essential for video-based world models, but most existing approaches learn camera motion implicitly, leading to inaccurate control under out-of-distribution trajectories. Explicit geometric conditioning improves controllability, but existing methods are non-autoregressive and rely on a static 3D cache built from an initial frame, which becomes ineffective once the viewpoint moves beyond the original frustum. We propose GeoStream, a framework that enables precise metric-scale camera control in autoregressive streaming video generation. Our method maintains a self-refreshing 3D cache that is periodically updated online from the model's own outputs: we estimate depth from the most recently generated frame, unproject to 3D, and reproject into the target view to produce point reprojections as geometric conditioning for subsequent synthesis. By the same principle, the conditioning seen during training is also rendered from the student's own generated frames, yielding a fully on-policy distillation that naturally aligns the train and inference conditioning distributions. Unlike prior work that uses off-policy condition noising, our approach trains the model against the exact error distribution it encounters at inference, mitigating both standard autoregressive drift and the second-order geometric feedback loop that arises when the cache itself is derived from generated outputs. Quantitative and qualitative results show that our approach substantially improves camera controllability.

</details>


### [60] [CausalDrive: Real-time Causal World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15341)
*Tianyi Yan, Huan Zheng, Dubing Chen, Meizhi Qu, Yingying Shen, Lijun Zhou, Mingfei Tu, Bing Wang, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Haiyang Sun, Cheng-zhong Xu, Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CausalDrive提出了一种用于自动驾驶的实时因果世界模型，仅依赖初始前视图、自车轨迹和文本提示，通过Context-Forced DMD架构（连续流匹配+自校正蒸馏）实现12 FPS的交互式神经模拟器，支持文本驱动的驾驶社会学控制、闭环评估、强化学习后训练和人在环仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型存在两大局限：基于布局条件渲染的方法依赖未来NPC轨迹的"预言"信息，无法实现反应式交互；纯动作条件预测器缺乏对复杂交互的语义控制，且扩散模型延迟过高，无法用于闭环策略学习。因此需要一种可控、实时的因果世界模型。

Method: 提出CausalDrive框架，核心创新包括：(1) 仅使用初始前视图、自车轨迹和宏观文本提示作为输入，不依赖未来NPC布局，迫使模型内在预测因果交互；(2) 提出Context-Forced DMD架构，结合连续流匹配（flow matching）与自校正蒸馏目标，实现12 FPS的交互式生成速度；(3) 支持文本驱动的"驾驶社会学"控制，可动态编排对相同自车动作的不同反事实反应。

Result: CausalDrive达到12 FPS的交互式生成速度；在闭环评估中显著减少了碰撞伪影；通过Video2Reward模块支持大规模强化学习后训练；支持实时人在环仿真；实验证明在CausalDrive反应式场景中训练的策略在真实世界中表现出更优的交互能力。

Conclusion: CausalDrive将被动视频生成器转变为可玩的神经模拟器，为自动驾驶提供了一种可控、实时的因果世界模型，在闭环评估、RL后训练和人在环仿真三个下游应用中均展现出有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CausalDrive，一种用于自动驾驶的可控实时因果世界模型，通过视频生成实现交互式神经模拟器，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: High relevance as a real-time world model for autonomous driving using continuous flow-matching and self-correcting distillation, directly aligning with priority subtopics on world models and continuous multimodal models. Signals: world model, continuous flow-matching, real-time interactive simulator, text-driven driving sociology, closed-loop evaluation

Abstract: World models have emerged as a promising paradigm for scaling autonomous driving (AD) data, yet existing video generative models fall short as interactive simulators. Layout-conditioned renderers rely on "oracle" future trajectories of all background agents, rendering them strictly non-reactive. Conversely, pure action-conditioned predictors lack semantic control over complex interactions and suffer from prohibitive diffusion latencies, hindering closed-loop policy learning. To bridge this gap, we present CausalDrive, a controllable, real-time foundation driving world renderer. CausalDrive operates solely on the initial front-view frame, the ego-vehicle's trajectory, and a macroscopic text prompt. By excluding future NPC layouts, we compel the model to intrinsically predict causal interactions, enabling text-driven control over Driving Sociology, allowing users to dynamically orchestrate diverse counterfactual reactions to identical ego-actions. To overcome the efficiency bottleneck and address the covariate shift in autoregressive generation, we propose a novel Context-Forced DMD architecture. This combines continuous flow-matching with a self-correcting distillation objective, achieving interactive speeds of 12 FPS. This breakthrough transforms the passive video generator into a playable neural simulator. We demonstrate its versatility across three downstream applications: (1) generative closed-loop evaluation with significantly mitigated collision artifacts, (2) large-scale Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training driven by a Video2Reward module, and (3) real-time human-in-the-loop simulation. Extensive experiments validate that policies trained within CausalDrive's reactive scenarios exhibit superior interaction capabilities in the real world.

</details>

*Kairos Team, Fei Wang, Shan You, Qiming Zhang, Tao Huang, Zuoyi Fu, Zhisheng Zheng, Yunlong Xi, Feng Lv, Xiaoming Wu, Zeyu Liu, Cong Wan, Pu Li, Ruiqing Yang, Xiaoou Li, Wei Wang, Kangkang Zhu, Yuwei Zhang, Shi Fu, Xiaoning Wu, Xuzeng Fan, Dacheng Tao, Xiaogang Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多作为被动视觉生成器，缺乏从异构经验中学习世界知识、维持长时序持久状态以及在真实部署约束下高效执行的能力。作者旨在构建一个作为 Physical AI 基础操作基础设施的原生世界模型栈。

Method: (1) 原生预训练范式：采用跨具身数据课程（Cross-Embodiment Data Curriculum），将开放世界视频、人类行为数据和机器人交互组织为渐进式发展路径。(2) 原生统一架构：混合线性时序注意力（Hybrid Linear Temporal Attention），滑动窗口注意力捕获局部动态，膨胀滑动窗口捕获中程依赖，门控线性注意力维持持久全局记忆，并给出理论误差界保证长时序状态传播。(3) 部署感知系统协同设计：支持在服务器和消费级硬件上进行低延迟 rollout 生成，实现真实观测-动作-反馈循环。

Result: 在具身世界模型、长时序和动作策略基准上达到顶级性能，同时提供强效率-能力权衡。

Conclusion: Kairos 作为一个统一的原生世界模型栈，为未来自进化物理智能提供了可操作的基础设施，证明了跨具身数据预训练与高效时序架构结合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Kairos，一个面向 Physical AI 的原生世界模型栈，涵盖跨具身数据预训练、统一理解/生成/预测架构（混合线性时序注意力）、长时序状态传播与部署感知系统设计。核心贡献属于视频世界模型与具身世界模型交叉领域，但论文重点在于通用世界模型架构（视频生成、预测、理解统一），与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances the user's top-priority world model and continuous multimodal reasoning with a native unified architecture and cross-embodiment pre-training. Signals: world model stack, native unified architecture, hybrid linear temporal attention, cross-embodiment data curriculum, deployment-aware system co-design

Abstract: World models are transitioning from passive visual generators to foundational, operational infrastructure for Physical AI: they must natively acquire world knowledge from heterogeneous experience, maintain persistent states over long horizons, and execute efficiently within real deployment constraints. We introduce Kairos, a native world model stack designed around these requirements. (1) Kairos learns the world by pioneering a Native Pre-training Paradigm governed by a Cross-Embodiment Data Curriculum, which organizes open-world videos, human behavioral data, and robot interactions into a progressive developmental pathway. (2) Kairos maintains the world by unified world understanding, generation, and prediction within a Native Unified Architecture equipped with Hybrid Linear Temporal Attention, where sliding-window attention captures local dynamics, dilated sliding windows capture mid-range dependencies, and gated linear attention maintains persistent global memory. We establish formal theoretical bounds demonstrating that this temporal factorization strictly limits error accumulation, mathematically guaranteeing state propagation across extended horizons. (3) Kairos runs the world by incorporating a Deployment-Aware System Co-Design to support low-latency rollout generation on server and consumer-grade hardware for real-world observation-action-feedback loops. Experiments on embodied world-model, long-horizon, and action-policy benchmarks show that Kairos achieves top level performance while offering a strong efficiency-capability trade-off. Together, these results position Kairos as a cohesive operational foundation for future self-evolving physical intelligence.

</details>
