<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗AI系统大多输出静态标签或评分，无法捕捉疾病随时间演变的动态过程，也无法模拟不同干预措施对疾病轨迹的影响。临床医生需要能够预测病情恶化、比较不同治疗方案后果的工具，而现有方法缺乏这种动态模拟能力。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了基础模型、纵向建模、疾病模拟、治疗效果估计、强化学习和数字孪生等领域的相关工作，围绕患者状态构建、临床动力学建模和干预决策支持三大能力组织分析框架，比较了各代表性系统的贡献与局限。

Result: 提出了医疗世界模型的系统框架和路线图，明确了三大核心能力（状态构建、动力学建模、干预决策）及其相互关系，识别了从概念到临床实用模拟器面临的关键挑战，包括数据稀疏性、因果推断、不确定性量化、临床验证等。

Conclusion: 医疗世界模型有望推动医疗AI从静态诊断预测向动态疾病模拟和干预决策支持演进，但实现临床可用的模拟器仍需克服数据、因果、验证等多方面挑战。该框架为未来研究提供了系统化的路线图。

Classification reason: 该论文是一篇关于医疗世界模型的综述，核心思想是将AI中的世界模型概念引入医疗领域，学习患者状态动态的内部模拟器，涵盖状态构建、临床动力学建模和干预决策支持。这与世界模型方向高度相关，且属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的广义范畴——虽然论文聚焦医疗而非视频，但"世界模型"的核心定义（学习动态系统内部模拟器、未来预测、动作条件建模）完全匹配该子主题的宽泛内涵。

Importance reason: This is a medical domain survey, not a technical advance in the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: medical world model, clinical dynamics, intervention policy, survey, no technical contribution

Abstract: Medical diagnosis and treatment are dynamic processes in which patient states evolve over time and clinical interventions alter future outcomes. Although current medical AI can detect disease, estimate risk and generate reports, many systems still return static labels or scores, offering limited insight into how illness may progress or how alternative interventions may reshape its trajectory. Medical world models adapt the world-model idea from artificial intelligence to healthcare by learning internal simulators of patient-state dynamics. Their long-term goal is to help clinicians anticipate deterioration, compare treatment-conditioned futures and tailor care to individual patients. Yet relevant work remains scattered across foundation models, longitudinal modelling, disease simulation, treatment-effect estimation, reinforcement learning and digital twins. To bridge this gap, this review outlines a roadmap for advancing medical AI from isolated diagnosis and prediction toward medical world models that simulate disease evolution and support intervention decisions. This roadmap is organized around three coupled capabilities: patient-state construction, clinical dynamics modelling and intervention decision support. Across representative systems, the comparison highlights what each capability contributes and how partial components can be integrated into more mature perception--dynamics--planning systems. Finally, we identify the challenges involved in turning plausible rollouts into clinically useful simulators. Related literature is available at .

</details>


### [63] [GraphWorld: Long-Horizon Planning with World Models for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16274)
*Ziying Song, Caiyan Jia, Lin Liu, Lei Yang, Shengkai Zhang, Feiyang Jia, Fengda Zhao, Peiliang Wu, Shaoqing Xu, Chen Lv, Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (86.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GraphWorld 是一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过潜世界建模显式增强长时序规划能力。它引入以自车为中心的交互图（Ego-Centric Interaction Graph）自适应建模关键邻接智能体，并通过世界状态条件规划（World-State-Conditioned Planning）学习潜世界表征来引导安全感知的长时序轨迹规划。在 Bench2Drive、NAVSIMv1/2 和 nuScenes 上的实验表明，该方法显著降低了碰撞率并提升了长时序规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法在短时决策上表现良好，但缺乏对长时序时间依赖关系的建模能力，在复杂高交互驾驶场景中泛化性和安全性受限。因此，作者希望显式引入潜世界模型来增强长时序规划。

Method: 1) Ego-Centric Interaction Graph：基于空间邻近度自适应建模关键邻接智能体，通过跨节点交叉注意力将关系上下文传播到规划查询。2) World-State-Conditioned Planning：学习以自车为中心的潜世界表征，建模自车与周围智能体的交互动态，捕捉关键交互动态和安全相关语义，作为条件信号引导长时序安全感知轨迹规划。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive、NAVSIMv1/2 和 nuScenes 三个基准上，GraphWorld 显著降低了碰撞率，并在长时序规划性能上优于现有方法，验证了其在复杂驾驶环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: GraphWorld 通过潜世界建模有效增强了端到端自动驾驶的长时序规划能力，在多个基准上取得了更安全、更鲁棒的规划性能，为自动驾驶中的世界模型应用提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GraphWorld，通过潜世界模型（latent world modeling）显式增强端到端自动驾驶中的长时序规划（long-horizon planning），属于世界模型方向。其核心是学习自车为中心的潜世界状态表征来建模交互动态并指导轨迹规划，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）中"未来预测、长时序视频建模与动作条件视频生成"的主题高度吻合，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strong world model for autonomous driving, but not directly aligned with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, autonomous driving, long-horizon planning, ego-centric interaction graph, latent world state

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has made significant progress by unifying perception, prediction, and planning within a single learning framework, achieving strong performance in short-horizon decision making. However, most existing E2E-AD methods remain confined to short-horizon planning and lack the ability to model long-term temporal dependencies, which severely limits their generalization and security in complex and highly interactive driving scenarios. In this work, we propose GraphWorld, an E2E-AD framework that explicitly enhances long-horizon planning through latent world modeling. We introduce an Ego-Centric Interaction Graph, which adaptively models critical neighboring agents based on spatial proximity, and propagates relational context to planning queries via cross-node cross-attention. We present a World-State-Conditioned Planning that learns ego-centric latent world representations by modeling interactions between an ego vehicle and surrounding agents. This latent world state captures key interaction dynamics and safety-relevant semantics, and serves as a conditioning signal to guide long-horizon, safety-aware trajectory planning. Extensive experiments on Bench2Drive, NAVSIMv1/2, and nuScenes demonstrate that GraphWorld significantly reduces collision rates and improves long-horizon planning performance, validating its effectiveness in complex driving environments.

</details>


### [64] [BadWorld: Adversarial Attacks on World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16519)
*Linghui Shen, Mingyue Cui, Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BadWorld 提出了一种针对自回归视觉世界模型的无标签对抗攻击框架，通过自监督速度攻击破坏早期去噪动力学，并结合轨迹自适应双层优化生成控制无关的扰动，揭示了 VWM 在安全关键系统中的结构性脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型（VWM）在安全关键系统中应用日益广泛，但其对对抗扰动的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。标准对抗攻击方法无法评估 VWM 的脆弱性，因为攻击者缺乏真实未来视频作为监督信号，也无法预测用户后续的控制输入。

Method: 提出 BadWorld 框架：(1) 自监督速度攻击（self-supervised velocity attack），通过破坏模型早期去噪动力学来绕过对未来监督的需求；(2) 轨迹自适应双层优化（trajectory-adaptive bi-level optimization），主动挖掘困难控制序列以生成控制无关的对抗扰动。

Result: 在具有连续和离散控制的代表性 VWM 上评估，BadWorld 暴露了严重的结构性脆弱性。视觉上不可区分的对抗图像能够可靠地引发未来 rollout 的灾难性退化，包括去噪不完整、结构坍塌和控制不一致。

Conclusion: 揭示了 VWM 在安全关键系统中部署的关键风险，同时展示了对抗攻击在隐私保护方面的潜在应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自回归视觉世界模型（VWM）的对抗攻击鲁棒性，属于世界模型方向。其核心研究对象是动作条件视频生成式世界模型，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly targets world model robustness, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel attack framework. Signals: world model adversarial attack, self-supervised velocity attack, trajectory-adaptive optimization, safety-critical vulnerability, control-agnostic perturbation

Abstract: Visual world models (VWMs) synthesize interactive, action-conditioned rollouts from a single context image. However, it remains an open question how robust these models are to adversarial perturbations. Standard adversarial attacks fail to assess this vulnerability because attackers lack ground-truth future videos and cannot predict subsequent user controls. We introduce BadWorld, a label-free adversarial framework tailored for autoregressive VWMs that systematically overcomes both constraints. First, to bypass the need for future supervision, we propose a self-supervised velocity attack that directly disrupts the early denoising dynamics of the model. Second, to ensure the attack generalizes across unpredictable user actions, we formulate a trajectory-adaptive bi-level optimization that actively mines hard control sequences to forge control-agnostic perturbations. Evaluated on representative VWMs with continuous and discrete controls, BadWorld exposes severe structural fragility. Visually indistinguishable adversarial images reliably trigger catastrophic degradation in future rollouts, leading to incomplete denoising, structural collapse, and control inconsistency. These findings reveal critical risks for deploying VWMs in safety-critical systems while highlighting a practical mechanism for privacy protection.

</details>


### [65] [DreamX-World 1.0: A General-Purpose Interactive World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16993)
*DreamX Team, Yancheng Bai, Rui Chen, Xiangxiang Chu, Rujing Dang, Hao Dou, Bingjie Gao, Qiwen Gu, Siyu Hong, Jiachen Lei, Geng Li, Jifan Li, Ruimin Lin, Qingfeng Shi, Bingze Song, Lei Sun, Jing Tang, Ruitian Tian, Jun Wang, Jiahong Wu, Pengfei Zhang, Shen Zhang, Jiashu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: DreamX-World 1.0 是一个通用的交互式文/图到视频世界模型，支持相机控制、场景记忆、事件指令和长时序自回归生成，通过因果强制、DMD蒸馏、长展开训练、记忆条件场景持久化等技术，在多种风格域上实现可控长视频生成，推理速度达 16 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长时序可控生成方面存在不足，缺乏对相机导航、场景记忆、事件控制等交互能力的统一支持，且生成质量随自回归步数累积而下降。

Method: 提出 DreamX-World 1.0，包含：(1) E-PRoPE 轻量级投影位置编码实现相机控制；(2) 将双向视频生成器通过因果强制、DMD蒸馏和长展开训练转化为自回归世界模型；(3) 记忆条件场景持久化（Memory-Conditioned Scene Persistence）通过相机几何检索恢复历史视角；(4) 残差循环（Residual Recycling）降低对不完美记忆潜变量的敏感性；(5) 事件指令微调（Event Instruction Tuning）实现可组合事件控制；(6) 强化学习对齐恢复蒸馏后的相机控制与视觉质量；(7) 混合精度 DiT、残差复用、75%剪枝VAE解码和异步流水线并行实现高效推理。

Result: 在 5 秒基础评测上，DreamX-World 1.0 的相机控制得分 73.75，综合得分 84.76，优于 HY-WorldPlay 1.5（80.79）和 LingBot-World（80.45）。在 8 张 RTX 5090 GPU 上可达 16 FPS。

Conclusion: DreamX-World 1.0 展示了通用交互式世界模型在长时序可控视频生成上的可行性，通过多种技术创新在生成质量、可控性和推理效率上均取得显著提升。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DreamX-World 1.0，一个通用的交互式文/图到视频世界模型，支持可控长时序生成、相机导航、事件控制等，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）的典型工作。同时其包含文生视频、视频扩散等技术，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances the user's top-priority world model and video generation subtopics with a general-purpose interactive system. Signals: world model, interactive video generation, camera control, autoregressive long-horizon, memory-conditioned scene persistence

Abstract: DreamX-World 1.0 is a general-purpose interactive text/image-to-video world model for controllable long-horizon generation. It supports camera navigation, revisits to previously observed regions, and promptable events across photorealistic, game-style, and stylized domains. Our data engine combines camera-accurate Unreal Engine rendering, action-rich gameplay recordings, and real-world videos with recovered camera geometry. For camera control, we introduce E-PRoPE, a lightweight variant of projective positional encoding that retains PRoPE's projective camera geometry while applying camera-aware attention to spatially reduced tokens. We convert a bidirectional video generator into a few-step autoregressive world model using causal forcing, DMD-style distillation, and long-rollout training. Training on self-generated long-horizon contexts exposes the model to its own generated history and reduces the style and color drift that accumulates across autoregressive chunks. Memory-Conditioned Scene Persistence retrieves earlier views through camera-geometry-based retrieval, while residual recycling makes the conditioning path less sensitive to imperfect memory latents. Event Instruction Tuning adds composable event control, and reinforcement learning alignment recovers camera control and visual quality after distillation. With mixed-precision DiT execution, residual reuse, 75\%-pruned VAE decoding, and asynchronous pipeline parallelism, DreamX-World 1.0 reaches up to 16\,FPS on eight RTX\,5090 GPUs. On our 5-second basic evaluation, DreamX-World 1.0 achieves a camera-control score of 73.75 and an overall score of 84.76, outperforming HY-WorldPlay 1.5 and LingBot-World in overall score, which achieve 80.79 and 80.45, respectively.

</details>


### [66] [Qwen-RobotWorld Technical Report: Unifying Embodied World Modeling through Language-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17030)
*Jie Zhang, Xiaoyue Chen, Anzhe Chen, Chenxu Lv, Deqing Li, Gengze Zhou, Hang Yin, Haoqi Yuan, Haoyang Li, Jiahao Li, Jiazhao Zhang, Jingren Zhou, Kaiyuan Gao, Kun Yan, Lihan Jiang, Ningyuan Tang, Pei Lin, Qihang Peng, Shengming Yin, Tianhe Wu, Tianyi Yan, Xiao Xu, Yan Shu, Yanran Zhang, Ye Wang, Yi Wang, Yilei Chen, Yixian Xu, Yiyang Huang, Yuxiang Chen, Zekai Zhang, Zhendong Wang, Zhixing Lei, Zhixuan Liang, Zihao Liu, Zikai Zhou, Xiong-Hui Chen, Chenfei Wu*

TL;DR: Qwen-RobotWorld是一个语言条件视频世界模型，通过自然语言作为统一动作接口，预测物理上合理的未来视觉轨迹。它采用双流MMDiT架构耦合Qwen2.5-VL语义与视频VAE潜变量，构建了8.6M视频-文本语料库EWK，并通过General+Expert渐进式课程训练策略实现通用视觉先验学习与具身专业化注入。在多个基准上取得SOTA结果，支持策略训练数据增强、虚拟环境评估和语言引导规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型面临两大挑战：一是不同具身任务（机器人操作、自动驾驶、室内导航等）的动作空间异构，难以统一建模；二是缺乏大规模、多场景、动作标注的视频数据。作者希望构建一个以自然语言为统一接口的视频世界模型，能够跨多种具身形态预测未来视觉轨迹，从而为策略训练、评估和规划提供通用基础。

Method: 模型由三部分组成：1) 双流MMDiT架构：60层双流扩散Transformer，通过逐层联合注意力机制将冻结的Qwen2.5-VL语义特征与视频VAE潜变量耦合；2) Embodied World Knowledge (EWK)：8.6M视频-文本语料库（2亿+帧），覆盖20+种具身形态和500+动作类别，建立动作-语言映射；3) General+Expert渐进式课程训练：两阶段策略，先学习通用视觉先验，再在共享语言接口下注入具身专业化知识。

Result: 在EWMBench和DreamGen Bench上排名第一，在WorldModelBench和PBench上超越所有开源模型。在RoboTwin-IF基准上的零样本分析进一步证明了强大的泛化能力和多视角一致性。

Conclusion: Qwen-RobotWorld验证了以自然语言为统一接口的视频世界模型在多种具身任务中的有效性，为合成数据生成、策略评估和语言引导规划提供了可行方案。其统一框架有望推动具身智能中世界模型的标准化发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Qwen-RobotWorld，一个语言条件视频世界模型，用于具身智能。它通过自然语言作为统一动作接口，预测物理上合理的未来视觉轨迹，涵盖机器人操作、自动驾驶、室内导航等场景。这完全匹配世界模型方向下的[CANONICAL]视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题，同时其视频生成部分也与多模态生成方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation for embodied AI, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: world model, video generation, language-conditioned, embodied intelligence, continuous multimodal

Abstract: We introduce Qwen-RobotWorld, a language-conditioned video world model for embodied intelligence. With natural language as a unified action interface, it predicts physically grounded future visual trajectories from current observations across robotic manipulation, autonomous driving, indoor navigation, and human-to-robot transfer. This unified formulation provides three promising application directions: synthetic data generation for policy training augmentation, scalable virtual environments for policy evaluation, and language-guided planning signals for downstream robot control. This is achieved through a three-part design: a) Double-Stream MMDiT with MLLM Action Encoding, where a 60-layer double-stream diffusion transformer couples frozen Qwen2.5-VL semantics with video-VAE latents through layer-wise joint attention; b) Embodied World Knowledge (EWK), an 8.6M video-text corpus (200M+ frames) with action-language mapping over 20+ embodiments and 500+ action categories; and c) General+Expert Progressive Curriculum, a two-stage training strategy that first learns general visual priors and then injects embodied specialization under a shared language interface. Extensive results show strong competitiveness: ranks 1st overall on EWMBench and DreamGen Bench, outperforms all open-source models on WorldModelBench and PBench. Additional zero-shot analyses on RoboTwin-IF benchmark further support robust generalization and multi-view consistency.

</details>

### [67] [TurboGS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting via Error-Guided Sparse Pixel Sampling and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15924)
*Zheng Dong, Daifei Qiu, Pinxuan Dai, Ke Xu, Jiamin Xu, Lili He, Rynson W.H. Lau, Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (64.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TurboGS 提出一种基于误差引导的稀疏像素采样与优化框架，通过分块稀疏采样、结构感知损失、误差驱动的密度控制和混合优化器，在单张 RTX 5090 上 100 秒内实现与原始 3DGS 相当或更优的渲染质量，训练速度提升最高 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级应用需要快速优化 3DGS 以实现高保真新视角渲染，但现有加速方法在冗余像素上仍消耗大量计算，同时牺牲了细节质量。因此需要一种能聚焦于信息量大的像素、避免冗余计算的高效训练框架。

Method: TurboGS 包含四个核心组件：(1) 基于多视图重建误差驱动的分块稀疏像素采样，优先处理困难区域，跳过已良好重建的像素；(2) 分块结构感知损失结合稀疏归一化互相关，提供稀疏但有效的监督以保留细节；(3) 误差驱动的高斯密度控制策略，动态分配模型容量并移除冗余基元；(4) 结合 Hessian 信息更新与 Adam 动量阻尼的混合优化器，在稀疏监督下稳定并改善收敛。

Result: 在标准基准上，TurboGS 在单张 RTX 5090 上 100 秒内达到与原始 3DGS 相当或更优的渲染质量，训练速度提升最高 10 倍。

Conclusion: TurboGS 通过误差引导的稀疏像素采样与优化，显著加速了 3DGS 的训练过程，同时保持了高保真渲染质量，为消费级 3D 应用提供了可行的加速方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于加速 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的训练与渲染，属于 3D 场景重建与生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS acceleration, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting acceleration, error-guided sparse sampling, training speedup, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Consumer-level applications require fast optimization of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with high-fidelity novel view rendering. However, existing 3DGS acceleration approaches still incur substantial computation on redundant pixels while sacrificing fine details. In this paper, we present TurboGS, an error-guided training framework that accelerates 3DGS by concentrating optimization on perceptually informative pixels. TurboGS is built upon four core components: (1) a tile-wise sparse pixel sampling, which, driven by multi-view reconstruction errors during training, prioritizes challenging regions and skips well-reconstructed ones to avoid redundant gradient computation; (2) a tile-wise structure-aware loss with sparse Normalized Cross-Correlation, which provides sparse yet effective supervision to preserve fine details and stabilize training; (3) an error-driven Gaussian density control strategy, which dynamically allocates model capacity and removes redundant primitives; and (4) a tailored hybrid optimizer that couples Hessian-informed updates with Adam moment damping to stabilize and improve convergence under sparse supervision. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that TurboGS can deliver on par or superior rendering quality within 100 seconds on a single RTX 5090 GPU card (up to 10x training speedup over vanilla 3DGS).

</details>


### [68] [PointDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Scene Completion in the Point Cloud Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16048)
*Chidera Agbasiere, Mikhail Sannikov, Faith Ogunwoye, Erik Shaikhiev, Alex Kozinov, Ilya Mikhalchuk, Iana Zhura, Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (61.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PointDiffusion，一种基于扩散模型的点云场景补全方法。通过多token高斯VAE实现场景级LiDAR压缩，结合anchor-based ICP地面真值精炼管道消除里程计漂移噪声，最终实现无脚手架单步扩散补全模型。在SemanticKITTI上Chamfer距离降低约16倍，推理延迟降低25-143倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏LiDAR点云重建稠密3D场景是自动驾驶中的基础挑战。现有方法存在三个问题：(1) 目标级自编码器在室外大场景下会坍缩为不稳定的全局表征；(2) 地面真值数据受里程计漂移污染，系统性地降低监督质量；(3) 多步扩散推理延迟过高，无法满足实时部署需求。

Method: 提出多token高斯VAE（Multi-Token Gaussian VAE）结合交叉注意力池化实现场景级LiDAR压缩，将点云编码为多个高斯潜变量token以保持局部结构信息；设计基于anchor的ICP地面真值精炼管道，消除训练监督中的漂移噪声；在此基础上实现无脚手架（scaffold-free）单步扩散补全模型，大幅降低推理延迟。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI seq. 08上，平方Chamfer距离从0.396 m²降至0.024 m²（约16倍提升）；超越LiDiff和ScoreLiDAR分别17-19%和10-11%；推理延迟降低25-143倍。

Conclusion: 数据质量在该任务中主导模型设计，多token潜空间为基于潜扩散的场景补全提供了稳定的第一阶段。所提方法在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散模型的稀疏LiDAR点云场景补全（3D场景生成与重建），属于3D生成范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D point cloud completion for autonomous driving, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D scene completion, diffusion model, LiDAR point cloud, autonomous driving, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Reconstructing dense 3D scenes from sparse LiDAR point clouds is a fundamental challenge in autonomous driving, where latent diffusion models offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches rely on object-level autoencoders that collapse into unstable global representations at outdoor scale and suffer from ground truth data corrupted by odometry drift that systematically degrades supervision quality. Furthermore, multi-step diffusion inference incurs prohibitive latency for real-time deployment. We propose a novel multi-token Gaussian VAE with cross-attention pooling for stable scene-scale LiDAR compression, combined with an anchor-based ICP ground truth refinement pipeline that eliminates drift-induced noise from training supervision. Together, these components enable a scaffold-free single-step diffusion completion model that achieves an approximately 16x reduction in squared Chamfer distance on SemanticKITTI seq. 08 (0.396 m^2 to 0.024 m^2), surpasses LiDiff and ScoreLiDAR by 17-19% and 10-11%, respectively, and operates at 25-143x lower inference latency. Our results demonstrate that data quality dominates model design in this regime and that multi-token latent spaces provide a stable first stage for latent diffusion-based scene completion.

</details>


### [69] [Uncertainty Quality of VGGT: An Analysis on the DTU Benchmark Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16479)
*Markus Hillemann, Robert Langendörfer, Steven Landgraf, Markus Ulrich*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 作为一种统一前馈网络，能够快速从多视图图像预测相机位姿、深度图和稠密 3D 结构，但对其不确定性预测的质量尚缺乏系统分析。高精度的不确定性估计对于建立信任和实现鲁棒的质量保证至关重要。

Method: 在 DTU 基准数据集上对 VGGT 输出的不确定性进行定量分析，通过设定置信度阈值对原始输出进行过滤，评估不同阈值下重建精度的变化，从而确定有效的置信度阈值。

Result: 找到了有效的置信度阈值用于过滤 VGGT 的原始输出，并证明提升不确定性质量能够显著改善 3D 重建的精度。

Conclusion: VGGT 的不确定性预测具有实用价值，通过适当的置信度过滤可以提升重建质量，未来在不确定性质量方面仍有改进空间。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VGGT 模型在 DTU 数据集上的不确定性质量分析，VGGT 是一种从多视图图像直接预测相机位姿、深度图和稠密 3D 结构的统一前馈神经网络，属于 3D 重建方法。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 "3D 生成与重建" 子主题。

Importance reason: While VGGT is a high-impact CVPR best paper, its focus on 3D reconstruction uncertainty analysis is peripheral to your core priorities in continuous language/video models and world models. Signals: CVPR 2025 Best Paper, 3D reconstruction uncertainty, DTU benchmark, confidence threshold analysis, feed-forward neural network

Abstract: Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT) has already attracted a great deal of attention in a short period of time, not least due to the Best Paper Award at CVPR-2025. Similar to DUSt3R and MASt3R, VGGT aims to bring about a paradigm shift by replacing established methods like bundle adjustment and feature matching with a simple, unified, feed-forward neural network that predicts camera poses, depth maps, and dense 3D structure directly from multiple images of a scene in a few seconds. A key aspect is its ability to process an arbitrary number of views consistently in a single forward pass without any post-processing or iterative optimization. For photogrammetry, this opens new possibilities for real-time, scalable, and accessible 3D reconstruction. In this context, not only high reconstruction accuracy but also high-quality uncertainty estimates are crucial, as they foster trust and enable robust quality assurance. This paper therefore investigates the quality of VGGT's uncertainty predictions. The analysis identifies an effective confidence threshold for filtering VGGT's raw output and demonstrates that enhancing uncertainty quality holds strong potential for improving the accuracy of its 3D reconstructions.

</details>


### [70] [SurroundNEXO: Ego-Centric Metric Bridging for Spatially Consistent Geometry in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16960)
*Shuai Yuan, Runxi Tang, Yuzhou Ji, Fudong Ge, Hanshi Wang, Yifei Wang, Xianming Zeng, Jianyun Xu, Xingliang Liu, Yanfeng Wang, Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: SurroundNEXO 提出一种面向低重叠多相机环视系统的度量深度估计框架，通过 Ego-Ray 位置编码、稀疏 LiDAR 度量锚点与渐进式特征交互，在自动驾驶场景中实现空间一致的度量 3D 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶依赖精确的度量 3D 理解，但车载环视相机视野重叠度低，传统多视图几何方法依赖密集视觉对应，难以在低重叠场景下工作。现有方法缺乏跨视图度量一致性。

Method: SurroundNEXO 包含三个核心设计：(1) Ego-Ray Positional Encoding，将图像 token 映射到全局一致的以自车为中心的射线方向；(2) 稀疏 LiDAR 测量作为度量锚点，传播绝对尺度信息；(3) 渐进式特征交互，从视图局部建模到分解的时空推理再到全局集成。

Result: 在 NuScenes、Waymo、DDAD 等低重叠自动驾驶基准上，相比 SOTA 方法，单视图误差降低 33.2%，跨视图一致性提升 10.5%，度量重建质量提升 25.6%。在极稀疏深度提示下仍保持鲁棒，并展现出对未见相机布局的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SurroundNEXO 通过以自车为中心的几何桥接，有效解决了低重叠多相机系统的度量深度估计问题，为自动驾驶中的空间一致 3D 感知提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多相机度量深度估计框架，用于自动驾驶场景的3D几何重建，属于3D场景重建范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving metric depth, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: autonomous driving, metric depth estimation, low-overlap multi-camera, ego-centric geometry, sparse LiDAR anchors

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving depends on accurate metric 3D understanding for perception, reconstruction, and planning, which in turn requires reliable multi-camera depth prediction. However, the outward-facing nature of vehicle-mounted surround-view camera rigs inherently limits visual overlap across views, challenging the correspondence-based assumptions that underpin conventional multi-view geometry. To bridge this gap, we present SurroundNEXO, named after the Spanish word nexo for a geometric link, a low-overlap multi-camera metric depth framework that grounds cross-view reasoning in ego-centric geometry rather than dense visual correspondences. Instead of directly enforcing early global fusion, SurroundNEXO first assigns image tokens globally comparable ego-frame viewing directions through Ego-Ray Positional Encoding, then uses sparse LiDAR measurements as metric anchors to propagate absolute scale cues, and finally expands feature interaction progressively from view-local modeling to decomposed spatio-temporal reasoning and global integration. This design enables metric-scale depth prediction with improved spatial consistency across weakly overlapping cameras. Across low-overlap autonomous driving benchmarks, including NuScenes, Waymo and DDAD, SurroundNEXO reduces single-view error by 33.2%, improves cross-view consistency by 10.5%, and enhances metric reconstruction quality by 25.6% compared with SOTA methods. It further remains robust under extremely sparse depth prompts and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to unseen camera layouts.

</details>

*Yi-Ruei Liu, Jie-Ying Lee, Zheng-Hui Huang, Yu-Lun Liu, Chih-Hao Lin*

TL;DR: BRDFusion 是一个统一物理渲染与生成模型的城市场景逆渲染框架，通过物理建模恢复显式场景属性，利用生成先验消除优化歧义并修复伪影，支持新视角重光照、夜间模拟和动态物体编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的渲染方法能精确控制光照物理，但存在重建和渲染伪影；生成模型能产生逼真视频，但缺乏一致性和可控性。现有方法无法同时兼顾物理准确性与视觉质量，因此需要一种统一框架结合两者优势。

Method: BRDFusion 将逆渲染与正渲染两个互补模型统一：逆渲染阶段通过物理建模恢复显式、一致的场景属性（如材质、几何），并利用生成先验缓解优化歧义；正渲染阶段由物理模型根据场景配置提供可控渲染，生成模型则负责去噪和修复伪影，最终输出高质量视频。

Result: 在真实和合成城市场景上均优于基线方法，支持新视角重光照、夜间模拟以及动态物体插入/编辑等应用，在保持精确控制的同时生成高质量视频。

Conclusion: BRDFusion 成功融合物理渲染与生成模型，实现了兼顾物理一致性与视觉质量的城市场景逆渲染，为内容创作和自动驾驶仿真等应用提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 BRDFusion 框架，结合物理渲染与生成模型进行城市场景逆渲染，支持新视角重光照、夜间模拟和动态物体插入/编辑，属于 3D 场景重建与神经渲染范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" (three_d_generation)。

Importance reason: Combines physics-based rendering with generative priors for urban scene inverse rendering, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: urban scene inverse rendering, physics meets generation, novel-view relighting, night simulation, dynamic object editing

Abstract: Inverse rendering of urban scenes from captured videos enables numerous applications, including content creation and autonomous driving simulation. Physically-based rendering methods follow and control lighting physics, but suffer from reconstruction and rendering artifacts. While generative models produce realistic videos, they offer limited consistency and controllability. We present BRDFusion, a unified framework that combines two complementary models for inverse and forward rendering. Specifically, BRDFusion recovers explicit, consistent scene properties with physical modeling and alleviates optimization ambiguity with generative priors. During forward rendering, the physical model provides controllable rendering from the scene configuration, and the generative model denoises and fixes artifacts. Therefore, our method produces high-quality videos while allowing precise control, outperforming baselines in real and synthetic scenes. Moreover, BRDFusion supports novel-view relighting, night simulation, and dynamic object insertion/editing. Project page:

</details>

### [72] [High-Fidelity 4D Hand-Object Capture via Multi-View Spatiotemporal Tracking and Physics-Aware Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15908)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和空间计算对高保真4D手-物交互数据的需求日益增长，但现有方法严重依赖预扫描物体模板和物理标记物，且对手和物体姿态的初始估计高度敏感，在严重遮挡下难以鲁棒重建。

Result: 在公开基准和内部数据集上验证，该方法实现了鲁棒、无伪影的重建，显著优于现有方法，为自动化4D资产生成提供了高效基础。

Conclusion: 本文证明了结合多视角时序Transformer初始化与物理感知高斯优化可以有效解决无模板4D手-物交互重建中的鲁棒性和精度问题，为自动化4D动态场景生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注从多视角视频中重建4D手-物交互动态场景，属于动态3D/4D场景生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 4D hand-object capture, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's key directions. Signals: 4D reconstruction, hand-object interaction, multi-view tracking, physics-aware Gaussians, no template or markers

Abstract: The growing demand for high-fidelity 4D hand-object interaction (HOI) data in embodied AI and spatial computing is currently bottlenecked by the reliance on pre-scanned object templates and physical markers. While recent methods have demonstrated promising results in reconstructing 4D hand-object interaction from videos, they are highly sensitive to initial estimates of hand and object poses. Yet, estimating these poses from images is challenging, in particular under severe occlusion which is inherent in hand-object interaction scenarios. We propose a novel system for the robust and accurate reconstruction of hands and objects from synchronized and calibrated multi-view videos without requiring any templates or markers. Our system consists of two main components with key innovations: (1) a multi-view feed-forward transformer model that aggregates cross-view geometry and temporal cues to provide a reliable, metric-consistent initialization for both poses and dense object geometry, and (2) a hand-object physics-aware Gaussian-based optimization framework to refine the initial estimates, integrating tetrahedral constraints, collision refinement, and appearance decomposition to produce physically plausible and visually accurate reconstruction. Validated on public benchmarks and an extensive internal dataset, our pipeline achieves highly robust, artifact-free reconstruction, providing an efficient foundation for automated 4D asset generation. Our project page are available at .

</details>

### [73] [Redirecting the Flow: Image Customization through Attention Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16866)
*Jie Li, Suorong Yang, Jian Zhao, Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CustomShift，一种基于条件注意力分布偏移的双分支架构，通过参考-对齐分支和交叉引导分支实现高效的主体驱动图像定制化，在 DreamBooth 和 Custom101 基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动图像定制化方法（如测试时微调、编码器方法、共享注意力空间中的 token 竞争）存在效率低、参考特征与生成过程对齐不足、无关信息干扰等问题。本文旨在解决这些局限性，提出更高效且保真度更高的定制化生成方法。

Method: 将定制化任务形式化为参考图像引入文生图过程导致的分布偏移，基于最大熵理论推导出条件注意力分布偏移（Conditional Attention Distribution Shift）公式。在此基础上提出 CustomShift 双分支架构：参考-对齐分支利用参考图像与主体名称之间的自注意力实现逐层潜表征对齐；交叉引导分支整合文本和参考线索引导生成。基于 Stable Diffusion 3 实现。

Result: 在 DreamBooth 和 Custom101 基准上，CustomShift 在语义保真度和主体一致性之间取得了更好的平衡，一致优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论推导和实验验证表明，将定制化任务视为注意力分布偏移并设计双分支对齐引导架构，能够有效提升主体驱动图像生成的效率和质量，为个性化生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究主体驱动图像定制化（subject-driven image customization），属于多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）子主题，与 DreamBooth 等个性化生成任务高度一致。

Importance reason: Strong on controllable generation but lacks direct connection to priority continuous language/multimodal models or world models. Signals: subject-driven generation, attention distribution shift, dual-branch architecture, DreamBooth benchmark, Stable Diffusion 3

Abstract: Subject-driven image customization aims to generate images that not only follow textual instructions but also preserve the identity of a given reference subject. Existing approaches, including test-time fine-tuning, encoder-based methods, and token competition in shared attention spaces, suffer from limited efficiency, misalignment between extracted reference features and the generative process, and interference from irrelevant information. To address these limitations, we formulate the customization task as a distribution shift induced by incorporating reference images into text-to-image generation, and derive a Conditional Attention Distribution Shift formulation grounded in maximum entropy theory. Building on this formulation, we propose CustomShift, a dual-branch architecture based on Stable Diffusion 3. The Reference-Alignment Branch leverages self-attention between reference images and subject names to achieve layer-wise alignment with latent representations, while the Cross-Guidance Branch integrates textual and reference cues to guide generation. Experiments on the DreamBooth and Custom101 benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving a better balance between semantic fidelity and subject consistency.

</details>

### [74] [Style-CCL: Content-Preserving Style Transfer via Curriculum Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14746)
*Shiwen Zhang, Haoyuan Wang, Xianghao Zang, Haibin Huang, Chi Zhang, Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: 本文提出 Style-CCL，一种基于多阶段课程持续学习的框架，用于解决扩散Transformer（DiT）在内容保持风格迁移中语义风格主导纹理风格学习的问题。通过反向三元组合成管道构建百万级训练集、双分支SC-DiT解耦风格与内容，以及从语义到纹理、从干净到合成数据的多阶段课程训练，配合随机记忆回放防止灾难性遗忘，在风格相似度、内容一致性和美学质量上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散Transformer（DiT）在风格迁移中，内容和风格特征高度纠缠，且单阶段混合风格类别训练会导致语义风格主导，抑制纹理风格学习，损害内容保持能力。

Method: 1) 提出反向三元组合成管道（reverse triplet synthesis pipeline）构建百万级训练集；2) 设计双分支Style-Content DiT（SC-DiT），通过分离的ROPE嵌入和因果掩码解耦风格与内容；3) 提出Multi-Stage Curriculum Continual Learning框架，从语义（简单）到纹理（困难）风格、从干净到合成数据分阶段训练，并引入随机记忆回放（Random Memory Rehearsal）防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在风格相似度、内容一致性和美学质量三个核心指标上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Style-CCL通过课程持续学习有效解决了DiT在风格迁移中语义风格主导的问题，实现了更好的内容保持与风格迁移效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究内容保持的风格迁移（Content-Preserving Style Transfer），属于图像编辑/风格化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel curriculum continual learning for DiT style transfer, but tangential to user's core priorities on continuous models and video generation. Signals: DiT style transfer, curriculum continual learning, content-style decoupling, reverse triplet synthesis, random memory rehearsal

Abstract: Content-Preserving Style transfer, given content and style references, remains challenging for Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) due to entangled content and style features. With a reverse triplet synthesis pipeline to build a million-scale training set and a dual-branch Style-Content DiT (SC-DiT) that decouples style and content via separate ROPE embeddings and causal masking, we observe that such a one-stage training paradigm on mixed style categories causes semantic styles to dominate, hindering texture style learning, and harming content preservation. To address these issues, we propose Style-CCL, a Multi-Stage Curriculum Continual Learning framework that trains SC-DiT from semantic (easy) to texture (hard) styles, and from clean to synthetic data, with Random Memory Rehearsal across stages to avoid catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our Style-CCL achieves state-of-the-art performance in three core metrics: style similarity, content consistency, and aesthetic quality.

</details>


### [75] [ReGenHuman: Re-Generating Human Appearances for Realistic Full-Body Video Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14972)
*Adam Sun, Eshaan Barkataki, Arnold Milstein, Gordon Wetzstein, Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (73.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGenHuman 提出了一种"重新生成而非编辑"的全身体视频匿名化方法，利用结构条件（姿态、分割、深度）微调视频扩散模型，在保护隐私的同时保持时序一致性和视觉真实感，并在下游任务（如视频问答）中保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频匿名化方法要么模糊/遮挡像素（损失真实感和下游可用性），要么逐帧生成（损失时序一致性）。需要一种同时满足真实感、时序一致性和匿名性的全身体视频匿名化方案。

Method: 提出"regenerate, don't edit"范式：将2D姿态、分割和单目深度组合为两个互补条件流（StructAll 和 StructHuman），用于微调视频到视频扩散骨干网络，在野外人体视频上训练，从无身份的结构线索中完全重新合成人体区域。

Result: 在隐私、质量和下游任务可用性三个维度上，ReGenHuman 相比现有基线取得了最佳权衡。匿名化后的视频在视频问答等下游任务中仍然有效。

Conclusion: ReGenHuman 是首个同时实现真实感、时序一致性和匿名性的全身体视频匿名化管线，验证了"重新生成而非编辑"范式的有效性，为隐私保护视频数据处理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出全身体视频匿名化管线，核心是视频到视频的扩散生成，将人体区域完全重新生成而非编辑/遮挡，属于视频编辑与可控生成范畴，与 canonical subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video anonymization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or world models. Signals: video anonymization, diffusion-based video editing, human-centric video, temporal consistency, downstream utility

Abstract: Anonymizing human-centric video data is an understudied problem. Prior anonymization techniques either blur or redact pixels at the cost of realism and downstream utility, or generate frame-by-frame at the cost of temporal coherence. We introduce ReGenHuman, the first full-body video anonymization pipeline that is simultaneously realistic, temporally consistent, and anonymous by construction. Contrary to past approaches which redact or edit the inputs directly, we propose a regenerate, don't edit paradigm. Our approach composites 2D pose, segmentation, and monocular depth into two complementary conditioning streams - StructAll and StructHuman, which are used to fine-tune a video-to-video diffusion backbone on in-the-wild human videos, synthesizing the human regions entirely from identity-free structural cues. We evaluate our model on privacy, quality, and utility, and show that our ReGenHuman achieves the best tradeoff across all three axes against current baselines. We further show that our anonymized videos remain effective for downstream tasks, including video question answering.

</details>


### [76] [RefGC-SR$^2$: Reference-guided Generated Content Super-Resolution and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15158)
*Jeahun Sung, Dahyeon Kye, Soo Ye Kim, Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (52.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RefGC-SR² 任务，在生成后处理阶段复用原始高分辨率参考图，同时恢复丢失细节、修正生成伪影并提升分辨率。构建了首个真实世界三元组数据生成流程，并设计了频率感知的扩散 Transformer 模型，在参考引导的精修与超分辨率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考引导生成（如物体合成、定制化）流程中，用户提供的高分辨率参考图在输入模型前被降采样为固定低分辨率，导致精细细节丢失；生成过程还会引入身份扭曲等伪影。现有精修方法（RefGCR）在低分辨率域操作，超分辨率方法（RefSR）假设自然图像退化，均无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 构建首个 RefGC-SR² 真实世界三元组数据生成流程，使用双联条件生成器合成低质量锚点图像；2) 提出频率感知的扩散 Transformer 模型，选择性注入高分辨率参考图的细节信息，同时去除生成伪影；3) 模型在精修阶段同时完成超分辨率、细节恢复和伪影去除三个任务。

Result: 实验表明，RefGC-SR² 模型在保持物体身份忠实度和恢复高分辨率细节方面显著优于现有 RefGCR 和 RefSR 基线方法，生成结果质量更高、实用性更强。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 RefGC-SR² 任务和模型有效弥合了参考引导生成中细节丢失与伪影问题的鸿沟，通过复用原始高分辨率参考图在生成后阶段同时完成超分辨率与精修，为参考引导生成的后处理提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出参考引导的生成内容超分辨率与精修（RefGC-SR²），属于生成后处理/精修任务，与图像编辑（visual_editing）中的可控编辑、内容修复高度相关。论文核心是使用高分辨率参考图对生成结果进行细节恢复和伪影去除，本质上是生成后编辑与精修。

Importance reason: Focuses on post-generation refinement for reference-guided generation, which is a niche application not aligned with the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: Reference-guided super-resolution, generative artifact correction, frequency-aware diffusion transformer, not autoregressive or continuous, no world model or video generation

Abstract: Reference-guided generation (e.g., object compositing, customization) has progressed rapidly, yet current pipelines share a fundamental limitation: the object-centric high-resolution reference image (HRRI) provided by users is downsampled to a fixed low-resolution (LR) before being fed into the model, so the fine-grained details are discarded before the output is even produced. In addition, the generation step then introduces its own artifacts (e.g., identity distortion) on top of this loss. Existing reference-guided generated content refinement (RefGCR) methods can correct some of these artifacts but still operate in the LR domain; reference-guided super-resolution (RefSR) methods recover resolution but assume natural-image degradations and ignore the artifact distribution of generative pipelines. To address both gaps in a single formulation, we introduce a new task: reference-guided generated content super-resolution-refinement (RefGC-SR$^2$), where the original HRRI is reused at the post-processing stage to recover lost details, refine generative artifacts, and upscale the output simultaneously. We construct the first real-world triplet data generation pipeline for this RefGC-SR$^2$ task, training a diptych-conditioned generator to synthesize paired low-quality anchors that public pretrained models cannot provide. We further present a frequency-aware diffusion transformer model for RefGC-SR$^2$ that selectively injects fine details from the HRRI while removing generative artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our RefGC-SR$^2$ model successfully (i) refines the object identity faithfully with respect to the reference, and (ii) recovers high-resolution details, so that the final result is significantly higher quality and practically more usable compared to existing RefGCR and RefSR baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling via Early-Step Latent Verification for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15188)
*Yue Yu, Yang Jiao, Jiayu Wang, Qi Dai, Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VeriLatent，一种即插即用的自适应推理时缩放框架，通过在早期步骤中对潜空间进行验证（latent-space verification），高效筛选初始噪声候选，从而在不解码为图像的情况下实现图像编辑质量的提升和计算效率的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于指令的图像编辑中，初始噪声的随机采样会影响编辑质量，尤其在复杂场景下。现有的推理时缩放方法采用"解码-验证"方案，存在效率与准确性的权衡：解码步数少则图像噪声大难以评估，解码充分则计算成本高。因此需要一种能在早期步骤高效评估候选噪声的方法。

Method: 提出 VeriLatent 框架，核心包括：(1) 潜空间编辑激活图（latent-space editing activation map）验证器，在早期扩散步骤中通过分析潜空间特征评估每个初始噪声能否在正确区域引发有效编辑；(2) 基于此验证器的自适应搜索策略，根据编辑难度动态分配推理预算，减少函数评估次数（NFE）。

Result: 在多个基准和不同基模型上的实验表明，VeriLatent 在提升编辑性能的同时显著提高了推理时缩放效率，优于现有的 decode-then-verify 方案。

Conclusion: VeriLatent 通过早期潜空间验证实现了高效的推理时自适应缩放，有效解决了图像编辑中初始噪声选择问题，为推理时计算扩展提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于指令的图像编辑（instruction-based image editing），属于图像编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on image editing, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: inference-time scaling, latent-space verification, image editing, efficiency-accuracy trade-off, adaptive search

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing has made notable progress with recent advances in generative models. However, the quality of the edited result is still influenced by the randomly sampled initial noise, particularly in complex editing scenarios. An unsuitable initial noise may lead to unsatisfactory editing results. Recent inference-time scaling methods address this issue by sampling multiple initial noises and selecting better candidates. Nevertheless, most of them follow a decode-then-verify scheme which introduces an efficiency-accuracy trade-off. When decoding is performed after limited inference steps, the decoded images often remain too noisy for reliable assessment, whereas sufficiently denoised images require much higher computational cost. To address this issue, we propose VeriLatent, a plug-and-play adaptive inference-time scaling framework with early-step latent verification for image editing. Specifically, we propose a novel verifier that scores each initial noise through a latent-space editing activation map at an early stage. It identifies promising candidates by assessing whether they can induce an effective edit in the correct region. This enables efficient early pruning without decoding latents into images. Building on this, we further develop an adaptive search strategy for inference-time scaling. It allocates inference budgets according to editing difficulty, thereby reducing the number of function evaluations (NFE). Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks and different base models demonstrate that VeriLatent consistently improves both editing performance and inference-time scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [78] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of SAM 3 for Automated ITV Generation from 4DCT Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15604)
*Changwoo Song*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (40.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级框架，通过 LoRA 参数高效微调 SAM 3，仅用 7 个标注 3D CT 体积即可实现 4DCT 图像中自动 ITV 生成，结合硬负样本挖掘和时序滤波，在肺部和心脏结构上取得高 Dice 分数和低 Hausdorff 距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 4DCT 的 ITV 勾画流程逐相位独立处理，忽略了呼吸周期的时间连贯性，导致轮廓易受相位特定伪影影响。需要一种数据高效、能利用时序信息的方法来自动化 ITV 生成。

Method: 使用 LoRA 对 SAM 3 进行参数高效微调，仅用 7 个标注 3D CT 体积将文本提示分割对齐到医学领域；引入硬负样本挖掘策略改善低对比度胸腔区域的边界判别；推理时通过相位相干时序滤波和空间连通性分析细化逐相位预测，利用呼吸运动的连续周期性抑制瞬态伪影。

Result: 肺部和心脏结构的中位 Dice 分数分别为 0.968 和 0.910，95 百分位 Hausdorff 距离分别为 0.998 mm 和 2.931 mm。有效消除了未适应 SAM 3 零样本推理中的严重假阳性预测，仅用 7 个标注体积即可保留全数据准确率的 95% 以上，整个流程可在单张消费级 GPU 上训练。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了数据高效、可扩展的 4DCT ITV 自动生成方案，适用于自适应放疗，验证了参数高效微调和大模型在医学图像分割中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 SAM 3 进行 4DCT 图像的 ITV 生成，本质是医学图像分割与编辑任务，涉及对分割结果的时序滤波和空间连通性分析，属于图像编辑/分割范畴。映射到 canonical subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image segmentation (4DCT ITV) with SAM 3 and LoRA, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical domain, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, LoRA, SAM 3, 4DCT

Abstract: Four-dimensional computed tomography (4DCT) captures the full respiratory cycle of thoracic anatomy, yet current Internal Target Volume contouring workflows process each phase in isolation, discarding temporal coherence and leaving contours vulnerable to phase-specific artifacts. We present a lightweight framework that applies parameter-efficient fine-tuning to the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) via low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to align its text-prompted segmentation with the medical domain using only seven annotated 3D CT volumes. Furthermore, the framework incorporates a hard negative mining strategy to improve boundary discrimination in low-contrast thoracic regions. At inference, phase-wise predictions are refined through phase-coherent temporal filtering and spatial connectivity analysis. Since respiratory motion is continuous and periodic, genuine anatomy appears in contiguous blocks of phases, whereas transient artifacts appear sporadically and are thus effectively suppressed. Experiments on pulmonary and cardiac structures yield median Dice scores of 0.968 and 0.910 with 95th-percentile Hausdorff distances of 0.998 mm and 2.931 mm, respectively. The proposed framework effectively eliminates the severe false-positive predictions inherent in the zero-shot inference of the unadapted SAM 3. With only seven annotated volumes, the framework retains over 95% of full-data accuracy, and the entire pipeline is trainable on a single consumer-grade GPU, demonstrating a scalable, data-efficient solution for adaptive radiotherapy.

</details>


### [79] [SceneCraft: Interactive System for Image Editing via Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16103)
*Duc-Manh Phan, Ngoc-Dai Tran, Duy-Khang Do, Tam V. Nguyen, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneCraft 是一个交互式图像编辑框架，通过将图像表示为可编辑的场景图（scene graph），让用户直接操作图结构进行空间和关系编辑，自动将图修改转换为精确的文本提示，并调度多个生成模型执行编辑，从而减少用户手动编写提示词的负担，提升复杂场景编辑的质量和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言的图像编辑系统在处理包含多个交互对象的复杂场景时表现不佳，因为用户需要反复尝试编写精确的文本提示词，存在语言歧义和认知负担。缺乏结构化的控制机制是当前系统的核心痛点。

Method: SceneCraft 将图像表示为场景图（scene graph），节点为物体，边为空间/关系。用户可直接在可视化图上进行修改（如添加/删除/移动物体、修改关系），系统自动将这些图操作翻译为精确、上下文感知的编辑提示词，并分发到多个 SOTA 生成模型（如 Stable Diffusion 等）执行编辑，最终融合输出结果。

Result: 在多种编辑场景上的评估表明，SceneCraft 提供了更直观的控制机制，显著降低了手动提示工程（prompt engineering）的认知负担，用户一致评价其生成输出在质量和保真度上更高。

Conclusion: SceneCraft 通过场景图这一结构化表示，有效弥合了用户意图与模型执行之间的鸿沟，为复杂场景图像编辑提供了一种更直观、更可控的交互范式，减少了语言歧义并提升了编辑质量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SceneCraft，一种通过场景图（scene graph）进行交互式图像编辑的框架，属于图像编辑方向。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 完全覆盖该工作。

Importance reason: Scene graph-based image editing is a niche application, not directly aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Interactive image editing, scene graph representation, prompt engineering reduction, multimodal generation scheduling, not autoregressive or continuous token based

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have enabled natural language-driven image editing, yet existing systems often fail in complex scenes with multiple interacting objects because they rely heavily on users crafting precise text prompts. To address the absence of structured control, we propose SceneCraft, a novel interactive framework that bridges user intent and model execution by representing images as editable scene graphs. Instead of guessing text prompts through trial and error, users interact directly with a visual graph to perform complex spatial and relational operations. These graph modifications are automatically translated into precise, context-aware editing prompts, effectively eliminating linguistic ambiguity. To ensure robust and diverse results, structured prompts are dispatched to multiple state-of-the-art generative models. Evaluations across diverse editing scenarios show that SceneCraft provides a more intuitive control mechanism, significantly reducing the cognitive burden of manual prompt engineering while generating outputs that users consistently rate as higher in quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [80] [Propagating Structural Guidance: Synthesizing Fluorescein Angiography from Fundus Images and Sparse OCT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16234)
*Tengfei Ma, Ruiqi Wu, Chenran Zhang, Ye Geng, Na Su, Xiangyuan Duanmu, Tao Zhou, Yi Zhou, Wen Fan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (35.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从彩色眼底照片(CFP)合成荧光素眼底血管造影(FFA)的框架，利用OCT提供的结构信息作为引导。构建了首个CFP-FFA-OCT三模态对齐的视网膜影像数据集（3676只眼），提出空间对齐跨模态融合模块(SACMF)将OCT深度特征投影到眼底平面，并通过Token级跨模态对齐(TCMA)对比学习策略显式对齐CFP与FFA的空间对应表征。方法在合成质量和下游疾病诊断性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFA是评估视网膜血管异常的关键检查，但具有侵入性且并非总是可行；而CFP无创且广泛可用。现有CFP-to-FFA合成方法仅依赖CFP表面纹理，无法重建功能性血管信息和细微病理变化。因此需要引入OCT提供的结构信息来弥补这一不足。

Method: 1) 构建了包含3676只患者眼睛的CFP-FFA-OCT三模态对齐数据集；2) 提出SACMF模块，将深度分辨的OCT特征投影到眼底平面，通过自适应层归一化注入CFP编码器；3) 提出TCMA策略，在token级别进行跨模态对比学习，显式对齐CFP和FFA在对应空间位置的表征。

Result: 在合成质量指标上优于现有SOTA方法；下游疾病诊断任务中，使用本文合成FFA相比其他方法能带来更大的诊断性能提升，验证了临床实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用OCT结构引导可以显著提升CFP-to-FFA合成质量，合成的FFA在临床诊断中具有作为非侵入性辅助决策工具的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从彩色眼底照片(CFP)合成荧光素眼底血管造影(FFA)，属于医学图像到图像的跨模态合成任务，本质上是图像生成/编辑范畴。映射到[多模态生成]方向下的[图像与视频编辑]规范子主题（visual_editing），因为该子主题涵盖图像编辑、可控编辑等，CFP-to-FFA合成可视为一种医学图像翻译/编辑任务。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on medical image synthesis, which is outside the user's configured priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical image synthesis, fluorescein angiography, OCT guidance, cross-modal fusion, clinical application

Abstract: Fundus fluorescein angiography (FFA) is critical for assessing retinal vascular abnormalities, but its acquisition is invasive and not always feasible. In contrast, color fundus photography (CFP) is non-invasive and widely accessible, which has motivated studies on CFP-to-FFA synthesis. However, prior works rely solely on CFP surface texture, fundamentally limiting the ability to reconstruct functional vascular information and subtle pathological changes. To address this, we propose a novel framework that synthesizes FFA from CFP with structural guidance provided by optical coherence tomography (OCT). We construct a multi-modal retinal imaging dataset with paired CFP, FFA, and OCT from 3,676 patient eyes--the first tri-modally aligned dataset in retinal imaging. To bridge the spatial gap between OCT and fundus modalities, we propose a Spatially Aligned Cross-Modal Fusion (SACMF) module that projects depth-resolved OCT features onto the fundus plane and injects them into the CFP encoder via adaptive layer normalization. Beyond feature fusion, we further introduce Token-wise Cross-Modality Alignment (TCMA), a token-level contrastive learning strategy that explicitly aligns CFP and FFA representations at corresponding spatial positions. Our method achieves superior synthesis performance compared to state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, extensive experiments demonstrate that the FFA images synthesized by our approach bring greater improvements in downstream disease diagnosis performance than existing methods, highlighting the clinical potential of our approach as a non-invasive decision-support tool in routine workflows. The code is available at .

</details>


### [81] [RealityBridge: Bridging Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting Driving Simulations and Real-World Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16278)
*Zhenhua Wu, Yun Pang, Mingkun Chang, Yuwei Ning, Liangzhi Wang, Yi Xiao, Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (72.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RealityBridge 是一个面向编辑后 3DGS 驾驶仿真视频的结构保持与资产感知的 Sim-to-Real 框架，通过多模态控制（渲染视频、前景掩码、边缘图、语义掩码）和轻量级 GateNet 自适应条件分配，结合自回归长视频训练与奖励引导后训练，有效去除 3DGS 伪影、协调光照并提升时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾危险场景对安全导向的自动驾驶至关重要，但难以大规模采集和复现。可编辑 3DGS 仿真虽能重建真实驾驶场景并支持可控编辑，但编辑后的 3DGS 渲染视频存在显著的 Sim-to-Real 差距，包括渲染伪影、前景资产退化、光照不一致和时序闪烁。现有修复和视频生成方法无法同时解决这些问题。

Method: RealityBridge 采用多模态控制（渲染视频、前景掩码、边缘图、语义掩码）作为条件输入，设计轻量级 GateNet 实现跨骨干层的自适应条件分配。构建了针对性的训练数据，并引入自回归长视频训练策略，结合奖励引导的后训练（reward-guided post-training）来提升修复质量、时序稳定性和幻觉抑制。

Result: 在内部和公开驾驶数据集上的大量实验表明，RealityBridge 在伪影去除、光照协调和长序列时序一致性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RealityBridge 有效弥合了编辑后 3DGS 驾驶仿真视频与真实视频之间的差距，为自动驾驶安全场景的仿真数据增强提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 RealityBridge 框架，用于修复和增强编辑后的 3DGS 驾驶仿真视频，弥合仿真到真实的差距（Sim-to-Real gap），涉及视频修复、光照协调、伪影去除等视频编辑与增强任务，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving simulation editing, not core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: 3DGS driving simulation, Sim-to-Real gap, multimodal control, autoregressive long-video training, reward-guided post-training

Abstract: Long-tail hazardous scenarios are essential for safety-oriented autonomous driving, yet they are difficult to collect and reproduce at scale. Editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) simulation offers a promising alternative by reconstructing real driving scenes and supporting controllable scene editing. However, edited 3DGS-rendered videos still suffer from a significant Sim-to-Real gap, including rendering artifacts, degraded foreground assets, inconsistent illumination, and temporal flickering. Existing restoration and video generation methods are insufficient for this task, as they often fail to jointly repair 3DGS-specific artifacts, improve visual realism, and ensure temporal consistency. To fill this gap, we propose RealityBridge, a structure-preserving and asset-aware Sim-to-Real framework for edited 3DGS driving videos. RealityBridge uses multimodal controls, including rendered videos, foreground masks, edge maps, and semantic masks, together with a lightweight GateNet for adaptive condition allocation across backbone layers. We further construct targeted training data and introduce autoregressive long-video training with reward-guided post-training to improve restoration quality, temporal stability, and hallucination suppression. Extensive experiments on internal and public driving datasets show that RealityBridge outperforms existing methods in artifact removal, illumination harmonization, and long-sequence temporal consistency.

</details>


### [82] [PermaVid: Consistent Video Generation Across Edits via Disentangled Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16449)
*Shuai Yang, Bingjie Gao, Ziwei Liu, Jiaqi Wang, Dahua Lin, Tong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 高优先级 (81.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PermaVid 提出一种基于解耦上下文记忆的多模态视频生成框架，通过 RGB 外观记忆和深度几何记忆的双记忆库设计，结合编辑感知的记忆更新与检索策略，在编辑操作后仍能保持视频生成的长期语义和结构一致性，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在经历编辑操作（如修改场景外观或布局）后，由于存储的上下文记忆可能过时或失效，难以保持跨时间和视角的长期一致性。需要一种能够感知编辑变化并持续保持记忆与观测对齐的机制。

Method: 提出 PermaVid 框架，包含：(1) 解耦的多模态上下文记忆——RGB 上下文记忆捕获外观感知观测并隐式编码几何，深度上下文记忆保留纯几何结构并与语义解耦；(2) 编辑感知的记忆更新与检索策略，使记忆演化与后续观测对齐；(3) 记忆引导的视频生成模型，在混合模态记忆上下文的参考条件下进行多模态特征融合。

Result: 实验表明，PermaVid 在编辑操作后能保持强长期语义和结构一致性，在多个指标上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 解耦外观与几何的上下文记忆设计，结合编辑感知的更新策略，是解决编辑后视频生成一致性问题的有效途径，为长期一致的视频编辑生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PermaVid，一种在编辑操作（如修改场景外观或布局）后仍能保持视频生成一致性的框架，核心是视频编辑后的连贯生成。这属于图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）范畴下的视频编辑与可控编辑方向，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Strong video generation consistency work but lacks direct connection to priority continuous language/multimodal models or world models. Signals: video generation consistency, disentangled context memory, edit-aware memory, long-term coherence, multimodal memory fusion

Abstract: Consistent video generation under editing operations requires persistence: when edits modify scene appearance or layout, subsequent generations should remain coherent across time and viewpoints. However, existing memory designs struggle to maintain long-term consistency after such modifications, as stored contexts may become outdated or invalid. To address this, we propose PermaVid, a novel framework built upon a multi-modal context memory that disentangles spatial context into semantic appearance and geometric structure, together with an edit-aware memory update and retrieval strategy that keeps memory evolution aligned with subsequent observations. Specifically, we develop two complementary memory banks: an RGB context memory that captures appearance-aware observations while implicitly encoding geometry, and a depth context memory that preserves geometry-only structure disentangled from semantics. Building on this design, we introduce a memory-guided video generation model that performs multi-modal feature fusion under reference conditions drawn from mixed-modality memory contexts. Experiments demonstrate that our method maintains strong long-term semantic and structural consistency after edits, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [83] [ResEdit: Residual embeddings for precise generative image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16457)
*Ahmet Canberk Baykal, Valentin Deschaintre, Yannick Hold-Geoffroy, Michael Fischer, Anna Frühstück, Cengiz Öztireli, Iliyan Georgiev*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型反演的图像编辑方法在保持图像身份和全局一致性方面存在困难，因为弱条件反演往往将冲突的图像特征嵌入噪声中，导致编辑质量下降。

Method: 引入残差图像编码作为额外条件，增强重建信号以减少对反演的依赖；采用梯度反转优化策略将残差编码与编辑条件解耦，确保残差不干扰期望的编辑效果。

Result: 在内在属性编辑和重光照任务上展示了高保真编辑结果，并提供了文本引导编辑的概念验证。

Conclusion: 残差图像编码结合梯度反转解耦策略能有效提升扩散模型反演编辑的身份保持能力和编辑质量，为精确生成式图像编辑提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于残差图像编码的扩散模型图像编辑方法，属于图像编辑（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on image editing with residual encodings, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: image editing, diffusion inversion, residual encoding, gradient reversal, identity preservation

Abstract: Conditional diffusion image generators can be repurposed for editing through inversion, without the need for large-scale paired fine-tuning data. However, producing high-quality, targeted edits while maintaining image identity and global consistency remains challenging, as weakly conditioned inversion often embeds conflicting image features into the noise. We demonstrate that incorporating a residual image encoding as additional conditioning enables both improved identity preservation and better editability. We optimize this residual encoding to provide a strong conditioning signal for reconstruction, thereby reducing the reliance on inversion and susceptibility to its aforementioned pitfalls. To ensure this residual does not interfere with desired edits, we incorporate a gradient reversal-based optimization strategy that disentangles the residual from the edited condition. We illustrate our method's ability to produce high-fidelity results across precise intrinsic-based editing and relighting, and show proof-of-concept text-guided manipulation.

</details>


### [84] [Text-Vision Co-Instructed Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16767)
*Chenxi Xie, Yuhui Wu, Qiaosi Yi, Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: 本文提出 TV-Edit，一种文本与视觉（拖拽/点）联合指令引导的图像编辑框架，通过构建文本-视觉指令配对数据集和语义感知控制表征，实现了比纯文本或纯拖拽编辑更精确、更忠实于意图的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本指令语义表达力强但空间控制粒度粗，视觉指令（如拖拽、点）空间精确但语义模糊。现有方法无法同时兼顾两者的优势，因此需要统一文本语义意图与视觉空间引导。

Method: 1) 从动态视频中构建了超过23K样本的文本-视觉指令配对数据集，提供跨模态对齐监督。2) 提出 TV-Edit 框架，将拖拽/点视觉指令与图像-文本语义进行上下文融合，提升为语义感知的控制表征，注入预训练编辑骨干网络。

Result: 在多个编辑骨干网络上，TV-Edit 在语义忠实度、空间对齐精度和结构一致性上均显著优于现有的基于指令和基于拖拽的 SOTA 基线方法。

Conclusion: 文本与视觉联合指令能够互补各自的不足，实现更精确且意图忠实的图像编辑，该范式具有良好的通用性和可迁移性。

Importance reason: Combines text and visual instructions for editing, but lacks strong connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: image editing, text-visual instruction, drag-based editing, dataset construction, benchmark

Abstract: Existing image editing methods can be generally categorized into textual instruction-based and visual prompt-based ones. Textual instructions are semantically expressive, but are limited by the coarse granularity of spatial control of the editing results. In contrast, visual prompts such as drag and point can provide precise spatial guidance, but are limited by the inherent ambiguity in semantic intent. To unify the strength of textual and visual prompts, we present Text-Vision Co-Instructed Image Editing, which jointly models textual instructions as semantic intent and sparse visual instructions as spatial guidance, aiming to achieve precise and intent-faithful image manipulation. To this end, we first construct a textual-visual instruction paired dataset with more than 23K samples derived from dynamic videos, enabling aligned supervision for cross-modal instruction. We then propose TV-Edit, a Textual-Visual instruction unified Editing framework to contextualize drag or point-based visual instructions with image-text semantics and lift them into semantic-aware control representations for pretrained editing backbones. By integrating semantic intent and spatial constraints, TV-Edit leads to more precise spatial control, less instruction ambiguity, and stronger structural consistency than text-only or drag-based alternatives. Finally, we establish TV-Edit-Bench, a deliberately designed benchmark to evaluate semantic faithfulness, spatial alignment, and visual consistency with ground-truth references and controlled textual-visual variations for reliable assessment. Our experiments across multiple editing backbones demonstrate that TV-Edit consistently yields more precise and intent-faithful edits, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art instruction-based and drag-based baselines.

</details>

### [85] [DC-Motion: Decoupling Semantics and Details via Discrete-Continuous Tokens for Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14721)
*Hequan Wang, Jiaxu Zhang, Zhengbo Zhang, Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 高优先级 (82.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DC-Motion 提出一种分解式人体动作生成框架，通过离散-连续 VAE 将动作分解为离散语义 token 和连续细节残差，分别用掩码自回归模型和残差扩散模型建模，在 HumanML3D 和 KIT-ML 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法使用同质化表征空间，难以捕捉人体动作的层次化特性：扩散模型在组合语义推理上表现不佳，自回归模型因量化损失了细粒度物理细节。因此需要一种能显式解耦语义与细节的生成框架。

Method: 提出 DC-Motion 框架，包含：(1) DC-VAE 将动作分解为离散 token（语义结构）和连续残差（细粒度动力学）；(2) 掩码自回归模型从文本预测离散结构；(3) 轻量级残差扩散模型恢复连续物理细节。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 和 KIT-ML 数据集上，DC-Motion 在 FID（动作真实感）和 R-precision（文本对齐）上均达到最优性能，有效提升了复杂长文本指令的跟随能力。

Conclusion: 通过离散-连续 token 显式解耦语义与细节，在语义可控性和物理真实感之间取得了有效平衡，为人体动作生成提供了一种高度可扩展的建模范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到人体动作生成（text-to-motion generation），属于多模态生成方向下的数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，该子主题明确包含"人体动作"（human motion）关键词。

Importance reason: Decouples semantics and details for human motion generation, but the subtopic is outside your priority list and lacks direct alignment with your top-weighted continuous multimodal or video generation directions. Signals: discrete-continuous VAE, masked AR + residual diffusion, SOTA on HumanML3D/KIT-ML, semantic-detail decoupling, human motion generation

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation requires synthesizing physically realistic dynamics that strictly follow complex and long-horizon textual instructions. Existing approaches rely on homogeneous representation spaces that may fail to capture the hierarchical nature of human motion, with diffusion models struggling at compositional semantic reasoning and AR models sacrificing fine-grained physical details due to quantization. To solve it, we introduce DC-Motion, a factorized generative framework designed to explicitly decouple semantics and details via discrete-continuous tokens. A Discrete-Continuous VAE (DC-VAE) first decomposes motion into discrete tokens for semantics and continuous residuals for fine-grained dynamics. Then, a masked AR model predicts the discrete structure from text, and a lightweight residual diffusion model recovers the continuous physical details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DC-Motion effectively improves the capability to follow complex instructions. By effectively balancing semantic controllability and physical realism, our approach offers a highly adaptable modeling paradigm for human motion generation. On both HumanML3D and KIT-ML datasets, DC-Motion achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering the best FID for motion realism and R-precision for text alignment.

</details>


### [86] [MotionVLA: Vision-Language-Action Model for Humanoid Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15142)
*Nonghai Zhang, Siyu Zhai, Yanjun Li, Zeyu Zhang, Zhihan Yin, Yandong Guo, Boxin Shi, Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (64.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MotionVLA，一种基于 Qwen3.5 的自回归人体动作生成模型。针对单共享码本量化无法同时表征低频姿态语义和高频物理动力学的问题，提出双流频率分词器 DSFT，将动作分解为 Base 流（低频姿态）和 Phys 流（高频物理信号），分别用 DCT 截断和 BPE 独立压缩，并在统一序列中先预测 Base token 再预测 Phys token。在 HumanML3D 和 MBench 上，2B 参数的轻量模型将 Diversity gap 降低 50% 以上，Motion-Condition Consistency 提升 3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用单一共享码本对动作进行量化，但人体运动数据在频域中低频姿态语义和高频物理动力学具有截然不同的统计特性——5个DCT系数可捕获93%的关节位置能量但仅37%的关节速度能量，导致量化偏向低频姿态而忽略高频速度分量。此外，标准自回归模型难以有效建模运动序列中的高频物理信号。

Method: 提出 DSFT（Dual-Stream Frequency Tokenizer），将人体运动分解为 Base 流（低频姿态语义）和 Phys 流（高频物理动力学），分别用 DCT 截断和 BPE 进行独立压缩。在此基础上构建 MotionVLA，基于 Qwen3.5-2B 的自回归模型，将 Base token 和 Phys token 排列为统一序列，先预测 Base token 再预测 Phys token，实现频率感知的双流解耦自回归动作生成。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 和 MBench 数据集上，MotionVLA（2B参数）将 Diversity gap 相比真实数据降低超过 50%，在 MBench 上 Motion-Condition Consistency 提升 3.8%，验证了频率感知双流解耦作为自回归动作生成范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 频率感知的双流解耦（DSFT）能有效解决单码本量化在人体动作生成中高低频信号不匹配的问题，为自回归动作生成提供了一种新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从场景图像和文本生成逼真的人体动作（humanoid motion），属于人体动作生成范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）明确涵盖人体动作生成，因此直接复用该分类。

Importance reason: Novel dual-stream frequency tokenizer for autoregressive human motion generation, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: autoregressive motion generation, dual-stream frequency tokenizer, humanoid motion, lightweight 2B model, diversity gap reduction

Abstract: Generating realistic humanoid motion from scene images and text involves both low-frequency pose semantics and high-frequency physical dynamics. However, many existing methods tokenize motion with a single shared codebook, forcing heterogeneous motion signals into the same quantization space. Our frequency-domain analysis of human motion data reveals a clear mismatch between single-codebook quantization and motion statistics: five DCT coefficients capture 93% of joint-position energy but only 37% of joint-velocity energy, which can bias quantization toward pose statistics and under-represent high-frequency velocity components. A second challenge lies in adapting a standard autoregressive model to effectively model high-frequency physical signals in motion sequences. Therefore, we propose DSFT, a dual-stream frequency tokenizer that separates motion into Base and physical streams and compresses them independently with DCT truncation and BPE. Furthermore, we present MotionVLA, a Qwen3.5-based model that arranges Base and physical tokens in a unified sequence, where Phys tokens are predicted after Base tokens. Experiments on HumanML3D and MBench show that, despite using a lightweight 2B backbone, MotionVLA reduces the Diversity gap to real data by over 50% on HumanML3D and improves Motion-Condition Consistency by 3.8% on MBench, supporting frequency-aware dual-stream decoupling as an effective formulation for autoregressive motion generation. Code: . Website: .

</details>


### [87] [SpatialAvatar-0: High-Quality 4D Head Avatar with Multi-Stage Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15659)
*Yiran Wang, Zeyu Zhang, Yuanming Li, Ziming Wang, Yang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialAvatar-0 提出了一种基于 FLAME 网格绑定高斯表示的高质量 4D 头部数字人重建方法，通过前馈生成器与布局保持的逐主体精化两阶段框架，在跨域零样本和单目基准上均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈预测器受限于单一数据集和固定源图像数量，存在域偏差；逐主体精化方法需要大量迭代且依赖自适应稠密化，破坏了前馈阶段的高斯布局，导致两种范式无法端到端共享表示。

Method: 提出 SpatialAvatar-0，基于共享的 FLAME 网格绑定高斯表示，包含：(1) 参数无关的 K 源均值池化前馈生成器；(2) 单目时序到多视角空间的两阶段训练策略；(3) 10K 迭代的布局保持逐主体精化循环，冻结 FLAME 绑定和高斯数量，用三分量抗尖峰正则化替代稠密化。

Result: 在 VFHQ/HDTF 跨域零样本上超越域内领先方法 GAGAvatar 1.5 dB PSNR；在 SplattingAvatar 单目基准上所有指标领先，超越 300K 迭代的 GeoAvatar 1.3 dB PSNR，逐主体精化速度比常见 SOTA 快 60 倍。

Conclusion: SpatialAvatar-0 通过共享高斯表示和布局保持精化策略，成功桥接了前馈预测与逐主体精化两种范式，实现了高质量、高效率的 4D 头部数字人重建。

Classification reason: 该论文研究高质量4D头部数字人（head avatar）的生成与重建，属于多模态生成方向下的数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: High-quality 4D head avatar reconstruction is a niche application, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 4D head avatar, FLAME mesh-bound Gaussian, multi-stage reconstruction, cross-domain zero-shot, layout-preserving refinement

Abstract: High-quality 4D head avatars from one or a few source portraits are central to telepresence, AR/VR, and digital-human interaction. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as the dominant representation, with two complementary regimes (generalizable feed-forward predictors and per-subject refiners) maturing in parallel. However, existing feed-forward predictors are trained on a single dataset family with a hard-coded source count, inheriting the corresponding domain bias. Per-subject refiners require 300K--600K iterations and rely on adaptive densification that destroys upstream Gaussian layouts, preventing the two regimes from sharing a representation end-to-end. To bridge both regimes we propose SpatialAvatar-0 on a shared FLAME-mesh-bound Gaussian representation: a feed-forward generator with a parameter-free K-source mean-pool and a monocular-temporal to multi-view-spatial two-phase schedule that anchors against identity-prior collapse onto the smaller multi-view set. We further introduce a 10K-iter layout-preserving per-subject refinement loop that freezes the FLAME-binding and Gaussian count and replaces densification with a three-component anti-spike regularization. On VFHQ/HDTF cross-domain zero-shot we surpass the in-domain leader GAGAvatar by +1.5 dB PSNR despite never training on either test domain, and on the SplattingAvatar monocular benchmark we lead every reported metric, surpassing the 300K-iter GeoAvatar by +1.3 dB PSNR at up to 60x shorter per-subject schedule than common SOTA baselines. Website: .

</details>


### [88] [EmoZone-Talker: Regional Semantic Control of Audio-Driven 3DGS Talking Heads via Facial Action Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15848)
*Tingting Chen, Shaojun Wang, Huaye Zhang, Diqiong Jiang, Chenglizhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: EmoZone-Talker 提出了一种基于3DGS的音频驱动说话头合成框架，通过 Synergy Zones with Prioritized Attention Bias (SZ-PAB) 和 Channel-Independent Temporal AU Encoder (CIT-AE) 实现面部动作单元（AU）的显式空间解耦与时序建模，从而在音频驱动下实现精细、可解释、可编辑的面部表情控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话头合成方法在音频驱动下难以实现精细、可解释且可编辑的面部表情控制，因为语音驱动的面部动态与显式表情信号之间存在内在冲突，隐式多模态融合导致空间纠缠和时间不稳定。

Method: 提出 Synergy Zones with Prioritized Attention Bias (SZ-PAB) 基于解剖学先验对模态贡献进行区域级显式解耦，以及 Channel-Independent Temporal AU Encoder (CIT-AE) 对动作单元（AU）进行时序一致性建模，将解耦后的表征集成到3D高斯变形中实现精确控制。

Result: 实验表明该方法在表情可控性和真实感上优于现有方法，尤其在上面部准确性和时序一致性方面有显著提升，同时保持高渲染质量和准确的唇形同步。

Conclusion: EmoZone-Talker 通过显式空间解耦和时序AU建模有效解决了音频驱动与表情控制之间的冲突，为3DGS说话头合成提供了更可控、可解释的面部动画方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音频驱动的3DGS说话头合成，属于虚拟人/说话头/面部动画生成方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS talking head control, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights and boost keywords. Signals: 3DGS talking head, audio-driven facial animation, facial action units, spatial-temporal coordination, expression control

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown strong potential for high-fidelity talking head synthesis. However, enabling fine-grained, interpretable, and editable facial expression control remains fundamentally challenging due to intrinsic conflicts between speech-driven facial dynamics and explicit expression signals. Existing methods rely on implicit multimodal fusion, leading to spatial entanglement and temporal instability. We present EmoZone-Talker, a novel framework that reformulates audio-driven facial animation as a structured spatial-temporal coordination problem under cross-modal conflicts. Our approach introduces an explicit spatial disentanglement and temporal dynamics modeling of facial motion. Specifically, we propose Synergy Zones with Prioritized Attention Bias (SZ-PAB) to explicitly decouple modality contributions via region-wise constraints guided by anatomical priors, and a Channel-Independent Temporal AU Encoder (CIT-AE) to model temporally coherent AU dynamics. By integrating these representations into 3D Gaussian deformation, EmoZone-Talker enables precise and interpretable control over facial expressions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method improves expression controllability and realism, with notable gains in upper-face accuracy and temporal coherence, while preserving high rendering quality and accurate lip synchronization. Code will be publicly released to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [89] [RGFVR: Reference-Guided Face Video Restoration with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16401)
*Cem Eteke, Batuhan Tosun, Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 RGFVR，一种基于参考帧引导的人脸视频恢复框架，利用预训练的流匹配文生视频模型，通过双模态感知-描述身份条件注入和两阶段训练策略，在保持身份一致性的同时恢复退化人脸视频的视觉保真度和时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸视频恢复方法要么是无参考的（导致身份丢失），要么是特定于主体的（泛化性差）。本文希望在主体无关（subject-agnostic）的前提下，通过参考帧引导实现身份保持的人脸视频恢复。

Method: 基于预训练的流匹配（flow matching）文生视频模型，引入双模态感知-描述身份条件（bimodal perceptual-descriptive identity conditioning），将参考帧的身份特征注入生成过程；采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段在高质量数据上微调，第二阶段引入退化数据增强身份引导。

Result: 在多种退化条件（下采样、模糊、噪声、压缩伪影）下，该方法在恢复保真度、时序一致性和身份保持方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将参考帧身份条件注入流匹配视频生成模型可以有效实现主体无关的身份保持人脸视频恢复，为退化人脸视频恢复提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人脸视频恢复（face video restoration），属于数字人/肖像动画/面部重演范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on face video restoration, not aligned with user's priority on autoregressive/diffusion video generation or world models. Signals: Flow matching for video restoration, Reference-guided identity preservation, Two-stage training strategy, Pretrained text-to-video model adaptation

Abstract: Face video restoration from degraded observations is challenging, as it requires simultaneously recovering visual fidelity, temporal consistency, and subject identity. Existing approaches are often either reference-free, which can lead to identity loss when person-specific facial details are lost, or subject-specific, which limits generalization to unseen identities. We propose a subject-agnostic, reference-guided framework for identity-preserving face video restoration. Our method introduces bimodal perceptual-descriptive identity conditioning into a pretrained flow-based text-to-video generator and employs a two-stage training strategy to strengthen identity guidance during restoration. Experiments show that our approach improves restoration fidelity, temporal consistency, and identity preservation, achieving superior performance under challenging video degradations, including downsampling, blur, noise, and compression artifacts. </details>
