### [90] [ControlMap: Controllable High-Definition Map Generation for Traffic Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15930)
*Marwan Farag, Steffen Wäldele, Yu Yao*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ControlMap，一种基于潜在扩散模型和 ControlNet 的可控高精地图生成方法，用于自动驾驶交通场景仿真。该方法首次将空间引导信号注入扩散模型进行 HD 地图合成，支持通过无分类器引导调节条件强度，并通过城市标签实现风格迁移。同时提出了两个新指标来评估控制信号遵循度和地图真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真中高精地图的创建成本高昂，需要大量数据采集和人工处理，导致场景多样性不足。现有生成模型缺乏对特定道路拓扑结构的细粒度控制能力。

Method: 采用潜在扩散模型（latent diffusion）结合 ControlNet 进行空间条件控制，将道路拓扑作为空间引导信号注入扩散过程。通过无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）调节条件强度，并利用城市标签条件实现城市级别风格迁移。

Result: 实验表明，模型能够生成逼真的 HD 地图，忠实遵循输入的道路拓扑结构，同时准确保留城市特定的细节特征。提出的两个新指标有效评估了对控制信号的遵循程度和与真实地图的相似度。

Conclusion: ControlMap 证明了数据驱动的可控 HD 地图生成在自动驾驶仿真中的可行性，为提升场景多样性提供了低成本、可控的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用潜在扩散模型（latent diffusion）和 ControlNet 进行高精地图生成，属于扩散模型在图像/地图生成领域的应用。虽然具体任务是 HD 地图生成，但核心方法是文生图/图像扩散中的 latent diffusion + ControlNet 范式，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Focuses on controllable HD map generation for autonomous driving, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Controllable generation, diffusion model, autonomous driving, HD map, spatial conditioning

Abstract: Simulation is central to validating autonomous driving systems, yet current pipelines are limited by insufficient scenario diversity due to costly High Definition (HD) map creation. Scaling HD maps requires expensive data collection and manual processing. Moreover, existing generative models lack the fine-grained control necessary to target specific road topologies during generation. This paper presents a data-driven pipeline for controllable HD map generation using latent diffusion and ControlNet for spatial conditioning. To our knowledge, we are the first to inject spatial guidance signals into a diffusion model for HD map synthesis. Furthermore, our model supports adjustable conditioning strength through classifier-free guidance and city-level style transfer via city label conditioning. To complement existing metrics, we introduce two novel metrics to evaluate adherence to the control signal and similarity to ground-truth maps. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates realistic HD maps that faithfully follow input road topologies while accurately preserving city-specific details.

</details>


### [91] [Lesion-DDPM: Lesion-Enhanced 3D Diffusion for MS MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15457)
*Weidong Zhang, Yongchan Jung, Shafayat Mowla Anik, Furen Xiao, Vasudevan Janarthanan, Enkhzaya Chuluunbaatar, Byeong Kil Lee, Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 普通相关 (47.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Lesion-DDPM 是一个面向多发性硬化症（MS）脑部 MRI 的 3D 条件扩散框架，通过多层级解剖掩膜注入和病灶加权重建损失，在保持全局脑结构的同时增强病灶区域的合成质量，在下游分割任务中显著优于现有 GAN 和扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的 MS 脑部 MRI 数据集规模小、跨扫描仪和协议差异大，且病灶稀疏微小，现有生成模型难以在保持全局结构的同时保真地合成病灶区域，限制了鲁棒神经影像模型的开发。

Method: 提出 Lesion-DDPM，一个 3D 条件扩散模型。核心设计包括：(1) 多层级解剖掩膜注入（multi-level anatomical mask injection），将脑组织分割掩膜作为条件输入扩散模型的不同层级；(2) 病灶加权重建损失（lesion-weighted reconstruction loss），对病灶体素赋予更高权重以强调病灶区域的生成质量。使用 MSLesSeg 数据集的子集进行训练和评估。

Result: Lesion-DDPM 在病灶区域重建误差上最低。下游 3D U-Net 分割任务中，仅用 Lesion-DDPM 生成数据训练的模型在真实 MRI 上 Dice 达 0.616（最佳对比方法为 0.569）；将生成数据加入真实训练集后 Dice 进一步提升至 0.685。

Conclusion: 病灶感知的 3D 条件扩散框架能有效合成高质量 MS 脑部 MRI，尤其能保真地生成微小稀疏病灶，且生成数据可显著提升下游分割模型的性能，展示了医学图像生成中病灶感知设计的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D扩散模型的MRI图像合成方法，属于图像生成范畴。虽然应用领域是医学影像，但核心方法是条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion），与[文生图与图像扩散]这一规范主题在方法论上高度一致（扩散模型、条件生成、图像合成），因此复用该规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical MRI synthesis for MS lesions, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: 3D diffusion, lesion-weighted loss, downstream segmentation improvement, medical imaging, not aligned with user's continuous multimodal or video generation focus

Abstract: 3D FLAIR MRI is widely recommended as one of the standard MRI sequences for brain imaging in multiple sclerosis (MS), but publicly available MS datasets remain relatively small and vary across scanners, acquisition protocols, and lesion patterns. This scarcity and variability hinder the development of robust neuroimaging machine learning models and are particularly challenging for generative models that aim to synthesize images while preserving small, sparse lesions. We propose Lesion-DDPM, a 3D conditional diffusion framework for lesion-aware FLAIR synthesis that incorporates multi-level anatomical mask injection together with a lesion-weighted reconstruction loss to emphasize lesion voxels while maintaining global brain structure. Using a curated subset of the MSLesSeg dataset, we compare Lesion-DDPM with representative state-of-the-art GAN- and diffusion-based models, assessing both image-generation metrics and downstream 3D U-Net segmentation. In our experiments, Lesion-DDPM achieved the lowest lesion-region reconstruction error among all methods. In a downstream 3D U-Net lesion segmentation task, a model trained only on Lesion-DDPM-generated scans and evaluated on real MRIs reached a Dice score of 0.616 compared with 0.569 for the best competing synthetic dataset. When Lesion-DDPM images were added to the real training set, the Dice score further increased to 0.685.

</details>


### [92] [DenseControl: Instance-Level Controllable Synthesis of Dense Crowd Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15592)
*Juncheng Wang, Lei Shang, Wang Lu, Baigui Sun, Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DenseControl 提出了一种密集人群图像生成管线，通过隔离对象嵌入图（IOE）和隐式尺度嵌入（ISE）实现实例级的位置与尺度精确控制，并支持背景、风格和属性控制，在密集人群合成任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集人群图像合成面临两个主要挑战：控制信号嵌入困难（多个实例的坐标/尺度信息难以被模型有效学习），以及拓扑完整性保持问题（实例尺度引导时容易破坏空间拓扑结构）。现有方法难以同时实现精确的实例级位置和尺度控制。

Method: 提出 DenseControl 管线：(1) 隔离对象嵌入图（IOE），将每个实例的位置信息编码为独立通道，避免实例间干扰；(2) 隐式尺度嵌入（ISE），与 IOE 结合编码精确的尺度信息；(3) 位置快捷机制（Position Shortcut），增强交叉注意力以缓解投影学习困难。整体基于扩散模型实现可控密集人群图像生成。

Result: 在多个控制条件下，DenseControl 在密集人群图像合成质量上达到 SOTA。在数据稀缺下的 crowd analysis 增强、迁移学习和天气泛化等下游应用中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: DenseControl 通过 IOE、ISE 和 Position Shortcut 三个创新设计，有效解决了密集人群图像合成中的控制信号嵌入和拓扑完整性挑战，为实例级可控生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出密集人群图像生成方法 DenseControl，属于可控图像生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散 子主题相关（尽管更侧重布局/实例级控制而非纯文生图，但该子主题涵盖图像扩散生成方法，且无更精确匹配的现有子主题）。

Importance reason: Controllable dense crowd generation is a niche application, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: Instance-level control, dense crowd synthesis, diffusion-based generation, controllable generation, subject-driven generation

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce DenseControl, a novel pipeline for generating dense crowd images. Specifically, DenseControl meticulously positions and sizes each generated instance to align precisely with the predefined coordinates and scales. Based on this, we further allow for control over the background, style, and attributes of instances. The motivation behind DenseControl stems from the observation of two main challenges in synthesizing crowd images: controlling signal embedding and maintaining topological integrity when imparting instance scale guidance. To address these, we first introduce the Isolated Object Embedding (IOE) map, a novel representation that facilitates spatial location control while mitigating the difficulties associated with learning projections for model. Secondly, we propose an Implicit Scale Embedding (ISE) strategy that seamlessly integrates with the IOE map to encode precise scale information. To further enhance the efficacy of combining ISE with the IOE map, we incorporate a Position Shortcut mechanism that enhances cross-attention to alleviate projection challenges. We evaluate DenseControl through two lenses: synthesis quality and applicability in latent applications. Experiments across different control conditions demonstrate DenseControl achieves state-of-the-art results in dense crowd image synthesis. Furthermore, we showcase applications in augmenting crowd analysis under data scarcity, transfer learning, and weather generalization scenes, to highlight the practical utility of DenseControl. The codebase will be released.

</details>


### [93] [Structure-Semantic Co-optimized Latent Diffusion Model for Fast Visual Anagram Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16241)
*Xiang Gao, Yunpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (65.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 S2CO-Anagram，一种基于结构-语义协同优化的潜扩散模型，用于快速生成高分辨率、高美学质量的视觉幻象图（visual anagram）。通过将像素级并行去噪算法适配到对抗蒸馏的潜扩散模型，并引入空文本结构对齐优化、语义增强优化和注意力引导噪声融合三个模块，在显著提升推理速度的同时生成视觉和谐且语义忠实的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉幻象图生成方法存在计算效率低、美学质量差、语义忠实度不足等问题，本文旨在以最小计算成本生成视觉质量大幅提升的视觉幻象图，推动智能幻觉数字艺术创作。

Method: 提出结构-语义协同优化（S2CO）框架，包含三个关键创新：(1) 空文本结构对齐优化——利用空文本引导保持翻转/旋转后的结构一致性；(2) 语义增强优化——增强不同视角下的语义表达；(3) 注意力引导噪声融合——通过注意力机制融合多视角噪声，提升视觉和谐度。同时将像素级并行去噪算法适配到对抗蒸馏的潜扩散模型，实现高分辨率快速生成。

Result: S2CO-Anagram 在视觉和谐度、语义忠实度上显著优于现有 SOTA 方法，同时推理速度大幅提升，能生成更高分辨率的幻象图。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 S2CO 框架有效解决了视觉幻象图生成中的效率与质量问题，为智能幻觉数字艺术创作提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文利用预训练的文生图扩散模型生成视觉幻象图（visual anagram），属于文本到图像生成范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「文生图与图像扩散」。

Importance reason: Focuses on visual anagram synthesis, a niche creative task, with limited direct relevance to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Visual anagram synthesis, Latent diffusion model, Parallel denoising, Structure-semantic co-optimization, Fast inference

Abstract: Visual anagram is an intriguing form of art creation wherein a single image presents different conceptual interpretations under transformations such as flipping or rotation. Recent work has achieved visual anagram synthesis by leveraging pretrained text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models, yet still suffers from several key limitations including computational inefficiency, suboptimal aesthetic quality, and weak semantic fidelity and expressiveness. This work focuses on generating visual anagrams with substantially improved visual quality at minimal computational cost, thereby advancing intelligent creation of illusionary digital art. To increase image resolution while reducing time overhead, we adapt the cutting-edge parallel denoising algorithm from pixel-based T2I model to the adversarially distilled latent-based one, and accordingly propose a structure-semantic co-optimization (S2CO) framework to counteract the consequent visual degradation. As the core of our approach, S2CO framework comprises three key innovations: (\romannumeral1) null-text structure alignment optimization; (\romannumeral2) semantic enhancement optimization; (\romannumeral3) attention-guided noise fusion. Building upon these components, our method dubbed \textbf{S2CO-Anagram} is able to generate higher-resolution anagram images with noticeably superior visual harmony and semantic faithfulness than related SOTA approaches, all while achieving substantially faster inference speed. Code will be publicly available.

</details>

### [94] [Steady-Forcing: Balancing Spatial Persistence and Motion Continuity in Long-Horizon Nature Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14732)
*Matiur Rahman Minar, Seunghun Oh, GangHyeon Jeong, Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Steady-Forcing，一种用于长时域固定相机自然视频扩散生成的记忆与训练框架，通过视觉锚点（V-Sink）、指数移动平均运动记忆（EMA-Sink）、块相对时序编码、周期性缓存净化以及从 Wan2.1-14B 教师模型蒸馏等技术，在保持背景一致性的同时维持水流、火焰等自然流体运动的连续性，在分钟级自回归 rollout 中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时域生成中面临稳定性与运动连续性的权衡：增强空间稳定性（如背景保持）的机制往往会抑制自然流体运动（如水、火、烟雾），导致运动停滞。现有方法在移动相机场景中难以分离这两种失败模式，而固定相机自然视频场景能更清晰地研究这一矛盾。

Method: 提出 Steady-Forcing 框架，包含五个核心组件：(1) V-Sink（视觉锚点），持久化背景视觉特征以保持空间一致性；(2) EMA-Sink（指数移动平均运动记忆），平滑运动轨迹以维持流体连续性；(3) 块相对时序编码，增强时序位置感知；(4) 周期性缓存净化，防止长 rollout 中的误差累积；(5) 从 Wan2.1-14B 教师模型蒸馏，结合运动奖励先验。整体采用任务聚焦配置进行训练。

Result: 在七个基线方法上的评估表明，Steady-Forcing 在长时域背景一致性和成像质量上均有提升。盲人用户研究显示更强的感知稳定性和运动连续性。同时发现通用 VBench 评分对固定相机伪影惩罚不足，且将漂移引起的光流误判为动态度（Dynamic Degree），而不直接惩罚纹理硬化或流动停滞，揭示了现有评测的局限性。

Conclusion: Steady-Forcing 有效缓解了长时域视频扩散中背景漂移与运动停滞的矛盾，在固定相机自然视频生成中取得了更好的稳定性-运动平衡。同时指出通用视频生成评测基准在固定相机场景下的不足，呼吁开发针对静态相机自然流场景的任务特定评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长时域自然视频扩散生成，属于视频扩散模型范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses long-horizon video diffusion with world-model-like stability and motion continuity, aligning with user's high-priority video generation and world model subtopics. Signals: video diffusion, world model, autoregressive video generation, long-horizon generation, motion continuity

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable streaming generation but often degrade over long rollouts: static scene layouts drift, while mechanisms that improve spatial stability tend to suppress motion, causing natural flows such as water, fire, or smoke to stagnate. We study this stability-motion trade-off in fixed-camera long-horizon nature video generation, where the two failure modes can be more clearly separated than in moving-camera settings. We propose Steady-Forcing, a memory and training framework combining a persistent visual anchor (V-Sink), an exponential moving-average motion memory (EMA-Sink), block-relative temporal encoding, periodic cache purification, and distillation from a Wan2.1-14B teacher with motion-rewarded priors under task-focused configurations. Together, these components are designed to preserve background identity while sustaining visually plausible fluid dynamics over multi-minute autoregressive rollouts. Evaluations across seven baselines show that Steady-Forcing improves long horizon background consistency and imaging quality, while a blind user study indicates stronger perceived stability and motion continuity. The benchmark evaluation further suggest that generic VBench aggregate scores under-penalize fixed-camera artifacts as well as rewarding drift-induced optical flow as Dynamic Degree while not directly penalizing texture hardening or flow stagnation - motivating future task-specific benchmarks for static-camera nature-flow evaluation. Project page:

</details>


### [95] [Closed-Loop Triplet Synergistic Generation for Long-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16184)
*Xinlei Yin, Xiulian Peng, Xiao Li, Zhiwei Xiong, Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (87.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CoTriSyGen，一个基于闭环视觉-文本-记忆三元组协同的智能体框架，通过 VLM 分析器进行镜头内精炼和镜头间精炼，结合实体中心记忆实现多镜头长视频的一致生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头长视频生成面临身份漂移和跨镜头不一致的挑战。现有故事板驱动方法以前馈方式执行，缺乏将已生成视觉证据反馈回后续条件生成的机制。

Method: 提出 CoTriSyGen 框架，将多镜头长视频生成建模为闭环视觉-文本-记忆三元组协同过程。包含：(1) 实体中心记忆，作为可变的视觉状态随故事演进；(2) VLM 分析器，通过镜头内精炼（检测语义/构图违规并触发针对性重生成）和镜头间精炼（基于已生成证据重写后续镜头提示）两条路径迭代修正；(3) 闭环反馈机制，将已生成视觉证据用于提示和记忆更新。

Result: 在 StoryBench 基准上的实验表明，该方法在跨镜头一致性、提示遵循度和电影连续性方面显著优于代表性方法。

Conclusion: 闭环视觉-文本-记忆三元组协同机制能有效解决多镜头长视频生成中的身份漂移和不一致问题，为长视频生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦多镜头长视频生成，属于文本到视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。同时论文使用了 VLM 进行闭环分析推理，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's high-priority multimodal generation and world model subtopics via closed-loop video generation with VLM-based refinement. Signals: long-form video generation, closed-loop visual-text-memory synergy, VLM-based intra-shot and inter-shot refinement, entity-centric memory for consistency, storyboard-driven pipeline improvement

Abstract: Multi-shot long-form video generation remains challenging due to identity drift and compounding inconsistencies across shots. While storyboard-driven pipelines improve controllability, they are often executed in a feed-forward manner, with limited mechanisms to incorporate generated visual evidence back into subsequent conditioning. We propose CoTriSyGen, an agentic framework that formulates multi-shot long video generation as a closed-loop visual-text-memory synergy process, where planned intent, persistent memory, and generated visuals are jointly leveraged for iterative correction and long-range coherence. A vision-language-model-based analyzer reasons over this triplet and produces updates to both prompts and memory along two pathways: (i) intra-shot refinement, which triggers targeted regeneration when semantic or compositional violations are detected and refines image-to-video prompt for coherent motions; and (ii) inter-shot refinement, which rewrites subsequent-shot prompts to propagate newly manifested entities or attributes and improve prompt quality (e.g., compositional grounding and cinematic fluency) based on generated evidence. The loop is grounded in an entity-centric memory modeled as a mutable visual state that evolves as the story progresses, which is continuously updated by both the generator and the analyzer by adding new and evolved entities to reflect appearance changes, accumulated multi-view evidence, and multi-entity compositions. Experiments on our curated StoryBench benchmark demonstrate substantial improvements in cross-shot consistency, prompt adherence, and cinematic continuity over representative methods.

</details>


### [96] [Temporally Consistent and Controllable Video Generation of 2D Cine CMR via Latent Space Motion Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14759)
*Yiheng Cao, Gustavo Andrade-Miranda, Jiatian Zhang, Guillaume Sallé, Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种文本到视频生成方法，用于合成时间一致且解剖结构一致的心脏电影MRI序列。框架将心脏空间结构与时间运动解耦：先用微调扩散模型从临床文本提示生成初始帧，再用基于心脏相位嵌入的条件潜流模型生成完整心脏运动。实验表明该方法能生成高保真、高时间一致性的医学数据，FID为31.68，CLIP得分为31.04。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏电影MRI是评估心脏功能的金标准，但公开数据集稀缺，限制了数据驱动模型的发展。现有方法难以生成时间一致且解剖结构准确的医学视频数据，因此需要一种可控的、高保真的合成方法来缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段文本到视频生成框架：1) 空间阶段：微调预训练扩散模型，从临床文本提示生成初始帧，控制解剖特征；2) 时间阶段：构建一个以心脏相位嵌入为条件的潜空间流模型（latent flow model），在潜空间中建模完整心脏运动，确保空间一致性和时间可控性。

Result: 模型生成的序列具有高时间连贯性和对输入提示的强忠实度，FID为31.68（图像真实感），CLIP得分为31.04（文本-图像对齐）。实验表明该方法能生成解剖和病理多样化的序列，为数据稀缺问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的解耦式文本到视频生成方法能有效合成高保真、时间一致的心脏电影MRI数据，有望为医学影像数据稀缺问题提供可扩展的解决方案，并支持按需生成多样化的解剖和病理数据。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个文本到视频生成框架，使用扩散模型生成初始帧，再通过潜空间流模型（latent flow model）生成完整心脏运动序列，属于视频扩散生成方法，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"文生视频与视频扩散"。

Importance reason: Medical domain video generation with low relevance to user's priority on general continuous multimodal models and autoregressive video generation. Signals: Medical domain, cardiac MRI, latent flow model, text-to-video, temporal consistency

Abstract: Cine cardiac magnetic resonance is the gold standard for assessing cardiac function, but the scarcity of public datasets limits the development of advanced data-driven models. To address this limitation, we propose a generative method for synthesizing temporally coherent and anatomically consistent cardiac sequences. Our text-to-video framework decouples cardiac spatial structure from temporal motion. First, a fine-tuned diffusion model synthesizes an initial frame from a clinical text prompt, controlling anatomical features. Then, a latent flow model conditioned on a cardiac phase embedding generates the complete cardiac motion, ensuring spatial consistency and temporal control. Our model generates anatomically and pathologically diverse sequences with high temporal coherence and strong fidelity to input prompts, achieving a FID of 31.68 for image realism and a CLIP score of 31.04 for text-image alignment. These experimental results highlight its potential to produce high-fidelity, on-demand medical data, offering a scalable solution to data scarcity.

</details>


### [97] [Track2View: 4D-Consistent Camera-Controlled Video Generation via Paired 3D Point Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15534)
*Feng Qiao, Zhaochong An, Zhexiao Xiong, Serge Belongie, Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Track2View 提出一种基于配对3D点轨迹的相机可控视频生成方法，通过双视角轨迹条件器将源视角视觉信息传递到目标视角，在400视频基准上实现了领先的视觉质量和相机精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新视角视频生成方法依赖逐帧位姿嵌入、噪声点云渲染或隐式对应关系，缺乏显式的、时间连续的源-目标像素对应链接，导致相机轨迹控制不精确、时序一致性差。

Method: 提出 Track2View，以配对3D点轨迹（稀疏场景点在源视角和目标视角中的投影轨迹）作为条件，输入视频扩散Transformer。核心是双视角轨迹条件器，通过无参数几何操作和学习的时序聚合将源视角视觉上下文传递到目标视角。同时提出数据构建流程，在时间拼接的多相机视角对上运行3D点追踪器提取一一对应的轨迹。

Result: 在包含静态和动态场景的400视频基准上，Track2View 在视觉质量、视角同步和相机精度上达到SOTA，旋转误差降低30-65%，平移误差降低61-72%。

Conclusion: 显式的3D点轨迹条件能够有效提升新视角视频生成的时序一致性和相机控制精度，且方法对任意相机轨迹具有泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于视频扩散Transformer（Video Diffusion Transformer）的相机可控视频生成方法，属于视频生成范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on video generation and world models, with explicit 3D point tracks offering a novel, temporally continuous conditioning for camera control. Signals: video generation, camera control, 3D point tracks, diffusion transformer, world model

Abstract: Re-rendering an existing video from a novel camera viewpoint requires the output to follow the prescribed camera trajectory while preserving the appearance and dynamics of the original scene across every frame. Existing methods rely on per-frame pose embeddings, noisy point-cloud renderings, or implicit learned correspondences, none of which provides an explicit, temporally continuous link between source and target pixels. We propose Track2View, which conditions a video diffusion transformer on paired 3D point tracks: sparse trajectories of scene points projected into both the source and target camera views. These tracks provide explicit spatiotemporal correspondences that are temporally continuous by construction, encoding what content should appear where and when. At the core of Track2View is a dual-view track conditioner that transfers visual context from source to target view through parameter-free geometric operations and learned temporal aggregation, ensuring generalization to arbitrary camera trajectories without memorizing specific motions. We further introduce a data curation pipeline that extracts one-to-one track correspondences by running a 3D point tracker on temporally concatenated multi-camera view pairs. On a 400-video benchmark spanning static and dynamic scenes, Track2View achieves state-of-the-art results across visual quality, view synchronization, and camera accuracy, reducing rotation error by 30-65% and translation error by 61-72% relative to leading baselines. Project page is available at this https URL:

</details>

### [98] [Temporal Difference Learning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15048)
*Qizhen Ying, Yangchen Pan, Victor Adrian Prisacariu, Junfeng Wen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (64.98) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时序差分（TD）学习目标，用于扩散模型的训练。通过将扩散过程重新表述为马尔可夫奖励过程，将去噪视为强化学习中的策略评估问题，推导出适用于离散和连续时间扩散的统一TD方法。该方法通过惩罚去噪轨迹上多步预测的不一致性来增强跨时间步的一致性，显著提升少步采样下的生成质量（FID），可作为扩散模型的通用插件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通常使用仅关注单个时间步（或相邻步）局部去噪目标的训练损失，这无法保证去噪轨迹上不同时间步预测之间的一致性。这种跨时间一致性的缺失在少步采样场景下尤为严重，导致生成质量下降。作者希望引入一种能强制跨时间一致性的训练目标来改善这一问题。

Method: 将扩散过程重新表述为马尔可夫奖励过程（MRP），去噪过程视为强化学习中的策略评估问题。推导出统一的时序差分（TD）目标，适用于离散时间和连续时间扩散公式。提出一种基于样本的 principled 重加权方法以稳定训练。TD目标作为额外的正则化项，惩罚模型在去噪轨迹上多步预测之间的不一致性。

Result: 实验表明，TD训练能显著提升样本质量（FID），尤其在采样步数较少时优势更明显，证明了其在低计算预算场景下的实用性。消融研究验证了成对损失重加权、正则化权重和一步跨度等设计选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 时序差分学习目标是一种通用的、即插即用的方法，能够强制扩散模型在去噪轨迹上的跨时间一致性，提升不同扩散生成模型的生成质量，特别适用于少步采样等低计算预算场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种基于时序差分学习的扩散模型训练方法，核心目标是提升少步采样（few-step sampling）下的生成质量，属于扩散模型加速与高效化的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for diffusion models, a priority subtopic, with a novel TD training method that improves few-step generation quality. Signals: diffusion model acceleration, temporal difference learning, cross-time consistency, few-step sampling, plug-and-play training objective

Abstract: Diffusion models are typically trained with objectives that focus on local denoising targets at individual time steps (or adjacent pairs), which do not enforce consistency between predictions along the denoising trajectory. This lack of cross-time consistency can degrade performance, especially for few-step samplers. We introduce a temporal difference (TD) objective that penalizes inconsistency of the model's multi-step progress along the denoising path. By reformulating the diffusion process as a Markov reward process and casting denoising as a policy evaluation problem in reinforcement learning, we derive a unified TD approach that applies to both discrete- and continuous-time diffusion formulations. We further propose a principled sample-based reweighting method that stabilizes training. Empirically, we show that using our TD training can significantly improve sample quality measured by FID, with stronger advantages when the number of sampling steps is small, highlighting its practical utility under low-computation-budget scenarios. We provide ablation studies to justify our design choices, including pairwise loss reweighting, regularization weight, and one-step stride. Overall, our TD approach can be a general drop-in that enforces cross-time consistency and improves generation quality across different diffusion generative models.

</details>


### [99] [Show the Signal, Hide the Noise: Spectral Forcing for Pixel-Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15236)
*Weichen Fan, Haiwen Diao, Penghao Wu, Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (79.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现像素空间扩散模型在训练时存在隐式的由粗到细结构，导致模型在噪声主导的高频区域浪费计算资源。为此提出 Spectral Forcing，一种参数无关的时变 2D-DCT 低通滤波器，在输入阶段显式分离信号与噪声频带，使去噪器专注于有效信号区域。在 ImageNet-256 和 SenseNova-U1 上均取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型在训练时接收全频带噪声图像，但自然图像具有幂律频谱，不同频带的信噪比随时间变化显著。标准去噪器需要隐式学习这一时变频带边界，在高频噪声主导区域浪费计算资源。作者希望显式地分离信号与噪声频带，使模型专注于有效信号区域，从而提升训练效率和生成质量。

Method: 提出 Spectral Forcing，一个参数无关的时变 2D-DCT 低通滤波器。其截止频率随扩散时间单调扩展，在数据端点退化为恒等映射。该滤波器在 patch embedder 之前作用于噪声输入，显式保留低频信号、抑制高频噪声。通过合成实验确定了该算子有效的条件：粗粒度 patch tokenization 且数据高频成分主要为噪声而非有效信号。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上使用 JiT-700M/32 模型，Spectral Forcing 在不同训练 epoch 下一致提升 FID 和 Inception Score；在更细粒度的 tokenization 下仍具有竞争力。在 SenseNova-U1 统一文生图模型中，该算子提升了 DPG-Bench 和 GenEval 指标，表明频域先验可迁移到类条件生成之外的场景。

Conclusion: Spectral Forcing 通过显式分离信号与噪声频带，有效缓解了像素空间扩散模型中的容量分配问题，在多个设置下带来一致的性能提升。这项工作为构建容量高效的像素空间扩散模型提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Spectral Forcing，一种参数无关的频域低通滤波方法，用于提升像素空间扩散模型的训练效率与生成质量，属于生成模型效率优化（加速与高效化）范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"。

Importance reason: Proposes a parameter-free spectral filtering method to improve pixel-space diffusion efficiency, directly relevant to model efficiency and generation acceleration. Signals: pixel-space diffusion, spectral forcing, 2D-DCT low-pass filter, capacity allocation, training efficiency

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models are trained on full-bandwidth noisy images, yet the useful signal available to the denoiser is strongly frequency dependent. Under rectified-flow diffusion and natural-image power-law spectra, the per-band data-to-noise contour $k^{*}(t) = (1-t)^{-2/\alpha}$ separates a signal-bearing low-frequency region from a noise-dominated high-frequency region at each time $t$. We show that this implicit coarse-to-fine structure is not merely descriptive: it induces a capacity-allocation problem. A standard pixel-space denoiser must discover the moving bandwidth boundary internally and can spend computation on frequency-time regions where the optimal prediction collapses to deterministic baselines rather than data-distribution modeling. To make this boundary explicit, we introduce Spectral Forcing, a parameter-free, time-conditional 2D-DCT low-pass operator applied to the noisy input before the patch embedder. Its cutoff expands monotonically with the diffusion time and becomes the identity at the data endpoint. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we identify the regime in which the operator is beneficial: coarse patch tokenization and data whose high-frequency content is predominantly noise rather than essential signal. On ImageNet-256 with JiT-700M/32, Spectral Forcing consistently improves both FID and Inception Score across different training epochs, demonstrating robust gains throughout training; at finer tokenization, the spectral forcing is still competitive. We further insert the unchanged operator into SenseNova-U1, a unified text-to-image model, where it improves DPG-Bench and GenEval, showing that the input-side spectral prior transfers beyond class-conditional generation. These results suggest a route to capacity-efficient pixel-space diffusion by showing the signal and hiding the noise.

</details>


### [100] [Distilling Drifting Transformers with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15553)
*Jiawei Zhang, Mengfei Xia, Gen Li, Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Drift-RAE，将 Drifting 蒸馏范式应用于表征自编码器（RAE）潜空间，通过理论分析揭示 RAE 潜空间的各向异性和曲率问题，并引入改进使蒸馏稳定，在 ImageNet 256 上以 10k 步达到 1.77 FID，超越现有 RAE 蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAE 利用 DINO 等预训练编码器提供了语义更丰富的潜空间，但在蒸馏阶段，其严重的各向异性（anisotropy）和大曲率阻碍了基于轨迹的蒸馏方法的收敛与性能。现有 Drifting 模型在极度分散的空间（如重建 VAE）上容易失败。因此需要将 Drifting 范式适配到 RAE 潜空间，解决蒸馏不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出 Drift-RAE，核心思路是将 Drifting 蒸馏范式直接应用于 RAE 潜空间。首先定量研究不同自编码器的曲率和各向同性统计量，理论揭示 Drifting 模型在重建 VAE 等极度分散空间上容易失败。然后对 Drifting 范式进行改进，通过理论对齐漂移场与其他框架来提升训练稳定性。具体包括在 RAE 潜空间中对预训练流模型进行蒸馏，无需辅助 MAE 特征提取器。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 数据集上，仅用 10k 蒸馏步骤即达到 1.77 FID，超越了现有的 RAE 蒸馏方法，并与原始 Drifting 模型性能相当，且无需辅助 MAE 特征提取器。

Conclusion: Drift-RAE 成功将 Drifting 蒸馏范式适配到 RAE 潜空间，解决了各向异性和大曲率带来的蒸馏不稳定问题，实现了高效、高质量的生成模型蒸馏，为连续潜空间中的蒸馏方法提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Drift-RAE，在表征自编码器（RAE）潜空间中对预训练流模型进行蒸馏，仅用10k步达到1.77 FID，属于生成模型蒸馏加速方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）。同时涉及连续视觉 tokenizer（RAE）和连续潜空间，因此也匹配 continuous_language_multimodal 方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent space distillation for generative models, a core priority subtopic. Signals: representation autoencoder, drifting distillation, continuous latent space, flow matching, efficient inference

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) have improved diffusion and flow models by semantically richer latent space owing to the strongly label-wise clustered DINO features in the pretrained encoders. Yet in the distillation stage, the severe anisotropy and large curvatures caused by the rich semantic representations would hinder the convergence and performance, making the trajectory-based distillation unstable. In this work, we argue that the RAE latent space is compatible with distillation via the newly proposed Drifting Models. We first quantitatively study the curvatures and isotropy statistics across different autoencoders, and theoretically reveal that Drifting Model itself is highly likely to fail on extremely scattered spaces like reconstruction-based VAEs. These motivate us to apply the drifting paradigm directly to representation autoencoders. Our proposed method, Drift-RAE, distills pretrained flow models in RAE latent spaces using Drifting, together with insightful modifications that improve training stability by thereotically aligning drifting fields with other frameworks. Regarding the experimental evidences, we achieve 1.77 FID on ImageNet 256 dataset using only 10k distillation steps, surpassing state-of-the-art RAE distillation methods and appearing comparative with the original Drifting Model without requiring an auxiliary MAE feature extractor. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [MoECa: Aligning Feature Reuse with Expert Decomposition in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15615)
*Maoliang Li, Haojing Chen, Jiayu Chen, Zihao Zheng, Xinhao Sun, Hailong Zou, Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (64.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MoECa 提出了一种面向 Diffusion Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts (DiT-MoE) 的细粒度缓存框架，在专家分支级别进行跨时间步特征复用，结合专家感知的自适应控制和同步缓存更新，实现最高 2.83 倍推理加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型缓存方法主要在 token 级别操作，但在 DiT-MoE 中每个 token 更新被分解为多个路由专家分支，token 级缓存无法有效捕捉跨时间步的冗余。作者发现 DiT-MoE 的跨时间步冗余更适合在专家分支级别刻画，因此需要一种分支级的缓存框架来更高效地加速推理。

Method: MoECa 包含三个核心设计：(1) 分支级特征复用——在专家分支级别缓存和复用跨时间步的特征，而非传统 token 级别；(2) 专家感知的自适应控制——根据专家分支的特性动态调整缓存策略；(3) 同步缓存更新——在 MoE 路径和注意力路径之间同步维护缓存状态，确保中间状态稳定。

Result: 在多个 DiT-MoE 模型上的实验表明，MoECa 相比现有缓存方法在速度-质量权衡上持续更优，最高可实现 2.83 倍推理加速，同时质量退化极小。

Conclusion: MoECa 证明了在 DiT-MoE 中，专家分支级别的特征复用比 token 级别更有效，为扩散模型的高效推理提供了新的细粒度缓存范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MoECa，一种针对 DiT-MoE 的细粒度缓存框架，通过分支级特征复用来加速扩散模型推理，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，与 [CANONICAL] efficient_generation 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for diffusion transformers, a top priority subtopic, with a novel expert-branch caching method. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion transformer, mixture-of-experts, inference acceleration, caching framework

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers with Mixture-of-Experts (DiT-MoE) improve model capacity under sparse activation, but diffusion inference is still bottlenecked by redundant computation across timesteps. Existing caching methods mainly operate at the token level, which becomes suboptimal in DiT-MoE because each token update is internally decomposed into multiple routed expert branches. Our analysis shows that cross-timestep redundancy in DiT-MoE is better characterized at the expert-branch level than at the whole-token level. Based on this observation, we propose MoECa, a fine-grained caching framework that performs branch-level feature reuse across timesteps. MoECa further introduces expert-aware adaptive control and synchronized cache updates across MoE and attention paths to maintain stable intermediate states. Experiments on multiple DiT-MoE models show that MoECa consistently achieves a better speed-quality trade-off than prior caching methods, with up to 2.83$\times$ inference speedup and minimal quality degradation.

</details>


### [102] [Mean-Field Parallel Decoding for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15805)
*Tamim Zoabi, Ameen Ali, Liran Ringel, Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (88.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的Mean-Field并行解码框架，通过变分松弛和固定点更新来协调离散扩散语言模型中多个位置的并行token提交，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型支持并行token生成，但独立按边际置信度选择token会导致不同位置提交的token之间产生不兼容配置，限制了并行解码的有效性。现有方法缺乏对并行更新之间协调机制的考虑。

Method: 提出一种训练无关的并行解码框架：在每个前向传播中，为每个掩码位置分配一个提交分数（commit score），并利用模型预测分布导出的成对交互来精炼这些分数。通过变分松弛推导出简单的固定点更新公式，在单次前向传播中抑制冲突的并行提交，从而协调多个位置的同步更新。

Result: 在推理和代码生成基准测试上，该方法在生成质量与延迟之间取得了持续改进的权衡，比基线方法在相同延迟下获得更高质量，或在相同质量下实现更低延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架轻量高效，无需辅助模型或重新训练，可直接嵌入现有扩散解码流程，为离散扩散语言模型的低延迟并行解码提供了一种实用的协调机制。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的并行解码框架，通过平均场变分松弛协调离散扩散语言模型中的并行token更新，在保持生成质量的同时加速解码过程。这属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴，该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖扩散加速、少步采样等效率优化方法，与本论文的并行解码加速目标高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for discrete diffusion language models, a key priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's model efficiency and continuous language model interests. Signals: training-free parallel decoding, discrete diffusion acceleration, quality-latency trade-off, variational fixed-point update, reasoning and code benchmarks

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models enable parallel token generation, offering a pathway to low-latency decoding. However, selecting tokens independently by marginal confidence limits effective parallelism: tokens that appear reliable in isolation can form incompatible configurations when several positions are updated at once. We introduce a training-free decoding framework that coordinates these parallel updates. At each forward pass, the method assigns a commit score to each masked position and refines these scores using pairwise interactions derived from the model's predictive distributions. A variational relaxation yields a simple fixed-point update that suppresses conflicting simultaneous commitments within a single forward pass. This mechanism allows the decoder to commit more tokens in parallel while maintaining competitive generation quality. The method is lightweight, requires no auxiliary model or retraining, and drops into existing diffusion decoding pipelines without modification. Experiments on reasoning and code-generation benchmarks show consistent improvements in the quality-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [103] [Variational Test-time Optimization for Diffusion Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15614)
*Hyunsoo Lee, Farrin Marouf Sofian, Kushagra Pandey, Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (60.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从最优控制理论出发，数学推导了扩散同步框架，在测试时通过优化控制变量协调多条扩散轨迹，无需额外训练即可提升多轨迹协作生成的质量，在多个模态的协作生成任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散同步方法严重依赖启发式策略，需要针对特定任务进行定制，缺乏通用性和理论保证。作者希望从最优控制角度为扩散同步提供严格的数学基础，并设计一种无需训练、即插即用的通用框架。

Method: 基于最优控制理论推导扩散同步框架，在采样过程中引入可优化的控制变量，通过变分测试时优化（variational test-time optimization）引导多条扩散轨迹向一致的解收敛，同时保持与预训练扩散先验的接近。该方法完全在测试时运行，无需额外训练。

Result: 在三个代表性的协作生成任务（覆盖多种模态和应用场景）上，该方法相比基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，验证了框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 本文为协作生成建立了基于最优控制的理论基础，提出了一种原则性的扩散同步方法，为将预训练生成模型扩展到新的协作生成场景开辟了道路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于最优控制的变分测试时优化方法，用于扩散同步（diffusion synchronization），在采样过程中优化控制变量以协调多条扩散轨迹。这属于扩散模型采样过程的优化与加速方法，与[生成模型加速与高效化]（efficient_generation）中的扩散加速、少步采样等主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Provides a principled optimal control framework for test-time diffusion synchronization, improving multi-trajectory generation without training. Signals: diffusion synchronization, optimal control, test-time optimization, collaborative generation, no training required

Abstract: Collaborative generation, which coordinates multiple diffusion trajectories to extend the capabilities of pretrained priors, has emerged as a powerful paradigm for extending the applicability of diffusion models. Among existing approaches, diffusion synchronization provides a scenario-agnostic solution by introducing general guidance mechanisms. However, current synchronization approaches rely heavily on heuristics and still require task-specific tailoring, which limits their generalizability and performance. In this work, we mathematically derive a synchronization framework based on optimal control, providing a principled explanation of diffusion synchronization. During sampling, we optimize control variables to guide multiple trajectories toward coherent solutions while remaining close to the underlying diffusion prior. Our method operates entirely at test-time without additional training, thereby enabling broad applicability across diverse generation scenarios when combined with strong pretrained priors. We demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines on three representative collaborative generation tasks, covering a wide range of modalities and applications. Beyond performance gains, our work establishes a novel foundation for collaborative generation, opening a principled path toward extending pretrained generative models to new collaborative generation settings.

</details>


### [104] [Shift-and-Sum Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16131)
*Jaehyeon Moon, Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.97) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉自回归模型(VAR)的后训练量化(PTQ)框架，通过Shift-and-Sum量化方法减少注意力值乘积的重建误差，并设计重采样策略对齐码本条目的采样频率与预测概率，在类条件图像生成、修复、外推和编辑任务上取得了SOTA量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化(PTQ)在深度网络高效部署中至关重要，但VAR模型的PTQ尚未被充分探索。现有PTQ方法应用于VAR时面临两大挑战：(1) 注意力值乘积的重建误差大，尤其在粗尺度上高注意力分数频繁出现；(2) 由于校准数据有限，码本条目的采样频率与预测概率之间存在不一致。

Method: 提出Shift-and-Sum量化方法：通过对值(value)token进行对称移位后分别量化再聚合，减少注意力值乘积的重建误差。同时提出校准数据重采样策略，使码本条目的采样频率与其预测概率对齐，从而缓解有限校准数据带来的分布偏移问题。

Result: 在类条件图像生成、修复、外推和类条件编辑任务上，该方法在多种VAR架构上均取得一致改进，建立了VAR后训练量化的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了VAR模型的PTQ问题，提出的Shift-and-Sum量化和重采样策略有效解决了VAR量化中的两大关键挑战，为视觉自回归生成模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉自回归模型(VAR)的后训练量化(PTQ)方法，属于生成模型的高效部署与量化优化，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化(efficient_generation)"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for autoregressive image generation, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel quantization method. Signals: Autoregressive image generation, Efficient inference, Post-training quantization, Visual autoregressive models, Model compression

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment of deep networks using a small set of data. Its application to visual autoregressive models (VAR), however, remains relatively unexplored. We identify two key challenges for applying PTQ to VAR: (i) large reconstruction errors in attention-value products, especially at coarse scales where high attention scores occur more frequently; and (ii) a discrepancy between the sampling frequencies of codebook entries and their predicted probabilities due to limited calibration data. To address these challenges, we propose a PTQ framework tailored for VAR. First, we introduce a shift-and-sum quantization method that reduces reconstruction errors by aggregating quantized results from symmetrically shifted duplicates of value tokens. Second, we present a resampling strategy for calibration data that aligns sampling frequencies of codebook entries with their predicted probabilities. Experiments on class-conditional image generation, inpainting, outpainting, and class-conditional editing show consistent improvements across VAR architectures, establishing a new state of the art in PTQ for VAR.

</details>


### [105] [teasr: training-efficient any-step diffusion transformer for real-world image super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16188)
*Xiang Gao, Chenxin Zhu, Yushun Fang, Qiang Hu, Xiaoyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (82.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TEASR提出了一种训练高效的任意步扩散框架用于真实图像超分辨率，通过自对抗蒸馏（无需辅助教师模型）和时序感知矫正策略，在单GPU上即可蒸馏20B参数扩散模型，支持从1步到多步的灵活采样，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在真实图像超分辨率中表现优异但迭代采样速度慢。现有一步蒸馏方法需要辅助教师模型，导致训练内存开销大、难以扩展到大规模架构，且固定步数的模型无法在速度与质量之间灵活权衡。

Method: 提出TEASR框架：(1) 自对抗蒸馏——在单个扩散模型内进行自蒸馏，无需辅助教师或判别器；(2) 时序感知矫正策略——稳定不同噪声水平下的单步生成；(3) 双分支扩散Transformer——解耦当前噪声状态与去噪目标的时序条件，提升采样质量。三者结合使得在单GPU上即可蒸馏20B参数扩散模型。

Result: TEASR支持无缝的任意步采样，在多个真实图像超分辨率数据集上一致优于现有最先进方法，且训练效率显著提升（单GPU即可蒸馏20B模型）。

Conclusion: TEASR通过自对抗蒸馏和时序感知矫正，实现了无需辅助教师的任意步扩散超分辨率框架，兼顾了训练效率、采样灵活性和生成质量，为大规模扩散模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出TEASR，一种面向真实图像超分辨率的任意步扩散框架，通过自对抗蒸馏实现单步与多步统一采样，核心贡献在于扩散模型的训练高效化和采样加速，属于[生成模型加速与高效化]（efficient_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion model efficiency for super-resolution, which is tangentially related to your priority on efficient inference but not directly on your core topics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: diffusion transformer, training-efficient distillation, any-step sampling, real-world super-resolution, single GPU distillation

Abstract: Diffusion models excel in Real-World Image Super-Resolution (Real-ISR) due to their powerful generative priors but suffer from slow iterative sampling. Although existing one-step distillation methods accelerate inference, they typically require auxiliary teacher models that inflate training memory and restrict scalability to large-scale architectures. Furthermore, these fixed-step models lack the flexibility to trade off speed for quality. In this paper, we propose TEASR, a training-efficient any-step diffusion framework for Real-ISR that enables both one-step and multi-step restoration within a unified model. Our key idea is to perform self-adversarial distillation within a single diffusion model, eliminating the need for auxiliary teachers or discriminators. Specifically, we propose a timestep-aware rectification strategy that stabilizes one-step generation across noise levels. These two designs further enables the distillation of 20B-parameter diffusion models on a single GPU, significantly improving training efficiency. Moreover, we introduce a dual-branch diffusion transformer with decoupled timestep condition to separate the current noise state and the denoising target to enhance sampling quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TEASR supports seamless any-step sampling and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [106] [Training-free sparse attention based on cumulative energy filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16317)
*Chunlu Li, Yixuan Pan, Bai Du, Zhenyuan Chen, Yanzhao Li, Hui Dong, Hui Wang, Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (86.67) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的稀疏注意力方法，基于累积能量过滤对Diffusion Transformer中的token进行动态阈值筛选，在保持精度的同时大幅提升稀疏度（从61.42%提升至82%），并与Flash Attention深度集成，实现约1.61倍的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中的Diffusion Transformer（DiT）计算开销巨大，稀疏注意力通过只计算重要token来加速，但现有方法（如Top-p和Top-k）无法同时最大化稀疏性和保证精度。本文旨在设计一种动态阈值策略，在维持固定召回率（保证精度）的同时提高稀疏度，从而更高效地加速视频生成。

Method: 将token过滤建模为双目标优化问题（最大化稀疏度、最小化精度损失），提出基于累积能量（cumulative energy）的动态阈值方案：通过维持固定召回率来保证精度，并动态调整阈值以提升稀疏度。该方法与Flash Attention深度集成，无需额外掩码计算开销。

Result: 在Wan 2.2视频生成模型上验证，相比同样集成Flash Attention的BLASST算法，稀疏度从61.42%提升至82%，VBench指标下降不到5%，注意力计算加速约15%，整体计算效率提升1.61倍（比BLASST高1.18倍）。

Conclusion: 本文证明了固定召回率足以保证精度而固定阈值对降低计算成本次优，提出的动态阈值稀疏注意力方法在视频DiT上实现了更优的稀疏-精度权衡，且无需额外训练开销，具有良好的实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于累积能量过滤的动态阈值稀疏注意力机制，用于加速视频扩散Transformer（DiT）的推理。这属于生成模型加速与高效化的范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for diffusion transformers, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel training-free sparse attention method. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion transformer, training-free, sparse attention, video generation acceleration

Abstract: Sparse attention accelerates Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for video generation by computing only the important tokens while skipping the rest. The token selection strategy is key to balancing sparsity and accuracy. We formulate the token filtering process as a dual-goal optimization problem: maximizing sparsity and minimizing accuracy degradation. Existing algorithms cannot fulfill both objectives simultaneously. For example, Top-p only considers the accuracy constraint, while Top-k maintains a fixed computational budget but loosens the accuracy constraint. This paper demonstrates that maintaining a fixed recall rate is sufficient for ensuring accuracy, whereas a fixed threshold is suboptimal for reducing computational cost. Therefore, we propose a dynamic thresholding scheme to improve sparsity while maintaining the same level of accuracy. Furthermore, our algorithm is deeply integrated with Flash Attention (FA), eliminating the need for any additional masking computation overhead. Experimental results on Wan 2.2 validate that, compared to the BLASST algorithm which is also integrated with FA, our dynamic thresholding strategy enhances sparsity from 61.42\% to 82\% with a VBench metric drop of less than 5\%. This results in an approximate 15\% in attention computation and a $1.61\times$ increase in computational efficiency, which is 1.18x higher than that of BLASST.

</details>


### [107] [Local-GS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting via Tile-Local Warp Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16566)
*Yang Luo, Yan Gong, Yongsheng Gao, Jie Zhao, Xinyu Zhang, Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (54.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Local-GS 提出了一种基于 tile-local warp coherence 的 3DGS 渲染加速范式，通过 hoisting、culling 和 blending 三个阶段优化 GPU 利用率，在多个数据集上实现最高 7.76 倍加速且不损失渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 中高斯原语的分布不规则，导致 GPU warp 发散和冗余计算，渲染效率低下。现有方法多从场景几何角度优化，忽略了 SIMT 执行边界对性能的影响。

Method: 提出 warp-coherent 渲染范式，包含三个阶段：(1) hoisting 阶段，在 tile 级别预计算共享参数；(2) culling 阶段，丢弃无贡献的 warp；(3) blending 阶段，用统一指令流替代逐像素分支。该方法作为即插即用优化模块，可集成到现有 3DGS 基线中。

Result: 在多个数据集上，Local-GS 在不降低渲染质量的前提下显著提升效率，在 Deep Blending 场景上实现 7.76 倍加速，且对所有测试基线均有额外性能提升。

Conclusion: Local-GS 通过对齐高斯原语与 SIMT 执行边界，有效解决了 3DGS 渲染中的 warp 发散问题，是一种高效且通用的 3DGS 加速方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Local-GS，通过 tile-local warp coherence 优化 3DGS 渲染效率，属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴，该 canonical subtopic 涵盖 3DGS 渲染加速等效率优化方法。

Importance reason: Strong engineering optimization for 3DGS rendering but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal language models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS acceleration, warp coherence, GPU utilization, rendering speedup, plug-and-play optimization

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has significantly advanced real-time novel view synthesis by representing scenes as dense collections of anisotropic 3D Gaussian primitives. However, the irregular spatial distribution of Gaussians often leads to poor GPU utilization, as warp divergence and redundant computation degrade rendering performance. To address this, we present Local-GS, a warp-coherent rendering paradigm that, organizes Gaussian primitives with respect to SIMT (Single Instruction, Multiple Threads) execution boundaries rather than scene geometry. Specifically, we propose three warp-coherent stages: a hoisting stage that precomputes shared parameters at tile level, a culling stage that discards warps with no contribution, and a blending stage that replaces per-pixel branching with a uniform instruction stream. Across extensive benchmarks on multiple datasets, Local-GS improves efficiency without compromising quality. As a plug-and-play optimization, it provides additional performance gains to all tested baselines, culminating in a $7.76\times$ speedup on Deep Blending scenes.

</details>


### [108] [Diffusion Flow Matching: Dimension-Improved KL Bounds and Wasserstein Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16610)
*Marta Gentiloni Silveri, Giovanni Conforti, Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对基于布朗运动的 Diffusion Flow Matching (DFM) 提供了改进的 KL 散度收敛界（维度依赖优于先前工作）以及 2-Wasserstein 距离下的收敛保证，在最小假设下达到了当前最优的维度缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFM 是一种新兴的生成建模框架，但其理论收敛性质（尤其是离散化误差）尚未被充分理解。现有理论工作在维度依赖上不够理想，需要更紧的收敛界来指导实践。

Method: 在有限矩条件和温和的分数可积性假设下，推导了 KL 散度的收敛界，改进了维度依赖关系。进一步在额外的一阶分数可积性和弱对数凹条件下，获得了 2-Wasserstein 距离下的收敛保证。

Result: 获得了当前已知最优的 KL 散度维度缩放（在最小假设下），并首次在弱对数凹条件下给出了与 KL 情况一致的 Wasserstein 距离收敛界。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了 DFM 的理论理解，证明了在温和条件下 DFM 具有优越的维度缩放性质，为流匹配生成模型的实践提供了理论支撑。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Diffusion Flow Matching (DFM) 的收敛理论，分析离散化误差在 KL 散度和 Wasserstein 距离下的界。这属于生成模型（扩散/流匹配）的理论收敛性分析，与生成模型加速/高效化（少步采样、离散化误差分析）紧密相关，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"。

Importance reason: Provides state-of-the-art theoretical convergence bounds for diffusion flow matching, which is a core technique for continuous multimodal models and generation acceleration. Signals: Improved KL dimension scaling, 2-Wasserstein guarantees, minimal assumptions, core theory for flow matching, relevant to continuous language/multimodal models

Abstract: Diffusion Flow Matching (DFM) has recently emerged as a versatile framework for generative modeling, yet its theoretical convergence properties remain only partially understood. In this work, we provide refined and novel convergence guarantees for Brownian motion based DFMs, focusing on the discretization error. Our analysis is conducted under the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and the 2-Wasserstein distance. Under finite-moment conditions and a mild score integrability assumption, we derive KL convergence bounds with improved dimensional dependence compared to prior work, achieving, up to our knowledge, state-of-the-art scaling under minimal conditions. We further extend the analysis to the 2-Wasserstein distance: under an additional first-order score integrability assumption and a weak log-concavity condition, we obtain convergence guarantees with dimensional dependence consistent with the KL case.

</details>

### [109] [TuneJury: An Open Metric for Improving Music Generation Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17006)
*Yonghyun Kim, Junwon Lee, Haiwen Xia, Yinghao Ma, Junghyun Koo, Koichi Saito, Yuki Mitsufuji, Chris Donahue*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.LG, eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (49.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TuneJury 是一个开源的、实例级别的成对奖励模型，用于文本到音乐生成中的偏好对齐。它基于多种公开的人类偏好数据训练，能够预测音乐偏好分数，并支持推理时最佳-of-N 选择、潜空间优化和后训练迭代等下游应用。同时提出锚定校准方法，以高效方式将奖励模型适配到新发布的生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐生成模型快速发展，但缺乏开放、可复现的偏好对齐奖励模型。现有方法依赖闭源或领域特定的奖励模型，限制了音乐生成对齐研究的可复现性和泛化能力。

Method: 收集并整合多种公开的人类偏好数据（竞技场式投票、度量对齐偏好对、众包成对比较、专家美学评分），训练一个基于文本提示和音频片段的成对奖励模型 TuneJury。提出锚定校准（anchor calibration），一种后验的、逐系统的 Bradley-Terry 校准方法，以高效适配新发布的生成器。

Result: TuneJury 在保留测试集上预测分数边际校准良好，在分布外基准上保持竞争力。锚定校准以远低于从头训练的数据效率恢复了一致性。在最佳-of-N 选择、DITTO 风格潜空间优化和专家迭代后训练三个下游应用中均带来一致的奖励轴增益。

Conclusion: TuneJury 提供了一个开放、有效的音乐生成偏好对齐工具，锚定校准进一步提升了其对新生成器的适配效率，为音乐生成领域的对齐研究奠定了可复现的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出文本到音乐生成的偏好对齐奖励模型 TuneJury，属于生成模型的对齐与奖励建模范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music generation alignment, which is outside your priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: music generation, preference alignment, reward model, open-source, anchor calibration

Abstract: We introduce TuneJury, an open, instance-level pairwise reward model for text-to-music that predicts a music preference score from a text prompt and an audio clip. The released checkpoint is trained on publicly available human-preference labels covering arena-style (A vs. B) votes, metric-alignment preference pairs, crowdsourced pairwise comparisons, and expert aesthetic ratings. The predicted score margin between two clips is well calibrated on our held-out test split, supporting data filtering via a simple score threshold. TuneJury generalizes to both held-out test pairs and out-of-distribution benchmarks, remaining competitive with prior baselines on the latter. For generators released after training, we introduce anchor calibration, a post-hoc, per-system Bradley-Terry calibration that recovers agreement at substantially better data efficiency than from-scratch retraining. The same frozen reward drives consistent reward-axis gains across three downstream applications: inference-time best-of-N selection, DITTO-style latent optimization, and expert-iteration post-training. TuneJury is available at .

</details>


### [110] [FairGen: Preference-Aligned Diffusion for Demographically Equitable Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14727)
*Zhimin Li, Ruichen Zhang, Zhen Tan, Howard J Aizenstein, Jingtong Hu, Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (44.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FairGen 是一个公平性感知的扩散框架，通过将医生偏好嵌入生成过程，合成人口统计学平衡的医学图像，在皮肤、胸部X光和脑MRI三个任务上分别实现95.9%、80.0%和35.2%的公平性提升，同时保持诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据存在严重的人口统计学不平衡（如不同肤色、性别、年龄的病例分布不均），且疾病在不同人群中的表现特征不同，导致某些表型自然稀缺。直接训练AI模型会放大诊断偏差，加剧医疗不平等。现有生成方法缺乏对公平性的显式控制。

Method: FairGen 基于扩散模型，引入医生对齐的偏好嵌入到生成过程中。具体地，通过偏好优化（类似RLHF/DPO范式）将临床医生的公平性偏好注入扩散模型的采样过程，在保持病理相关视觉特征的同时，改善生成样本在人口统计学子群中的覆盖均衡性。

Result: 在皮肤影像、胸部X光和脑MRI三个基准任务上，FairGen 分别实现95.9%、80.0%和35.2%的公平性提升。诊断准确性与在原始临床数据上训练的模型相当。临床专家评审和外部独立数据集验证表明，这些增益超越了标准保真度指标，且不局限于原始分布内数据集。

Conclusion: FairGen 证明了通过偏好对齐的扩散生成可以有效缓解医学影像中的群体偏差，在提升公平性的同时不牺牲诊断性能，为公平医疗AI提供了一条可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FairGen，一个将医生偏好嵌入扩散模型以实现人口统计学公平的医学图像合成框架，核心贡献在于生成模型的对齐（偏好对齐与公平性对齐），与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image fairness, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: Medical image synthesis, fairness-aware diffusion, demographic balance, diagnostic accuracy, physician-aligned preferences

Abstract: Medical imaging is central to modern diagnostics, and artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly used to support image-based analysis by improving efficiency, accuracy, and access to care. However, inequities in healthcare access and differential disease prevalence create severe demographic imbalances in clinical image data. Such imbalances are compounded by the fact that diseases can manifest with distinct features across demographic groups, rendering certain phenotypic presentations naturally rare. AI models trained on such imbalanced data risk perpetuating diagnostic bias and widening healthcare disparities. Here we introduce FairGen, a fairness-aware diffusion framework that synthesizes demographically balanced medical images while preserving pathology-relevant visual features. By embedding physician-aligned preferences into the generation process, FairGen improves subgroup coverage during synthesis and downstream classification. Applied to dermatology, radiology, and neuroimaging benchmark tasks, FairGen achieves fairness improvements of 95.9% for skin images, 80.0% for chest radiography, and 35.2% for brain MRI, while maintaining competitive diagnostic accuracy relative to models trained on original clinical data. Clinician-facing expert review and external validation on independent cohorts further support that these gains extend beyond standard fidelity metrics and are not confined to the original in-distribution datasets.

</details>


### [111] [Divide-and-Denoise: A Game-Theoretic Method for Fairly Composing Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14756)
*Abhi Gupta, Polina Barabanshchikova, Vikas Garg, Samuel Kaski, Tommi Jaakkola*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Divide-and-Denoise，一种基于博弈论公平分配的多扩散模型组合采样方法。通过将噪声样本划分为不同区域并公平分配给各模型负责去噪，实现模型间的有效协作，在条件图像生成中优于基线方法，解决了物体缺失和属性不匹配等常见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型数量丰富，组合多个模型可以发挥各自专长，但现有组合方法存在一个模型主导或模型间分歧的问题，导致生成质量下降。需要一种公平且高效的机制来协调多个扩散模型在采样过程中的分工。

Method: 提出 Divide-and-Denoise 方法，核心是"分配（allocation）"概念，定义每个模型对噪声样本各区域的责任。在每个时间步：(i) 通过求解公平分配博弈更新分配，在公平约束下最大化总效用将样本划分为不同区域；(ii) 根据分配引导各模型在其指定区域内去噪。去噪过程与分配过程协同演化。

Result: 在条件图像生成任务上，使用 GenEval 等多个质量指标评估，Divide-and-Denoise 优于基线方法，有效解决了物体缺失和属性不匹配等常见组合失败问题。实验表明该方法能充分利用每个模型的专长而不忽视任何模型。

Conclusion: Divide-and-Denoise 通过博弈论公平分配机制实现了多个预训练扩散模型的有效组合，在保持各模型专长的同时解决了模型间冲突问题，为扩散模型组合提供了一种新的公平高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Divide-and-Denoise 方法，通过博弈论公平分配机制协调多个预训练扩散模型在采样过程中的协作，解决模型主导和分歧问题。这属于多模型组合生成中的对齐与可控性优化，与 [CANONICAL] 生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）主题高度相关，该主题涵盖 guidance、可控性、偏好优化等方向。

Importance reason: Novel game-theoretic composition for diffusion models, but tangential to user's priority on continuous/autoregressive models and world models. Signals: diffusion model composition, fair division game, conditional image generation, GenEval benchmark, object missing resolution

Abstract: The abundance of pre-trained diffusion models provides an opportunity for composition. Combining several models, however, runs the risk of one model dominating or models disagreeing with each other. Here, we propose Divide-and-Denoise, a method for coordinating multiple pre-trained diffusion models during sampling. Much like managing a specialized workforce, our method creates a fair but efficient division of labor across models. Central to our method is the notion of an allocation which defines the responsibility of each model to every region of the noisy sample. At every timestep, we then denoise by (i) updating the allocation by solving a fair division game, where we divide the sample into regions that maximize total utility under fairness constraints, and (ii) aligning the models with this allocation, where we guide each model to denoise within its assigned region. This leads to a new composite denoising process that evolves in tandem with a division process. We evaluate Divide-and-Denoise on conditional image generation. Across several quality metrics, including the GenEval benchmark, our method outperforms baselines and resolves common failures including missing objects and mismatched attributes. Experiments show that Divide-and-Denoise utilizes each model's expertise without neglecting any other model.

</details>


### [112] [DifFRACT: Diffusion Feature Reconstruction and Attribution for Circuit Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15796)
*Artyom Mazur, Nina Konovalova, Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (88.45) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出DifFRACT方法，将transcoder电路追踪扩展到多模态扩散Transformer（FLUX.1[schnell]），通过训练时间步条件transcoder近似MLP子层，实现精确的特征到特征归因，揭示属性绑定和跨流语义传播机制，并提供比SAE更精确的电路引导干预控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer（如FLUX）的机制可解释性工具匮乏，现有方法（注意力图、稀疏自编码器）无法揭示非线性MLP层中特征的变换与组合过程，缺乏对语义信息在去噪步骤间传播以及文本-图像表征交互的理解。

Method: 训练时间步条件transcoder来近似FLUX.1[schnell]中MLP子层的输入-输出行为，用transcoder替换MLP并线性化剩余计算，实现精确的特征到特征归因，恢复紧凑可解释的电路。

Result: Transcoder在稀疏性-忠实性权衡上匹配或略优于SAE；揭示的电路阐明了属性绑定和跨流语义传播机制，并为系统性生成错误提供了因果解释；电路引导干预比标准SAE干预更精确有效。

Conclusion: 基于transcoder的电路分析对最先进的扩散Transformer是可行的，为理解和控制多模态生成模型提供了强大框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散Transformer（FLUX）的机制可解释性，通过transcoder进行电路追踪和因果分析，最终实现电路引导的干预控制（circuit-guided interventions）。这属于生成模型的可控性与对齐范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的可控性（guidance/controllability）方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances mechanistic interpretability for multimodal diffusion transformers, a key direction for controllable generation and world model understanding. Signals: multimodal diffusion transformer, circuit tracing, transcoder, interpretability, FLUX.1

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks to explain neural network behavior by decomposing model computations into interpretable features and circuits. While transcoder-based circuit tracing has recently enabled detailed causal analyses of large language models, multimodal diffusion transformers for image generation remain comparatively opaque. We still lack tools for understanding how semantic information propagates across denoising steps and how text and image representations interact within double-stream MM-DiT architectures. Existing methods provide only partial insight: attention maps expose a limited view of token interactions, while sparse autoencoders can discover interpretable features but do not directly reveal how these features are transformed and composed through nonlinear MLP layers. In this work, we extend transcoder-based circuit tracing to multimodal diffusion transformers. We train timestep-conditioned transcoders that faithfully approximate the input-output behavior of MLP sublayers in FLUX.1[schnell]. By replacing MLPs with transcoders and linearizing the remaining computation, we obtain exact feature-to-feature attribution and recover compact, interpretable circuits. Empirically, our transcoders match or slightly outperform sparse autoencoders on the sparsity-faithfulness tradeoff. The resulting circuits reveal mechanisms underlying attribute binding and cross-stream semantic propagation, and provide causal explanations for systematic generation errors. Moreover, circuit-guided interventions are substantially more precise and effective than standard SAE-based steering. Our results demonstrate that transcoder-based circuit analysis is feasible for state-of-the-art diffusion transformers and provides a powerful framework for understanding and controlling multimodal generative models. The code is available at

</details>


### [113] [SACE: Concept Erasure at the Semantic Singularity in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15819)
*Siya Yang, Nanxiang Jiang, Zhaoxin Fan, Yunfeng Diao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (81.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SACE框架，首次针对视觉自回归（VAR）模型实现概念擦除。基于"语义奇点公理"（Scale-0锁定目标语义），结合熵正则化擦除目标与恢复性保留损失，在第一个尺度上精确干预，避免语义崩溃和视觉伪影，实现高效安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除技术主要针对扩散模型的同质化去噪步骤设计，直接应用于VAR模型会导致灾难性的语义崩溃和视觉伪影。VAR模型作为新兴的高保真文生图范式，其安全对齐问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出语义奇点公理（Semantic Singularity Axiom），认为提示中的目标语义概念在Scale-0（第一个尺度）被锁定。通过增量语义显著性分析（ISSA）验证该公理。基于此，提出尺度感知的概念擦除框架SACE：1）严格将干预限制在第一个尺度；2）熵正则化擦除目标防止高熵采样退化；3）恢复性保留损失保护纠缠的良性先验完整性。

Result: 实验表明SACE在多个领域实现了精准的概念擦除，训练开销极小，有效解决了VAR架构中的安全漏洞，避免了语义崩溃和视觉伪影。

Conclusion: 本文首次为VAR模型提供了有效的概念擦除方案，验证了语义奇点公理的正确性，为新兴自回归视觉生成架构的安全对齐开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文关注VAR模型的安全对齐与概念擦除（concept erasure），属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel safety alignment for VAR models, but tangential to user's core priority of continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: First concept erasure for VAR, Semantic Singularity Axiom, ISSA analysis, entropy-regularized erasure, restorative preservation loss

Abstract: The rapid progress of visual autoregressive (VAR) models has unlocked a transformative frontier for high-fidelity text-to-image synthesis, while heightening concerns over the safety alignment of generated content. Naive application of existing erasure techniques to VAR models causes catastrophic semantic collapse and visual artifacts, since they are predominantly designed for the homogeneous denoising steps of diffusion models. To address this foundational challenge, we first propose the Semantic Singularity Axiom, which posits that any target semantic concept embedded within a prompt is definitively locked at Scale-0. Then rigorously validate this axiom through our proposed Incremental Semantic Saliency Analysis (ISSA),which also enable the community to transparently inspect the coarse-to-fine semantic injection process. Guided by this insight, we introduce the first scale-aware concept erasure framework (SACE) for VAR models. By strictly confining interventions to the first scale, our approach couples an Entropy-Regularized Erasure Objective to prevent high-entropy sampling degeneration, alongside a restorative preservation loss to safely anchor the integrity of entangled benign priors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves surgical concept erasure performance across various domains with minimal training overhead, timely and elegently resolute the critical safety vulnerabilities inherent in emerging VAR architectures. Code is available at: }{ .

</details>


### [114] [Decision-Weighted Flow Matching for Contextual Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16790)
*Jize Xie, Haomiao Wu, Qiang Chen, Xiu Su, Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (71.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM)，一种将条件生成模型（flow matching）与下游随机优化目标对齐的训练框架。通过决策敏感的重加权 velocity 回归目标，DW-FM 在合成投资组合、半真实金融和交通 CVaR 优化任务上显著降低了决策遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型常用于随机优化的场景生成，但标准训练目标（如均匀分布拟合）与下游决策质量之间存在目标不匹配（objective mismatch）：统计上常见区域的误差对决策遗憾影响小，而决策敏感区域的误差可能导致次优行动。现有方法缺乏将生成模型训练直接对齐下游决策遗憾的框架。

Method: 提出 Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM)，在标准 flow matching 的 velocity 回归目标中引入决策敏感的重加权。通过损失诱导的决策差异（loss-induced decision discrepancy）和伴随传输（adjoint transport）论证，将下游遗憾与路径级 velocity 不匹配联系起来，推导出遗憾对齐的代理目标及实用的端点加权目标，并给出遗憾保证。

Result: 在三个 CVaR 驱动的上下文随机优化基准（合成投资组合、半真实金融、交通 CVaR）上，DW-FM 相比标准 flow matching 和基线方法显著降低了下游决策遗憾。

Conclusion: DW-FM 提供了一种简单有效的框架，将生成模型训练与下游决策目标对齐，弥合了生成建模与随机优化之间的目标不匹配，在决策敏感场景中具有广泛适用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM)，通过决策敏感的重加权将 flow matching 生成模型与下游优化目标对齐，属于生成模型对齐（alignment）范畴，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on decision-aligned flow matching for stochastic optimization, which is tangential to the user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Decision-aligned flow matching, contextual stochastic optimization, CVaR regret reduction, not aligned with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Conditional generative models are increasingly used as scenario generators for stochastic optimization, but standard training objectives emphasize uniform distributional fit rather than the downstream decisions induced by generated scenarios. This creates an objective mismatch: errors in statistically common regions may have little effect on decision regret, whereas errors in decision-sensitive regions can substantially change the optimal action. We propose Decision-Weighted Flow Matching (DW-FM), a regret-aligned training framework that preserves the simplicity of standard flow matching while reweighting its velocity-regression objective using decision-sensitive endpoint information. Theoretically, we connect downstream regret to pathwise velocity mismatch through a loss-induced decision discrepancy and an adjoint transport argument, yielding an ideal regret-aligned surrogate and practical endpoint-weighted objectives with regret guarantees. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of DW-FM on three CVaR-based contextual stochastic optimization benchmarks spanning synthetic portfolio, semi-real financial, and traffic-CVaR tasks, where DW-FM improves downstream regret over standard baselines.

</details>

### [115] [Agentic Retrieval and Reinforcement Learned Equation Chains: A Controlled Generation Framework for Complex and Novel Physics Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15591)
*Tirthankar Mittra*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (41.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ARVRE框架，结合离线时序差分学习构建有效物理方程链，并利用智能体检索增强生成（RAG）选择主题概念，再由LLM将方程链转化为自然语言物理问题，实现复杂、新颖且可解的物理文字题受控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理文字题生成方法多从数学文字题生成改编而来，生成的问题常存在歧义、不可解、结构简单、语言多样性不足等问题。需要一种能保证数学正确性同时提升问题复杂度和语言多样性的可控生成方法。

Method: ARVRE两阶段框架：第一阶段使用离线时序差分学习构建有效的物理方程链，同时通过智能体RAG动态选择主题相关概念和词汇，实现对问题结构和难度的显式控制；第二阶段由LLM将方程链和检索到的概念转化为自然语言物理问题。

Result: 人工和自动评测表明，ARVRE生成的物理文字题在复杂度、新颖性和可解性方面均优于现有方法，能够生成数学正确且语言丰富多样的高质量物理问题。

Conclusion: 结合强化学习、检索增强生成和LLM的方法能够可靠地生成教育物理内容，为教育内容生成领域提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ARVRE框架用于生成物理文字题（PWP），属于教育内容生成任务。虽然论文主要聚焦于物理问题生成而非图像/视频生成，但其核心是受控文本生成（controlled generation），且论文中明确进行了生成质量评测（复杂度、新颖性、可解性等）。最接近的现有子主题是"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark），因为论文重点之一是评估生成问题的质量，且该子主题涵盖生成模型的评测方法。此外，论文也涉及生成模型对齐中的RL/偏好优化思想，但整体更偏向生成与评测。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on educational physics word problem generation, which is far outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Physics word problems, educational content generation, agentic retrieval, reinforcement learning, equation chains

Abstract: Generating high-quality Physics Word Problems (PWPs) that are novel, complex, and solvable remains a challenging and underexplored problem in educational content generation. Existing approaches, many adapted from Math Word Problem (MWP) generation, often produce ambiguous, unsolvable, or structurally simple questions with limited linguistic diversity. We introduce ARVRE (Agentic Retrieval Value Reinforced Equation-chain), a two-stage framework for generating diverse and mathematically valid PWPs. In the first stage, a form of offline temporal-difference learning is used to construct valid chains of physics equations, while an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework dynamically selects topic-specific concepts and vocabulary. This design enables explicit control over problem structure and difficulty. In the second stage, a Large Language Model (LLM) converts the equation chain and retrieved concepts into a natural-language physics question. By grounding generation in valid equation chains, our method preserves mathematical correctness while promoting linguistic diversity and contextual richness. Human and automated evaluations demonstrate that ARVRE generates PWPs that are more complex, novel, and solvable than those produced by existing approaches. These results highlight the potential of combining reinforcement learning, retrieval, and LLMs for reliable generation of educational physics content.

</details>


### [116] [Auditing Reward Hackability in Code RL Training Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16062)
*Shreshth Rajan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (75.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了代码RL训练环境（SWE-bench Verified和R2E-Gym）中测试套件的"可被hack"程度，发现28.5%的SWE-bench任务和25%的R2E-Gym任务存在弱测试套件，使得错误补丁能通过验证。元分析显示模型在可hack任务上的Pass@1比稳健任务高14.14个百分点。作者提出了一种基于LLM裁判+Docker金标准门控的测试加固流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码RL训练环境依赖测试套件作为奖励信号，但弱测试套件会导致奖励被hack（即错误代码通过测试），从而误导强化学习训练。现有工作缺乏对代码RL环境中测试套件漏洞率的系统量化评估。

Method: 1) 在SWE-bench Verified的49个任务和R2E-Gym的20个任务上，使用Docker验证的"错误补丁"测试弱测试套件的通过率。2) 对134个前沿模型在SWE-bench上的提交进行随机效应元分析，比较可hack任务与稳健任务上的Pass@1差异。3) 提出测试加固流程：LLM生成测试用例，经Docker金标准门控（检查测试是否在正确补丁上失败）过滤，配合多样性偏置重试。

Result: SWE-bench Verified中28.5%的任务存在弱测试套件；R2E-Gym中25.0%的任务可被单次hack。元分析显示可hack任务上Pass@1高出14.14个百分点（p<10^-6）。加固流程中LLM生成的测试有61.9%被金标准门控标记为缺陷，最终9/11的任务收敛到加固后的测试套件。

Conclusion: 代码RL训练环境中的测试套件存在严重的可hack问题，显著影响模型评估和训练信号的质量。提出的LLM裁判+金标准门控加固流程能有效修复大部分弱测试，为构建更可靠的代码RL训练环境提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文审计代码RL训练环境（SWE-bench、R2E-Gym）中测试套件的漏洞率，属于对生成/代码生成模型评测基准的可靠性分析，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"。

Importance reason: Focuses on code RL environment auditing, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Code RL, test suite weakness, reward hacking, SWE-bench, LLM judge

Abstract: We measure the rate at which code RL environments accept incorrect solutions as correct. On a 49-task sample of SWE-bench Verified, 28.5% of tasks have test suites weak enough that a Docker-verified incorrect patch passes them. On 20 R2E-Gym tasks across 6 repositories, the same pipeline at single-shot exploit generation yields 25.0%. A random-effects meta-analysis over 134 frontier model submissions to SWE-bench Verified finds, within the same human-rated difficulty stratum, model Pass@1 is +14.14 percentage points higher on flagged-hackable tasks than on robust ones (95% CI [+11.80, +16.48]; one-sided p < 10^-6; I^2 = 0%; 123 of 134 models positive). We then describe a procedure for hardening the broken tasks. An inline LLM judge with a Docker gold-sanity gate runs each generated test against the gold solution before the judge is consulted. On the 11 broken tasks in the audit, the gate flags 65 of 105 decisive LLM-generated tests as failing on the gold patch itself, a 61.9% per-augmentation defect rate the LLM judge alone misses. With diversity-biased retry, the loop converges 9 of 11 tasks to a gated upgrade.

</details>


### [117] [An Extensive Benchmark for Single-round and Multi-round Instruction-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15570)
*Yiwei Ma, Ke Ye, Weihuang Lin, Jiayi Ji, Xiaoshuai Sun, Tat-Seng Chua, Rongrong Ji*
