TL;DR: 本文提出了 I2EBench2.0，一个面向单轮和多轮指令式图像编辑（IIE）模型的全面评测基准，包含16个单轮和7个多轮评估维度，通过大规模用户研究确保与人类判断对齐，并对8个现有IIE模型进行了系统评测与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式图像编辑（IIE）领域发展迅速，但缺乏一个能够精确衡量编辑质量并提供有价值基准的稳健评估框架，现有评估方法难以应对指令的复杂性和编辑的多样性。

Method: 构建 I2EBench2.0 基准，同时支持单轮和多轮指令编辑评估；设计16个单轮评估维度（涵盖高层语义和低层像素质量）和7个多轮评估维度（关注编辑一致性和累积效果）；对每个维度进行大规模用户研究以对齐人类判断；在8个最新IIE模型上进行系统测试和对比分析。

Result: 在8个IIE模型上进行了全面评测，通过16个单轮和7个多轮维度揭示了各模型的优缺点，提供了指导未来研究方向的关键洞察。

Conclusion: I2EBench2.0 为IIE领域提供了一个全面、多维度、与人类判断对齐的评估框架，能够有效指导未来指令式图像编辑模型的研究与改进。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向指令式图像编辑（IIE）的全面评测基准 I2EBench2.0，涵盖单轮和多轮编辑评估，包含16个单轮和7个多轮评估维度，并进行了人类对齐研究。这完全符合 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）的定义。

Importance reason: Provides a comprehensive benchmark for instruction-based image editing, but the subtopic is not a top priority for the user. Signals: Benchmark for image editing, Single-round and multi-round evaluation, User study alignment, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: In recent years, there have been notable advancements in the area of instruction-based image editing (IIE), which focuses on the automatic alteration of input images using a model. Nevertheless, assessing the effectiveness of these editing models poses a considerable challenge due to the intricate nature of instructions and the wide variety of edits. To tackle this problem, one urgent task in this domain is the development of a robust evaluation framework that can precisely gauge the quality of editing outcomes and offer valuable benchmarks to guide future improvements. To address this challenge, we present a comprehensive evaluation benchmark named I2EBench2.0, designed for single-round and multi-round assessment of IIE models. I2EBench2.0 has four key features: 1) Evaluation Across Single and Multi-rounds: I2EBench2.0 simultaneously evaluates both single-round and multi-round instruction-based edits, assessing the precision and consistency of the edits. 2) Extensive Evaluation Criteria: I2EBench2.0 encompasses a broad range of criteria, evaluating both high-level and low-level aspects of each IIE model. Specifically, it incorporates 16 dimensions for single-round evaluations and 7 for multi-round evaluations. 3) Alignment with Human Judgment: To ensure our benchmark aligns with human evaluation, we conducted a comprehensive user study for each criterion. 4) Research-driven Insights: By analyzing the strengths and weaknesses of current IIE models across all 16 single-round and 7 multi-round dimensions, we provide critical insights aimed at directing future research in this area. We tested eight recently developed IIE models using I2EBench2.0 and derived academic insights through meticulous comparison and analysis. The related code, dataset, and images generated by all IIE models are available on GitHub: .

</details>


### [118] [XPASS-Vis: A Dataset for Cross-Domain Personalized Image Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15629)
*Takato Hayashi, Hiroaki Takahara, Candy Olivia Mawalim, Hiromi Narimatsu, Akisato Kimura, Shiro Kumano, Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 XPASS-Vis，首个专门用于跨域个性化图像美学评估（PIAA）的数据集，包含来自艺术、时尚和风景三个视觉领域的 6,526 张图像，由 129 名标注者提供 87,836 条交互记录。论文还建立了无监督域适应（UDA）基线，实验表明最佳方法在完全无监督设置下可恢复约 60% 的有监督上限性能，证明个性化美学偏好在一定程度上可跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PIAA 数据集和方法大多局限于单一视觉领域，或每个领域内每个标注者的样本太少，无法支持跨域个性化。然而美学偏好既高度个性化又在不同视觉域间存在一定一致性，跨域泛化问题尚未被探索。

Method: 构建 XPASS-Vis 数据集：从艺术、时尚、风景三个领域收集 6,526 张图像，由 129 名标注者每人每域标注 200+ 张图像，包含整体美学评分和九种美学情感评分。在此基础上，采用无监督域适应（UDA）方法建立基线，将源域标注模型迁移到无标签目标域。

Result: 最佳 UDA 方法在完全无监督设置下 Spearman's ρ = .28，恢复约 60% 的有监督上限性能。但仍有显著差距，表明需要 PIAA 特定的适应策略。

Conclusion: XPASS-Vis 为跨域 PIAA 研究提供了首个专用数据集和基线，证明个性化美学偏好在一定程度上可跨视觉域迁移，但仍有较大改进空间，需要开发 PIAA 专用的域适应策略。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向跨域个性化图像美学评估的新数据集 XPASS-Vis，属于生成模型评测与数据集范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Dataset paper on aesthetic assessment lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Cross-domain PIAA dataset, aesthetic preference transfer, UDA baseline, no continuous token or video generation focus, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Personalized image aesthetic assessment (PIAA) seeks to model, at the individual level, the subjective nature of aesthetic judgments toward artworks and photographs. Aesthetic preference is known to be both deeply personal and partially consistent across visual domains. Yet existing PIAA datasets and methods are largely confined to a single domain, or provide too few samples per annotator within each domain to enable personalization across domains. Consequently, the cross-domain generalization of personalized aesthetic preferences remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce XPASS-Vis, the first dataset explicitly designed for cross-domain PIAA. XPASS-Vis comprises 6,526 stimuli from three visual domains -- art, fashion, and landscape -- rated by 129 annotators, yielding 87,836 user-stimulus interactions, each annotated with an overall aesthetic score and nine aesthetic-emotion ratings. Notably, each annotator rated more than 200 stimuli per domain, providing sufficient per-domain coverage to support personalization both within and across domains. Moreover, we establish baseline models for cross-domain PIAA under unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA), where a model trained on a labeled source domain is transferred to an unlabeled target domain. A systematic evaluation of representative UDA approaches shows that the best-performing method recovers approximately 60\% (Spearman's $\rho$ = .28) of the supervised upper bound under a fully unsupervised setting. This provides encouraging evidence that personalized aesthetic preferences are, to a meaningful extent, transferable across visual domains. At the same time, a substantial gap remains, highlighting the need for PIAA-specific adaptation strategies. XPASS-Vis and the accompanying baselines provide a foundation for future research on cross-domain PIAA. All datasets and code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [119] [CogCanvas: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Subject Reference-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15867)
*Long-Bao Nguyen, Quang-Khai Tran, Tam V. Nguyen, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CogCanvas是一个多主体参考图像生成的评测基准，包含1,952张参考图像、1,361个组合提示，覆盖100个名人身份、115个物体/时尚物品和29个真实背景场景。它支持三个任务（多主体参考图像生成、文生图组合生成、参考检索），并引入BG-Sim和Attr-VQA两个新指标。对五种SOTA方法的评测显示，当群体规模从2人增加到5人时，所有模型性能显著下降，超过3人时物体/时尚绑定几乎完全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估单一方面（如单主体保持或背景一致性），缺乏同时评估多身份保持、人物-物体交互、背景绑定和空间合理性的联合基准。多主体参考图像生成这一实际需求场景缺乏系统评测。

Method: 构建包含100个名人身份、115个物体/时尚物品和29个真实背景场景的1,952张参考图像数据集，通过DINOv2去重、两阶段美学过滤和结构化交互/位置图自动推导，生成1,361个组合提示。提出六轴统一评测协议，引入BG-Sim（基于DINOv3特征相似度评估背景保真度）和Attr-VQA（使用多模态LLM验证属性绑定和人物交互）两个新指标。

Result: 对五种SOTA方法（包括Stable Diffusion、IP-Adapter等）的评测表明：所有模型在群体规模从2人增加到5人时性能显著下降；超过3人时物体/时尚绑定几乎完全失败；背景保真度和空间合理性也随人数增加而退化。

Conclusion: CogCanvas揭示了当前扩散模型在多主体参考图像生成任务中的严重局限性，特别是在多身份保持和物体绑定方面。该基准为未来研究提供了系统评测框架，有助于推动该方向的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多主体参考图像生成的新评测基准CogCanvas，包含数据集、评测协议和新指标，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向。该论文也涉及多模态理解（使用MLLM进行属性验证），因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Benchmark for multi-subject reference generation, but not directly aligned with your priority subtopics. Signals: multi-subject reference generation, benchmark, diffusion models, attribute binding, group size scaling

Abstract: Multi-subject reference-based image generation requires jointly preserving multiple human identities, binding per-person objects and fashion items, and respecting a specified background scene, a regime where current diffusion models remain brittle. Existing benchmarks evaluate only one axis at a time and none jointly captures multi-identity composition with human-object interaction, background grounding, and spatial plausibility. We introduce CogCanvas, a benchmark of 1,952 curated reference images spanning 100 celebrity identities, 115 distinctive objects and fashion items, and 29 real-world background scenes including landmarks, from which we construct 1,361 compositional prompts covering 2-5 person group sizes. The curation pipeline combines DINOv2-based deduplication, two-stage aesthetic filtering, and automated derivation of structured interaction and position graphs that serve as ground-truth supervision. CogCanvas supports three tasks, reference-based multi-human-object generation (primary), text-to-image compositional generation, and reference retrieval, under a unified six-axis evaluation protocol. We introduce two metrics tailored to the multi-reference setting: BG-Sim, which scores background fidelity on SAM 3-masked regions via DINOv3 feature similarity, and Attr-VQA, which uses a multimodal LLM to verify per-subject attribute binding and inter-person interactions against the structured graphs. Benchmarking five SOTA methods reveals that every model degrades substantially as group size grows from 2 to 5, with near-complete failure on object/fashion binding beyond three subjects.

</details>

### [120] [MUNI: Multimodal Unified Latent Diffusion for Coherent Any-to-Any Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16408)
*Kyeongmin Yeo, Yunhong Min, Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (82.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MUNI 是一个端到端的多模态潜扩散框架，通过共享随机潜变量统一子集条件跨模态生成与无条件联合采样，提出路由训练目标实现潜变量的最小充分性，在图像-文本-音频基准上达到或超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Any-to-Any 多模态生成模型大多基于 LLM，难以利用专用模态生成器且需要文本配对数据；而扩散/流方法的扩展仍依赖文本对齐嵌入、全配对训练或等维确定性映射。MUNI 旨在解决这些问题，实现无需文本对齐的端到端统一多模态生成。

Method: MUNI 包含两个核心贡献：(1) 架构上，将潜扩散扩展到端到端多模态 Any-to-Any 生成，联合训练模态专用编码器、表达性解码器和共享的基于流的先验；(2) 训练目标上，提出路由训练目标（routed training objective），通过结构选择使共享潜变量同时满足生成模态间的连贯性、子集潜变量的预测充分性以及潜内容的最小性，实现最小充分性表征。

Result: 在 PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels 和大规模图像-文本-音频基准上，MUNI 在条件生成任务上匹配或超越最强基线，在无条件生成连贯性上取得最大优势。

Conclusion: MUNI 证明了端到端多模态潜扩散框架在 Any-to-Any 生成中的有效性，路由训练目标能够有效对齐共享潜变量与连贯性、充分性和最小性准则，为统一多模态生成提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MUNI，一个端到端的多模态潜扩散框架，支持 Any-to-Any 生成（子集条件跨模态生成与无条件联合采样），属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型。该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 和 multimodal_understanding_generation 两个方向下均有同名条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Proposes a unified latent diffusion framework for any-to-any generation, directly aligning with the user's high-priority unified multimodal model and continuous multimodal representation subtopics. Signals: unified multimodal latent diffusion, any-to-any generation, shared stochastic latent, routed training objective, minimal sufficiency

Abstract: We introduce MUNI, an end-to-end multimodal latent diffusion framework for any-to-any generation that unifies subset-conditioned cross-modal generation and unconditional joint sampling through a shared stochastic latent. Existing multimodal generative models are largely LLM-based, which limits leveraging modality-specific generators and requires text-paired data for training. Recent diffusion- and flow-based any-to-any extensions take a different direction but still rely on text-aligned embeddings, fully-paired training, or matched-dimensionality deterministic mappings. MUNI rests on two complementary contributions, one architectural and one in the training objective. First, we extend latent diffusion to multimodal any-to-any generation end-to-end: instead of the standard two-stage recipe that precomputes a frozen latent space and then fits a prior over it, MUNI jointly trains modality-specific encoders, expressive decoders, and a single shared flow-based prior under one objective. Second, we identify that the standard aggregation rules of multimodal variational inference are insufficient once coupled with a learned prior and expressive decoders. A suitable shared latent must simultaneously satisfy coherence across generated modalities, predictive sufficiency of subset latents, and minimality of the latent content. We propose a routed training objective whose structural choices align the latent with these criteria and admit a minimal-sufficiency characterization in the realizable setting. Experiments on PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels and a large-scale image-text-audio benchmark show MUNI matching or exceeding the strongest baselines on conditional generation while opening its largest margins on unconditional coherence. Project page: .

</details>


### [121] [MMDiff: Extending Diffusion Transformers for Multi-Modal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16673)
*Yagmur Akarken, Orest Kupyn, Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (82.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMDiff 将冻结的扩散Transformer扩展为多模态生成系统，通过轻量解码器头联合生成图像与多种密集感知模态（语义分割、显著目标检测、深度估计），利用多时间步特征融合和概念驱动注意力提取实现高效的多模态联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在去噪过程中计算了丰富的感知表征，但这些表征在图像渲染完成后被丢弃。作者希望利用这些已有的丰富特征，在不重新训练或微调扩散模型的前提下，将其扩展为多模态生成系统，同时生成图像和多种密集感知模态。

Method: MMDiff 基于冻结的扩散Transformer（DiT），在其去噪轨迹的不同时间步提取中间特征，通过多时间步特征融合（multi-timestep feature fusion）和空间变化聚合权重（spatially varying aggregation weights）来整合信息。同时采用概念驱动注意力提取（concept-driven attention extraction）提供可解释的空间引导。仅训练轻量级解码器头，保持扩散骨干网络冻结。

Result: 多时间步特征融合相比单时间步提取在语义分割上提升高达28.7% mIoU。冻结的扩散特征与DINOv3等SOTA编码器具有竞争力和互补性。在语义分割、显著目标检测和深度估计上均取得强性能，并支持大规模合成数据生成。

Conclusion: MMDiff 证明了冻结的扩散Transformer中蕴含丰富的感知信息，通过轻量解码器即可实现高质量的多模态联合生成，为利用预训练扩散模型进行多模态理解与生成一体化提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文将冻结的扩散Transformer扩展为多模态生成系统，联合生成图像与密集感知模态（语义分割、深度估计等），属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型方向。该论文同时匹配多模态生成（图像+密集预测联合生成）和多模态理解与生成（利用扩散特征进行感知任务）两个方向。现有taxonomy中"统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）是最佳匹配，该子主题已存在于multimodal_generation和multimodal_understanding_generation两个方向下。

Importance reason: Extends frozen diffusion transformers for multi-modal generation, aligning with unified multimodal and efficient inference priorities. Signals: multi-modal generation, frozen diffusion transformer, lightweight decoder heads, multi-timestep feature fusion, concept-driven attention

Abstract: Diffusion transformers have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities, yet the rich perceptual representations computed across their denoising trajectory are discarded once the content is rendered. We present MMDiff, a framework that transforms a frozen diffusion transformer into a multi-modal generative system that jointly produces images alongside any combination of dense perceptual modalities using lightweight decoder heads. Our central finding is that perceptual information is temporally distributed along the denoising trajectory, and that multi-timestep feature fusion with spatially varying aggregation weights is essential, improving semantic segmentation results by up to 28.7% mIoU over single-timestep extraction. We further adopt concept-driven attention extraction for interpretable spatial guidance, and show that frozen diffusion features are competitive with and complementary to state-of-the-art encoders such as DINOv3. By training only lightweight decoder heads on a frozen backbone, we achieve strong performance in semantic segmentation, salient object detection, and depth estimation, and demonstrate that this framework enables effective synthetic data generation at scale.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_generation-audio_av_generation'></div>

音乐/语音生成、TTS、音视频联合生成、跨模态条件音频生成。

### [122] [An Empirical Study on Learning Latent Representations for Emotional Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14922)
*Vinh Dang Quang, Huy Ngo Quang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, eess.AS, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (41.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文针对VLSP 2022情感语音合成任务，在FastSpeech 2基础上集成说话人嵌入和韵律瓶颈特征，实现了单说话人情感语音生成（子任务1）和跨说话人风格迁移（子任务2），在保留目标说话人身份的同时迁移源说话人的情感表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成技术已能生成高可懂度和自然度的语音，但控制语音的表现力（如情感表达）仍是一大挑战。本文旨在解决VLSP 2022情感语音合成任务，生成带有指定情感的自然语音。

Method: 基于FastSpeech 2架构，集成说话人嵌入（speaker embedding）和韵律瓶颈特征（prosody bottleneck），分别处理单说话人情感语音生成（子任务1）和跨说话人风格迁移（子任务2，从有情感数据的源说话人迁移到仅有中性数据的目标准说话人）。

Result: 在VLSP 2022数据集上，所提系统在子任务1（单说话人情感合成）和子任务2（跨说话人风格迁移）上均取得了有竞争力的结果，能够生成带有期望情感表达的自然语音。

Conclusion: 通过将说话人嵌入和韵律瓶颈集成到FastSpeech 2中，可以有效实现情感语音合成和跨说话人风格迁移，为可控表现力语音合成提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究情感语音合成（Emotional Speech Synthesis），属于TTS（文本到语音）生成任务，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on emotional speech synthesis with speaker embedding and prosody bottleneck, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with boost keywords. Signals: Emotional speech synthesis, FastSpeech 2, speaker embedding, prosody bottleneck, VLSP 2022 task

Abstract: For the last couple of years, the field of speech synthesis has improved dramatically thanks to deep learning. There are more and more deep learning-based TTS systems developed to make it possible to produce voices with high intelligibility and naturalness. Meanwhile, controlling the expressiveness is yet a big deal, generating speech in different styles or manners has received a lot of attention from community recently. This paper aims to give our solutions to deal with the task emotional speech synthesis (ESS) at VLSP 2022 which allows to generate humanlike natural-sounding voice from a given input text with desired emotional expression. By integrating speaker embedding, prosody bottleneck into FastSpeech 2, our systems can promisingly generate emotional speech of a single speaker (Sub-task 1), transfer speaking styles from another speaker to the target speaker with neutral non-expressive data while retaining the target speaker's identity (Sub-task 2).

</details>


### [123] [FreeSonic: Training-Free Temporal-Aware Decoupled Attention for Precise Audio Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15186)
*Yuxuan Jiang, Mingyang Han, Yusheng Dai, Andong Wang, Tianhong Zhou, Jiaxin Ye, Dongxiao Wang, Haoxiang Shi, Boyu Li, Jun Song, Cheng Yu, Bo Zheng, Weibei Dou, Zehua Chen, Jun Zhu*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FreeSonic 是一种免训练的音频编辑框架，基于 Rectified Flow 的 TangoFlux 模型，通过优化的反转-逆过程、联合文本-音频注意力图以及调度注意力解耦机制，实现了精确且一致的音频编辑，支持音频替换、移除和非刚性替换等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到音频生成取得了显著进展，但精确且一致的音频编辑仍然面临挑战，现有方法难以平衡时序一致性与背景保留。

Method: FreeSonic 利用基于 Rectified Flow 的 TangoFlux 模型，采用优化的反转-逆过程（inversion-reverse process）和联合文本-音频注意力图实现精确的目标片段提取；提出调度注意力解耦（scheduled attention decoupling）机制，将修改限制在目标区域同时保留原始声学上下文；引入任务导向的噪声注入以增强音频移除和非刚性替换等任务的灵活性。

Result: 大量实验表明 FreeSonic 在精确且一致的音频编辑上实现了高保真度和高效率的优越平衡。

Conclusion: FreeSonic 提供了一种免训练的高效解决方案，在音频编辑任务中实现了时序一致性与背景保留的良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出FreeSonic，一种基于Rectified Flow的免训练音频编辑框架，属于音频生成与编辑方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）涵盖音频生成、TTS、音视频联合生成等，音频编辑是该方向的合理延伸，因此复用该subtopic。

Importance reason: Audio editing is outside your priority subtopics and negative keywords are absent, but the training-free approach and attention decoupling are moderately interesting for efficient inference. Signals: training-free audio editing, rectified flow, attention decoupling, temporal consistency, background preservation

Abstract: Text-to-audio (TTA) generation has made significant strides, yet achieving precise and consistent audio editing remains a major challenge. However, existing methods struggle to balance temporal consistency with background preservation. In this paper, we propose FreeSonic, a training-free framework leveraging the state-of-the-art Rectified Flow-based TangoFlux model. FreeSonic utilizes an optimized inversion-reverse process and joint text-audio attention maps for precise target segment extraction. For content editing, a novel scheduled attention decoupling confines modifications to target regions while preserving original acoustic context. Furthermore, task-oriented noise injection enhances versatility for tasks such as audio removal and non-rigid replacement. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that FreeSonic achieves a superior balance by providing a high-fidelity and efficient solution for precise and consistent audio editing. Project and demos:

</details>

### [124] [OSGuard: A Benchmark for Safety in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15034)
*Mina Mohammadmirzaei, Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OSGuard 是一个双粒度安全评测基准，用于评估计算机使用智能体在良性用户指令下的安全性，包含动作级基准和风险增强执行套件，揭示了当前多模态护栏在局部判断与端到端安全之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体评测仅关注任务完成度，忽略了智能体可能通过不安全捷径达成目标的问题，缺乏对安全性的系统评估。

Method: 构建双粒度基准：(1) 动作级基准，包含上下文化的提议动作并标注为允许、无关或不安全；(2) 风险增强执行套件，在 OSWorld 任务基础上引入潜在危险（如破坏性覆盖等），并配以状态安全不变量的增强评估器。

Result: 当前多模态护栏在孤立动作判断上表现良好，但风险增强执行暴露了局部监督与可靠端到端安全之间的差距。

Conclusion: 双粒度设计能够更精确地诊断模型是否能识别不安全提议动作，以及在作为护栏部署时能否提升全任务安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OSGuard，一个面向计算机使用智能体的安全评测基准，属于多模态安全评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Safety benchmark for computer-use agents is valuable but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: safety benchmark, computer-use agents, multimodal guardrails, action-level safety, end-to-end safety

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated by whether they complete realistic desktop and web tasks. However, task success alone can miss failures in which an agent reaches the nominal goal through an unsafe shortcut. We introduce OSGuard, a dual-granularity benchmark suite for evaluating safety in computer-use agents under benign, unchanged user instructions. OSGuard contains an action-level benchmark for local guardrail decisions and a risk-augmented execution suite for end-to-end evaluation. The action-level benchmark consists of contextualized proposed actions labeled as allowed, unrelated, or unsafe, each judged relative to the original instruction and current interface state. The execution suite contains manually constructed OSWorld-derived task variants in which the original task remains achievable, but the environment is modified to introduce latent hazards such as destructive overwrites, etc. Each variant is paired with augmented evaluators that retain the original task-success criterion while adding explicit state-based safety invariants, allowing us to distinguish safe completions from unsafe completions that satisfy the nominal task objective. Our experimental results on OSGuard show that current multimodal guardrails can perform well on isolated action judgments, while risk-augmented execution exposes remaining gaps between local oversight and reliable end-to-end safety. This dual-granularity design enables more precise diagnosis of whether models can both recognize unsafe proposed actions and improve full-task safety when deployed as guardrails.

</details>


### [125] [The Vision Encoder as a Privacy Boundary: Visual-Token Side Channels in Encoder-Free Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14783)
*Chenyu Zhou, Qiliang Jiang, Shuning Wu, Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现无编码器视觉语言模型（VLM）因移除视觉编码器而暴露了隐私攻击面：中间视觉token成为预输出侧信道。攻击者可通过解码视觉token流从Gemma4和Fuyu等模型中恢复可识别的图像结构和可读文本，而基于编码器的对照模型则无法恢复精确字符串。该侧信道还存在于KV缓存中，且能抵抗加噪和量化等防御，有效缓解需减少空间采样密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLM中的视觉编码器在压缩图像时隐式充当了隐私边界，保留了语义但削弱了像素级细节。无编码器VLM移除了这一边界，将图像patch直接路由到语言模型token流中，可能暴露隐私攻击面。作者旨在揭示并量化这一安全风险。

Method: 作者在两种无编码器VLM（Gemma4和Fuyu）上实施token访问攻击：训练解码器从视觉token流中逆向恢复图像结构和嵌入的访问码。与基于编码器的对照模型（如LLaVA-NeXT）进行对比实验。通过消融实验分析空间采样保真度、token数量等因素的影响，并测试KV缓存的可逆性以及防御措施的鲁棒性。

Result: 无编码器VLM的视觉token流可被逆向恢复出可识别的图像结构和可读文本（如访问码），而编码器对照模型仅能定位区域但无法恢复精确字符串。关键因素是视觉token网格的空间采样保真度（尤其是字符方向采样密度）。Gemma4的layer-0 KV缓存也可直接逆向。攻击在杂乱背景、文档退化、零样本迁移下依然有效，且能抵抗加噪和量化防御。

Conclusion: 无编码器VLM因移除视觉编码器而引入了架构性的隐私侧信道风险，有效缓解必须减少空间采样密度。部署无编码器VLM时应将移除视觉编码器视为一级隐私决策。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无编码器VLM中视觉token侧信道攻击，属于多模态模型的安全与隐私攻击问题，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel privacy attack on encoder-free VLMs, but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: Privacy attack, encoder-free VLM, visual-token side channel, KV cache leakage, spatial sampling defense

Abstract: A vision encoder compresses image pixels into semantic embeddings, implicitly acting as a privacy boundary by preserving semantic content while attenuating pixel-local detail required for exact text recovery. Encoder-free vision-language models (VLMs) remove this boundary by routing image patches directly into the language-model token stream, thereby exposing an architectural privacy attack surface: intermediate visual tokens become a pre-output side channel. Under a token-access adversary, decoders invert visual-token streams from two encoder-free VLMs, Gemma4 and Fuyu, recovering recognizable image structure and readable held-out access codes, whereas matched encoder-based controls localize target regions but recover no exact strings. Within-model ablations show that the operative factor is spatial sampling fidelity of the visual-token grid, especially character-direction sampling density, rather than token or value count. The leakage is not limited to exported tokens: Gemma4 layer-0 key-value cache tensors are directly invertible, placing the side channel within KV caches commonly persisted by production serving stacks for decoding efficiency. The attack survives clutter, realistic document degradation, and zero-shot transfer to public document images, and it resists value-level defenses such as additive noise and quantization. Effective mitigation must therefore reduce spatial sampling, making removal of the vision encoder a first-class privacy decision in VLM deployment.

</details>


### [126] [Calibrated Triage, Not Autonomy: Confidence Estimation for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15910)
*Reza Khanmohammadi, Kundan Thind, Mohammad M. Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (73.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了七种置信度估计方法在五个开源医学视觉语言模型上的表现，发现标准指标（如判别力）无法区分方法优劣，真正关键的是高置信区间的错误率——最差基线在高置信区间有41-45%的错误，而最佳探针仅1-4%。结论是当前医学VLM的可用角色是"校准分诊"而非"自主决策"：仅当置信度超过阈值时才自动化，其余转交临床医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型可能看似自信地回答关于医学图像的问题，但实际上几乎不使用图像信息，而是依赖语言先验。这种"自信但错误"的失败模式在医学场景中最为危险。因此，需要可靠的置信度估计来告诉系统何时应该拒答（abstain），从而确保安全部署。

Method: 将问题建模为有界选择性预测（bounded selective prediction）：仅当置信度超过阈值时自动化处理病例，否则转交临床医生。评估了七种置信度估计方法（包括softmax响应、温度缩放、各种探针等），在五个开源LVLM和三个医学VQA数据集（涵盖临床影像、放射学、病理学）上进行实验，所有探针仅在自然图像上训练且不做领域适应。

Result: 标准判别力指标几乎无法区分不同置信度方法；弱校准的简单自报告方法通过离域温度缩放即可廉价改善校准性但不改变可用产出。真正区分可用性的指标是高置信区间的错误率：最差基线在高置信区间有41-45%的错误，最佳探针仅1-4%。没有一种方法在所有领域和模型上一致最优。基础模型能力设定了天花板——在放射学中，良好校准的分数可在20%错误容忍度下恢复约三分之一的病例，但在病理学中几乎无法恢复。

Conclusion: 当前医学VLM的可用角色是"校准分诊"而非"自主决策"：自动化那些被校准置信度标记为安全的病例，其余转交临床医生。安全交接受两个层面控制：基础模型能力设定天花板，置信度层决定能触及天花板的多大比例。

Classification reason: 该论文研究医学视觉语言模型的置信度估计与选择性预测（abstention/拒答），核心关注模型何时应安全地自动回答、何时应转交人类医生。这属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴，与现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）中的防御/安全评测方向高度相关，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Strong empirical study on medical VLM safety, but outside user's priority subtopics and contains negative keyword "medical". Signals: medical VLM, confidence estimation, selective prediction, calibrated triage, high-confidence error rate

Abstract: A vision-language model can answer a question about a medical image fluently and confidently while barely using the image, leaning instead on language priors. In medicine this is the failure that matters most, because the answer looks trustworthy and is not, and the only protection is a confidence score reliable enough to tell the system when to abstain. We ask a deployment question rather than an accuracy one: how much imaging work a model can safely handle alone, and which confidence signal makes that possible. We evaluate seven confidence estimators across five open-weight LVLMs and three medical visual-question-answering datasets spanning broad clinical imaging, radiology, and pathology, with every probe trained only on natural images and applied without adaptation. Recast as bounded selective prediction (automate a case only when confidence clears a threshold, defer the rest), the comparison is cautionary. The standard metrics are poor guides: discrimination barely separates the methods, and the weak calibration of a cheap self-report is cheaply removed by off-domain temperature scaling without changing deployable yield. What distinguishes a usable estimator is the high-confidence region a clinician acts on: the weakest baselines are confidently wrong on 41 to 45 percent of their errors against 1 to 4 percent for the best probe, and no estimator is reliably best across domains or models. Safe handoff is governed at two levels: base-model competence sets a ceiling, so a well-calibrated score recovers roughly a third of radiology cases at a 20 percent error tolerance but almost none of pathology; the confidence layer then decides how much of that ceiling is reachable. The usable role today is calibrated triage, not autonomy: automate the cases a calibrated score marks safe, route the rest to a clinician. We release all outputs, correctness judgments, and confidence scores, with code.

</details>


### [127] [XAI-Grounded Explanation Generation for Speech Deepfake Detection with Training-Free Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16137)
*Yupei Li, Qiyang Sun, Xiaoliang Wu, Chenxi Wang, Berrak Sisman, Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (67.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的解释生成框架，将传统XAI（梯度归因）生成的证据信号与多模态大语言模型结合，为语音深度伪造检测系统生成基于事实的、具体的自然语言解释，在PartialSpoof数据集上使内部准确率提升超过45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音深度伪造检测（SDD）的解释方法存在两难：传统XAI方法（如梯度归因）产生低层归因信号，与模型决策紧密耦合但难以被人类理解；而基于LLM的解释生成因缺乏启发式证据和任务特定监督，往往产生通用且无依据的描述。因此需要一种能结合XAI证据与LLM生成能力的框架，产生既忠实于模型决策又易于理解的解释。

Method: 提出一个无需训练的解释框架，包含两个阶段：1）使用传统XAI方法（如梯度归因）从SDD模型生成低层归因信号作为证据；2）将这些XAI证据作为提示输入到多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中，引导其生成基于事实的、具体的自然语言解释。在PartialSpoof数据集上构建了带标注的解释数据集用于评估。

Result: 在PartialSpoof数据集上，结合XAI证据的方法相比无XAI的基线，内部准确率提升超过45%。通过人工评估和忠实性检查验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将传统XAI证据与多模态大语言模型结合是一种有效的无需训练的解释生成范式，能够为语音深度伪造检测系统生成既忠实于模型决策又易于人类理解的自然语言解释，显著提升了解释的质量和准确性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语音深度伪造检测（SDD）的可解释性，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）结合XAI证据生成可解释的文本说明，属于多模态安全与攻防（检测伪造/欺骗）范畴下的可解释性/可信赖性方向。虽然更偏向可解释性而非直接攻击/防御，但多模态安全与攻防子主题涵盖可信赖决策与安全评测，是现有最相关的规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on speech deepfake detection explanation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: speech deepfake detection, XAI explanation, training-free MLLM, low priority subtopic, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Speech deepfake detection (SDD) systems require trustworthy explanations for reliable decision-making. Existing explanation ways mainly fall into two categories. Traditional explainable AI (XAI), such as gradient-based attribution, produces low-level attribution signals tightly coupled with model decisions, and harder to be understood by human than natural language explanations. Meanwhile, large language model (LLM)-based explanation generation often produces generic and ungrounded descriptions due to the lack of heuristic evidence and task-specific supervision, stemming from limited grounded explanation datasets for SDD. We therefore propose a training-free explanation framework that integrates XAI evidence with multimodal LLMs to generate grounded and specific explanations. Using the PartialSpoof dataset, we construct a grounded explanation dataset and show that methods with XAI increase inside accuracy by over 45\%, verified through human evaluation and faithfulness checks.

</details>


### [128] [Analyzing Visual Aircraft Representations with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15468)
*Deepshik Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文在ConvNeXt分类器上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），分解FGVC-Aircraft数据集上的中间视觉表征为可解释特征，通过激活分析、消融实验验证了SAE能揭示部分可解释的、与飞机识别相关的视觉特征，同时暴露了多义性和空间定位粗糙等局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型在分类任务上表现优异，但其内部表征难以解释。现有可解释性方法往往依赖注意力可视化或概念激活向量，缺乏对中间表征进行细粒度分解的手段。本文旨在探索稀疏自编码器能否将视觉模型的中间表征分解为可解释的稀疏特征，从而揭示模型识别飞机类别时所依赖的视觉模式。

Method: 在ImageNet预训练的ConvNeXt-Tiny模型上，在FGVC-Aircraft数据集上进行微调。提取模型最后一个特征阶段的3D空间激活（7×7×768），展平后训练一个稀疏自编码器（SAE），包含编码器、解码器和L1稀疏正则化。通过top-activating图像块、激活强度和类别选择性分析学习到的稀疏特征，并采用输入空间消融（模糊图像块）和特征空间消融（抑制稀疏特征）评估特征对分类logits、分类边界和预测置信度的影响。

Result: 定性分析显示部分稀疏特征对应可识别的飞机结构（如机翼、尾翼、发动机）和视觉模式（如天空背景、跑道）。消融实验表明，模糊top-activating图像块会降低对应类别的logits和分类边界；抑制特定稀疏特征也会降低模型对该特征的依赖。但部分特征表现出多义性（同时激活于不同视觉模式），且空间定位较为粗糙（7×7网格分辨率有限）。

Conclusion: 稀疏自编码器能够将视觉模型的中间表征分解为部分可解释的、与类别相关的视觉特征，为理解视觉模型内部机制提供了有效工具。但当前方法存在多义性和空间分辨率不足等局限，未来工作可探索更高分辨率的特征图、更精细的稀疏正则化策略以及与其他可解释性方法的结合。

Classification reason: 该论文使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析视觉模型内部表征的可解释性，属于多模态模型可解释性/安全分析范畴。虽然论文本身不涉及攻击或防御，但SAE用于模型内部表征分析是多模态安全与可解释性方向的重要工具，且现有taxonomy中"多模态安全与攻防"是唯一涵盖模型内部表征分析/可解释性的[CANONICAL]子主题，故映射至此。

Importance reason: Sparse autoencoders for vision interpretability are not a priority subtopic, and the aircraft domain is narrow. Signals: Sparse autoencoder, vision interpretability, FGVC-Aircraft, ConvNeXt, limited personal relevance

Abstract: Vision models can achieve strong performance on classification tasks, but the internal representations supporting their predictions are often difficult to interpret. This work investigates whether sparse autoencoders can decompose intermediate representations of a vision model into interpretable features. We train a ConvNeXt classifier on the FGVC-Aircraft dataset, extract spatial activations from its final feature stage, and train a sparse autoencoder on these activations. The learned sparse features are analyzed using top-activating image patches, activation strength, and class selectivity. Qualitative visual inspection reveals that several features correspond to recognizable aircraft structures and visual patterns. We evaluate a subset of selected features using input-space and feature-space ablations, measuring how blurring image patches and suppressing sparse features affect class logits, classification margins, and prediction confidence. The results suggest that sparse autoencoders can reveal partially interpretable, class-relevant visual features associated with aircraft recognition, while also exposing limitations such as polysemanticity and coarse spatial localization.

</details>


### [129] [On the Adversarial Robustness of Multimodal LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15608)
*Zihan Wang, Guansong Pang, Zelin Liu, Wenjun Miao, Jin Zheng, Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RobustMLLMJudge 框架，系统评估多模态大语言模型作为自动评判器时的对抗鲁棒性，并设计 MGSIA 攻击方法，通过语义诱导与流形对齐实现高效可迁移的分数膨胀攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 越来越多地被用作自动评判器（如图像质量评估、安全评估），但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，这威胁到自动评判的公平性和可靠性。

Method: 提出 RobustMLLMJudge 评估框架，覆盖多种攻击方法；进一步提出 MGSIA（流形引导语义诱导攻击），结合肯定性语义诱导（最大化模型输出"Yes"等肯定回答的概率）与高分流形对齐（将对抗表征正则化到高评分中心），生成可迁移的分数膨胀扰动。

Result: 实验表明不同 MLLM 评判器对分数膨胀攻击高度脆弱，MGSIA 在欺骗多种先进 MLLM 评判器方面具有优越性和泛化性。

Conclusion: MLLM 评判器的对抗鲁棒性亟待加强，本文的工作揭示了其脆弱性并提供了评估框架和更强的攻击方法，为后续防御研究奠定基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型作为自动评判器时的对抗鲁棒性，提出攻击框架和新的攻击方法，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial robustness of MLLM judges, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions. Signals: MLLM adversarial robustness, score-inflating attack, MGSIA method, multimodal understanding generation, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges, e.g., for image quality and safety assessment. However, their adversarial robustness remains largely unexplored, threatening the fairness and reliability of automated judging. To bridge this gap, we introduce RobustMLLMJudge, the first general framework for evaluating the adversarial robustness of general-purpose MLLMs when functioning as judges. It covers diverse attacks against popular judge approaches across quality and safety evaluation scenarios. Using RobustMLLMJudge, we reveal that i) different MLLM judges are highly vulnerable to score-inflating adversarial attacks; and ii) although effective, these attack methods face a critical challenge due to unique constraints in the evaluation protocols of MLLM judges. We further propose MGSIA, namely Manifold-Guided Semantic Induction Attack, a novel method that bypasses these constraints to enable more effective and transferable attacks on MLLM judges. The core idea of MGSIA is to combine affirmative semantic induction with high-score manifold alignment: it maximizes the probability that judges yield affirmative responses (e.g., "Yes") to binary semantic queries, while regularizing adversarial representations toward high-score centers estimated from proxy protocols. Together, these objectives yield transferable score-inflating perturbations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority and generalizability of MGSIA in deceiving advanced MLLM judges under different evaluation scenarios, highlighting the need for robust MLLM judges. Code and data will be made available at .

</details>


### [130] [Deep Residual Injection for Full-Spectrum Forensic Signal Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15880)
*Kaiqing Lin, Zhiyuan Yan, Ruoxin Chen, Ke-Yue Zhang, Yue Zhou, Caiyong Piao, Bin Li, Taiping Yao, Bo Wang, Youchang Xiao, Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Visual Residual MLLM (Deep-VRM)，通过层分析发现MLLM中语义信息主要形成于浅中层，直接微调学习伪造伪影会破坏语义表征。为此设计深度残差注入机制，将伪影视觉信号作为残差路径注入中间层，与语义token融合后传播，使模型能联合建模语义推理与信号级取证线索，实现鲁棒的AI生成图像检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成图像越来越逼真，仅依赖语义级不一致性难以可靠检测伪造。现有MLLM在取证中虽有语义理解优势，但能否同时捕获低层生成器伪影（全频谱取证信号感知）而不损失预训练语义知识，是一个关键问题。

Method: 首先对MLLM进行逐层分析，发现语义信息主要在浅中层形成，而直接微调学习伪影会破坏这些语义表征。基于此提出Deep-VRM：保留早期语义处理层，将伪影特定的视觉信号作为残差路径注入到中间层，与语义token表征融合后通过后续可训练层传播，使模型能联合建模语义推理和信号级取证线索。

Result: 在多个AI生成图像检测基准上达到最先进性能，模型能根据输入自适应地利用不同层次的取证信号，展现出鲁棒且可泛化的检测能力。

Conclusion: 通过深度残差注入策略，MLLM可以在保留预训练语义知识的同时有效感知低层伪造伪影，实现全频谱取证信号感知，为AI生成内容检测提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究利用MLLM进行AI生成图像取证（伪造检测），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴下的防御/检测方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM forensic detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM forensic detection, deep residual injection, layer-wise analysis, AI-generated image detection, full-spectrum signal perception

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted in forensics for their robust semantic understanding. As AI-generated images become realistic, semantic-level inconsistencies alone are often insufficient for reliable detection. This motivates a critical question: whether MLLMs can achieve full-spectrum forensic signal perception, i.e., capturing low-level generator artifacts without sacrificing pre-trained semantic knowledge. We further perform a layer-wise analysis of forensic signal perception in MLLMs, showing that semantic information is primarily formed in the early-to-middle layers, whereas direct fine-tuning for artifact learning disrupts these semantic representations. Based on this insight, we propose Deep Visual Residual MLLM (Deep-VRM) to preserve early semantic processing while injecting artifact-specific visual signals as a residual path into an intermediate layer, where they are fused with semantic token representations and propagated through subsequent trainable layers. This enables later layers to jointly model semantic reasoning and signal-level forensic cues, and surprisingly, the model learns to adaptively leverage different levels of forensic signals depending on the input, achieving robust and generalizable detection performance. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art across most benchmarks. The code and data are available at .

</details>


### [131] [MIRAGE: Auditing Anti-Muslim Bias in Frontier LLMs Across Reasoning, Agentic, and Time-Coupled Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16562)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad, Tamim Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一个针对前沿LLM中反穆斯林偏见的评测基准，包含1200个提示，覆盖直接补全、思维链推理和模拟Agent决策三种部署场景。实验发现思维链会放大偏见（12-34%），Agent决策存在9-22个百分点的不对称性，且偏见与检索到的新闻上下文强相关（增加18-27%）。现有提示缓解方法在直接补全场景有效，但对Agent场景效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏见评测大多局限于单轮提示补全，这已无法反映前沿LLM的实际部署方式（如思维链推理、Agent决策等）。作者旨在系统评估反穆斯林偏见在更真实部署条件下的表现。

Method: 构建MIRAGE基准，包含1200个提示，覆盖三种条件：直接补全、思维链推理、模拟Agent决策（内容审核、贷款审核、难民申请摘要、招聘筛选）。在6个前沿模型上评测偏见程度，并测试现有提示缓解方法的迁移效果。

Result: 思维链推理相比直接补全将穆斯林-暴力关联放大12-34%；Agent决策在相同证据下对穆斯林与非穆斯林案例存在9-22个百分点的处理不对称；偏见在近期冲突检索下增加18-27%；现有提示缓解方法在直接补全场景有效，但对Agent场景效果有限。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在更真实的部署条件下（推理、Agent决策）存在更严重的反穆斯林偏见，现有缓解方法无法有效迁移到这些场景，亟需针对性的偏见缓解研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM中的反穆斯林偏见评测与安全审计，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）范畴下的偏见评测与安全对齐问题。虽然论文主要关注文本LLM而非多模态，但该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖LLM安全评测（包括偏见、越狱、对抗攻击等），且论文的agentic决策、推理条件等设置与多模态安全评测方法高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on social bias auditing, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Bias evaluation benchmark, Chain-of-thought amplifies bias, Agentic asymmetry, Time-coupled bias, Not aligned with user's technical priorities

Abstract: Five years after the discovery of persistent anti-Muslim bias in large language models, most evaluations remain confined to single-turn prompt completion, a setting that no longer reflects how frontier LLMs are deployed. We introduce \textbf{MIRAGE} (Muslim-Identity Reasoning and Agentic Generation Evaluation), a benchmark of 1{,}200 prompts spanning three deployment-realistic conditions: direct completion, chain-of-thought reasoning, and simulated agentic decision-making across content moderation, lending triage, refugee claim summarization, and hiring screens. Across six frontier models, we find that (i) chain-of-thought reasoning \emph{amplifies} rather than suppresses Muslim-violence associations by 12--34\% relative to direct completion, (ii) agentic decisions exhibit a 9--22 percentage-point asymmetry between Muslim and matched non-Muslim cases on identical evidence, and (iii) bias is sharply time-coupled to retrieved news context, increasing 18--27\% under recent-conflict retrieval. Existing prompt-based mitigations transfer poorly across our three conditions, suppressing direct-completion bias while leaving agentic asymmetry largely intact. We release MIRAGE and an open evaluation harness to support targeted mitigation research.

</details>


### [132] [Cascaded Sparse Autoencoders Learn Multi-Level Visual Concepts in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16193)
*Yusong Zhao, Hengyi Wang, Tanuja Ganu, Akshay Nambi, Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (79.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出级联稀疏自编码器（CSAEs），通过在第一个SAE的解码器权重上训练第二个SAE，实现对MLLM中多层级视觉概念的学习，相比现有SAE方法在层次化概念一致性和概念引导方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在视觉语言任务上表现优异，但其内部视觉表征难以解释。现有SAE方法只能恢复扁平的特征字典，无法显式组织多层级概念结构。

Method: 提出级联稀疏自编码器（CSAEs），不采用嵌套或堆叠SAE稀疏激活码的方式，而是在第一级SAE的解码器权重上直接训练第二级SAE，将学习到的低级特征方向作为高级抽象的输入，从而学习"概念的概念"。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL、Gemma-3和LLaVA等多个视觉数据集上，CSAEs在层次化概念一致性上优于现有SAE基线方法。概念引导实验表明，学习到的概念分组支持有效的组级干预。

Conclusion: CSAEs能够有效学习MLLM中的多层级视觉概念，提升模型可解释性，并支持概念级别的输出干预，为理解MLLM内部表征提供了新工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究MLLM内部视觉表征的可解释性，使用级联稀疏自编码器（CSAEs）学习层次化视觉概念。虽然论文本身不直接涉及安全攻防，但SAE可解释性方法在多模态安全（如幻觉检测、越狱防御）中有重要应用。然而更准确地说，该论文的核心是MLLM内部表征的可解释性分析，与"多模态安全与攻防"方向关联较弱。实际上，该论文更接近"多模态评测与基准"或"视觉语言模型与MLLM"中的可解释性主题，但现有taxonomy中缺乏专门的可解释性子主题。考虑到论文核心是MLLM内部表征分析，最接近的现有子主题是"多模态安全与攻防"（涉及内部表征理解），但匹配不够理想。不过根据规则应优先复用现有子主题，故选择该子主题。

Importance reason: Novel hierarchical SAE method for MLLM interpretability, but user's priority subtopics focus on generation and continuous models, not interpretability. Signals: cascaded sparse autoencoders, multi-level visual concepts, MLLM interpretability, concept steering, not aligned with user's generation/continuous model priorities

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on vision-language tasks, yet their internal visual representations remain difficult to interpret. Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) provide a scalable way to decompose dense model activations into sparse, interpretable features. However, existing SAE architectures primarily recover flat feature dictionaries and are less suited for explicit multi-level concept organization. In this paper, we introduce cascaded sparse autoencoders (CSAEs) for learning hierarchical visual concepts in MLLMs. Rather than nesting or stacking SAE sparse activation codes, CSAEs train a second-level SAE directly on the decoder weights of the first-level SAE, treating learned low-level feature directions as inputs for higher-level abstraction. This design enables CSAEs to learn "concepts of concepts" while avoiding drawbacks from the shared-prefix coupling of nesting, Matryoshka-style hierarchies and the bottlenecks of naively stacked SAEs. Experiments across Qwen3-VL, Gemma-3, and LLaVA on multiple visual datasets show that CSAEs improve interpretability in terms of hierarchical concept coherence over state-of-the-art SAE baselines. Results on concept steering further demonstrate that the learned concept groups support effective group-level interventions in MLLM outputs.

</details>


### [133] [Semantic Flip: Synthetic OOD Generation for Robust Refusal in Embodied Question Answering and Spatial Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16898)
*Dongbin Na, Chanwoo Kim, Giyun Choi, Dooyoung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Semantic Flip框架，通过合成辅助的分布外（OOD）样本，使具身VLM在面对视觉记忆无法支持的查询时能够正确拒答（回答"我不知道"），无需外部OOD标注。该方法在具身问答和空间定位两个基准上显著优于强提示基线，并在新提出的SpaceReject基准上达到0.9559的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM在具身智能场景中常对不可回答的查询产生过度自信的回答，这在具身问答中可能误导用户，在空间导航中可能给出错误坐标并引导用户前往错误位置。现有研究很少直接解决具身VLM何时以及如何拒答的问题。

Method: Semantic Flip通过独立变换查询和视频记忆来构造辅助OOD对（缺乏视觉依据的查询-记忆对），利用这些合成OOD样本在冻结的预训练VLM之上训练一个轻量级拒绝模块，无需重新训练底层模型或外部OOD标注。

Result: 在两个互补基准上，Semantic Flip一致优于强提示基线。在新提出的SpaceReject基准（针对长视频记忆中故意不可回答的空间定位查询）上达到0.9559的F1分数。

Conclusion: Semantic Flip提供了一种简单有效的框架，通过合成OOD样本增强具身VLM的拒答能力，可附加到任何现有VLM管道上，无需重新训练底层模型，显著提升了具身智能系统的安全性和可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注具身VLM在面对不可回答查询时生成过度自信回答的安全问题，提出Semantic Flip框架通过合成OOD样本训练拒绝模块来增强模型拒答能力。这属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied VLM refusal and OOD detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions. Signals: Embodied VLM, OOD detection, refusal generation, spatial localization, no external OOD annotations

Abstract: Detecting unanswerable user queries remains essential for the reliable deployment of real-world embodied agents. However, modern vision-language models (VLMs) often generate overly confident answers even when the available visual memory cannot support the query. Such overconfidence poses various task-dependent risks. The agent may provide misleading information to the user in Embodied Question Answering and select an arbitrary coordinate and physically guide the user there in spatial reasoning for navigation. Despite these high stakes, only a few prior studies directly address when and how an embodied VLM should respond with "I do not know." This work proposes Semantic Flip, a simple yet effective framework that synthesizes auxiliary out-of-distribution (OOD) samples for embodied refusal without requiring external OOD annotations. The key idea is to independently transform the query and video memory to construct auxiliary OOD pairs that lack sufficient visual grounding. These synthesized pairs enable training a lightweight rejection module on top of a frozen pretrained VLM. The module attaches to any existing VLM-based pipeline without retraining the underlying model. Across two complementary benchmarks, Semantic Flip consistently outperforms strong prompting baselines. This work also introduces SpaceReject, a new refusal benchmark for spatial localization with deliberately unanswerable queries over long video memory, where Semantic Flip achieves an $F_1$ score of 0.9559. The source codes and datasets are publicly available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_hallucination'></div>
