### [134] [FUSE: Quantifying Uncertainty in Vision-Language Models by Bayesian Fusing Epistemic and Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14728)
*Harry Zhang, Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FUSE 提出一种贝叶斯融合框架，将VLM的aleatoric（嵌入级不确定性，源于输入歧义）和epistemic（语义响应多样性，源于模型知识不足）两种不确定性进行解析融合，输出标量不确定性度量，用于可靠预测模型输出正确性，在不确定性校准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人等安全关键场景中应用日益广泛，但现有VLM缺乏可靠的不确定性量化能力，无法判断输出是否正确。现有方法仅关注单一不确定性来源（如仅基于logits或仅基于响应多样性），未能系统融合互补的不确定性类型。

Method: 提出FUSE框架：(1) Aleatoric不确定性：通过视觉-语言嵌入空间的概率建模，量化输入数据本身的歧义性；(2) Epistemic不确定性：通过多次采样VLM的语义响应，基于响应多样性估计模型知识不足带来的不确定性；(3) 贝叶斯融合机制：将两种不确定性解析融合为标量不确定性度量，用于预测输出正确性。

Result: 在多个VLM和任务上，FUSE的不确定性校准效果优于现有基线方法，达到SOTA性能。融合后的不确定性度量能更可靠地预测模型输出是否正确。

Conclusion: FUSE通过贝叶斯融合两种互补的不确定性来源，为VLM提供了更可靠的不确定性量化方法，有助于提升VLM在安全关键应用中的可信度。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM的不确定性量化（uncertainty calibration），核心目标是提升模型输出的可靠性与置信度校准，属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性范畴。因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic '多模态幻觉与可靠性'（multimodal_hallucination）。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM reliability via uncertainty quantification, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: VLM uncertainty, Bayesian fusion, aleatoric epistemic, calibration SOTA, not generation or continuous token focus

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are playing an increasingly important role across multiple domains. In many applications, such as robotics, it is crucial to quantify the uncertainty in the output of these models. } We develop FUSE, a probabilistic framework for capturing two complementary sources of uncertainty in vision-language modeling: (i) aleatoric embedding-level uncertainty derived from input data vision-language ambiguity, and (ii) epistemic model-level uncertainty estimated from the semantic response diversity of VLMs. Our approach formulates a Bayesian fusion mechanism that analytically combines these uncertainty sources to produce a scalar measure of uncertainty. This measure can be used to reliably predict the model's output correctness for downstream applications. We demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines and achieves SOTA uncertainty calibration.

</details>


### [135] [Disentangling Hallucinations: Orthogonal Semantic Projection for Robust Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14758)
*Emirhan Bilgiç, Baptiste Caramiaux, Zhi Yan, Gianni Franchi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VLM可解释性方法中语义幻觉的根本原因——高维嵌入空间中的线性语义泄漏，并提出统一理论框架LSA和几何干预方法OSP，通过正交化查询向量来消除共享特征干扰，从而最小化幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在安全关键场景中部署时，其解释的可信度至关重要。现有XAI方法存在语义幻觉问题——即使输入错误的文本描述，归因图仍会高亮显著图像区域。该问题缺乏形式化的数学分析。

Method: 提出线性语义归因(LSA)统一理论框架，证明语义幻觉是高维嵌入空间中线性语义泄漏的必然结果。提出OSP方法，利用OMP的残差性质，通过将查询向量与干扰概念正交化，使归因模型对共享特征"盲视"，同时保持对正确提示的忠实性。

Result: 理论上证明了OSP最小化幻觉的有效性，实验上验证了OSP在多种架构和设置下显著降低语义幻觉，同时保持对正确提示的归因保真度。

Conclusion: 语义幻觉是可解释性方法在高维嵌入空间中的固有现象，OSP通过几何正交化干预有效缓解该问题，为构建可信赖的VLM解释提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM解释中的语义幻觉问题，提出正交语义投影(OSP)方法缓解幻觉，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM hallucination with a geometric intervention, but falls outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: VLM interpretability, semantic hallucination, orthogonal projection, linear semantic leakage, CLIP embeddings

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, the trustworthiness of their explanations becomes crucial. Explainable AI (XAI) methods for Vision-Language Models often suffer from semantic hallucination, where attribution maps highlight prominent image regions even when prompted with incorrect text descriptions (e.g., highlighting a dog when prompted ``cat''). Although this problem is widespread, a formal mathematical analysis of XAI methods and CLIP embeddings is largely missing in the literature. We demonstrate that this phenomenon is not specific to a single architecture but is a fundamental consequence of Linear Semantic Leakage in high-dimensional embedding spaces. We propose a unified theoretical framework, Linear Semantic Attribution (LSA), which generalizes across discriminative methods. We introduce OSP, a geometric intervention that utilizes the residual property of OMP to disentangle unique semantic signals from shared concepts. We prove theoretically and demonstrate empirically that OSP minimizes hallucination by orthogonalizing the query vector against distractor concepts, rendering the attribution model blind to shared features while preserving fidelity for correct prompts. Our code is available at:

</details>


### [136] [Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Multimodal Systems through Retrieval-Augmented Reliability-Aware Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15782)
*Pratheswaran Hariharan, Haiping Xu, Donghui Yan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出检索增强的可靠性感知推理框架，通过外部视觉证据库和多个可靠性指标（相似度、类别支持一致性、证据边际、熵不确定性等）评估预测可信度，并基于决策门控机制选择接受、谨慎回答或弃权，从而减少多模态系统的视觉幻觉和过度自信错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉证据不足或语义不一致时仍会产生过度自信的预测和幻觉输出，现有方法缺乏对实例级预测可靠性的量化机制和识别错误视觉输出的能力。

Method: 构建外部视觉证据数据库，使用预训练视觉嵌入和最近邻检索获取相似样本；设计多个可靠性指标（相似度强度、类别支持一致性、证据边际、熵不确定性、聚合可靠性分数）；基于这些信号设计决策门控机制，决定接受预测、谨慎回答或弃权/回退；最后根据可靠性决策生成面向用户的响应。

Result: 在ImageNet-100上，接受预测准确率从85.84%提升至88.88%（覆盖率为89.04%），幻觉类错误接受率从14.16%降至11.12%。

Conclusion: 集成检索证据、可靠性估计和选择性决策门控可以在不重新训练大模型的情况下改善校准性能并减少过度自信的视觉错误。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于缓解多模态大模型的视觉幻觉（hallucination），通过检索增强的可靠性感知推理来减少过度自信的错误预测，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses visual hallucination in MLLMs but focuses on reliability estimation rather than the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal hallucination, retrieval-augmented reliability, decision gating, ImageNet-100 evaluation, no retraining required

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in vision-language understanding and natural-language response generation. However, these systems can still produce overconfident predictions and hallucination-like outputs, particularly when the visual evidence is weak, ambiguous, or semantically inconsistent. Most existing approaches focus on improving multimodal representation alignment or retrieval-augmented generation, while providing limited mechanisms to quantify instance-level prediction reliability or identify incorrect visual outputs. This work proposes a retrieval-augmented reliability-aware inference framework for trustworthy multimodal visual understanding. The proposed framework constructs an external visual evidence database using pretrained visual embeddings and nearest-neighbor retrieval over normalized feature representations. Retrieved evidence is used to estimate prediction trustworthiness through multiple reliability indicators, including similarity strength, class-support agreement, evidence margin, entropy-based uncertainty, and an aggregate reliability score. Based on these signals, a decision gate determines whether the system should accept the prediction, answer with caution, or abstain/fallback when evidence is insufficient. A multimodal response-generation layer then produces a final user-facing response conditioned on the reliability decision. Experiments on ImageNet-100 demonstrate that the proposed reliability-aware framework improves accepted prediction accuracy from 85.84\% to 88.88\% at 89.04\% coverage. The hallucination-like accepted wrong-answer rate is reduced from 14.16\% to 11.12\%. These results show that integrating retrieval evidence, reliability estimation, and selective decision gating can improve calibration and reduce overconfident visual errors without retraining large multimodal models.

</details>


### [137] [Uncertainty Is Not a Safety Net for Clinical VQA, but Can It Anticipate Model Failure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16583)
*Arnisa Fazla, Alberto Testoni, Ameen Abu-Hanna, Barbara Plank, Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (42.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了8种不确定性估计方法在12个临床VLM上的表现，发现UE质量并非方法固有属性，而是随模型准确率变化——在模型最薄弱处UE反而最差。当隐藏正确答案（NOTA扰动）时，准确率骤降但不确定性几乎不变，模型校准严重失效。然而，未扰动输入上的不确定性可以可靠预测哪些预测会在NOTA下崩溃，表明UE可作为诊断模型脆弱性的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床VLM的安全部署需要可靠的不确定性估计（UE）来指示何时应信任预测或交由临床医生处理。然而，当前UE方法是否真的能提供这种信号尚不清楚，因此需要系统评估。

Method: 在12个临床VLM上基准测试8种UE方法，包括基于softmax、温度缩放、MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble、Mahalanobis距离、RDE、熵和能量分数。通过NOTA（None Of The Above）扰动进行压力测试，即隐藏正确答案使模型被迫选择错误选项，分析UE在正常和扰动条件下的表现。

Result: UE质量与模型准确率高度相关，在模型最薄弱处UE最差。NOTA扰动下准确率骤降但不确定性几乎不变，模型系统性校准失败。然而，正常输入上的不确定性可以可靠预测哪些预测会在NOTA下崩溃。

Conclusion: 当前临床VLM的UE不能作为安全网直接用于临床部署，但可作为诊断工具识别脆弱预测。基于NOTA扰动的评估是推动安全临床部署的有效路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究临床VLM的不确定性估计（UE）作为模型可靠性信号，探讨UE能否预测模型失败。这属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）主题高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical VQA uncertainty estimation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: Clinical VLM, Uncertainty estimation, NOTA perturbation, Model calibration, Diagnostic tool

Abstract: Safe deployment of clinical vision-language models (VLMs) requires reliable uncertainty estimation (UE): a signal indicating when predictions should be trusted or escalated to a clinician. We test whether current UE methods actually deliver this signal. Benchmarking 8 methods across 12 VLMs on clinical visual question-answering (VQA), we find that UE quality is not an intrinsic property of the UE method: it tracks model accuracy, degrading precisely where the model performance is weakest, and therefore where reliability is most needed. When we stress-test models by hiding the correct option among the multiple-choice answers (NOTA perturbations), accuracy collapses while uncertainty barely changes, leaving models systematically miscalibrated. Yet, we find that uncertainty on the unperturbed input reliably anticipates which predictions will collapse under NOTA, indicating that UE in current VLMs carries diagnostic information about model fragility. Our results position UE as a diagnostic tool for identifying fragile predictions and motivate perturbation-based evaluation as a path toward safe clinical deployment.

</details>


### [138] [LOCUS: Local Visual Cue Search for Enhancing Fine-Grained Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16586)
*Zhou Tao, Fang Zhang, Zewen Ding, Shida Wang, Xiaokun Sun, YongXiang Hua, Haoyu Cao, Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LOCUS提出一种训练框架，通过训练时提供局部裁剪作为视觉线索，并利用IoU奖励优化模型恢复其在全图中的空间位置，从而让MLLM内化细粒度证据搜索能力，在不改变推理接口的情况下提升细粒度感知和缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在细粒度视觉感知任务上不可靠，即使高分辨率输入保留了局部细节，模型仍可能在冗余视觉上下文中忽略关键证据（称为visual context rot）。现有方法依赖外部工具或额外推理步骤，缺乏让模型内化证据搜索能力的训练范式。

Method: LOCUS在训练时提供局部裁剪图像作为视觉线索，要求模型在全图中定位该裁剪的空间位置（即预测边界框），并使用IoU奖励进行强化学习优化。训练完成后，推理时仅使用标准图像-问题输入，无需额外线索或工具。

Result: 在细粒度感知、幻觉、通用理解和推理等多个基准上，LOCUS提升了定位敏感的视觉理解能力，同时保持了广泛能力。注意力分析表明模型更聚焦于任务相关的证据区域。

Conclusion: 训练时的视觉线索搜索为MLLM内化细粒度证据选择提供了一条有效路径，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升了细粒度感知的可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于提升MLLM的细粒度视觉感知能力，通过训练时局部视觉线索搜索来缓解模型在冗余视觉上下文中忽略关键证据的问题（即visual context rot），这与多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）中缓解视觉幻觉、提升忠实性的主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Addresses MLLM fine-grained perception and hallucination, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM fine-grained perception, hallucination mitigation, visual context rot, IoU reward training, no inference change

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable on fine-grained visual perception, even when high-resolution inputs preserve the necessary local details. We identify this limitation as visual context rot: decisive evidence may exist in the full image, yet fail to be reliably selected and used amid redundant visual context. We propose LOCUS (LOcal visual CUe Search), a training framework that teaches MLLMs to internalize local evidence search through a verifiable proxy task. During training, LOCUS provides a local crop as a visual cue and optimizes the model to recover its spatial support in the full image using an IoU-based reward. The visual cue is used only during training, leaving the standard image-question inference interface unchanged. Experiments across fine-grained perception, hallucination, general understanding, and reasoning benchmarks show that LOCUS improves localization-sensitive visual understanding while preserving broad capabilities. Attention analyses further indicate stronger focus on task-relevant evidence regions, suggesting that training-time visual cue search provides an effective route to internalized fine-grained evidence selection.

</details>


### [139] [Look Again Before You Abstain:Budgeted Conformal Evidence Acquisition for Reliable Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16667)
*Jian Xu, Delu Zeng, John Paisley, Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出预算约束的共形证据获取（BCEA）方法，通过将选择性预测从二选一（回答/弃权）扩展为三选一（回答/弃权/获取更多视觉证据），在保证幻觉率有界的前提下，显著降低弃权率，提升模型覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形过滤方法虽然能提供分布无关的幻觉率保证，但代价极高——在平衡目标存在性基准上，为将幻觉率控制在5%以下，需弃权超过80%的声明。这种弃权是浪费的，因为获取更多视觉证据（如缩放、裁剪）的成本很低。

Method: BCEA将选择性预测从二选一（回答/弃权）扩展为三选一（回答/弃权/获取证据）。核心发现：简单地将证据获取插入校准后的过滤器会破坏可交换性，导致统计保证失效（风险超目标最多17个百分点）。解决方案是将整个获取策略折叠到评分函数中，并在获取后的评分上重新校准，从而恢复有限样本保证。BCEA还使用结构化的、声明类型特定的干预策略。

Result: 在POPE基准和COCO构建的存在性/空间关系声明上，对四个开源VLM进行实验，BCEA在目标水平上控制幻觉率，并持续优于保证弃权的基线方法，显著提升了覆盖率。

Conclusion: 通过将证据获取策略纳入共形校准框架，可以在保持统计保证的同时有效降低弃权率，为可靠VLM提供更实用的选择性预测方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（LVLM）的幻觉问题，提出预算约束的共形证据获取（BCEA）方法，通过选择性预测和证据获取来保证幻觉率可控。这直接属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM hallucination with a principled conformal method, but the subtopic is not in your priority list and the method is not directly about continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: conformal prediction, selective prediction, hallucination mitigation, evidence acquisition, vision-language model reliability

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) hallucinate: they assert visual details that the image does not support. A principled remedy is selective prediction with a distribution-free guarantee-verify each claim and abstain when the claim is not grounded, so that the hallucination rate among asserted claims is provably bounded. We show, however, that this guarantee is bought at a brutal price: to keep the hallucination rate below $5\%$ on a balanced object-existence benchmark, a state-of-the-art conformal filter must abstain on more than $80\%$ of claims. We argue that abstention is wasteful when more visual evidence is cheaply available, and introduce Budgeted Conformal Evidence Acquisition (BCEA), which replaces the binary answer/abstain decision with a three-way choice: answer, abstain, or acquire additional visual evidence by re-examining the image (zooming, cropping, or applying a claim-specific intervention) under a bounded compute budget. We make two observations. First, acquisition that is plugged naively into a calibrated filter breaks the statistical guarantee -- realized risk overshoots the target by up to $17$ points -- because the acquisition step destroys the exchangeability that conformal calibration relies on. Second, folding the entire acquisition policy into the score function and re-calibrating on post-acquisition scores \emph{restores} the finite-sample guarantee while still recovering coverage. BCEA further uses structured, claim-type-specific interventions. Across the POPE benchmark and COCO-constructed existence and spatial-relation claims, on four open VLMs, BCEA controls the hallucination rate at the target level and consistently improves coverage over a guaranteed-abstention baseline.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_rl_alignment'></div>

## 多模态强化学习与对齐 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 RL、RLHF、DPO、偏好学习、多模态对齐。

### [140] [MatchLM2Lite: A Scalable MLLM-to-Lite Framework for Reproduced Content Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14786)
*Xiaotian Fan, Hiok Hian Ong, David Yuchen Wang, Zirui Zhu, Kanchan Sarkar, Kun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MatchLM2Lite 是一个面向视频平台的内容复制检测（RCI）系统，利用 MLLM 作为教师模型（MatchLM）蒸馏出轻量学生模型（MatchLite），联合建模视频、音频和文本信号进行成对视频的细粒度复制评分。MatchLite 在保持较高精度的同时将计算成本降低 35 倍，端到端延迟低于 30 秒，上线后使平台复制视频观看率降低 2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视频平台需要大规模检测低价值复制内容，以保障内容原创性、保护创作者并提升用户体验。现有方法难以在实时、高吞吐的生产环境中同时兼顾高精度和低延迟。

Method: 提出 MatchLM2Lite 两阶段框架：1) MatchLM 作为高容量 MLLM 教师模型，联合建模视频、音频和文本信号，对视频对进行细粒度复制评分；2) 通过知识蒸馏将 MatchLM 的能力迁移到轻量学生模型 MatchLite 中，实现低延迟、高吞吐的成对视频推理。

Result: MatchLM 相比之前生产模型 F1 提升 +8.57；蒸馏后 MatchLite 保留 +6.55 的 F1 提升，计算成本降低 35 倍；系统端到端延迟低于 30 秒，上线后复制视频观看率降低 2.5%，且不损害用户参与度。

Conclusion: MatchLM2Lite 证明了 MLLM 蒸馏到轻量模型在工业级内容复制检测中的有效性，能够在保持高精度的同时实现实时、大规模部署，为视频平台内容审核提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用MLLM作为教师模型，通过知识蒸馏将大模型能力迁移到轻量学生模型，用于视频内容复制检测（RCI），属于多模态模型的对齐与蒸馏优化，与多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的偏好学习、对齐蒸馏方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on content moderation distillation, not core user priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: MLLM distillation, content moderation, video-audio-text modeling, production deployment, not autoregressive generation

Abstract: Content moderation is critical for online video platforms to ensure content safety, protect creators, and sustain positive user experiences. Beyond filtering harmful content, platforms must guarantee content authenticity at scale so that users are exposed to diverse, original videos rather than low-value reproductions. We present MatchLM2Lite, a real-time, production-grade reproduced content identification (RCI) system that leverages the powerful understanding of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) distilled into a small and fast-inference model. Our system jointly models video, audio, and text signals, operating on pairs of videos to produce fine-grained reproduction scores. The system comprises two modules, MatchLM and MatchLite, and a two-stage training recipe. First, our high-capacity MLLM, MatchLM, serves as a teacher model to define the upper bound of RCI performance. Its capabilities are then distilled into a compact student model, MatchLite. This design allows MatchLite to deliver low-latency, high-throughput inference on video pairs while preserving much of MatchLM's accuracy, making it suitable for integration into real-time recommendation systems. MatchLM achieves an F1-score improvement of +8.57 compared to our previous production model. After knowledge distillation, MatchLite retains a +6.55 gain in F1-score while reducing computational cost by 35x. Deployed at scale, MatchLM2Lite enables efficient, pairwise multimodal RCI, stably serving online traffic at high queries per second (QPS) with an end-to-end latency below 30 seconds. This system has reduced the reproduced video view rate on our platform by 2.5% without degrading user engagement, demonstrating its effectiveness in a large-scale production environment.

</details>


### [141] [MAF: Multimodal Adaptive Few-shot Prompting for Sentiment Analysis with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15694)
*Hangling Xie*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (67.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAF（Multimodal Adaptive Few-Shot Prompting）框架，通过动态检索与查询相关的多模态演示示例，并利用轻量级系数生成网络实时计算加权融合权重，结合多数投票策略，提升 MLLM 在多模态情感分析任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在情感分析中对提示设计高度敏感，静态统一的提示难以捕捉不同输入中变化的细微多模态线索，因此需要一种自适应、上下文敏感的少样本提示方法。

Method: MAF 框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 演示检索模块，综合编码面部表情、场景上下文和文本语义，并引入唇部运动幅度检测机制以在多说话人场景中准确识别说话人；(2) 轻量级系数生成网络，实时输出查询条件化的融合权重，实现多模态相似度分数的加权聚合，检索 top-K 最相关演示；(3) 多数投票策略，对 MLLM 生成的多个候选输出进行投票以提升预测稳定性。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAF 相比对应的骨干变体取得了显著且一致的性能提升，并与强多模态情感分析基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: MAF 通过自适应检索和加权融合查询相关演示，有效提升了 MLLM 在多模态情感分析中的表现，验证了动态少样本提示策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型（MLLM）在情感分析中的少样本提示（few-shot prompting）方法，通过动态检索与查询相关的多模态演示示例来提升情感推理能力，属于多模态对齐与提示优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on sentiment analysis prompting, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: sentiment analysis, few-shot prompting, multimodal large language models, dynamic retrieval, weighted fusion

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding complex multimodal content. However, their performance in sentiment analysis exhibits acute sensitivity to prompt design, rendering static, uniformly applied prompts inherently suboptimal for capturing the nuanced multimodal cues that vary across inputs. To address this limitation, we propose a Multimodal Adaptive Few-Shot Prompting (MAF) framework, which dynamically retrieves and integrates query-relevant demonstrations to elicit the sentiment reasoning capabilities of MLLMs in a context-sensitive manner. MAF constructs a demonstration retrieval module that holistically encodes facial expressions, scene context, and textual semantics, with a lip movement amplitude detection mechanism introduced for accurate speaker identification in multi-person scenarios. Departing from conventional fixed-weight fusion, a lightweight coefficient generation network is trained to output query-conditioned fusion weights in real time, enabling weighted aggregation of multimodal similarity scores to retrieve the top-K most informative demonstrations. Prediction stability is further enhanced through majority voting over multiple candidate outputs generated by the MLLM. Extensive experiments on public benchmark datasets demonstrate that MAF achieves substantial and consistent performance improvements over the corresponding backbone variants and remains competitive with strong multimodal sentiment-analysis baselines.

</details>


### [142] [SHARD: Safe and Helpful Alignment via Self-Reframing Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15517)
*Viswonathan Manoranjan, Amogh Gupta, Anvesh Rao Vijjini, Thomas Hofweber, Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SHARD 提出一种自重构蒸馏方法，通过将敏感提示重写为揭示良性意图的表述，再将模型原始回答重构为安全且更有帮助的回复，最后在自重构数据上微调模型，从而在保持安全性的同时提升有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理敏感提示时往往要么直接拒绝回答，要么给出通用的安全套话，无法满足用户合法的信息需求。现有方法难以在安全性和有用性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: SHARD 包含三步：(1) 利用哲学准则（如密尔伤害原则、言论自由原则）将敏感提示重写为揭示良性意图的表述；(2) 将模型对原始提示的回答重构为安全且更有帮助的版本；(3) 在自重构的回答上对模型进行微调，使模型内化安全且有用的行为。

Result: 在 DNA 和 LINGUASAFE（英语子集）数据集上，SHARD 在大多数模型族上提升了有用性，同时保持了安全性。该方法与使用更大教师模型进行蒸馏的效果相当，表明模型可以从自身生成的安全有用行为中学习。

Conclusion: SHARD 证明模型可以通过自我重构蒸馏的方式，在不牺牲安全性的前提下显著提升对敏感提示的有用性回应，且无需依赖外部教师模型，具有较好的实用性和可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM的安全与有用性对齐方法（SHARD：自重构蒸馏），属于对齐（alignment）范畴。虽然论文仅涉及文本LLM而非多模态，但目标方向中 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" 是唯一涵盖对齐方法的子主题，且该子主题的描述包含RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法，SHARD作为一种蒸馏对齐方法与之合理相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM safety alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: LLM safety alignment, self-reframing distillation, helpfulness improvement, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Large language models often struggle with sensitive prompts. They may refuse outright, provide generic safety boilerplate, or fail to address the user's legitimate informational needs that can be answered safely. We introduce SHARD, a self-reframing distillation method to improve safe-helpfulness. It first rewrites sensitive prompts to surface benign intent using philosophical guidelines, then reframes its original responses into safe, more helpful ones, and finally fine-tunes the model on its self-reframed responses. Across DNA and the English subset of LINGUASAFE, SHARD improves helpfulness for most model families while preserving safety. It also remains competitive with distillation from a larger teacher model, suggesting that models can internalize safe and helpful behavior elicited from their own. Warning: This paper contains content that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [143] [BALTO: Balanced Token-Level Policy Optimization for Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15893)
*Ning Li, Zixuan Guo, Yan Xu, Wenbo Fei, Yifan Niu, Chang Luo, Yasheng Wang, Weiwen Liu, Yong Yu, Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BALTO提出了一种平衡的token级策略优化框架，通过将细粒度事实性验证与token级信用分配相结合，有效缓解大模型幻觉问题，在忠实性与信息性之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的幻觉缓解方法使用response级忠实性奖励存在粒度不匹配问题：局部幻觉会导致对整体响应的错误惩罚。虽然已有工作引入细粒度反馈（如claim级验证、token级奖励），但不平衡的信用分配仍会引入长度、冗长或优化噪声偏差。

Method: BALTO框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 从生成响应中提取可验证的事实性claims，并对照参考上下文进行验证；(2) 将claim级的判断结果投影到token级标签；(3) 引入平衡的token级信用分配机制，将概率质量从无依据内容重新分配到忠实内容上，而非抑制整个响应。论文还从理论上分析了response级奖励的局限性，并证明了BALTO在训练稳定性和优化效率上的优势。

Result: 在ConFiQA、RAGTruth和FinLLM-Eval三个基准上的实验表明，BALTO在所有6个模型-基准设置中均取得了最高的忠实性分数，在Q-Score上持续优于现有后训练基线方法，展现了更强的忠实性-信息性权衡。

Conclusion: BALTO通过平衡的token级策略优化有效缓解了幻觉问题，证明了细粒度、平衡的信用分配机制在忠实性优化中的有效性，为LLM在知识密集型场景中的可靠部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出BALTO框架，使用强化学习（token-level policy optimization）来缓解大模型的幻觉问题，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴。虽然论文主要针对LLM，但其方法（token级RL、偏好优化、忠实性奖励）与多模态RL对齐方向高度相关，且该方向涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等广义对齐方法，因此复用[多模态强化学习与对齐]这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM hallucination mitigation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Token-level policy optimization, Hallucination mitigation, RL for faithfulness, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Hallucinations remain a major obstacle to deploying large language models (LLMs) in knowledge-intensive settings, where generated responses must be faithfully grounded in provided evidence. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a promising direction for hallucination mitigation, but response-level faithfulness rewards suffer from a granularity mismatch: localized hallucinations can cause supported content to receive spurious penalties. Although recent work introduces fine-grained feedback such as claim-level verification and token-level rewards, unbalanced credit assignment can still induce length, verbosity, or optimization-noise biases. We propose BALTO, a Balanced Token-level Policy Optimization framework for hallucination mitigation. BALTO extracts checkable factual claims, verifies them against the reference context, and projects claim-level judgments to token-level labels. A balanced token-level credit assignment mechanism is introduced into the framework. This design redistributes probability mass from unsupported content toward faithful content, rather than suppressing the entire response. We systematically analyze the limitations of response-level rewards from a theoretical standpoint, and prove BALTO's advantages in training stability and optimization efficiency for hallucination mitigation. Experiments on ConFiQA, RAGTruth, and FinLLM-Eval show that BALTO achieves the highest faithfulness across all six model--benchmark settings and consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in Q-Score, demonstrating a stronger faithfulness--informativeness trade-off.

</details>


### [144] [SpecAlign: Efficient Specification-Grounded Alignment of Large Language Models via Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16276)
*Wenjie Wang, Yue Huang, Zhengqing Yuan, Han Bao, Shiyi Du, Yuchen Ma, Yue Zhao, Yanfang Ye, Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出规范对齐（specification-grounded alignment）新范式，并实现SpecAlign框架，通过从模型规范文档中合成对齐数据（结构化规则标注、可控规范实例化、多智能体对抗数据合成），生成细粒度偏好对来训练LLM，使其行为精确遵循提供商或应用特定的结构化规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法通常基于单一普适的安全/有用性概念或静态基准，缺乏系统机制来将提供商或应用特定的、长篇幅、结构化且频繁更新的模型规范作为训练信号进行对齐。

Method: SpecAlign框架包含三个核心组件：1）结构化规则标注（Structured Rule Annotation）——将规范文档解析为结构化规则；2）可控规范实例化（Controllable Specification Instantiation）——基于规则生成合规行为样本；3）多智能体对抗数据合成（Multi-Agent Adversarial Data Synthesis）——通过多智能体对抗生成有意义的违规样本，构建边界感知的偏好对。

Result: 在多种模型规范和多个骨干模型上的实验表明，使用SpecAlign训练能持续提升规则合规性，同时保持通用能力并避免过度保守行为。

Conclusion: 将对齐过程显式地建立在模型规范之上，能够实现快速、精确且可扩展的LLM行为适应，以应对不断变化的策略需求。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于模型规范的对齐方法（SpecAlign），属于LLM对齐领域。虽然论文主要聚焦文本LLM，但其对齐范式（specification-grounded alignment）与多模态对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）在方法论上高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖RLHF、偏好学习、对齐等核心概念，是最佳匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM alignment via synthetic data, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: synthetic data, alignment, specification-grounded, LLM, multi-agent adversarial

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, alignment is no longer governed by a single universal notion of safety or helpfulness, but instead by provider- or application-specific model specifications. These specifications are typically long, structured, and frequently updated, yet existing alignment pipelines lack a systematic mechanism to operationalize them as training signals. In this paper, we propose specification-grounded alignment, a new alignment paradigm that treats provider-authored model specifications as the primary alignment target rather than abstract principles or static benchmarks. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce SpecAlign, a framework that synthesizes alignment data directly from specification documents. SpecAlign combines structured rule annotation, controllable specification instantiation, and multi-agent adversarial data synthesis to generate fine-grained, boundary-aware preference pairs that capture both compliant behaviors and meaningful specification violations. Experiments across multiple model specifications and backbone models demonstrate that training with SpecAlign consistently improves rule compliance while preserving general capabilities and avoiding over-conservative behavior. These results suggest that grounding alignment in explicit model specifications enables rapid, precise, and scalable adaptation of LLM behavior to evolving policy requirements.

</details>


### [145] [Context-Aware RL for Agentic and Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17053)
*Peiyang Xu, Bangzheng Li, Sijia Liu, Karthik R. Narasimhan, Pramod Viswanath, Prateek Mittal, Xingyu Fu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (56.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ContextRL 提出一种上下文感知的强化学习方法，通过间接辅助目标（让模型从两个相似上下文中选择支持查询-答案对的那个）来提升 LLM 在长程推理和多模态问答中的细粒度上下文定位能力，在编程智能体和视觉问答任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在需要从长上下文或复杂上下文中识别微小但关键证据时经常失败（如工具追踪中的一行代码或图像中的细微细节），现有 RL 方法仅监督最终答案，缺乏对细粒度上下文定位的显式激励。

Method: 提出 ContextRL，一种上下文感知的 RL 方法。核心是间接辅助目标：给定查询、答案和两个高度相似的上下文，模型需选择支持该查询-答案对的正确上下文。在编程智能体领域，用轨迹作为上下文构建 1K 对比对；在多模态推理领域，用图像作为上下文通过生成式编辑和相似性搜索构建 7K 对比对。该方法在标准 GRPO 基础上增加上下文选择奖励。

Result: 在 5 个长程推理基准上平均提升 +2.2%，在 12 个多样化 VQA 基准上平均提升 +1.8%。与使用相同对比数据作为标准查询-上下文-答案示例的数据增强基线相比，ContextRL 的增益显著，证明提升来自上下文选择目标而非额外数据本身。

Conclusion: ContextRL 通过间接的上下文选择目标有效提升了 LLM 在长上下文和多模态场景中的细粒度定位能力，验证了该辅助目标优于单纯增加数据量，为 RL 训练多模态 LLM 提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ContextRL，一种上下文感知的强化学习方法，用于提升 LLM 的长程推理和多模态性能，核心是多模态 RL 与偏好学习，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel RL objective for fine-grained grounding but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Context-aware RL, multimodal grounding, long-horizon reasoning, contrastive context data, GRPO improvement

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often fail when answering requires identifying a small but decisive piece of evidence within a long or complex context, such as a single line in a tool trace or a subtle detail in an image. We propose ContextRL, a context-aware reinforcement learning (RL) method that improves long-horizon reasoning and multimodal performance through an \emph{indirect} auxiliary objective. Instead of supervising only the final answer, ContextRL presents the model with a query, an answer, and two highly similar contexts, and rewards it for selecting the context that supports the query--answer pair, thereby encouraging fine-grained grounding. We construct contrastive context data in two domains: for coding agents, trajectories serve as contexts, yielding 1k pairs built via condition filtering; for multimodal reasoning, images serve as contexts, yielding 7K pairs built via generative editing and similarity search. ContextRL achieves average gains of +2.2% over standard GRPO on 5 long-horizon benchmarks, and +1.8% across 12 diverse visual question answering benchmarks. To disentangle the effect of the proposed objective from that of additional data, we compare against data-augmentation baselines that repurpose the same contrastive contexts as standard query--context--answer examples. These baselines provide little to no improvement, showing that the gains arise from the proposed context-selection objective rather than from the contrastive data alone.

</details>


### [146] [OmniOPSD: Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Affective Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15920)
*Zebang Cheng, Shuimu Chen, Boxue Yang, Yuanshen Guan, Jingyi Chen, Zheng Lian, Xiaojiang Peng, Fei Ma, LaiZhong Cui, Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniOPSD 提出了一种基于 rationale 特权的 on-policy 自蒸馏框架，利用前沿模型生成的证据感知 rationale 作为教师信号，为 MLLM 在多模态情感计算任务中提供密集的 token 级监督，缓解强化学习中的奖励稀疏问题，在 MER-UniBench 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂推理任务中面临严重的奖励稀疏问题，尤其在涉及状态、情感、意图和行为的以人为中心场景中，异质多模态信号和主观人为因素使得高质量思维链标注昂贵且难以获取。直接使用专家标注的 ground-truth 标签进行监督微调可能鼓励多模态感知中的捷径学习，且对安全关键的人机交互缺乏透明度。

Method: 提出 OmniOPSD 框架，包含：(1) 利用前沿模型（如 GPT-4o）生成证据感知 rationale 作为教师侧的 privileged evidence；(2) 学生模型从原始多模态输入中采样自己的 rollout；(3) rationale-privileged 教师对相同 token 进行评分，提供密集的 token 级监督；(4) 学生在其自身轨迹分布上学习，不直接模仿前沿模型的完整输出，推理时无需标签、rationale、CoT 标注或闭源模型访问。

Result: 在 MER-UniBench 上，OmniOPSD 以平均 84.19 分达到 SOTA 性能，消融实验进一步验证了 rationale-privileged 教师指导的价值。

Conclusion: OmniOPSD 通过 rationale-privileged on-policy self-distillation 有效缓解了多模态情感计算中 RL 的奖励稀疏问题，在不依赖推理时额外标注或闭源模型的情况下提升了性能，为安全关键的人机交互提供了更透明的多模态推理框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 OmniOPSD，一种基于 on-policy self-distillation 的框架，利用前沿模型生成的 rationale 作为教师信号为 MLLM 提供密集奖励，解决多模态情感计算中强化学习的奖励稀疏问题。核心贡献在于多模态 RL 中的奖励设计与对齐，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on affective computing and MLLM alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or video generation. Signals: MLLM reinforcement learning, reward sparsity, on-policy self-distillation, affective computing, MER-UniBench SOTA

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is often hindered by severe reward sparsity in complex reasoning tasks. This challenge is particularly pronounced in human-centered scenarios involving states, emotions, intentions, and behaviors, where heterogeneous multimodal signals and subjective human factors make high-quality chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations expensive and difficult to obtain. Although many multimodal datasets provide expert-annotated ground-truth labels, directly using these labels for supervised fine-tuning may encourage shortcut learning in multimodal perception and provides limited transparency for safety-critical human--AI interaction. To address these limitations, we propose OmniOPSD, a Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation framework that uses frontier-generated rationales as teacher-side privileged evidence rather than student imitation targets. OmniOPSD uses frontier-generated evidence-aware rationales only as training-time privileged evidence context for a local teacher. The student samples its own rollout from the original multimodal input, while the rationale-privileged teacher scores the same tokens and provides dense token-level supervision. Thus, the student learns on its own trajectory distribution without directly imitating frontier-model completions, and inference requires no labels, rationales, CoT annotations, or closed-source model access. Experiments on MER-UniBench show that OmniOPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average score of $84.19$, and ablations further support the value of rationale-privileged teacher guidance.

</details>


### [147] [Multimodal Evaluator Preference Collapse: Cross-Modal Contagion in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16682)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态自演化智能体中存在评估者偏好崩溃（EPC）现象，且在多模态设置下被显著放大（单一策略吸收48.4%权重，是纯文本的3.2倍）。作者提出跨模态传染（cross-modal contagion）新现象，即在一个模态上习得的评估者偏好会转移到另一个模态并破坏策略选择。通过四阶段隔离训练范式测量传染系数，发现跨模型评估产生强双向传染，高轮次导致单策略主导崩溃，而自评估几乎完全免疫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI智能体使用语言模型在反馈循环中自我评估输出时，会产生系统性偏差。现有研究主要关注纯文本场景，但多模态设置下这种偏差如何被放大、模态间偏好如何相互影响尚不清楚。

Method: 使用GPT-4o评估DeepSeek-chat在文本和视觉任务上的表现，设计四阶段隔离训练范式测量跨模态传染系数，在四种评估器配置下进行统计验证（N=53次独立重复，15,592次API调用），引入传染矩阵（contagion matrix）进行量化分析。

Result: 单一策略step_by_step吸收48.4%权重（纯文本的3.2倍）；跨模型评估（GPT-4o）产生强对称双向传染（gamma_T->V=1.176, gamma_V->T=1.089）；高轮次（50轮）导致70%零传染的单策略主导崩溃；自评估（DeepSeek-chat）97%运行零传染。

Conclusion: 多模态设置显著放大评估者偏好崩溃；跨模态传染是真实存在的现象，且跨模型评估架构是偏好传染的主要风险因素；自评估具有近乎完全的免疫性。研究为多模态自演化系统的安全设计提供了重要启示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态自演化智能体中评估者偏好崩溃（EPC）与跨模态偏好传染现象，核心关注多模态评估中的偏好学习与对齐问题，属于多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）范畴。

Importance reason: Novel finding on cross-modal contagion in self-evolution, but focuses on RL alignment rather than user's priority subtopics. Signals: Evaluator Preference Collapse, Cross-modal contagion, Multimodal self-evolution, Preference collapse amplification, Contagion matrix

Abstract: When AI agents use language models to evaluate their own outputs in a feedback loop, systematic biases emerge. We show that Evaluator Preference Collapse (EPC) is dramatically amplified in multimodal settings. Using GPT-4o to evaluate DeepSeek-chat across text and visual tasks, we find that a single strategy (step_by_step) absorbs 48.4% of all weight -- 3.2x the collapse observed in text-only self-evaluation -- while three visual-domain strategies receive only 9.1% combined weight. We then demonstrate a novel phenomenon we term cross-modal contagion: evaluator preferences acquired on one modality transfer to and corrupt strategy selection on another. Through a four-phase isolation training paradigm, we measure contagion coefficients and document strategy inversion -- the optimal strategy for a modality reverses after cross-modal exposure. A Phase 3 statistical validation across four evaluator configurations (N=53 total independent repetitions, 15,592 API calls) reveals a clear hierarchy: cross-model evaluation (GPT-4o, N=8) produces strong but symmetric bidirectional contagion (mean gamma_{T->V}=1.176, gamma_{V->T}=1.089, Delta=-0.088, p=0.575, Cohen's d=0.29); high round counts (DashScope, 50 rounds) cause collapse to single-strategy dominance (70% zero contagion); and self-evaluation provides near-complete immunity -- 97% of runs (N=30, DeepSeek-chat) yield exactly zero contagion (mean gamma=0.033, 95% CI [-0.031, 0.010], p=0.642, d=0.07). No evaluator condition shows statistically significant directional asymmetry. We introduce the contagion matrix indexed by evaluator identity, release the MM-EPC experimental framework, and identify cross-model evaluator architecture as the primary risk factor for preference contagion.

</details>


### [148] [KeepLoRA++: Continual Learning with Layer-Scaled Residual Gradient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16256)
*Mao-Lin Luo, Yi-Lin Zhang, Zi-Hao Zhou, Yankun Hong, Xialiang Tong, Mingxuan Yuan, Tong Wei, Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KeepLoRA++ 提出了一种层缩放残差梯度自适应方法，通过分析Transformer中知识分布的层间与层内结构特性（浅层/主子空间编码通用知识，深层/残差子空间编码任务特定知识），将新任务的LoRA参数更新限制在残差子空间并配合浅到深层缩放，实现了视觉语言模型持续学习中预训练知识保留、旧任务知识保留与新任务可塑性的三重平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习视觉语言模型需要同时平衡三个相互竞争的目标：保留预训练知识、保留已学任务序列的知识、以及保持获取新知识的可塑性。现有方法难以同时满足这三者。

Method: 提出KeepLoRA++，包含双维度知识保留机制：(1) 层间维度：浅层编码通用可迁移知识，深层编码任务特定适应，因此采用浅到深的层缩放策略，浅层更新幅度小、深层更新幅度大；(2) 层内维度：参数主子空间编码通用知识，残差子空间编码任务特定知识，因此将新任务的LoRA梯度投影到与预训练主子空间和旧任务特征主导方向正交的残差子空间上。

Result: 在图像分类、视觉问答和视频理解任务上，KeepLoRA++ 持续优于代表性基线方法，成功平衡了三个竞争目标。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证，KeepLoRA++ 有效解决了视觉语言模型持续学习中的三重目标平衡问题，为预训练模型的知识保留与持续适应提供了统一框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型的持续学习（continual learning），核心是平衡预训练知识保留、旧任务知识保留与新任务可塑性三个目标，属于多模态模型的对齐与优化范畴。虽然不直接涉及RLHF/DPO，但"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）是现有[CANONICAL]中最接近的类别，涵盖多模态模型的学习策略与知识保留对齐问题。

Importance reason: Focuses on continual learning for vision-language models, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: continual learning, vision-language model, LoRA, knowledge retention, layer-scaled gradient

Abstract: Continual learning for pre-trained vision-language models requires balancing three competing objectives: retaining pre-trained knowledge, preserving knowledge from a sequence of learned tasks, and maintaining the plasticity to acquire new knowledge. This paper presents KeepLoRA++, balancing these objectives through a unified dual-dimensional knowledge retention mechanism. We analyze knowledge distribution of Transformer architecture from both inter-layer and intra-layer perspectives. The inter-layer perspective examines how retention is distributed across layers, while the intra-layer perspective focuses on the parameter space within each layer. Our analysis reveals a structural property: general transferable knowledge is mainly encoded in the shallow layers and the principal subspace of the parameters, while task-specific adaptations are localized in the deep layers and the residual subspace. Motivated by this insight, KeepLoRA++ introduces a layer-scaled residual gradient adaptation method. New tasks are learned by restricting LoRA parameter updates to the residual subspace, combined with a shallow-to-deep layer scaling, to prevent interference with previously acquired capabilities. Specifically, the gradient of a new task is projected onto a subspace orthogonal to both the principal subspace of the pre-trained model and the dominant directions of previous task features, while simultaneously assigning smaller update magnitudes to shallow layers and larger ones to deeper layers. Our theoretical analysis and empirical evaluations confirm that KeepLoRA++ successfully balances these three competing objectives, consistently outperforming representative baselines across image classification, visual question answering, and video understanding tasks.

</details>


### [149] [DifferAD-R1: A Difference-Guided IndustrialAnomaly Localization with Multimodal LargeLanguage Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16601)
*Dingrong Wang, Xian Tao, Zhen Qu, Hengliang Luo, Xinyi Gong, Fei Shen, Zhengtao Zhang, Guiguang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DifferAD-R1 提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的强化学习框架，通过差异引导的双图范式将工业异常定位转化为一次性差异定位问题，并设计了双一致性定位奖励与难度感知策略，在工业异常定位任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常定位方法存在两大局限：传统闭集方法跨场景泛化能力差；基于MLLM的方法要么采用问答范式与定位需求不匹配，要么使用标准GRPO优化方法无法为细微缺陷提供有效学习信号。因此需要一种更适合工业异常定位的MLLM增强框架。

Method: 提出DifferAD-R1框架：(1) 差异引导双图范式，将定位任务重构为一次性差异定位问题，利用正常与异常图像的差异进行定位；(2) 双一致性定位奖励（DCLR），针对难检测异常设计，增强优化稳定性与鲁棒性；(3) 难度感知策略，包含自适应重加权与分组重采样，优先学习困难样本；(4) 构建AD-DualDiff数据集，包含13K配对图像覆盖20个类别。

Result: 实验结果表明，DifferAD-R1显著优于现有基线，并与大规模模型Qwen3-VL（235B参数）相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DifferAD-R1通过差异引导范式与强化学习优化，有效解决了工业异常定位中跨场景泛化与细微缺陷检测的挑战，为MLLM在工业视觉检测中的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的强化学习框架用于工业异常定位，核心贡献在于设计差异引导的双图范式与双一致性定位奖励，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Focuses on industrial anomaly localization, a niche application not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: MLLM for anomaly localization, GRPO optimization, dual-image paradigm, industrial application, not autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: Industrial anomaly localization aims to accurately identify and localize abnormal regions in industrial products, addressing the critical challenge of detecting unseen defect categories in real-world scenarios. Traditional closed-set methods often suffer from poor cross-scenario generalization, while existingMultimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approachesface two core limitations: they either adopt QA-style paradigmsmisaligned with the practical demands of localization, or relyon standard optimization techniques such as Group RelativePolicy Optimization (GRPO), which fails to deliver effectivelearning signals for subtle defects. To tackle these issues, thispaper proposes DifferAD-R1, an MLLM-augmented reinforcement learning framework tailored for industrial anomaly localization. We design a Difference-Guided dual-image paradigm,which reformulates the localization task as a one-shot difference grounding problem to effectively explore cross-scenarioanomalies. A Dual-Consistency Localization Reward is developedfor hard-to-detect anomalies, enhancing optimization stabilityand robustness. Additionally, we integrate a difficulty-awarestrategy with adaptive reweighting and group-wise resamplingto prioritize learning on challenging instances. To facilitateevaluations in real-world industrial settings, we construct theAD-DualDiff dataset, comprising 13K paired images across 20categories. Experimental results demonstrate that DifferADR1 significantly outperforms existing baselines and achievescompetitive performance compared to large-scale models likeQwen3-VL (235B parameters). Our code is publicly availableat: .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_reasoning_cot'></div>

### [150] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang, Changtao Miao, Jinbo Su, Zhaowen Zhou, Chunxia Zhang, Xukai Wang, Ruiqi Liu, Kaiyuan Zheng, Jiansheng Cai, Bo Zhang, Zhe Li, Shiming Xiang, Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Visual-Seeker 提出了一种视觉原生的多模态深度搜索智能体，通过主动视觉推理机制，在搜索过程中动态关注细粒度视觉细节并收集视觉证据，在五个多模态搜索基准上达到 SOTA，超越部分闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在复杂开放场景中缺乏事实性 grounding，而多模态搜索智能体主要依赖简单图像和纯文本证据轨迹，缺乏视觉原生的多跳跨模态推理与搜索能力。

Method: 提出 Visual-Seeker 视觉原生多模态深度搜索智能体，将视觉视为动态输入而非静态输入，在搜索过程中主动关注细粒度视觉细节并动态收集视觉证据。设计主动视觉推理数据流水线，合成 5K 高质量多模态轨迹用于模型训练。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上达到 SOTA 性能，甚至超越多个闭源模型，验证了视觉原生推理与搜索在真实网络环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: Visual-Seeker 通过主动视觉推理实现了视觉原生的多模态深度搜索，证明了在复杂开放场景中动态收集视觉证据对于提升多模态搜索智能体性能的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Visual-Seeker，一种通过主动视觉推理进行多模态深度搜索的智能体，核心是多跳跨模态推理与视觉证据追踪，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Advances multimodal search with active visual reasoning, but is not directly aligned with top-priority continuous language models or generation tasks. Signals: multimodal search agent, active visual reasoning, visual-native search, SOTA on benchmarks, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: .

</details>


### [151] [Formalize Once, Edit the Rest: Efficient Lean-Based Answer Selection for Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15972)
*Ji Feng, Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出BASE方法，通过先形式化一个候选答案再编辑其余答案的方式，在数学推理中利用Lean进行高效答案选择，同时提升准确率并降低形式化成本约5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Lean的答案选择方法需要对每个候选答案独立进行自动形式化，计算成本高昂。作者希望设计一种更高效的流水线，在保持或提升选择准确率的同时大幅减少形式化开销。

Method: 提出BASE（base-and-edit）流水线：对每个问题只形式化一个基础候选答案，训练LEANSCRIBE重写模型定位基础形式化中的答案表达式并生成可复用的编辑函数，将其余K-1个候选答案的表达式替换到基础形式化中，从而避免重复调用自动形式化模型。

Result: 在4个基准、3个求解器、12种配置下，BASE同时提升了答案选择准确率并降低了形式化成本，在K=8时自动形式化调用减少约5倍，且随K增大收益更大。

Conclusion: BASE通过"一次形式化，编辑其余"的策略，实现了数学推理中Lean验证的高效答案选择，是帕累托改进，为大规模测试时计算扩展提供了更实用的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注大语言模型在数学推理中的答案选择，利用Lean形式化验证提升推理可靠性，属于多模态推理与思维链（数学/科学推理）范畴，因此映射到CANONICAL subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"。

Importance reason: Focuses on Lean-based math verification, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: math reasoning, formal verification, Lean, answer selection, cost reduction

Abstract: With large language models (LLMs) increasingly applied to mathematical reasoning, formal proof assistants such as Lean can be leveraged to verify reasoning outputs with machine-checkable rigor, enabling use cases such as answer selection in test-time scaling with K sampled candidate answers. However, employing Lean requires that LLM outputs, originally in natural language, first be formalized. Existing Lean-based answer-selection work uses an autoformalization model to generate a formal statement in Lean for each candidate answer independently, incurring a significant computational cost. We propose BASE, a base-and-edit pipeline that formalizes a single base candidate per problem and derives the remaining K-1 statements by editing the answer expression in place. To facilitate this, we train a rewriter model LEANSCRIBE to localize the answer in the base formalization and generate a reusable edit function for the other K-1 candidates. BASE simultaneously improves selection accuracy and reduces formalization cost - a Pareto improvement that holds on all 12 (dataset, solver) configurations across four benchmarks and three solvers, cutting autoformalizer calls by about 5x at K=8, with the reduction expected to become larger as K grows. Code is available at .

</details>


### [152] [RoboPIN: Grounded Embodied Reasoning via Pinned Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15753)
*Yaoting Huang, Yifu Yuan, Linqi Han, Chengwen Li, Shuoheng Zhang, Xianze Yao, Hongyao Tang, Yan Zheng, Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PinCoT（Pinned Chain-of-Thought），一种将具身推理的每一步都锚定到视觉证据的结构化推理范式。通过引入"推理锚点"（ReasoningAnchor）将每个实体绑定到包含实体名称、唯一标识、视图索引和空间定位的结构化视觉锚点上，实现跨步骤和跨视图的一致实体追踪。作者构建了自动化数据生成管道，并训练了 RoboPIN 模型（4B参数），在14个基准上超越7B级开源具身模型，平均提升12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在具身推理中依赖纯文本或坐标增强的思维链，实体引用隐式且模糊，导致推理过程与视觉证据脱钩、实体引用跨步骤漂移、推理轨迹与最终答案之间存在因果断裂，在多视图场景下问题更为严重。

Method: 提出 PinCoT 结构化推理范式，引入 ReasoningAnchor 概念将每个实体绑定到包含名称、唯一ID、视图索引和空间定位的结构化视觉锚点。构建自动化数据生成管道生成 PinCoT 格式数据集。通过三阶段后训练（渐进注入具身知识→结构化推理能力→过程监督对齐）训练 RoboPIN 模型，奖励函数直接约束推理过程中的锚点定位和身份一致性。

Result: 在14个涵盖具身空间推理、多视图推理和指点的基准上，仅4B参数的 RoboPIN 一致超越7B级开源具身模型，相比最强7B基线 Mimo-Embodied 平均提升12%。进一步分析表明 PinCoT 提高了定位准确性和跨步骤身份一致性，验证了过程监督的有效性。

Conclusion: PinCoT 通过将推理步骤显式锚定到视觉证据，有效解决了具身推理中实体引用模糊和跨步骤漂移的问题。过程监督对齐进一步增强了推理的因果一致性和定位准确性，为具身多模态推理提供了新的结构化范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Pinned Chain-of-Thought (PinCoT)，将推理步骤锚定到视觉证据上，属于多模态推理与思维链方向。该论文也涉及具身世界模型中的空间推理，因此同时匹配 world_model 方向下的 embodied_world_model 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied reasoning with chain-of-thought, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Embodied reasoning, Chain-of-thought, Visual grounding, Multi-view reasoning, Process supervision

Abstract: Embodied reasoning requires models to perceive task-relevant objects and spaces in physical environments and maintain consistent visual grounding throughout multi-step reasoning. However, current vision-language models rely on text-only or coordinate-augmented chain-of-thought, where entity references remain implicit and ambiguous. This may cause the reasoning process to decouple from visual evidence, entity references to drift across steps, and a causal disconnection between the reasoning trajectory and the final answer, with these problems further amplified in multi-view scenarios due to cross-view appearance changes. To address these issues, we propose Pinned Chain-of-Thought (\pincot{}), a structured reasoning paradigm that pins every reasoning step to visual evidence. \pincot{} introduces the concept of \reasoninganchor{}, which binds each task-relevant entity to a structured visual anchor with entity name, unique identity, view index, and spatial grounding, enabling consistent entity tracking across reasoning steps and views. We build a fully automated data generation pipeline to construct \dataset{}, a high-quality \pincot{}-formatted reasoning dataset. We then train \method{} through three-stage post-training that progressively injects embodied knowledge, structured reasoning ability, and process-supervised alignment, with rewards that directly constrain both anchor localization and identity consistency during reasoning. On 14 benchmarks covering embodied spatial reasoning, multi-view reasoning, and pointing, \method{} with only 4B parameters consistently outperforms 7B level open-source embodied models, achieving a 12\% average improvement over the strongest 7B baseline, Mimo-Embodied. Further analysis shows that \pincot{} improves grounding accuracy and cross-step identity consistency, validating the effectiveness of process supervision.

</details>


### [153] [Thinking with Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16122)
*Junkai Zhang, Yihe Deng, Kai-Wei Chang, Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (67.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉接地思维（visually grounded thinking），让VLM在推理时用自然语言交织显式的点或框接地来指代图像证据。通过可扩展的合成管道蒸馏推理轨迹并用SAM3提取接地监督，结合接地感知强化学习（结合答案正确性奖励和密集接地奖励），在计数和空间推理基准上显著提升Gemma3-4B-IT性能，甚至在某些任务上超越Gemma3-27B-IT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM虽然能生成自然语言推理轨迹，但这些轨迹往往隐式地引用图像区域，难以验证和监督。作者希望让模型在推理过程中显式地将中间思考与图像证据（点或框）绑定，从而提高推理的可验证性和准确性。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展的合成管道：蒸馏正确的视觉推理轨迹，提取轨迹所需的视觉对象，用SAM3-based agent生成掩码，从中导出对齐的点/框监督信号。2) 提出接地感知强化学习：结合答案正确性奖励和密集接地奖励，接地奖励根据生成的物体引用是否匹配正确的图像证据进行评分。

Result: 在两个计数基准和四个空间推理基准上，视觉接地思维显著提升了Gemma3-4B-IT的性能，超越了原始模型和无接地思维的基线。在空间推理任务上，4B模型匹配甚至在某些情况下超越了Gemma3-27B-IT。分析表明点接地适合计数任务，框接地在显式接地奖励下对空间任务最有利。

Conclusion: 当VLM的中间思考与使其为真的图像区域显式绑定时，模型能更好地进行推理。视觉接地思维是一种有效的推理范式，可提升模型的可验证性和性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉接地思维（visually grounded thinking），在推理过程中将自然语言思考与显式的点/框接地（grounding）交织，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"。

Importance reason: Directly advances multimodal reasoning with explicit visual grounding, a key subtopic for your priority on multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: visual grounding, reasoning trace, reinforcement learning, spatial reasoning, counting

Abstract: Visual thinking should not only sound right; it should show its evidence. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) can produce natural-language reasoning traces, these traces often leave the supporting image regions implicit, making them hard to verify and difficult to supervise. We introduce visually grounded thinking, a reasoning process in which models interleave natural-language thoughts with explicit point or box groundings of the visual evidence used at each step. This lets the model express intermediate reasoning in language while grounding key objects in the image regions they refer to. To train this behavior, we construct a scalable synthesis pipeline that distills correct visual reasoning traces, extracts the visual objects required by the traces, grounds them with a SAM3-based agent, and derives aligned point and box supervision from the resulting masks. We further propose grounding-aware reinforcement learning, which combines answer correctness rewards with dense grounding rewards that score whether generated object references match the correct image evidence. Across two counting benchmarks and four spatial reasoning benchmarks, adding visually grounded thinking to Gemma3-4B-IT consistently improves performance over the original model and the non-grounded thinking baseline. On spatial reasoning, the visually grounded thinking 4B models match, and in some cases surpass, Gemma3-27B-IT from the same model family. Our analysis shows that point grounding is well suited to counting, while box grounding benefits most from explicit grounding rewards on spatial tasks. Overall, our results show that VLMs think better when their intermediate thoughts are tied to the image regions that make them true.

</details>


### [154] [From Frames to Temporal Graphs: In-Context Egocentric Action Recognition with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15417)
*Bessie Dominguez-Dager, Francisco Gomez-Donoso, Miguel Cazorla, Marc Pollefeys, Daniel Barath, Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将第一视角视频转换为时序动作图（Temporal Action Graphs），通过多阶段提示流水线先生成密集自然语言叙述，再形式化为结构化图表示，从而将视觉感知与符号推理解耦。在EGTEA和Epic-Kitchens-100数据集上，图表示的少样本上下文学习显著优于零样本帧级和图级推理，且跨11个开源VLM（2B-235B参数）验证了VLM作为符号推理器比直接视觉观察更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在直接处理原始像素进行第一视角视频动作推理时表现不佳，难以捕捉手-物交互的细粒度时序变化。作者希望将视频推理从像素级感知解耦到符号化推理，以更好地利用VLM的推理能力。

Method: 提出多阶段提示流水线：1) 将视频分割为短时序窗口，生成密集自然语言叙述作为语义瓶颈；2) 将叙述形式化为开放词汇的时序动作图（Temporal Action Graphs）表示；3) 在图表示上进行少样本上下文学习推理。该方法无需微调，仅通过上下文学习即可实现。

Result: 在EGTEA和Epic-Kitchens-100数据集上，图表示的少样本演示相比零样本帧级和图级推理均取得显著准确率提升。零样本设置下图推理与像素级推理竞争力相当。跨11个开源VLM（2B-235B参数，6个模型家族）验证了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 当前VLM作为符号推理器比直接视觉观察更有效。通过将视频投影到语言域，提供了一种可扩展、免微调的替代方案，能更好地利用VLM的潜在推理优势。

Classification reason: 该论文利用VLM进行第一视角视频中的动作推理，通过将视频转换为时序动作图（Temporal Action Graphs）实现符号化推理，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on egocentric action recognition via temporal graphs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with the user's key boost keywords. Signals: Egocentric action recognition, Temporal Action Graphs, VLM symbolic reasoning, In-context learning, Fine-tuning-free

Abstract: Action reasoning in egocentric video requires capturing fine-grained transitions of hand-object interactions, a task where general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle when operating directly on raw pixels. We propose to decouple visual perception from symbolic reasoning by converting videos into Temporal Action Graphs. In a multi-stage prompting pipeline, we first generate dense natural language narratives over short temporal windows as a semantic bottleneck, then formalize them into structured, open-vocabulary graph representations. On the EGTEA and Epic-Kitchens-100 datasets, the symbolic representation unlocks efficient in-context learning: few-shot graph demonstrations yield substantial accuracy gains over zero-shot frame and graph-based inference alike. Even in the zero-shot setting, graph-based reasoning remains competitive with pixel-based inference despite potential pretraining contamination favoring the latter. Across 11 open-weight VLMs from 6 model families ranging from 2B to 235B parameters, our findings indicate that current VLMs are more effective as symbolic reasoners than as direct visual observers. By projecting video into the language domain, we provide a scalable, fine-tuning-free alternative to end-to-end approaches that better leverages these models' latent reasoning strengths. The code will be made public.

</details>


### [155] [Tensor-Coord: Algebraic Decomposition of Joint Plan Tensors for Conflict-Free Multi-Agent LLM Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16478)
*Mudit Rastogi*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Tensor-Coord 提出用三阶张量表示多智能体联合规划，通过 CP/Tucker 分解识别协调结构、量化冲突并生成自然语言约束，实现无冲突的多智能体 LLM 规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体规划中独立生成计划会导致空间碰撞、资源竞争、时间死锁等协调失败，现有方法缺乏系统性的冲突检测与协调机制。

Method: 将 N 个智能体的联合计划表示为三阶张量 T ∈ R^{N×H×A}（智能体×时间步×动作），使用 CP 和 Tucker 分解提取潜在协调结构。定义最小 ε-近似 CP 秩 R* 计算协调复杂度 CC(P)=(R*-N)/N，证明 R*=N 是计划独立性的充要条件。残差 E=T-T_{R*} 定位冲突，Tucker 因子提供可解释的角色/阶段/动作聚类并转化为自然语言约束，用于迭代 LLM 重规划。

Result: 在多机器人配送任务中，2 智能体场景 100% 收敛到无冲突计划（平均 1.4 次迭代），3 智能体 80%（3.2 次迭代），4 智能体 60%（4.0 次迭代）。CP 秩近似线性增长 R*(N)=3.9N+0.5，可作为协调复杂度的预测指标。

Conclusion: 张量分解为多智能体 LLM 规划中的冲突检测与协调提供了一种可计算、可解释的代数框架，协调复杂度度量与迭代重规划策略有效提升了多智能体规划的可靠性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Tensor-Coord 框架，利用张量分解分析多智能体 LLM 规划中的协调冲突，属于多智能体规划与推理范畴。虽然不涉及视觉模态，但 LLM 规划与推理属于 multimodal_reasoning_cot 的广义推理范畴，且该 canonical 主题涵盖多模态推理与思维链，可容纳此工作。

Importance reason: Novel tensor decomposition for multi-agent planning, but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Multi-agent planning, Tensor decomposition, LLM coordination, Conflict detection, Small-scale grid experiments

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain limited in multi-agent planning because independently generated plans can create coordination failures such as spatial collisions, resource contention, and temporal deadlocks. We introduce Tensor-Coord, a multilinear algebra framework that represents the joint plan of N agents as a third-order tensor \(T \in R^{N \times H \times A}\) over agents, timesteps, and actions. Canonical Polyadic (CP) and Tucker decompositions are used to identify latent coordination structure. The minimal epsilon-approximate CP rank R* defines a computable coordination complexity measure, with \(CC(Pi)=(R*-N)/N\). We prove that R*=N is necessary and sufficient for plan independence. The residual \(E=T-T_{R*}\) defines a conflict score over agent pairs, timesteps, and actions, localizing failures without domain-specific rules. Tucker factors provide interpretable agent roles, temporal phases, and action clusters that are converted into natural language constraints for iterative LLM replanning. Experiments on multi-robot delivery tasks across Easy (2 agents, 5x5 grid), Medium (3 agents, 5x5 grid), and Hard (4 agents, 5x5 grid) settings show convergence to conflict-free plans in 100% of 2-agent cases within 1.4 iterations on average, 80% of 3-agent cases within 3.2 iterations, and 60% of 4-agent cases within 4.0 iterations. CP rank scaled approximately linearly as \(R*(N) = 3.9N + 0.5\), supporting its use as a predictor of coordination complexity.

</details>


### [156] [NeRD: Neuro-Symbolic Rule Distillation for Efficient Ontology-Grounded Chain-of-Thought in Medical Image Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15617)
*Hongxi Yang, Yiwen Jiang, Siyuan Yan, Jamie Chow, Eunis Li, Charlotte Poon, Stephanie Fong, Xiangyu Zhao, Deval Mehta, Yasmeen George, Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NeRD 提出神经符号规则蒸馏框架，通过从概念瓶颈模型中蒸馏出基于诊断本体的符号规则，生成高效且可解释的 Chain-of-Thought 推理链，用于医学图像诊断，在皮肤病变数据集上取得良好性能与临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释医学图像诊断方法存在两大问题：概念瓶颈模型（CBM）需要医生对所有预定义概念进行评分和干预，负担沉重；基于生成式 rationale 的方法按类别区分性选择概念，可能偏离诊断本体。亟需一种既能生成符合诊断本体的推理链，又高效且不增加医生负担的方法。

Method: NeRD 框架包含三个步骤：(1) 从概念瓶颈模型中蒸馏出符号规则，将概念选择转化为基于本体的逻辑规则；(2) 利用这些规则生成 ontology-grounded 的 Chain-of-Thought 推理链，确保概念选择既充分又无冗余；(3) 支持专家在环（expert-in-the-loop）干预，医生可在推理链层面进行概念级修正，实现高效交互式诊断。

Result: 在两个皮肤病变数据集上，NeRD 在保持诊断性能的同时显著提升了可解释性。盲法专家评估确认 NeRD 生成的推理链在临床上是合理的。首次实现了基于多模态 Chain-of-Thought 诊断的专家在环研究，验证了概念级干预的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: NeRD 通过神经符号规则蒸馏，成功在医学图像诊断中实现了高效、本体对齐且可解释的 Chain-of-Thought 推理，为可信任的医学 AI 诊断提供了新范式，并展示了专家在环交互的可行性与临床价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 NeRD 框架，在医学图像诊断中利用神经符号规则蒸馏生成基于本体的 Chain-of-Thought 推理链，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，且涉及专家介入的交互式诊断，与现有 canonical 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image diagnosis with ontology-grounded reasoning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical image diagnosis, ontology-grounded reasoning, concept bottleneck models, clinical interpretability, skin lesion datasets

Abstract: Interpretability is essential for trustworthy medical image diagnosis. However, existing concept-driven interpretable methods have key limitations: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) require scoring all predefined concepts at inference time and for manual intervention, imposing a substantial burden on clinicians, while rationale-based generative approaches often select concepts by class discriminability, which can drift from diagnostic ontologies. To address these issues, we propose Neuro-Symbolic Rule Distillation (NeRD), a framework that produces efficient, ontology-grounded reasoning chains that are sufficient yet non-redundant, without manually crafting diagnostic rules. Experiments on two skin datasets demonstrate strong diagnostic performance and interpretability, and blinded expert evaluation confirms the clinical plausibility of NeRD rationales. Our method further enables a first expert-in-the-loop study for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought-based diagnosis, achieving efficient and effective concept-level intervention.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Questioning Vision-Language Models: Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15651)
*Saraswathy Amjith*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自提问框架，利用GRPO强化学习训练VLM在回答复杂视觉推理问题时自动分解为子问题并逐步回答，无需人工标注的思维链数据。在CLEVR和A-OKVQA上验证了有效性，自提问方法在A-OKVQA上达到52.2%准确率，优于未训练模型（46.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在组合视觉推理任务中表现不佳，现有方法依赖人工标注的逐步解释来提升推理能力，但标注成本高且难以规模化。因此需要一种无需人工标注、能自动学习分解复杂视觉问题的方法。

Method: 提出自提问框架（Self-Questioning Framework），使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习算法训练VLM。模型在训练中从未见过问题分解示例，仅通过奖励信号引导：奖励包含两部分——输出中是否包含子问题（sub-question）以及最终答案是否正确。模型在3B参数VLM上训练，使用CLEVR（合成几何场景）和A-OKVQA（真实照片）数据集。

Result: 在A-OKVQA上，自提问方法准确率52.2%，标准RL方法51.6%，均优于未训练模型46.8%。这是首个通过奖励子问题生成而非仅奖励最终答案来实现自提问VLM的工作。

Conclusion: 教会AI系统自我提问中间问题是处理复杂视觉推理的有效策略，尤其当问题难度需要显式的逐步分解时。强化学习可以自动发现问题分解策略，无需人工标注的思维链数据。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM的组合视觉推理，通过强化学习让模型自我提问并分解问题为子问题，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses RL for self-questioning in VLM reasoning, but lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal token or generation priorities. Signals: GRPO reinforcement learning, compositional visual reasoning, self-questioning without human annotations, A-OKVQA benchmark, 3B parameter VLM

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are AI systems that process both images and text, yet they often struggle with compositional visual reasoning questions that require chaining multiple steps together, such as identifying objects, counting them, and comparing the results. Existing approaches improve this reasoning by training models on human-written step-by-step explanations, but creating these annotations is expensive and difficult to scale. We propose a self-questioning framework that trains a VLM to break visual questions into smaller sub-questions and answer each one before producing a final response, using a reinforcement learning algorithm called Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). The model is never shown examples of how to decompose questions, it discovers this behavior on its own, guided by a reward signal that scores whether the output contains sub-questions and whether the final answer is correct. We apply this framework to a 3-billion-parameter model, training on both synthetic scenes of geometric shapes (CLEVR) and real-world photographs (A-OKVQA). On A-OKVQA, both self-questioning and standard reinforcement learning substantially improve accuracy over the untrained model (52.2% and 51.6% vs. 46.8%). We introduce the first self-questioning VLM by rewarding not only the final answer like standard RL but additionally for generating intermediate sub-questions, enabling it to discover compositional decomposition strategies. These results suggest that teaching AI systems to ask themselves intermediate questions is a promising strategy for complex visual reasoning, particularly when the difficulty of a question warrants explicit step-by-step decomposition.

</details>


### [158] [Mind the Gap: Diagnosing Constraint Discovery Failures in Text-in-Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15982)
*Rui Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过文本-图像编辑中的约束发现任务，系统诊断多模态大模型在识别隐含视觉依赖关系时的失败模式。在461个诊断案例中，模型在无引导提示下仅达到46%的召回率，而显式提供约束时可达94%，表明失败主要源于模型无法自主发现未声明的依赖关系。因果解释是最有效的部分引导方式，且发现更高的自发现召回率并不一定提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理的关键挑战不仅在于识别可见内容，更在于判断在特定任务下哪些视觉依赖关系变得相关。现有研究缺乏对模型在隐含约束发现方面失败原因的系统诊断。

Method: 构建了461个文本-图像编辑诊断案例，涵盖19种约束子类型；通过无引导提示、显式提供约束、以及Oracle场分解（因果解释、区域名称、类型标签）等多种条件进行对比实验，系统分析MLLM在约束发现中的表现。

Result: 无引导提示下模型仅达46%的案例级宏召回率，显式提供约束时达94%；因果解释是最有效的部分引导（0.782召回率），优于区域名称（0.610）和类型标签（0.646）；下游实验表明更高的自发现召回率因引入假阳性而未必提升任务性能。

Conclusion: 多模态模型在文本-图像编辑中的约束发现失败主要源于无法自主识别隐含依赖关系，而非缺乏相关知识；因果线索是有效的引导信号；需要精度感知的约束发现策略来平衡召回与假阳性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态模型在文本-图像编辑中自动发现隐含约束（即推理出哪些区域需要同步修改）的能力，核心是多模态推理中的因果推理与约束发现，属于多模态推理范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal reasoning failure diagnosis, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Constraint discovery failure, MLLM reasoning gap, Causal explanation effectiveness, Precision-aware elicitation, Text-in-image editing

Abstract: A key challenge in multimodal reasoning is determining which visual dependencies become relevant under a specific task, rather than merely recognizing visible content. We study this through edit-induced constraint discovery in text-in-image editing, a controlled diagnostic setting where a local text change can activate secondary consistency constraints: given a valid editing instruction and an image, can a model identify the secondary regions that must also change? Across 461 diagnostic cases, four MLLMs, and 19 constraint subtypes, models recover only 46% case-level macro recall under unguided prompting versus 94% when constraints are explicitly provided, suggesting that a substantial portion of the failure arises when models must decide which unstated dependencies to surface. Oracle-field decomposition shows that case-specific causal explanations are the most effective partial guidance (0.782 recall), above region names (0.610) or type labels (0.646), suggesting that edit-specific causal cues account for much of the oracle gain. A downstream experiment further shows that higher self-discovery recall does not necessarily improve task performance: unverified self-discovery introduces false positives that offset recall gains, motivating precision-aware constraint elicitation.

</details>


### [159] [Focus When Necessary: Adaptive Routing and Collaborative Grounding for Training-Free Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16158)
*Yifan Wang, Peiming Li, Shiyu Li, Zhiyuan Hu, Xiaochen Yang, Wenming Yang, Yang Tang, Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LazyMCoT 是一个无需训练的视觉定位框架，通过自适应路由机制（基于首次 token 统计的不确定性评估 + 共形校准）区分简单与困难样本，对困难样本使用协同定位模块（模型交叉注意力 + 外部视觉专家两阶段精炼）生成精确局部裁剪，在提升推理精度的同时降低平均推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在处理复杂高分辨率图像时难以感知细粒度细节。训练自由的视觉定位方法通过图像缩放和局部裁剪来缓解，但无差别地应用这些操作会导致简单查询的计算冗余，且可能因截断全局上下文或引入无关背景噪声而降低精度。

Method: 提出 LazyMCoT 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 自适应路由（Adaptive Routing）—— 通过单次前向传播中首个 token 的统计量评估预测不确定性，利用共形校准（conformal calibration）高效跳过置信样本，同时保证困难样本的召回；(2) 协同定位（Collaborative Grounding）—— 对困难样本，结合模型自身的交叉模态注意力与外部视觉专家（如 grounding 模型），通过两阶段精炼过程生成精确的局部显示区域。

Result: 在多个基准上，LazyMCoT 在提升推理精度的同时降低了平均推理延迟，性能可与基于训练的方法相媲美。

Conclusion: LazyMCoT 通过自适应路由和协同定位，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了精度与效率的平衡，为训练自由的视觉定位提供了一种动态、高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LazyMCoT，一种无需训练的动态视觉定位框架，通过自适应路由和协同定位提升 MLLM 在复杂高分辨率图像中的细粒度感知与推理能力。这属于多模态推理（visual grounding 是视觉推理的核心任务之一），且涉及推理时自适应计算分配，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链 高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel adaptive routing for visual grounding, but outside core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: training-free visual grounding, adaptive routing, collaborative grounding, MLLM fine-grained perception, conformal calibration

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in cross-modal reasoning, they often struggle to perceive fine-grained details in complex high-resolution images. Recent training-free methods address this through image scaling and localized cropping. However, applying these manipulations indiscriminately introduces computational redundancy for simple queries and can degrade accuracy by truncating essential global context or introducing irrelevant background noise. To this end, we propose LazyMCoT, a dynamic and training-free framework that adaptively allocates visual grounding efforts based on sample difficulty. The framework features an Adaptive Routing mechanism that evaluates predictive uncertainty using first-token statistics from a single forward pass. This efficiently bypasses confident cases while ensuring the recall of difficult samples via conformal calibration. For these challenging cases, a Collaborative Grounding module integrates the inherent cross-modal attention of the model with an external visual expert through a two-stage refinement process. This refinement process generates a precise localized display to recover small or occluded targets. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that LazyMCoT rivals training-based approaches by simultaneously improving reasoning accuracy and reducing average inference latency. Our code is availble at .

</details>


### [160] [Gen-VCoT: Generative Visual Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Diffusion-Based RGB Intermediate Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16783)
*Zhiqiang Zhou, Junliang Dai, Xu ling*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Gen-VCoT 提出一种生成式视觉思维链框架，利用扩散模型生成 RGB 图像（分割图、深度图等）作为多模态推理的中间表征，通过自适应路由器选择推理深度，在空间和深度推理任务上取得显著提升，但简单事实问答可能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的思维链推理依赖文本中间步骤，缺乏可解释的视觉中间表征；而使用隐式 token 或外部工具的方法要么不透明，要么不够灵活。作者希望建立一种使用显式 RGB 图像作为推理中间步骤的新范式，使推理过程更可解释、更可控。

Method: Gen-VCoT 包含三个阶段：(1) 视觉定位阶段——使用 SAM 生成分割图；(2) 几何推理阶段——使用 Marigold 生成深度图；(3) 语义推理阶段——集成 Qwen2-VL 进行语义理解。同时设计了一个自适应路由器，根据问题复杂度动态选择推理深度（跳过不必要的阶段）。所有中间结果均为 RGB 图像，可直接可视化。

Result: 在空间推理任务上提升 25%，深度推理任务上提升 50%；但在简单事实性问答上可能下降。在 CLEVR 数据集上，文本 CoT 准确率 91.2% 远高于视觉中间表征的 62.5%，表明最优表征形式与任务类型相关。

Conclusion: Gen-VCoT 建立了可解释多模态推理的新范式，证明了 RGB 图像作为推理中间步骤的有效性，同时揭示了任务依赖的最优表征形式——复杂空间/几何推理适合视觉中间表征，而简单事实推理仍以文本 CoT 为优。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Gen-VCoT，使用扩散模型生成的 RGB 图像（如深度图、分割图）作为视觉推理中间步骤，属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴。虽然其使用视觉中间表征而非文本 CoT，但核心仍是多模态推理中的 chain-of-thought 范式，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Uses diffusion for visual reasoning intermediates but lacks continuous token/flow matching focus and may hurt simple QA. Signals: generative visual chain-of-thought, diffusion-based RGB intermediates, adaptive router, spatial/depth reasoning gains, task-dependent performance drop

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at visual reasoning but rely on text-based chain-of-thought (CoT), lacking interpretable visual intermediates. Existing methods use opaque tokens or external tools, missing key properties. We propose Gen-VCoT, a framework using expert vision models to generate RGB images as reasoning intermediates. It has three stages: visual grounding (SAM segmentation), geometric reasoning (Marigold depth maps), and semantic reasoning (Qwen2-VL integration). An adaptive router selects reasoning depth. Evaluations show Gen-VCoT improves spatial (25% better) and depth (50% better) questions, but may hurt simple factual queries. Text CoT outperforms visual intermediates on CLEVR (91.2% vs 62.5%), showing task-dependent optimal representations. Gen-VCoT establishes a new paradigm for interpretable multimodal reasoning.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_retrieval'></div>

## 多模态检索 [[Back]](#toc)
