### [161] [MAGE-RAG: Multigranular Adaptive Graph Evidence for Agentic Multimodal RAG in Long-Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15906)
*Yilong Zuo, Xunkai Li, Jing Yuan, Qiangqiang Dai, Hongchao Qin, Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.DB

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MAGE-RAG 提出了一种多粒度自适应图证据框架，用于长文档多模态问答。它离线构建包含页面节点和元素节点的证据图，在线通过迭代激活、打开、搜索和剪枝构建证据子图，为 LVLM 提供紧凑且相关的多模态上下文。在 LongDocURL 和 MMLongBench-Doc 上取得了显著优于传统 RAG 方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档多模态 RAG 方法在文本检索和页面级视觉检索之间存在静态权衡：文本检索丢失视觉和布局信息，页面级检索引入大量无关区域。缺乏一种能自适应平衡证据覆盖、噪声和推理成本的方法。

Method: MAGE-RAG 包含离线证据图构建和在线证据控制两个阶段。离线阶段构建包含页面节点和元素（文本块、表格、图像等）节点的证据图，编码包含关系、阅读顺序、布局邻接、章节层次和语义邻居关系。在线阶段，证据控制器在显式预算下迭代执行激活、打开、搜索和剪枝操作，生成紧凑的证据子图，渲染为结构化多模态输入供 LVLM 消费。

Result: 在 LongDocURL 上达到 52.75 总体准确率，在 MMLongBench-Doc 上达到 53.26 准确率和 51.19 F1。细粒度分析、预算-性能曲线、消融实验和轨迹分析表明，查询时证据子图构建能有效平衡分散证据覆盖与上下文噪声控制。

Conclusion: MAGE-RAG 通过多粒度自适应图证据框架有效解决了长文档多模态问答中证据覆盖与噪声控制的权衡问题，为多模态 RAG 提供了一种新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长文档多模态问答中的检索增强生成（RAG），核心是多粒度自适应图证据构建，属于多模态检索与文档理解范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）涵盖跨模态检索与文档检索，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong engineering for long-doc multimodal QA but outside user's priority subtopics and negative for retrieval focus. Signals: Multimodal RAG, Long-document QA, Graph evidence, Agentic retrieval, No continuous language model

Abstract: Long-document multimodal question answering requires a system to locate sparse evidence in long PDFs and integrate clues from text, tables, images, charts, and complex layouts. Existing RAG methods mostly rely on fixed Top-k retrieval over text chunks or pages. Text retrieval can compress the context but often loses visual and layout information; page-level visual retrieval preserves the original page, yet it also sends large irrelevant regions to the reader, leading to a static trade-off among evidence coverage, noise, and inference cost. This paper proposes MAGE-RAG, a multigranular adaptive graph evidence framework for long-document multimodal QA. MAGE-RAG uses page retrieval as the entry point for query-time evidence construction. Offline, it builds an evidence graph with page nodes and element nodes, encoding containment, reading order, layout adjacency, section hierarchy, and semantic-neighbor relations. At query time, an online evidence controller iteratively activates, opens, searches, and prunes evidence under explicit budgets. The resulting evidence subgraph is then rendered into structured multimodal reader input, allowing the LVLM to consume compact and relevant evidence within a limited context. On LongDocURL and MMLongBench-Doc, we establish a unified comparison and analysis protocol covering Direct MLLM, Text RAG, Page-level Visual RAG, and Graph/Agentic RAG. Experiments show that MAGE-RAG achieves 52.75 overall accuracy on LongDocURL, and 53.26 accuracy with 51.19 F1 on MMLongBench-Doc. Fine-grained breakdowns, budget-performance curves, ablations, and trace-based analysis further show that query-time evidence subgraph construction can balance dispersed evidence coverage with context-noise control. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [162] [Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
*Zhemin Zhang, Weijie Chen, David Le, Amara Tariq, Alex Wallace, Matthew Stib, Juan Maria Farina, Chadi Ayoub, Reza Arsanjani, Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于基础模型的跨模态表征对齐框架，用于CT影像与纵向EHR数据的时间-事件预测。通过四种融合策略（晚期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力、共注意力）在共享潜空间中对齐两种模态，并在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病两个临床任务的大规模多机构数据集上验证。结果表明对比对齐融合在模态贡献均衡时最稳定，且任务感知的多模态对齐是鲁棒泛化的必要设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床数据（如CT影像与EHR）的时间-事件预测面临模态不平衡和分布偏移的挑战，现有融合方法缺乏系统性分析，且缺乏可跨任务和跨机构泛化的通用框架。

Method: 使用领域特定的基础模型分别编码CT影像（如CXR foundation model）和EHR数据（如CLMBR），在共享潜空间中通过四种融合策略进行跨模态对齐：晚期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力、共注意力。在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病（MACE）两个任务上，使用大规模多机构数据集进行训练和内外验证。

Result: 融合方法在模态贡献均衡时比单模态基线提升1.5-5.4%的concordance index。对比对齐融合（尤其使用CLMBR表征）在PE死亡率预测中表现最稳定且统计显著；对于MACE，交叉注意力（one-hot）在内部测试中最佳，图像引导的共注意力在外部测试中最佳。

Conclusion: 任务感知的多模态对齐是鲁棒泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则，不同融合策略在不同任务和模态不平衡条件下表现各异，不存在"一刀切"的最优融合方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究CT影像与EHR电子健康记录之间的跨模态表征对齐，通过对比学习、交叉注意力等策略将不同模态映射到共享潜空间，属于多模态检索/对齐范畴。现有[多模态检索]（multimodal_retrieval）子主题涵盖跨模态表征对齐与检索，与该论文核心方法高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical time-to-event prediction with CT and EHR, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical diagnosis, clinical, multimodal fusion, time-to-event, foundation model

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Scalar Distances: Semantic Attribute Gradients from Frozen MLLMs for Visual Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15134)
*Shubhang Bhatnagar, Dheeraj Baiju, Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 高优先级 (85.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAGA 利用冻结的 MLLM 通过 GRPO 强化学习为视觉编码器提供属性级语义梯度监督，替代传统标量距离训练，显著提升零样本图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉检索编码器使用类标签监督训练，每个训练对仅用一个标量距离统一拉近或推远嵌入，忽略了不同视觉属性（如颜色、形状、纹理）的差异。MLLM 能够描述这些细粒度属性，但如何将这种语言驱动的属性感知能力转化为编码器的训练信号尚未被探索。

Method: SAGA 框架包含三个部分：(1) 使用 GRPO 对冻结的 MLLM 进行奖励，使其基于视觉编码器的 token 预测图像对是否属于同一类，从而将属性级梯度反向传播到编码器；(2) 辅助注意力蒸馏损失，将编码器的嵌入锚定到 MLLM 注意力关注的 token 上；(3) 标准度量学习损失（如 triplet loss）塑造嵌入几何结构用于最近邻检索。MLLM 在推理时被丢弃，不增加部署成本。

Result: 在 CUB-200-2011、Cars-196、FGVC-Aircraft 和 iNaturalist Aves 四个细粒度数据集上，SAGA 在零样本图像检索任务中 Recall@1 比 SOTA 基线提升 3-6 个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGA 证明了利用冻结 MLLM 的语言属性感知能力可以为视觉编码器提供比传统标量距离更丰富的监督信号，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升检索性能，为视觉表征学习开辟了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SAGA 框架，利用冻结的 MLLM 通过 GRPO 强化学习为视觉编码器提供属性级监督信号，以提升图像检索性能。核心任务是视觉嵌入的检索（零样本图像检索），属于多模态检索范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "多模态检索" (multimodal_retrieval)。

Importance reason: Novel use of frozen MLLM and GRPO for attribute-level supervision in visual embeddings, but subtopic is retrieval, not the user's priority continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: GRPO for visual embedding training, attribute-level semantic gradients, zero-shot retrieval improvement, frozen MLLM supervision, attention-distillation loss

Abstract: Vision encoders for retrieval are typically trained with class-label supervision: each training pair reduces to a scalar that uniformly pushes the embedding apart or pulls it together, as if every visual attribute either differed or matched. A multimodal large language model (MLLM), shown the same pair, can articulate those attributes and use them to predict whether the images share a class. We propose \textbf{SAGA}, a framework that turns this language-grounded, attribute-aware perception into a training signal for the encoder itself. Specifically, we use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to reward the MLLM for correct predictions on the vision encoder's tokens. Since correct predictions require those tokens to expose the specific attributes that differ or match between the pair, the gradient pushes the encoder to encode them, replacing the uniform pair-level scalar with attribute-resolved supervision. An auxiliary attention-distillation loss anchors the encoder's embedding to tokens the MLLM attended to, and a standard metric-learning loss shapes the embedding geometry for nearest-neighbour retrieval. The MLLM is frozen throughout and discarded at inference, matching the deployment cost of a metric-learning baseline. SAGA improves Recall@1 by 3 to 6 points over state-of-the-art baselines on CUB-200-2011, Cars-196, FGVC-Aircraft, and iNaturalist Aves on zero-shot image retrieval.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>
