### [164] [GridVQA-X: A Framework for Evaluating Multimodal Explainability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14740)
*Sujay Belsare, Sudarshan Nikhil, Sushant Kumar, Ponnurangam Kumaraguru, Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 GridVQA-X，首个专门用于评估跨模态可解释性方法的诊断框架。通过闭世界合成逻辑生成具有数学保证的解释，并训练一对架构相同但推理行为不同的模型（纯空间推理模型 vs. 依赖跨模态捷径的模型），揭示现有可解释性方法无法区分真实跨模态推理与浅层捷径，存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态可解释性（MxAI）方法的发展落后于视觉语言模型的快速发展，现有评估协议缺乏区分真实跨模态推理（如空间组合）与浅层跨模态捷径（如词袋属性匹配）所需的标准答案，无法判断MxAI方法是否忠实捕捉了协同交互还是仅仅幻觉了推理过程。

Method: 提出 GridVQA-X 诊断框架，采用闭世界合成逻辑生成具有数学保证的解释。在受控环境中训练一对架构相同但行为不同的模型：M_pure（学习鲁棒的空间关系推理）和 M_spur（结构上被迫依赖跨模态捷径）。通过对比两种模型上的解释差异来评估可解释性方法的忠实度。

Result: 广泛使用的可解释性方法无法区分依赖真实空间关系推理的模型与利用跨模态捷径的模型，暴露出这些方法在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面的关键缺陷，错误地呈现了多模态模型的实际决策过程。

Conclusion: 现有MxAI方法无法忠实反映多模态模型的推理机制，GridVQA-X框架为评估和推动更可靠的多模态可解释性方法提供了重要工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GridVQA-X，一个用于评估多模态可解释性方法的诊断框架，属于多模态模型评测范畴。虽然聚焦于可解释性评测，但本质上是一个新的多模态评测基准/协议，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal explainability evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Multimodal explainability evaluation, Diagnostic framework, Cross-modal shortcuts, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: With the increasing development of Vision-Language Models, it becomes imperative that their predictions are readily explainable to relevant stakeholders. However, the field of explainability has not kept pace with the multimodal surge. While recent Multimodal Explainable AI (MxAI) methods generate explanations to attribute the interaction between different modalities, current evaluation protocols lack the ground truth required to distinguish between true cross-modal reasoning (e.g., spatial composition) and shallow cross-modal shortcuts (e.g., Bag-of-Words attribute matching). It remains unknown whether MxAI methods faithfully capture synergistic interactions or merely hallucinate reasoning on models acting as simple feature detectors. In this paper, we introduce GridVQA-X, the first diagnostic framework specifically designed to evaluate cross-modal explainability. Unlike natural datasets, GridVQA-X leverages a closed-world synthesis logic to generate unique, mathematically guaranteed explanations. We utilize this controlled environment to train paired ground-truth models on identical architectures: $M_{\text{pure}}$, which learns robust spatial-relational reasoning and $M_{\text{spur}}$, which is structurally forced to rely on cross-modal shortcuts. This behavioral divergence creates a rigorous testbed: a faithful explainer must report distinct reasoning pathways for each model. Our findings reveal that widely used methods fail to distinguish between models relying on genuine spatial-relational reasoning and those exploiting cross-modal shortcuts, highlighting a critical gap in capturing true cross-modal synergy and misrepresenting how multimodal models actually make decisions.

</details>


### [165] [Context Compression Is Not One Thing: Readable Symbolic Re-expression vs. Coherent Summary at Matched Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14875)
*Sisong Bei, Mikhail L. Arbuzov, Ziwei Dong, Dmitri Kalaev, Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (64.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Telegraph English，一种可读的符号化格式，将检索到的段落重写为结构化实体-关系陈述，在相同token预算下比自然语言压缩和连贯摘要更有效地保留推理证据，在多跳QA数据集上取得13-20 F1点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文压缩对于小语言模型处理长上下文至关重要，但现有压缩方法（如截断、随机采样）会丢失关键推理证据。作者希望设计一种在有限token预算下能最大限度保留实体关系和推理路径的压缩格式。

Method: 提出Telegraph English格式，将检索到的文本段落重写为结构化的实体-关系三元组陈述（如"Entity1 - relation -> Entity2"），保留推理证据的同时降低token消耗。在MuSiQue、TwoWiki、HotpotQA三个多跳QA数据集上，与字符级删除、截断、随机子采样三种匹配预算的基线以及同一编码器生成的连贯摘要进行对比实验。

Result: Telegraph English在所有数据集上均优于三种匹配预算的压缩基线，F1提升13-20个百分点；在最难的数据集上也优于连贯摘要。预注册的深度交互假设（优势随推理深度增长）未被验证，表明优势并非来自深度推理，而是来自更密集的实体内容保留。

Conclusion: 可读的符号化重表达（Telegraph English）在匹配token预算下比自然语言或连贯摘要更密集地保留实体内容，从而更有效地支持多跳推理。上下文压缩并非单一问题，不同压缩策略在不同任务场景下各有优劣。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多跳问答中的上下文压缩方法，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准范畴。论文在MuSiQue、TwoWiki、HotpotQA等数据集上进行受控实验，提出Telegraph English格式并评测其效果，核心是评测不同压缩策略对推理性能的影响，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]。

Importance reason: Focuses on context compression for QA, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: context compression, symbolic re-expression, multi-hop QA, small language models, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: We study context compression for multi-hop question answering with small language models. We propose Telegraph English, a readable symbolic format that rewrites retrieved passages into structured entity-relation statements, preserving reasoning evidence at lower token cost. In controlled experiments on MuSiQue, TwoWiki, and HotpotQA, Telegraph English outperforms three matched-budget compression baselines (character-level deletion, truncation, and random sub-sampling) on every dataset, with gains of 13 to 20 F1 percentage point. It also outperforms a coherent prose summary produced by the same encoder on the hardest dataset. A pre-registered depth-interaction hypothesis is null: the advantage does not grow with reasoning depth within datasets. We interpret these results as evidence that readable symbolic re-expression preserves entity content more densely than either natural language or coherent summarization at matched token budget.

</details>


### [166] [MMLongEmbed: Benchmarking Multimodal Embedding Models in Long-Context Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14747)
*Haitian Wang, Ruoxi Sun, Quantong Qiu, Juntao Li, Junhui Li, Hua Chen, Jinxiong Chang, Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMLongEmbed是首个系统评估多模态嵌入模型在长上下文场景中表现的基准，包含文本、文档和视频三种模态的四个检索任务，覆盖多个上下文长度范围。实验发现现有模型依赖浅层特征匹配，难以捕获深层语义和结构依赖，且性能退化与上下文长度及关键信息位置系统相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态嵌入模型的理论上下文窗口不断扩展，但长上下文输入的有效理解和表征能力仍是实际部署的关键瓶颈，而现有评测缺乏对此场景的系统评估。

Method: 构建MMLongEmbed基准，包含四个检索任务（覆盖文本、文档、视频模态），设计多个上下文长度范围和关键信息位置变化，系统评估当前最先进的多模态嵌入模型。

Result: 现有模型严重依赖浅层特征匹配，难以捕获深层语义和结构依赖；性能退化与上下文长度和关键信息位置系统相关；模型对不同模态冗余上下文信息的鲁棒性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: MMLongEmbed揭示了当前多模态嵌入模型在长上下文场景中的关键瓶颈，为未来模型改进提供了评测基础和分析方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MMLongEmbed，首个针对多模态嵌入模型在长上下文场景下的评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-context multimodal embedding evaluation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Multimodal embedding benchmark, long-context evaluation, retrieval tasks, superficial feature matching, performance degradation analysis

Abstract: Recent advancements have significantly expanded the theoretical context windows of Multimodal Embedding Models (MEMs). However, larger context windows do not necessarily translate into effective comprehension and representation of long-context multimodal inputs, which remains a critical bottleneck for real-world deployment. To address the lack of systematic evaluation in this setting, we introduce MMLongEmbed, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating MEMs in long-context scenarios. MMLongEmbed comprises four retrieval tasks spanning multiple context-length ranges, covering text, document, and video modalities. Through extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art models, we find that current architectures rely heavily on superficial feature matching and struggle to capture deep semantic and structural dependencies. We further observe that performance degradation varies systematically with context length and key information placement. Moreover, models exhibit substantially different robustness to redundant contextual information across modalities. For reproducibility, the benchmark and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [167] [Can Agents Read the Room? Benchmarking Visual Social Intelligence in Multimodal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15152)
*Shijun Wan, Xuehai Wu, Jiwen Zhang, Siyuan Wang, Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SocialSim 基准，用于评估多模态智能体在社交模拟中的视觉社会智能。包含 240 个场景、585 个角色实例和 4 种角色级任务（表情、特征、交互调节、交互结果），评测了 7 个 MLLM，发现局部角色扮演接近饱和，但交互调节和视觉结果达成仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交智能体基准大多基于文本，很少测试多模态智能体是否能利用视觉线索（如面部表情、姿势、目光、情绪变化）来指导社交互动，缺乏对视觉社会智能的系统评估。

Method: 构建 SocialSim 基准，包含 240 个场景、585 个角色实例和 2,340 个角色-任务实例，结合对齐的文本-视觉证据、结构化角色档案和四种角色级任务（表情任务、特征任务、交互调节任务、交互结果任务）。在 verbalized-vision 和 direct-vision 两种设置下评估 7 个 MLLM。

Result: 局部角色扮演（角色特定表情和冲突处理）接近饱和，而交互调节和基于视觉的结果达成仍然困难得多，表明当前 MLLM 在视觉社会智能方面存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 多模态智能体在视觉社会智能方面仍有显著提升空间，特别是在需要全局交互理解和视觉引导的社交场景中，为未来多模态社交智能研究提供了评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个评估多模态社会智能的基准（SocialSim），属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态评测与基准" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on social intelligence benchmarking, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: social intelligence benchmark, multimodal simulation, MLLM evaluation, interaction regulation challenge, not generation or world model

Abstract: Social interaction depends on both language and visible social signals, such as facial expressions, posture, gaze, and emotional shifts. Yet existing social-agent benchmarks are largely text-based and rarely test whether multimodal agents can use visual cues to guide interaction. We introduce \textsc{\benchmarkname{}}, a benchmark evaluating visual social intelligence in multimodal social simulation. It contains 240 scenarios, 585 role instances, and 2,340 role-task instances, combining aligned textual-visual evidence, structured role profiles, and four role-level tasks: expression task, characteristic task, interaction regulation task, and interaction outcome task. Evaluating seven recent MLLMs under verbalized-vision and direct-vision reveals a clear gap between local role enactment and interaction management: role-specific expression and conflict handling are near saturation, whereas interaction regulation and visually grounded outcome achievement remain substantially more difficult. The code is released at , and the dataset is available at .

</details>


### [168] [YTClickbait21K: Human-Annotated Multimodal Dataset for YouTube Clickbait Detection Across Diverse Channels and Content Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14780)
*Md. Minhazul Islam, Md. Tanbeer Jubaer, Amith Khandakar, Shovon Sarker, Sumaiya Rahman, Md. Masum Mia, Mohamed Arselene Ayari, Hamed Noori*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YTClickbait21K是一个包含21,238个YouTube视频的大规模人工标注多模态点击诱饵检测数据集，覆盖40个频道、29个国家、多种内容类别，每个样本包含结构化元数据和缩略图，由三名标注者独立标注，具有较高的标注一致性（k=0.65），为多模态点击诱饵检测提供了可靠的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频分享平台上的点击诱饵内容严重威胁信息可靠性，但现有自动检测方法受限于缺乏大规模、高质量的多模态数据集。

Method: 从40个频道（29个国家）收集21,238个YouTube视频，涵盖新闻、娱乐、教育、游戏等类别；每个样本包含标题、描述、互动统计等元数据及缩略图；三名标注者使用标准化决策框架（结合文本、视觉和跨模态一致性线索）独立标注，通过多数投票确定最终标签。

Result: 数据集具有较高的标注者间一致性（Cohen's kappa=0.65），表明标注可靠；提供了多模态基准用于训练和评估点击诱饵检测模型。

Conclusion: YTClickbait21K通过规模、标注严谨性和多模态丰富性的结合，为跨模态语义理解和自动内容审核系统的研究提供了稳健的基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个大规模人工标注的多模态YouTube点击诱饵检测数据集YTClickbait21K，包含视频元数据与缩略图，属于多模态评测基准范畴，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Dataset is for clickbait detection, not aligned with user's priority on generative models or continuous multimodal representations. Signals: Multimodal dataset, Clickbait detection, Human-annotated, Low priority subtopic, No generative or continuous model focus

Abstract: Clickbait content on video-sharing platforms poses a significant challenge to information reliability, yet progress in automated detection has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, high-quality multimodal datasets. We present YTClickbait21K, a human-annotated YouTube clickbait dataset comprising 21,238 videos collected from 40 channels across 29 countries, covering diverse content categories such as news, entertainment, education, and gaming. Each sample includes structured metadata (title, description, engagement statistics) along with associated thumbnail images, enabling comprehensive multimodal analysis. To ensure annotation quality, every video was independently labeled by three annotators using a standardized decision framework that incorporates textual, visual, and cross-modal consistency cues, with final labels determined through majority voting. The dataset exhibits substantial inter-annotator agreement (k=0.65), confirming reliable labeling despite the inherent subjectivity of clickbait detection. By combining scale, annotation rigor, and multimodal richness, this dataset provides a robust benchmark for developing and evaluating machine learning models, facilitating research in cross-modal semantic understanding, and advancing automated content moderation systems.

</details>


### [169] [Position: The Systemic Lack of Agency in Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14795)
*Yizhao Huang, Haoyang Chen, Shiqin Wang, Pohsun Huang, Jiayuan Li, Haoyuan Du, Yandong Shi, Zheng Wang, Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出当前视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉推理中缺乏"主动性"（Agency），即模型倾向于被动语义检索而非主动视觉探索。作者提出了V-IRD基准来评测VLM的隐式推理能力，实验表明即使强大的VLM也难以利用参考对象和自主寻找视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在视觉推理任务中表现良好，但作者认为这主要依赖被动语义检索能力，而非真正的主动视觉探索与隐式推理。当前基准未能有效区分这两种能力，导致VLM在需要自主发现隐藏视觉证据的场景中表现不足。

Method: 提出了Visual Implicit Reasoning Diagnosing Benchmark (V-IRD)，要求模型通过自主视觉分析而非显式指定目标来推导答案。该基准设计用于评测模型在缺乏明确指引时，能否主动利用参考对象和寻找视觉线索进行推理。

Result: 实验表明，尽管主流VLM在语义识别上表现强劲，但在需要自主视觉探索的隐式推理任务中表现不佳。强语义识别能力并不等同于主动视觉探索能力，揭示了当前VLM在推理主动性方面的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: VLM存在系统性的主动性缺失问题，视觉推理不应仅被视为被动语义检索，而应作为依赖自主视觉探索的主动情境推理。V-IRD基准为这一缺失的能力维度提供了有效的评测手段。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个新的视觉隐式推理诊断基准（V-IRD），用于评测VLM的主动视觉探索与隐式推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper critiques VLM agency but focuses on a benchmark for implicit reasoning, which is not a top priority subtopic. Signals: VLM agency critique, visual reasoning benchmark, implicit reasoning evaluation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: This paper argues that a systemic lack of Agency constrains the implicit reasoning capabilities of current Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Implicit reasoning refers to the ability to autonomously discover and utilize hidden visual evidence to bridge information gaps, rather than merely relying on explicitly specified targets. This capacity underlies human visual understanding and everyday reasoning. We argue that this limitation arises from a tendency to approach visual reasoning primarily as passive semantic retrieval, rather than as active, situated reasoning that depends on autonomous visual exploration. As a result, most existing benchmarks primarily assess Passive Capacity, leaving this aspect of reasoning largely unmeasured. To address this gap, we introduce the Visual Implicit Reasoning Diagnosing Benchmark (V-IRD), which targets this missing quadrant by requiring models to derive answers strictly through autonomous visual analysis. Our results show that, despite strong retrieval abilities, prominent VLMs struggle to utilize reference objects and to attend to visual evidence that requires self-directed inquiry. Simply put, strong semantic recognition does not equate to active visual exploration, revealing a critical gap in current VLMs. More information can be found at

</details>


### [170] [MVEB: Massive Video Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14958)
*Adnan El Assadi, Roman Solomatin, Isaac Chung, Chenghao Xiao, Deep Shah, Manan Dey, Shriya Sudhakar, Zacharie Bugaud, Wissam Siblini, Ayush Sunil Munot, Yashwanth Devavarapu, Rakshitha Ireddi, Michelle Yang, Márton Kardos, Niklas Muennighoff, Kenneth Enevoldsen*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MVEB是一个大规模视频嵌入评测基准，包含23个任务（分类、零样本分类、聚类、配对分类、检索和视频问答），评估了33个模型。发现没有单一模型在所有任务上占优：MLLM嵌入在分类、聚类、配对分类和问答上领先；多模态绑定在检索和零样本分类上领先；未经过对比适应的生成式MLLM在跨模态任务上表现崩溃。音频的贡献取决于数据集标注来源——当标签来自多模态时音频有帮助，当仅来自视觉时则有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频嵌入评测缺乏统一、全面的基准，不同研究使用不同的评估协议和数据集，难以公平比较不同视频嵌入方法（如MLLM嵌入、多模态绑定等）的性能。

Method: 从184个任务的MVEB+池中筛选出23个代表性任务构建MVEB，涵盖6大类任务（分类、零样本分类、聚类、配对分类、检索、视频问答）。评估33个模型，包括MLLM-based嵌入、多模态绑定模型、生成式MLLM等。集成到MTEB生态系统中实现文本、图像、音频、视频的统一评测。

Result: MLLM-based嵌入在分类、聚类、配对分类和问答上领先；多模态绑定在检索和零样本分类上领先；生成式MLLM未经对比适应则在跨模态任务上崩溃。音频的贡献与数据集标注来源相关：多模态标注时音频有帮助，仅视觉标注时音频有害，差距达6个百分点。

Conclusion: MVEB为视频嵌入提供了全面、标准化的评测基准，揭示了不同视频嵌入方法在不同任务上的优劣，以及音频模态贡献与数据标注来源的关联性，为视频嵌入研究提供了重要参考。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个大规模视频嵌入评测基准（MVEB），涵盖23个任务和33个模型评估，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a comprehensive video embedding benchmark but lacks direct focus on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Video embedding benchmark, 23 tasks, 33 models, MLLM embeddings, multimodal binding

Abstract: We introduce the Massive Video Embedding Benchmark (MVEB), a 23-task benchmark for video embeddings spanning classification, zero-shot classification, clustering, pair classification, retrieval, and video-centric question answering. We evaluate 33 models and find that no single model dominates: MLLM-based embeddings lead on classification, clustering, pair classification, and QA; multimodal binding leads on retrieval and zero-shot classification; generative MLLMs without contrastive adaptation collapse on cross-modal tasks. Paired video-only vs. audio+video evaluations show that audio's contribution depends on dataset annotation provenance: audio helps when labels were produced from both modalities and hurts when they were produced from visuals alone, a six-point gap consistent across model families. MVEB is derived from MVEB+, a 184-task pool, and is designed to maintain task diversity while reducing evaluation cost. It integrates into the MTEB ecosystem for unified evaluation across text, image, audio, and video. We release MVEB and all 184 tasks along with code and a leaderboard at .

</details>


### [171] [Where Did It Go Wrong? Process-Level Evaluation of Web Agents with Semantic State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15673)
*Jiwan Chung, JiHyuk Byun, Vibhav Vineet, Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 WebStep 基准，包含 1800 个带语义状态追踪的 Web 任务实例，通过过程级分析揭示终端成功率无法反映的智能体行为差异，并定位具体技能缺陷和关键错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Web Agent 评测仅关注终端成功率，忽略了交互过程中的丰富信息，无法为智能体改进提供细粒度指导。

Method: 构建 WebStep 基准，每个网站暴露确定性语义 MDP 与 GUI 并行，自动记录高层语义状态和转移轨迹，实现无需人工标注的细粒度过程分析。基于语义轨迹计算过程指标（探索覆盖 vs 执行准确率），按技能分解分析，并进行分叉点分析定位关键错误。

Result: 三个成功率相近（31-33%）的智能体在过程指标上表现出显著差异；同一网站内不同技能维度上智能体表现排名相反（如 Housing 上 CUA 在 commit 动作领先 23.7% 但在 filtering 落后 15.6%）；分叉分析显示关键错误是智能体特异的而非共享的；任务难度增加时过程差异进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 过程级分析为 Web Agent 评测开辟了新方向，能提供细粒度、可操作的改进方向，揭示终端成功率无法反映的智能体行为差异。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 WebStep 基准，用于 Web Agent 的过程级细粒度评测，属于多模态智能体评测范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Process-level evaluation of web agents is methodologically sound but far from the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: web agent evaluation, process-level analysis, semantic state tracking, benchmark, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Web agents act through long interaction sequences, yet existing benchmarks evaluate only terminal success, discarding all process information and offering little guidance on improvement. In this work, we conduct a process-level analysis of web agents. We introduce WebStep, a benchmark of 1,800 task instances with controlled difficulty and automatic semantic state tracking. Each website exposes a deterministic semantic MDP alongside the GUI: the agent operates on the interface, while the environment records high-level states and transitions in the background, enabling fine-grained analysis without manual annotation. Based on the semantic trajectory, we first show that process metrics reveal differences invisible to outcome evaluation: three agents whose success rates cluster within 31-33% diverge in exploration reach versus execution accuracy. Then, decomposing by skill characterizes the nature of these differences, exposing opposite per-skill rankings hidden within the same website: e.g., on Housing, OpenAI CUA outperforms Qwen3.5 by 23.7% on commit actions yet underperforms it by 15.6% on filtering, pinpointing a concrete skill to improve even within a domain. Bifurcation analysis further localizes the decisive error that loses the task and shows that this error is agent-specific rather than shared. Finally, these differences widen as tasks grow harder: success rate is similar on easy tasks but separates sharply as exploration becomes more demanding. Our process-level analysis opens a new avenue in web agent evaluation, providing fine-grained and actionable insight into where and how each agent should be improved.

</details>


### [172] [FinBalance: A Multi-Document Accounting Reconciliation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15949)
*Sasank Tumpati, Devansh Agarwal, Ayush Kedia, Arjun Neekhra, Murari Mandal, Krishna Garg, Yash Sinha, Suman Gupta, Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FinBalance 是一个面向多文档会计对账的评测基准，涵盖8个行业、3种周期类型和5个难度级别。该基准通过确定性生成器构建包含商业场景、会计政策、税务/外汇处理、文档模式、干扰项和不一致性模板的源文档包，并生成会计分录、资产负债表和23种不一致性标签。评测6个当代LLM，最高仅达46%的精确资产负债表准确率，揭示了模型在文档绑定和一致性聚合方面的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融NLP基准主要评测已准备好的文档（如申报文件、表格、提取值），但真实的会计工作始于更早的阶段：源文档必须被对账为带引用的会计分录，汇总为资产负债表，并检查矛盾。目前缺乏评估LLM完成这一完整会计对账流程能力的基准。

Method: 构建了一个确定性生成器，组合人类撰写的商业场景、会计政策、税务/外汇处理、文档模式、干扰项和不一致性模板，生成源文档包。通过分类账系统生成会计分录、资产负债表和23种不一致性标签。在710条记录上评测了6个当代LLM，并设计了BS_exact（模型直接报告的资产负债表）和BS_recon（通过分类账重放模型分录得到的资产负债表）两种评估指标。

Result: 6个LLM最高仅达46%的精确资产负债表准确率。4个模型在BS_exact和BS_recon之间存在26-41个百分点的差距。模型能生成数值上合理的分录，但无法将其绑定到支持文档并一致地聚合。引用压力提示对文档链接错误几乎没有改善，而分类账反馈消融实验显著改善了报告的资产负债表，同时暴露了不一致性检测的权衡。

Conclusion: FinBalance 揭示了当前LLM在多文档会计对账这一复杂金融任务中的根本性缺陷——模型能生成看似合理的数值但无法正确绑定文档和一致聚合。该基准为评估和改进LLM的文档推理、一致性检测和多步聚合能力提供了有价值的测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多文档会计对账基准 FinBalance，属于多模态（文本/表格/文档）评测与基准方向，与 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial NLP benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Financial domain benchmark, multi-document accounting, low LLM accuracy, not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched

Abstract: Existing financial-NLP benchmarks mostly evaluate prepared artifacts such as filings, tables, or extracted values. Real accounting begins earlier: source documents must be reconciled into cited journal entries, aggregated into a balance sheet, and checked for contradictions. We introduce FinBalance, a multi-document accounting reconciliation benchmark built from source-document bundles across eight industries, three period types, and five difficulty levels. Human-authored business scenarios, accounting policies, tax/FX treatments, document schemas, distractors, and inconsistency templates are composed by a deterministic generator whose ledger produces journal entries,balance sheets, and 23 inconsistency-code labels. On a 710-record evaluation split, six contemporary LLMs reach at most 46% exact final-balance-sheet accuracy. Four models show a 26-41 pp gap between BS_exact, the model's reported balance sheet, and BS_recon, the balance sheet obtained by replaying its entries through our ledger. Models often recover numerically plausible entries but fail to bind them to supporting documents and aggregate them consistently. Citation-pressure prompting barely changes document-linking errors, while ledger-feedback ablations substantially improve reported balance sheets and expose inconsistency-detection trade-offs. Expert finance reviewers validate the benchmark design and labels.

</details>


### [173] [Keep It in Mind: User Centric Continual Spatial Intelligence Reasoning in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15200)
*Yun Wang, Junbin Xiao, Han Lyu, Yifan Wang, Jing Zuo, Zhanjie Zhang, Hong Huang, Dapeng Wu, Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出UCS-Bench，一个面向第一视角视频流中持续空间智能推理的大规模基准数据集（170+小时、8.1K+问题），并提出了DirectMe框架，通过增量构建和维护结构化空间记忆，显著提升了多模态大模型在长时空间推理、视角歧义消解和动态场景适应方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准多关注静态图像或短时视频，缺乏对第一视角视频流中持续空间智能（动态空间推理、长期记忆、与用户实时位置对齐）的系统评测，亟需一个专门的基准和解决方案来推动该方向研究。

Method: 提出UCS-Bench数据集（170+小时第一视角视频，8.1K+时间戳问题），覆盖空间推理、记忆召回、视角歧义消解等任务。提出DirectMe框架，从流式第一视角观测中增量构建和维护结构化空间记忆，将视觉感知与记忆更新、空间推理紧密耦合，支持长时查询。

Result: DirectMe显著提升了主流多模态大模型的空间推理能力，并超越了多个空间感知和长视频流模型。

Conclusion: UCS-Bench和DirectMe为第一视角AI助手的空间智能研究提供了基准和方法基础，有望推动持续空间智能推理的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了UCS-Bench数据集（170+小时第一视角视频，8.1K+带时间戳问题），用于评测第一视角视频流中的持续空间智能推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for egocentric spatial reasoning but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: egocentric video, spatial reasoning, continual memory, multimodal LLM benchmark, long-horizon queries

Abstract: We introduce UCS-Bench, a dataset spanning 170+ hours of egocentric visual observations with 8.1K+ timestamped questions for diagnosing User-Centric Continual Spatial intelligence in egocentric video streams. UCS-Bench targets a new problem that emphasizes dynamic spatial reasoning, long-term memory, and their alignment with users' real-time locations. We propose DirectMe, a framework that incrementally constructs and maintains a structured spatial memory from streaming egocentric observations. DirectMe enables robust tracking and recall of object locations, all relative to the user's movement over time. By tightly coupling visual perception with memory updates and spatial reasoning, our approach supports long-horizon queries that require recalling interactions, resolving viewpoint-induced ambiguities, and adapting to dynamic scenes. Our experiments show that DirectMe significantly improves the spatial reasoning of leading multimodal LLMs; it also surpasses many spatially aware and long-form streaming video models. We hope our benchmark and solution will advance spatial intelligence research for egocentric AI assistants. Data and code are available at .

</details>


### [174] [Comparing Human Gaze and Vision-Language Model Attention in Safety-Relevant Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15202)
*Marta Vallejo, Siwen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过眼动追踪实验收集人类在安全相关场景中的注视数据，并与GPT-4o、Gemini、Claude等VLM生成的注意力显著性图进行对比，发现VLM无需眼动训练数据即可大致预测人类在安全场景中的视觉注意力分布，其中Gemini Pro在空间定位上表现最佳，GPT-4o在分布匹配上最接近人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉注意力在感知和应对潜在风险环境中起关键作用。研究旨在探索大型视觉语言模型能否在没有眼动训练数据的情况下，识别出安全相关场景中吸引人类注意力的区域，从而为近似人类注意力模式提供可扩展的工具。

Method: 收集10名被试在33张不同风险等级场景图像上的眼动数据（使用Pupil Invisible可穿戴眼镜），生成群体平均注视热力图。同时通过OpenAI Vision API等接口提示GPT-4o、Gemini Pro、Gemini Flash、Claude生成空间注意力预测，转换为显著性图。使用Pearson相关系数、NSS、KL散度和AUC-Judd四种指标评估人类热力图与模型显著性图的空间对齐程度。

Result: GPT-4o与人类注视的Pearson相关系数为0.515±0.117，NSS为0.988±0.323，KL散度为1.766±0.844，AUC-Judd为0.806±0.076。所有模型均超过AUC-Judd随机基线0.5且NSS为正。Gemini Pro在三个指标上空间定位最强，GPT-4o在KL散度上分布匹配最佳。

Conclusion: 大型视觉语言模型能够识别与人类视觉注意力大致对应的场景区域，无需眼动训练数据。VLM有潜力作为可扩展的工具来近似人类注意力模式，尤其在安全相关场景中具有应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM（GPT-4o、Gemini、Claude）的注意力预测与人类眼动注视模式的对齐程度，属于多模态模型的能力评测。虽然聚焦于安全场景下的视觉注意力，但核心是评估VLM的视觉注意力预测能力，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper is a VLM attention benchmark study, not directly advancing the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM attention alignment, human gaze comparison, safety-relevant scenes, cross-model evaluation, no eye-tracking training needed

Abstract: Human visual attention plays an important role in how people perceive and respond to environments containing potential risks. This study investigates whether large vision-language models can identify the same regions of a scene that attract human attention in safety-relevant environments. Eye-tracking data were collected from ten participants viewing 33 scene images representing environments with varying levels of potential risk using Pupil Invisible wearable glasses. Gaze coordinates were mapped onto stimulus images to generate population-averaged human gaze heatmaps. In parallel, GPT-4o was prompted through the OpenAI Vision Application Programming Interface (API) to generate spatial predictions of visual attention, which were converted into saliency maps for comparison with human gaze patterns. Spatial alignment between human gaze heatmaps and model-generated saliency maps was evaluated using four complementary metrics: Pearson correlation (r = 0.515 +- 0.117), Normalised Scanpath Saliency (NSS = 0.988 +- 0.323), Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL = 1.766 +- 0.844), and Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve using the Judd formulation (AUC-Judd = 0.806 +- 0.076). A cross-model comparison with Gemini Pro, Gemini Flash, and Claude showed that all models exceeded the AUC-Judd chance baseline of 0.5 and achieved positive NSS scores. Gemini Pro demonstrated the strongest spatial localisation according to three of the four metrics, whereas GPT-4o produced the closest distributional match to human attention as measured by KL divergence. These findings suggest that large vision-language models can identify regions that broadly correspond to where humans direct visual attention in safety-relevant scenes without requiring eye-tracking training data. The results highlight the potential of vision-language models as a scalable tool for approximating human attentional patterns.

</details>


### [175] [UrbanWell: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Spatio-Temporal Urban Wellbeing Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15890)
*Yanxin Xi, Xiang Su, Jie Feng, Yu Liu, Sasu Tarkoma, Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UrbanWell 是一个大规模基准，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在城市福祉分析中的时空推理能力，涵盖 38 个城市、多种环境/空间/感知指标，并包含静态预测、未来值预测和趋势分类等任务，对 15 个 SOTA MLLM 进行了零样本评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 MLLM 在城市福祉分析中的时空推理能力缺乏系统性的评测基准，现有工作多关注静态视觉理解，难以评估模型对异构时空信号的联合建模能力。

Method: 构建 UrbanWell 基准，整合卫星图像和街景图像，覆盖 38 个城市多年数据，包含环境（CO₂、NO₂、PM2.5、NDVI）、空间可达性、城市形态、城市活力和主观感知五大类指标，所有指标在网格级别对齐。定义静态预测、未来值预测和趋势分类三类任务，对 15 个 SOTA MLLM 进行零样本评测。

Result: MLLM 能够捕捉显著的空间和感知线索，但在不同城市指标（如环境 vs. 主观感知）上的表现差异显著，整体性能仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: UrbanWell 为城市智能中的多模态时空推理提供了标准化评测平台，揭示了当前 MLLM 在城市福祉分析中的能力边界与不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 UrbanWell，一个用于评估 MLLM 在城市福祉分析中时空推理能力的大规模基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for MLLM spatial-temporal reasoning but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal benchmark, urban wellbeing, spatial-temporal reasoning, zero-shot MLLM evaluation, no continuous token or video generation focus

Abstract: Understanding urban wellbeing from multimodal data requires integrating heterogeneous spatial and temporal signals, posing significant challenges for current multimodal large language models (MLLMs). We introduce UrbanWell, a large-scale benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the spatio-temporal reasoning capabilities of MLLMs for urban wellbeing analytics through joint modeling of satellite and street view imagery. UrbanWell spans 38 cities across multiple years and includes diverse indicators covering (1) environmental conditions (CO$_2$, NO$_2$, PM${2.5}$, and Normalized Difference Vegetation Index), (2) spatial accessibility (minimum distance to supermarkets and restaurants), (3) urban form (road length, road density, and land use), (4) urban vitality (population, economic activity diversity, and land use diversity), and (5) subjective perception attributes (e.g., safety, beauty, liveliness, wealth, and quietness). All indicators are aligned at grid level to enable standardized evaluation. Beyond static prediction, UrbanWell defines temporal reasoning tasks, including future value forecasting from historical observations and temporal trend classification. We benchmark 15 state-of-the-art representative MLLMs in a zero-shot setting, providing a comprehensive comparative evaluation across spatial and temporal dimensions. Experimental results indicate that while MLLMs capture salient spatial and perceptual cues, their performance varies substantially across heterogeneous urban indicators spanning environment and subjective perception. UrbanWell serves as a unified benchmark for evaluating multimodal spatial and temporal reasoning in urban wellbeing analytics, offering a standardized testbed for systematic assessment and future research on multimodal urban intelligence. Our codes and datasets are accessible via .

</details>


### [176] [Conditional Multi-Event Temporal Grounding in Long-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15320)
*Yuanhao Zou, Arthad Kulkarni, Lucas Tonanez, Lincoln Spencer, Guangyu Sun, Tianxingjian Ding, Andong Deng, Yi Li, Shuangjun Liu, Yuan Li, Dashan Gao, Ning Bi, Taotao Jing, Shuai Zhang, Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoMET-Bench，一个面向长视频中条件多事件时序定位的新基准，包含 2789 个查询、600 个平均时长 33.8 分钟的视频，覆盖 5 个真实场景。每个查询包含 4 个时间条件、3 个空间条件及负查询子集。同时提出统一评测协议和新指标 Rejection-F1，并构建了 CoMET-Agent 无训练智能体框架，在 GPT-5 基础上提升 F1@0.5 达 6.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时序定位基准仅定位单时刻、计数不考虑时间条件，或将定位与计数视为分离任务，无法满足真实应用中定位所有满足组合时空条件事件的需求。

Method: 1) 构建 CoMET-Bench 基准，包含 600 个长视频（平均 33.8 分钟）、2789 个查询，每个查询由 4 个时间条件和 3 个空间条件组成，并包含负查询子集。2) 提出统一评测协议，联合衡量计数、定位和负查询识别，引入 Rejection-F1 指标防止"总是空"的投机行为。3) 提出 CoMET-Agent，一个无训练智能体框架，将任务重构为结构化搜索与聚合。

Result: 现有 MLLM、基于智能体和专用定位方法在该任务上表现不佳。CoMET-Agent 通过结构化推理在 GPT-5 基础上提升 F1@0.5 达 6.1%。失败分析揭示了三个开放方向：细粒度实体追踪、位置统一检索和因果事件配对。

Conclusion: 条件多事件时序定位是一个尚未解决的重要任务，现有方法远未达到实际应用要求。CoMET-Bench 和 CoMET-Agent 为这一方向提供了基准和方法基础，未来需要进一步研究细粒度实体追踪、位置统一检索和因果事件配对等挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 CoMET-Bench，一个用于长视频中条件多事件时序定位的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时提出了 CoMET-Agent 方法，但核心贡献是基准与评测协议。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specialized video grounding benchmark, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Long-form video temporal grounding, Conditional multi-event benchmark, Rejection-F1 metric, CoMET-Agent framework, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have made rapid progress in video temporal grounding, yet real-world applications routinely require localizing every event that satisfies compositional temporal and spatial conditions. Existing benchmarks fall short: they localize only a single moment per query, count without temporal conditions, or treat grounding and counting as disjoint tasks. We introduce CoMET-Bench for Conditional Multi-Event Temporal Grounding in long-form video, comprising 2789 queries over 600 videos averaging 33.8 minutes across five real-world domains, with each query composed from 4 temporal conditions, 3 spatial conditions, and a dedicated negative-query subset. We further propose a unified evaluation protocol jointly measuring counting, grounding, and negative-query recognition, including a new Rejection-F1 metric that prevents trivial gaming by lazy "always-empty" models. Benchmarking a broad suite of MLLMs, agent-based, and grounding-specialized methods reveals that existing approaches remain far from solving this task. Building on these findings, we propose CoMET-Agent, a training-free agentic framework that reformulates the task as structured search-and-aggregate, improving F1@0.5 by 6.1% over GPT-5 purely through structural reasoning. Failure analysis further surfaces three open directions: fine-grained entity tracking, position-uniform retrieval, and causal event pairing.

</details>


### [177] [GRACE: Step-Level Benchmark for Faithful Reasoning over Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16151)
*Hoang Pham, Dong Le, Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GRACE 是首个步骤级别的忠实性评测基准，包含人工标注的推理步骤忠实性标签、数据驱动的错误分类体系（推理错误和事实性错误各4类），覆盖10个模型在4个数据集上的CoT轨迹。实验表明当前模型在该基准上表现不佳，将步骤级忠实性信号融入强化学习可提升下游准确率和推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅在响应级别检测幻觉，无法定位推理链中具体哪一步出错以及错误类型。需要细粒度的步骤级忠实性评测来诊断和提升模型的推理可靠性。

Method: 构建GRACE基准：从4个源数据集收集10个模型的CoT轨迹，人工标注每个步骤的忠实性、错误类别和自然语言解释；通过无监督聚类自底向上发现错误分类体系（GRACE-Inference和GRACE-Grounding各4类）；将步骤级忠实性信号作为奖励融入强化学习流程。

Result: 当前模型在GRACE上表现不佳，存在较大提升空间；将步骤级忠实性信号融入RL训练可同时提升下游准确率和推理可靠性。

Conclusion: GRACE提供了细粒度的步骤级忠实性评测，揭示了现有模型在上下文推理中的忠实性缺陷，并为通过强化学习提升推理可靠性提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了GRACE，首个步骤级别的忠实性评测基准，用于评估模型在上下文推理中的忠实性。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本推理（cs.CL），但其方法论——构建细粒度评测基准、错误分类体系——与多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）高度契合，且该评测框架可扩展至多模态场景。因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning faithfulness evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: step-level faithfulness benchmark, CoT reasoning evaluation, error taxonomy, reinforcement learning for reasoning, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks require models to reason over input context, from document-grounded question answering to rule-based deduction. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting produces traces that appear transparent, yet individual steps can silently deviate from the source evidence, even when the final answer is correct. Existing methods detect hallucinations at the response level but fail to identify where in the chain a failure occurs or what type it is. We introduce GRACE, the first human-annotated step-level faithfulness benchmark with a data-driven error taxonomy for context-grounded textual reasoning. GRACE covers CoT traces from 10 models across 4 source datasets, with each step annotated for faithfulness, error category, and natural language explanation. A data-driven taxonomy, discovered bottom-up via unsupervised clustering, organizes failures into two tracks: GRACE-Inference (deductive errors) and GRACE-Grounding (factual grounding errors), with four categories each. The evaluation set is human-annotated and challenging by design. Our experiments reveal substantial headroom for current models. In addition, integrating step-level faithfulness signals into reinforcement learning pipelines improves both downstream accuracy and reasoning reliability.

</details>


### [178] [Weaving Multi-Source Evidence for Biomedical Reasoning: The BioMedHop Benchmark and BioWeave Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16211)
*Xingyu Tan, Shiyuan Liu, Xiaoyang Wang, Qing Liu, Xiwei Xu, Xin Yuan, Liming Zhu, Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioMedHop 基准和 BioWeave 框架，用于评估和增强生物医学问答中的多源证据图推理能力。BioMedHop 包含 10,045 个实例，覆盖知识图谱、文献、网页及混合证据场景；BioWeave 通过检索 KG 路径、收集文档和网页线索、构建统一证据图并验证答案，在 BioMedHop 上超越基线方法 10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学问答基准主要关注考试式知识、文献理解或短程多跳推理，缺乏对源条件图推理和证据拓扑结构构建的探索，无法评估模型在分散于知识图谱、文献和网页等多源证据中进行推理的能力。

Method: 提出 BioMedHop 基准（10,045 个实例，涵盖 KG/文档/网页/混合四种证据设置及四种推理类型）和 BioWeave 框架（检索生物医学 KG 路径、收集文档和网页线索、组装为统一证据图、通过实体级证据支持验证答案）。

Result: BioWeave 在 BioMedHop 上整体平均性能最佳，超越强基线 ToG-2 达 10.5%；能一致提升不同 LLM 骨干的性能，使 Qwen3-4B 等小模型达到接近 GPT-4-Turbo 的推理表现。

Conclusion: 多源证据图推理是生物医学问答中的重要挑战，BioMedHop 提供了有效的评测平台，BioWeave 展示了源感知推理框架的优越性，且能帮助小模型实现与大模型相当的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 BioMedHop 基准，用于评估生物医学推理中的多源证据图推理能力，属于多模态/多源评测基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on biomedical reasoning, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: biomedical QA, multi-source evidence graph, benchmark, not in priority subtopics, negative keyword "clinical"

Abstract: Biomedical question answering (QA) increasingly requires reasoning over interacting entities, where supporting evidence is scattered across biomedical knowledge graphs, literature documents, and web-accessible resources. However, existing biomedical QA benchmarks mainly focus on exam-style knowledge, literature comprehension, or short-range multi-hop inference, leaving source-conditioned graph reasoning and evidence topology construction underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce BioMedHop, a multi-source graph-grounded benchmark for evaluating biomedical reasoning over structured evidence topologies. BioMedHop contains 10,045 instances across KG, document, web, and hybrid evidence settings, covering shared-neighbor matching, intersection reasoning, path-based reasoning, and counting, with option-based, open-ended, and numeric count renderings. To support this benchmark, we further propose BioWeave, a source-aware reasoning framework that retrieves biomedical KG paths, gathers supporting clues from documents and web sources, assembles them into a unified evidence graph, and verifies answers through entity-level evidence support. Comprehensive experiments show that BioWeave achieves the best overall performance among compared methods on BioMedHop, outperforming the strong hybrid baseline ToG-2 by 10.5% in the overall average. Moreover, BioWeave consistently improves different LLM backbones and enables smaller models, such as Qwen3-4B, to achieve reasoning performance comparable to GPT-4-Turbo.

</details>


### [179] [PAL-Bench: Evidence-Grounded Profile Reconstruction from Longitudinal Personal Albums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16175)
*Qiwei Yan, Zhiqiang Yuan, Zexi Jia, Nanxing Hu, Kailin Lyu, Jie Zhou, Jinchao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PAL-Bench 是一个受控的多模态基准，用于评测从纵向个人相册中重建用户档案（身份、关系、事实）的能力，包含 50 个合成用户、36,659 张照片和 2,799 个目标，并提供了隐私保护的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉、视频、文档和 lifelog 基准只测试子问题，无法评估相册级别的档案重建（包括社会身份绑定和证据引用）。由于真实相册涉及隐私，无法公开标注 ground truth，因此需要一个可控的合成基准来推动该方向的研究。

Method: 构建 PAL-Bench：通过 Evidence Compiler 构建潜在私有世界，编程目标级证据路径，渲染相册像素，通过感知管线重新测量，并导出经过审计的公开/私有视图。智能体仅获得感知导出的公开记录，目标、标识符映射和证据路径保持隐藏。包含 50 个合成用户、36,659 张照片和 2,799 个目标。同时提出 PAL-TRACE 参考框架，先冻结身份绑定再进行主人事实挖掘。

Result: 在七个系统和两个计算匹配的诊断上，七指标评测协议揭示了合理的档案摘要与忠实的社会重建之间存在差距：系统能恢复部分主人事实，但在重复出现的身份识别和证据引用方面表现不佳。PAL-TRACE 表现最佳，但困难的身份解析问题远未解决。

Conclusion: PAL-Bench 为感知实体解析、多模态数据整合、时序证据聚合和证据感知的结构化预测提供了一个测试平台。当前系统在身份解析和证据引用方面仍有显著不足，需要进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PAL-Bench，一个面向纵向个人相册中基于证据的身份档案重建任务的新基准，涉及多模态数据（人脸、文本、时间戳、位置等）的整合与评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal album profiling, not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark, personal album reconstruction, weak-schema database, evidence citation, privacy-preserving evaluation

Abstract: Longitudinal personal albums are weak-schema multimodal databases: noisy perceptual records whose key facts require joins across faces, text, timestamps, locations, and repeated events. Existing visual, video, document, and lifelog benchmarks test sub-problems, but not album-scale profile reconstruction with social identity binding and evidence citation. Benchmarking this task is difficult because the ground truth needed for evaluation--owner profiles, social graphs, face-name maps, and evidence provenance--is private state that real albums cannot safely release. We introduce PAL-Bench, a controlled benchmark for evidence-grounded reconstruction under a public-record contract. Its Evidence Compiler builds latent private worlds, programs target-level evidence paths, renders album pixels, re-measures them through perception pipelines, and exports audited public/private views. Agents receive only perception-derived public records; targets, identifier maps, and evidence paths remain hidden. PAL-Bench contains 50 synthetic users, 36,659 public photo records, and 2,799 targets over owner facts, identities, and relations. A privacy-preserving audit with 10 participants confirms that PAL-Bench evidence structures match real private albums, though equivalent releases remain privacy-prohibitive. Across seven systems and two compute-matched diagnostics, a seven-metric protocol reveals a gap between plausible profile summarization and faithful social reconstruction: systems recover some owner facts but struggle with recurring identities and evidence citation. PAL-TRACE, a reference framework that freezes identity bindings before owner-fact mining, performs best but leaves hard identity resolution far from solved. PAL-Bench provides a testbed for perceptual entity resolution, multimodal data integration, temporal evidence aggregation, and provenance-aware structured prediction.

</details>


### [180] [Lost at the End: Primacy Bias in Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16494)
*Jieyuan Liu, Jianyang Gu, Shijie Chen, Jefferson Chen, Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态KB-VQA中VLM reader存在"首因偏差"（primacy bias）：黄金段落放在首位比放在末尾的准确率高16-26个百分点，与纯文本LLM的U型"lost-in-the-middle"效应相反，称为"Lost at the End"。通过消融实验定位到指令微调reader的prompt slot 0，并发现检索侧修复无法消除该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯文本长上下文LLM中存在U型的"lost-in-the-middle"效应（上下文两端信息被更好利用，中间被忽略），但该效应是否迁移到多模态KB-VQA场景中尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统探测多模态检索增强问答中reader侧的位置依赖。

Method: 设计gold-position protocol：在KB-VQA中固定黄金段落的位置（从slot 0到slot k-1），其余位置填充干扰段落，控制变量地测量不同位置对准确率的影响。在三个开源7B/8B VLM reader（LLaVA-NeXT、Phi-3-vision、InternVL2）和两个KB-VQA基准（OK-VQA、Infoseek）上，k最大为20进行实验。同时进行文本对照、图像位置消融、干扰项打乱消融等分析。

Result: 1) 多模态KB-VQA中呈现首因偏差（primacy bias），黄金段落放在首位比末尾高16-26个百分点，与纯文本的U型效应相反。2) 文本对照实验表明多模态设置将已有的文本首因偏差放大了2.2-4.5倍。3) 消融实验将问题定位到指令微调reader的prompt slot 0。4) 三种检索侧修复（MMR、oracle reranking、rank-based reordering）均无法缩小该差距。

Conclusion: 多模态KB-VQA中VLM reader存在显著的首因偏差，recall@k不是评估KB-VQA系统的正确指标，缩小该差距需要reader侧的干预。本文发布的gold-position protocol可作为评估此类干预措施的标准工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态检索增强问答中的位置偏差（primacy bias），设计了一个受控的评测协议（gold-position protocol）来探测VLM reader在KB-VQA中的上下文位置依赖效应。这属于多模态系统的评测方法与基准构建，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Primacy bias in multimodal RAG is a solid finding but falls outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: multimodal RAG, primacy bias, lost-in-the-middle, KB-VQA, VLM reader

Abstract: Knowledge-based visual question answering (KB-VQA) lets vision-language systems answer questions that exceed their parametric knowledge by conditioning a reader on passages retrieved from a Wikipedia-scale knowledge base. In pure-text long-context LLMs, retrieved-context use follows the U-shaped "lost-in-the-middle" effect of Liu et al. (2024): information at the start and end of context is used, the middle is lost. Whether this transfers to deployed multimodal KB-VQA is open. To close this gap, we design the first controlled probe of reader-side position dependence in multimodal KB-VQA: a gold-position protocol in which only the gold passage's prompt slot varies within question. We run it on three open-source 7B/8B VLM readers and two KB-VQA benchmarks at k up to 20. The shape flips from U to primacy: gold-at-first beats gold-at-last by 16 to 26 points on every reader-by-benchmark cell, an effect we call "Lost at the End". Three targeted ablations narrow the cause: a text-only control shows the multimodal setting amplifies an already-present text-mode primacy 2.2 to 4.5 times, and image-position and distractor-shuffle ablations together pin the locus to prompt slot 0 of the instruction-tuned reader. On a frozen reader, three retrieval-side fixes (MMR, oracle reranking, rank-based reordering) all leave the gap intact (no separable improvement). Our findings indicate that recall@k is the wrong metric for deployed KB-VQA and that closing the gap requires reader-side intervention; we release our protocol as a controlled instrument for evaluating such interventions.

</details>


### [181] [OneFocus: Enabling Real-World X-ray Security Screening with a Unified Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15663)
*Jiali Wen, Hongxia Gao, Litao Li, Yixin Chen, Kaijie Zhang, Qianyun Liu, Xiaoqin Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMXray 基准（52,124 对 X 光违禁品图像-文本对，28 类细粒度违禁品）和 OneFocus 统一视觉语言模型，支持 VQA、违禁品定位、分类和图像理解四项任务，在 X 光安检领域达到 SOTA 并展现跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 X 光违禁品检测方法难以适应新型违禁品种类且缺乏视觉理解能力，而 VLM 虽有强泛化能力但受限于缺乏高质量的 X 光图像-文本配对数据。

Method: 构建 MMXray 基准（52,124 对图像-文本，28 类细粒度违禁品）；提出 CleanDET 合成数据集和 AnyContraSyn 可控合成方法以模拟真实遮挡模式；开发 OnePipe 系统化数据整理流程；基于 MMXray 训练 OneFocus 统一 VLM，支持 VQA、定位、分类和图像理解四项任务。

Result: OneFocus 在 X 光违禁品理解任务上达到 SOTA 性能，并展现出鲁棒的跨域泛化能力，为安检场景建立了强视觉语言基线。

Conclusion: 通过构建高质量 X 光安检基准数据集 MMXray 和统一 VLM 模型 OneFocus，有效弥合了 VLM 在安检领域的数据缺口，证明了 VLM 在 X 光违禁品检测中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 MMXray 基准数据集（52,124 对 X 光违禁品图像-文本对），并提出了统一 VLM 模型 OneFocus 支持 VQA、定位、分类和理解四项任务。核心贡献在于新基准与评测，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on X-ray security screening, a niche application far from user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: X-ray security, unified VLM, domain-specific benchmark, contraband detection, cross-domain generalization

Abstract: X-ray contraband detection is critical for security in large-scale logistics and transportation, yet conventional detectors struggle to adapt to emerging contraband types and lack fundamental visual understanding. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong generalization but are hindered by the scarcity of high-quality X-ray image-caption data. To bridge this critical gap, we present MMXray, a meticulously curated benchmark of 52,124 image-caption pairs spanning 28 fine-grained classes of X-ray contraband. To enrich MMXray with realistic occlusion patterns, we further introduce CleanDET, a dedicated synthesis dataset containing clean foreground contraband images from 28 categories and background images with diverse density levels, together with AnyContraSyn, a controllable synthesis method designed to operate on CleanDET. We also develop OnePipe, an extensible pipeline for systematic data curation. Built on MMXray, we propose OneFocus, a unified VLM that supports four core tasks: visual question answering, contraband localization, classification, and image understanding. OneFocus achieves state-of-the-art performance in X-ray contraband understanding and demonstrates robust cross-domain generalization, establishing a strong vision-language baseline for security screening.

</details>


### [182] [OmniTraffic: A Controllable Generation Pipeline and Benchmark for Spatio-Temporal Traffic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15749)
*Maonan Wang, Zhengyan Huang, Kemou Jiang, Yuhang Fu, Jiayue Zhu, Yuxin Cai, Xingchen Zou, Qiaosheng Zhang, Yi Yu, Ding Wang, Xi Chen, Ben M. Chen, Yuxuan Liang, Zhiyong Cui, Man On Pun, Yirong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, eess.SY, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OmniTraffic 提出了一个可控生成 pipeline 和基准，用于时空交通推理。基于12个真实路口构建可编辑3D环境，生成800万同步多视角VQA样本和3000个人工验证测试集，涵盖场景感知、多视角/时序推理和决策支持三个层次。评测11个前沿MLLM发现人类-模型差距巨大，尤其在拓扑和时空推理任务上。在模拟数据上微调轻量MLLM可提升真实交通场景性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通多模态基准主要关注被动视觉识别或孤立的视频理解，缺乏对结构感知的交通推理（如车道拓扑、多视角几何、时序演化、信号相位语义）的受控评估能力。作者希望构建一个可扩展、可控的生成pipeline和基准，以系统评估多模态大模型在交通场景中的时空推理能力。

Method: 基于12个真实世界路口重建为可编辑3D交通环境，结合两个国家的监控视频数据，构建OmniTraffic pipeline。利用结构化交通元数据（车辆状态、车道功能、信号相位等）生成同步多视角VQA样本。定义三级任务层次：场景感知、多视角与时序推理、决策支持。生成800万VQA样本和3000个人工验证测试集。在11个前沿MLLM上进行评测，并在模拟数据上微调轻量MLLM验证其迁移价值。

Result: 11个前沿MLLM在OmniTraffic上表现远低于人类，尤其在拓扑接地和时空推理任务上差距最大。在模拟OmniTraffic数据上微调轻量MLLM，可显著提升其在真实交通场景上的性能，证明了仿真生成监督对交通多模态推理的价值。

Conclusion: OmniTraffic填补了交通场景中结构感知时空推理评测的空白，提供了一个可扩展的可控生成pipeline和基准。实验表明当前MLLM在交通推理任务上仍有巨大提升空间，而仿真数据微调是一种有效的提升策略。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向交通场景的时空推理基准（OmniTraffic），包含8M VQA样本和3K人工验证测试集，并评测了11个前沿MLLM。核心贡献是构建多模态评测基准，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（CANONICAL）。同时，该工作也涉及可控生成pipeline（生成多视角VQA数据），因此也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic-specific multimodal reasoning benchmark, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Traffic reasoning benchmark, Controllable generation pipeline, MLLM evaluation, Simulation-to-real transfer, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Traffic scene understanding requires models to reason beyond object recognition, including lane topology, multi-view geometry, temporal evolution, and signal-phase semantics. However, existing traffic-oriented multimodal benchmarks largely emphasize passive visual recognition or isolated video understanding, offering limited support for evaluating structure-aware traffic reasoning under controlled conditions. We introduce OmniTraffic, a controllable generation pipeline and benchmark for spatio-temporal traffic reasoning. Built around 12 real-world intersections reconstructed into editable 3D traffic environments and complemented by surveillance footage from two countries, OmniTraffic supports both controlled and natural-condition evaluation. It defines a three-level task hierarchy spanning scene perception, multi-view and temporal reasoning, and decision support. Using structured traffic metadata, OmniTraffic generates synchronized multi-view VQA samples covering vehicle states, lane functions, view--BEV correspondence, temporal dynamics, and signal-phase analysis, resulting in 8M VQA samples and a 3K human-verified test set. Evaluation of eleven frontier MLLMs reveals a large human--model gap, with the most pronounced failures in topology-grounded and spatio-temporal reasoning tasks. Fine-tuning a lightweight MLLM on simulated OmniTraffic data further improves performance on real-world traffic scenes, demonstrating the value of simulation-generated supervision for traffic-specific multimodal reasoning. Beyond a fixed dataset, OmniTraffic provides an extensible pipeline with configurable intersections, camera views, traffic demands, signal phases, visual conditions, and rare events.

</details>


### [183] [LabOSBench: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents for Scientific Instrument Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16802)
*Anqi Zou, Han Deng, Chengyu Zhang, Junquan Hu, Yu Wang, Yuxiang Xing, Aokai Zhang, Hanling Zhang, Zhaoyang Liu, Ben Fei, Zhihui Wang, Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LabOSBench 是一个基于 Web 科学仪器模拟器的多模态 GUI 智能体评测基准，包含 8 种仪器模拟器上的 96 个子任务，覆盖从样品加载到数据检查的完整工作流，用于评估通用 VLM、专用 GUI 模型和高级智能体框架在科学仪器控制场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用基准主要关注虚拟化系统中的软件操作任务，而科学仪器场景需要复杂的界面协调控制和反馈驱动的参数调整。直接在物理高精度仪器上评估智能体成本高、风险大、可重复性差，因此需要一个模拟但真实的测试平台来支持可扩展且安全的评测。

Method: LabOSBench 构建了一套基于 Web 的科学仪器模拟器，通过浏览器直接操作，避免资源密集的 OS 虚拟化，支持灵活的任务配置和基于执行的评估。包含 8 种仪器模拟器上的 96 个子任务，涵盖样品加载、对准、参数调优、数据采集和结果检查等工作流。在子任务和端到端两个层面评估通用 VLM、专用 GUI 智能体模型和高级智能体框架。

Result: 实验表明，现有智能体可以完成许多结构化的 GUI 子任务，但在需要反馈驱动的操作和长时序工作流执行方面仍然存在困难。

Conclusion: LabOSBench 为将计算机使用智能体推向科学仪器控制提供了一个可重复、低成本的测试平台，揭示了当前智能体在反馈驱动操作和长时序工作流方面的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 LabOSBench，一个面向科学仪器控制的 GUI 智能体多模态评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agent benchmarking for scientific instruments, not on core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: Multimodal GUI agent benchmark, Scientific instrument control, Web-based simulator, Low relevance to continuous language/video generation, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Current computer-use benchmarks primarily focus on software operation tasks in virtualized systems, whereas scientific instrumentation scenarios require coordinated control over complex interfaces, and feedback-driven parameter adjustment. However, directly evaluating agents on physical high-precision instruments is impractical due to high cost, safety risks, limited accessibility, and difficulty in ensuring reproducible evaluation. This motivates the need for a simulated yet realistic testbed that preserves the operational challenges of scientific instruments while enabling scalable and safe benchmarking. To this end, we introduce LabOSBench, a challenging benchmark for multimodal GUI agents built on a suite of web-based scientific-instrument simulators. Operating directly via a browser, LabOSBench avoids resource-heavy OS virtualization while supporting flexible task configuration and execution-based evaluation. Specifically, LabOSBench constructs 96 subtasks across eight instrument simulators, covering workflows from sample loading, alignment, parameter tuning, and data acquisition to result inspection. We evaluate general-purpose vision-language models, specialized GUI agent models, and advanced agentic frameworks at both subtask and end-to-end levels. Our experiments reveal that while existing agents can complete many structured GUI subtasks, they still struggle with feedback-driven operations and long-horizon workflow execution. Overall, LabOSBench provides a reproducible, low-cost testbed for advancing computer-using agents toward scientific-instrument control.

</details>


### [184] [Benchmarking LLM Agents on Meta-Analysis Articles from Nature Portfolio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17041)
*Anzhe Xie, Weihang Su, Yujia Zhou, Yiqun Liu, Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSyn数据集，包含442个来自Nature Portfolio的专家策展元分析案例，用于评测LLM Agent在系统化科学推理（文献检索、PI/ECO筛选、统计聚合）全流程上的表现。实验发现，尽管检索阶段在K=200时召回率可达90.9%，但所有系统最多只能恢复52.7%的已纳入文献，揭示了当前LLM在筛选环节存在严重瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元分析是一种要求严格的证据综合形式，涉及文献检索、PI/ECO（人群/干预/对照/结局）引导的研究筛选和统计聚合。其结构化、可验证的工作流程非常适合评估系统化科学推理能力，但现有基准缺乏覆盖完整检索-筛选-合成管线的真实标注数据。

Method: 构建MetaSyn数据集：从Nature Portfolio期刊收集442个专家策展的元分析案例，每个案例包含研究问题、PI/ECO标准、14万篇PubMed文章的检索语料库、已验证的阳性研究、主题相似但不符合PI/ECO的困难负样本、完整搜索策略和时间范围。在此基础上，对12种管线配置（9种RAG变体和一种协议驱动Agent）进行基准测试。

Result: 尽管检索阶段在K=200时召回率可达90.9%，但所有系统最多只能恢复52.7%的已纳入文献。当前LLM无法可靠地将符合条件的研究与主题相关但不符合PI/ECO的干扰项区分开来。阶段归因指标揭示了系统在哪些环节成功或失败，单一端到端分数无法反映全貌。

Conclusion: 元分析全流程评测揭示了LLM在科学文献筛选环节存在严重瓶颈，即使检索召回率很高，模型也难以准确执行PI/ECO标准的细粒度筛选。这为未来提升LLM系统化科学推理能力指明了方向，即需要重点改进筛选阶段的判别能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了MetaSyn数据集，用于评测LLM Agent在元分析（meta-analysis）任务上的系统化科学推理能力，涉及文献检索、筛选和统计聚合的全流程评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为论文核心贡献是一个新的评测基准和数据集，用于评估LLM在结构化科学推理任务上的表现。虽然论文不涉及视觉模态，但"多模态评测与基准"作为广义的评测基准类别，涵盖各类LLM评测基准，且该论文的评测范式（检索-筛选-聚合）与多模态评测中的系统性评估思路一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on meta-analysis benchmarking, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: LLM agent evaluation, scientific reasoning benchmark, meta-analysis dataset, screening bottleneck, PI/ECO criteria

Abstract: Meta-analysis is a demanding form of evidence synthesis that combines literature retrieval, PI/ECO-guided study selection, and statistical aggregation. Its structured, verifiable workflow makes it an ideal substrate for evaluating systematic scientific reasoning, yet existing benchmarks lack ground truth across the full retrieval-screening-synthesis pipeline. We introduce MetaSyn, a dataset of 442 expert-curated meta-analyses from Nature Portfolio journals. Each entry pairs a research question with PI/ECO criteria, a retrieval corpus of 140k PubMed articles, verified positive studies, hard negatives that are topically similar but PI/ECO-ineligible, and complete search strategies and date bounds. Benchmarking twelve pipeline configurations (nine RAG variants and a protocol-driven agent) reveals a critical screening bottleneck: despite a retrieval ceiling of 90.9% recall at K=200, no system recovers more than 52.7% of ground-truth included literature. Current LLMs fail to reliably separate eligible studies from PI/ECO-failing distractors in pools of comparable topical relevance. Stage-attributed metrics capture where systems succeed and fail; a single end-to-end score does not.

</details>


### [185] [Tool-IQA: Augmenting Image Quality Assessment with Simple Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16082)
*Guanyi Qin, Junjie Zhang, Chunming He, Yibing Fu, Jie Liang, Tianhe Wu, Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Tool-IQA 提出了一种工具增强的图像质量评估方法，通过为 VLM 配备放大镜（Magnifier）和伽马校正器（Gamma Corrector）两个简单工具，将静态单次评分转变为动态工具辅助的评估流程，在多个 IQA 基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM 的 IQA 方法采用静态单次评分范式，但人类评估图像质量时会动态调整视角来检查细节和伪影。单次全局观察存在两个局限：一是仅全局感知限制了局部细节评估；二是原始图像强度分布可能掩盖可见性，导致质量检查不充分。

Method: Tool-IQA 为 VLM 配备两个简单工具：Magnifier（放大镜）用于检查局部细节，Gamma Corrector（伽马校正器）用于揭示可见性和隐藏伪影。评估流程分为三步：初始观察并记录评分依据、工具增强的深度检查、最终量化校准评分。此外，引入批量感知训练策略，奖励能产生积极贡献的工具交互而非简单鼓励使用工具。

Result: 在多个 IQA 基准上显著超越现有 SOTA 模型，例如在具有挑战性的 CLIVE 数据集上达到 PLCC 0.854。

Conclusion: 通过将被动评分转变为工具增强的工作流，Tool-IQA 有效提升了 VLM 在图像质量评估任务上的表现，验证了简单工具（放大镜和伽马校正）在辅助视觉质量评估中的价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像质量评估（IQA），属于多模态评测范畴。虽然论文提出了新的方法而非新基准，但IQA本身是多模态理解评测的重要子领域，且现有[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）是CANONICAL subtopic，可覆盖该方向。

Importance reason: Novel tool-augmented IQA method, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords not triggered. Signals: Tool-augmented IQA, VLM-based assessment, dynamic visual inspection, SOTA on CLIVE, batch-aware training

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been increasingly adopted for Image Quality Assessment (IQA). However, current methods typically employ a static one-shot scoring paradigm, despite the fact that humans assess image quality through dynamic visual inspection, e.g., selectively adjusting views to verify details and subtle artifacts. Specifically, relying solely on a single-pass observation introduces two primary limitations: first, perceiving the image only at a global scale restricts the assessment of finer local details; second, the original intensity distribution of the image may overwhelm the visibility, leading to insufficient inspection of image quality. To address these issues, we propose Tool-IQA, shifting the assessment mechanism from passive scoring to a tool-augmented workflow. In particular, we equip VLMs with simple yet effective view tools: a Magnifier to inspect local details, and a Gamma Corrector to uncover visibility and hidden artifacts. The assessment follows a structured pipeline that consists of an initial observation with rubric notes, a tool-augmented in-depth inspection, and a final quantification for calibrated quality score. Furthermore, to ensure efficient and purposeful tool callings, we introduce a batch-aware training strategy to reward tool interactions that can yield positive contributions rather than simply encouraging usage. Experiments on a variety of IQA benchmarks demonstrate that, with effective tool calling and calibrated assessment, our proposed Tool-IQA significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art models, e.g., it achieves a PLCC of 0.854 on the challenging CLIVE dataset.

</details>


### [186] [Chronological Blindness: Benchmarking Temporal Reasoning in Vision-Language Models with CHRONOSIGHT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16334)
*Parthaw Goswami, Jaynto Goswami Deep*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CHRONOSIGHT 基准，系统评测 VLM 在视觉时序推理五个维度（排序、定位、时间间隔估计、反转检测、异常检测）上的能力。人类平均准确率 0.89，最佳开源模型 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 仅 0.40，揭示 VLM 存在"时序盲"现象。轻量 LoRA 微调可显著提升性能，表明瓶颈部分在于指令跟随而非视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉感知天然具有时序性，能判断水果是成熟还是腐烂、建筑是施工还是拆除等。当前 VLM 是否具备这种视觉时序推理能力尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的评测基准。

Method: 构建 CHRONOSIGHT 基准，包含 1000 个样本，覆盖 8 类过程（生物生长、食物变化、物理风化、建筑、环境变化、人类衰老、天文现象、城市动态），时间跨度从分钟到千年。设计五个子任务：CHRONORANK（排序）、CHRONOLOCATE（单图阶段定位）、CHRONODELTA（时间间隔估计）、CHRONOREVERSE（反转检测）、CHRONOODD（异常检测）。评测 8 个开源 VLM（500M-19B），两种提示策略，并收集人类基线。

Result: 人类平均准确率 0.89；最佳开源模型 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 在直接提示下仅 0.40。在 151 个样本上 LoRA 微调后，CHRONODELTA 准确率从接近零提升至 0.43，并零样本迁移到 CHRONOODD（0.37）和 CHRONOREVERSE（0.64）。

Conclusion: VLM 存在显著的"时序盲"（chronological blindness）现象，与人类能力差距巨大。轻量微调可缓解该问题，表明瓶颈部分在于指令跟随而非视觉感知。该基准为未来 VLM 时序推理能力提升提供了重要评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 CHRONOSIGHT 基准，系统评测 VLM 在视觉时序推理（排序、定位、时间间隔估计等）五个维度的能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Solid VLM temporal reasoning benchmark but falls outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: VLM temporal reasoning benchmark, chronological blindness gap, LoRA fine-tuning improves, not in priority subtopics, no autoregressive/diffusion generation

Abstract: Human perception of visual scenes is inherently temporal. We instinctively recognise whether a fruit is ripening or rotting, whether construction is progressing or being demolished, and approximately how much time separates two photographs of the same subject. Whether large vision-language models (VLMs) share this competence remains an open and practically important question. We introduce CHRONOSIGHT, a rigorously controlled benchmark evaluating five dimensions of visual temporal reasoning: CHRONORANK (chronological ordering of image sequences), CHRONOLOCATE (ordinal stage localisation from a single image), CHRONODELTA (estimation of time elapsed between two images on a logarithmic scale), CHRONOREVERSE (detection of temporally reversed sequences), and CHRONOODD (identification of a temporal outlier within a set). The benchmark comprises 1{,}000 items across eight process families (biological growth, food transformation, physical weathering, construction, environmental change, human ageing, astronomical phenomena, and urban dynamics) spanning timescales from minutes to millennia. We evaluate eight open-source VLMs (500 M to 19 B parameters) under two prompting regimes and collect human performance baselines. Human performance averages 0.89 across tasks; the best open model (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) reaches 0.40 under direct prompting, a gap we term chronological blindness. Lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on 151 examples raises CHRONODELTA accuracy from near-zero to 0.43, transferring zero-shot to related tasks (CHRONOODD: 0.37; CHRONOREVERSE: 0.64)suggesting the bottleneck is partly instruction following rather than visual perception. Benchmark, code, and predictions will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [187] [Vision-Language Models as Zero-Annotation Oracles in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16658)
*Vishal Jain, Giorgio Buzzanca, Sarah Cechnicka, Maarten Naesens, Priyanka Koshy, Tri Nguyen, Jesper Kers, Candice Roufosse, Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (47.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出利用通用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为零标注oracle进行病理图像前景分割，并构建了Leica-75基准数据集。方法采用粗到细策略，将前景分割重新定义为视觉感知任务，在特殊染色（Jones银染、EVG）上显著优于监督基线，且跨染色方差降低7倍。通过自动上下文少样本提示和VLM标注审查，可蒸馏出轻量学生模型，为数字病理学提供可扩展的基础设施解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理图像前景分割方法依赖手工启发式规则或监督模型，这些模型对特定染色和扫描仪分布过拟合，在特殊染色（如Jones银染、Elastica van Gieson）上会静默失败。需要一种无需标注、泛化能力强的通用分割方法。

Method: 提出粗到细的前景分割框架：1) 将组织与背景的区分重新定义为自然图像识别问题，利用通用VLM（如GPT-4o、Gemini、Claude）作为零标注oracle；2) 粗阶段使用VLM对图像块进行二分类（组织vs背景）；3) 细阶段通过自动上下文（Auto-context）少样本提示处理困难样本；4) VLM用于标注质量审查（模糊检测、分割掩码审核）；5) 用生成的伪标签蒸馏轻量学生模型。

Result: 在Leica-75基准上，方法在OOD染色上达到最高分割质量（Jones: Dice 0.858±0.027, EVG: 0.853±0.041），跨染色方差比最佳监督基线低7倍。Auto-context将Stress-32困难子集上2B模型的Dice从0.470提升至0.819。VLM标注审查与人类专家共识高度一致（模糊检测kappa=0.989，分割掩码审核平均精度/召回率0.708 vs 人类0.646）。

Conclusion: 本文证明通用VLM可以作为零标注oracle有效解决病理图像前景分割这一基础设施瓶颈，在特殊染色上泛化能力远超领域专用监督模型。VLM生成的伪标签可用于蒸馏轻量学生模型，实现低成本高性能部署。该方法为数字病理学提供了一种原则性、可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了Leica-75基准数据集用于病理切片前景分割评测，并利用VLM作为零标注分割工具，属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时论文涉及VLM在医学领域的应用，但核心贡献是新的评测基准和VLM作为标注oracle的方法，因此映射到[multimodal_benchmark]。

Importance reason: Focuses on histopathology segmentation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: domain-specific pathology, zero-shot VLM application, no continuous language/multimodal or video generation, no autoregressive or diffusion generation, no world model

Abstract: Foreground segmentation is the critical first step of every computational pathology pipeline, yet existing methods rely on hand-tuned heuristics or supervised models that overfit to narrow stain and scanner distributions, failing silently on specialised stains such as Jones silver or Elastica van Gieson. We propose a coarse-to-fine approach that recasts foreground segmentation as a visual perception task and leverages general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-annotation oracles. Our key insight is that tissue-versus-background discrimination is a natural-image recognition problem, not a histopathological one, so VLMs trained on internet-scale corpora generalise where domain-specific models cannot. We introduce Leica-75, a benchmark of 75 renal transplant whole-slide images spanning three stain families. On Leica-75, our method achieves the highest segmentation quality on out-of-distribution stains (Dice 0.858 +/- 0.027 on Jones, 0.853 +/- 0.041 on EVG) with 7x lower cross-stain variance than the best supervised baseline, while remaining competitive on in-distribution H&E. Few-shot prompting with automatically curated exemplars (Auto-context) rescues hard cases on Stress-32 (n=32), a curated stress-test subset (Dice 0.470 to 0.819 for the 2B model). VLM-based annotation review matches human expert consensus (kappa=0.989 for blur detection; mean precision/recall grading accuracy 0.708 vs. human 0.646 for segmentation mask review). The resulting pseudo-labels are used to distil lightweight student models that are as performant as the teacher model while running for a fraction of the cost. Our framework provides a principled, scalable solution to a persistent infrastructure bottleneck in digital pathology.

</details>


### [188] [A Multi-Center Benchmark for Abdominal Disease Diagnosis and Report Generation from Non-Contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16991)
*Mariam Elbakry, Aliaa Sayed Sheha, Salma Hassan Tantawy, Aya Yassin, Concetto Spampinato, Karim Lekadir, Xiaomeng Li, Marawan Elbatel*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多中心基准，用于从非增强CT（NCCT）进行腹部多器官疾病诊断和自动化放射学报告生成。作者构建了配对NCCT-CECT数据集，包含两个中心的内部集和外部验证集，并评测了五种深度学习架构。实验表明NCCT保留了诊断信号，内部集平均多器官AUC达69.1%，外部集达63.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强CT（CECT）虽广泛用于腹部病变诊断，但存在造影剂肾病风险、采集负担重且增加放射科医生工作量。本文旨在探索能否从非增强CT（NCCT）中合成增强CT的诊断信息，实现更安全、资源高效的腹部影像诊断流程。

Method: 构建了一个多中心配对NCCT-CECT数据集（含对应放射学报告），设计了统一的评测协议，并基准测试了五种深度学习架构：胸部专用、腹部专用和通用多模态模型，用于从NCCT进行疾病诊断和报告生成。

Result: 内部集平均多器官AUC为69.1%，外部验证集为63.1%，表明NCCT中确实保留了可用的诊断信号。不同架构在疾病诊断和报告生成任务上表现各有优劣。

Conclusion: NCCT可以作为一种更安全的CECT替代方案用于腹部疾病诊断，该基准和公开数据集将推动无造影剂腹部影像工作流的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多中心腹部疾病诊断与报告生成的基准（benchmark），包含数据集、评测协议和多种方法的对比实验，属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时涉及医学影像报告生成（多模态生成），但核心贡献是基准构建，因此映射到 canonical 的 multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical imaging benchmark for CT, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous language models, video generation, and world models. Signals: medical imaging, non-contrast CT, abdominal disease diagnosis, report generation, benchmark

Abstract: Multiphasic contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) is widely used for abdominal lesion characterization, yet it carries inherent risks of contrast-induced nephropathy, escalates acquisition burden, and heavily contributes to radiologist workload. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel multi-center benchmark for multi-organ abdominal disease diagnosis and automated radiology report generation, which learns to synthesize contrast-enhanced findings from single-phase non-contrast CT (NCCT). To support this, we curated a large-scale dataset of paired NCCT-CECT studies and their corresponding contrast-enhanced radiology reports from two centers, partitioned into internal sets and an external validation cohort. Under a unified evaluation protocol, we benchmarked five contemporary deep learning architectures encompassing chest-specific, abdomen-specific, and general-purpose multimodal domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NCCT retains diagnostic signals, achieving an average multi-organ AUC of 69.1% on the internal cohort and 63.1% on the external cohort, respectively. By releasing this dataset and standardized benchmark publicly, this study aims to catalyze future research into safer, resource-efficient, and globally accessible contrast-free abdominal imaging workflows. Code is available at: .

</details>
