### [189] [Last But Not Least: Boundary Attention CalibratiON for Multimodal KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14782)
*Tianhao Chen, Yuheng Wu, Kelu Yao, Xiaogang Xu, Xiaobin Hu, Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BACON 是一种即插即用的多模态 KV 缓存压缩校准方法，通过结合观察窗口注意力与最后查询注意力，并利用层内一致性和层间持久性抑制噪声，在激进压缩下平均提升 7.5% 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM KV 缓存压缩方法依赖观察窗口注意力来估计 token 重要性，但这种聚合方式会稀释稀疏的视觉证据，在激进压缩下丢弃对回答至关重要的 token。作者发现最后查询注意力可作为补充信号恢复这些证据，但其包含的与回答无关的噪声会误导保留决策。

Method: 提出 BACON（Boundary Attention CalibratiON），一种即插即用的校准方法：1）用最后查询注意力校准观察窗口注意力，恢复被稀释的稀疏视觉证据；2）通过层内一致性（intra-layer coherence）和层间持久性（inter-layer persistence）抑制孤立噪声，实现更准确的 token 重要性估计。

Result: 在多种基准、模型、压缩预算和压缩方法上，BACON 在最激进压缩预算下平均提升多模态 KV 压缩性能 7.5%，最高提升达 30.9%。

Conclusion: BACON 有效解决了现有 MLLM KV 缓存压缩方法在激进压缩下丢失关键视觉证据的问题，是一种通用且高效的即插即用校准方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的 KV 缓存压缩，属于 MLLM 推理效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses critical KV cache compression for MLLMs with a novel calibration method, directly supporting efficient multimodal inference. Signals: KV cache compression, multimodal LLM efficiency, boundary attention calibration, plug-and-play method, strong benchmark gains

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong vision-language reasoning, but long visual contexts enlarge the KV cache and increase decoding latency. Existing compression methods rely on observation window attention for stable token-importance estimation, yet this aggregation can dilute sparse visual evidence and discard answer-critical tokens under aggressive compression. Therefore, we identify last-query attention as a complementary source for recovering such evidence, but its answer-irrelevant signals can mislead retention. We propose BACON, a plug-and-play method that calibrates observation window attention with last-query evidence and suppresses isolated noise via intra-layer coherence and inter-layer persistence. Across diverse benchmarks, models, budgets, and compression methods, BACON improves multimodal KV compression by 7.5% on average under the most aggressive budget, with gains up to 30.9%.

</details>


### [190] [Stepwise Token Selection for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16067)
*Landi He, Shawn Young, Lijian Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于指针式序列决策的视觉 token 选择方法，将 token 剪枝建模为逐步决策过程，通过可微松弛实现端到端训练，在 LLaVA-v1.5-7B 和 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上显著优于固定比例剪枝基线，在去除 88.9% 视觉 token 时仍保持 94.6% 的原始准确率并实现 1.88 倍预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的推理开销主要由视觉 token 前缀主导，现有方法通常为每个 token 分配独立重要性分数并保留固定数量的 top-ranked token，这隐含假设了 token 间的独立性以及统一的压缩比例，忽略了不同输入间最优压缩率的差异以及 token 间的依赖关系。

Method: 本文将视觉 token 剪枝重新建模为序列决策过程：引入指针式选择机制，迭代地选择信息量大的 token，每次选择都基于已选 token 进行条件决策；同时通过一个学习到的终止动作动态决定何时停止选择。为实现端到端训练，设计了一种基于方差保持噪声插值方案的可微松弛方法，使梯度能够通过离散选择过程传播。

Result: 在 LLaVA-v1.5-7B 和 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上的实验表明，该方法在不同压缩率下均一致优于固定比例基线。在去除 88.9% 视觉 token 的激进剪枝下，该方法保留了 94.6% 的原始准确率，同时实现了 1.88 倍的预填充延迟加速。

Conclusion: 将视觉 token 剪枝建模为序列决策过程能够有效捕捉 token 间依赖关系并自适应确定压缩率，相比固定比例方法在效率与性能之间取得更好的平衡，为 MLLM 的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 中视觉 token 的高效剪枝与选择，属于多模态模型效率优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MLLMs with a novel differentiable token selection method, aligning with user's high priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: efficient inference, token pruning, multimodal large language model, differentiable relaxation, LLaVA

Abstract: In multimodal large language models (MLLMs), inference cost is largely dominated by the visual token prefix rather than the language backbone, making token reduction a key factor for improving efficiency. Existing approaches typically assign independent importance scores to visual tokens and retain a fixed number of top-ranked tokens, implicitly assuming token independence and a uniform compression ratio across inputs. In this work, we reformulate visual token pruning as a sequential decision-making process. Specifically, we introduce a pointer-style selection mechanism that iteratively chooses informative tokens, conditioning each decision on previously selected ones, and dynamically determines when to stop via a learned termination action. This enables joint optimization of both the selected subset and its size. To enable end-to-end training under standard language modeling objectives, we design a differentiable relaxation based on a variance-preserving noise interpolation scheme, allowing gradients to propagate through the discrete selection process. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-v1.5-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms fixed-ratio baselines across different compression levels. Under aggressive pruning that removes 88.9% of visual tokens, our method preserves 94.6% of the original accuracy while achieving a 1.88x speed-up in prefill latency.

</details>


### [191] [Learned Image Compression for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16253)
*Hyeonjun Kim, Jegwang Ryu, Sangbeom Ha, Junhyeok Lee, Jun-Hyuk Kim, Hyemin Ahn, Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (74.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SPARC 是一个面向 VLA 机器人的学习型图像压缩框架，通过空间自适应比特率分配和倾斜率损失，在带宽受限场景下显著提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型依赖高频多摄像头观测，视觉通信成为带宽受限或分布式部署场景下实时机器人控制的主要瓶颈。现有图像/视频编解码器旨在保持通用视觉保真度，而非优化下游 VLA 策略的控制性能。

Method: 提出 SPARC 框架，包含：(1) 轻量级时序掩码选择器，根据任务相关性自适应分配不同摄像头视角和空间区域的比特率；(2) 利用时序上下文进行高效编码；(3) 倾斜率损失（tilted rate loss），缓解熵目标过度抑制罕见但任务关键视觉模式的倾向，稳定训练。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365、VLABench、LIBERO 等多个机器人基准上，SPARC 在相同比特率预算下始终优于传统图像/视频编解码器和近期学习型压缩方法。在远程控制真实场景中，显著改善了比特率-成功率权衡。

Conclusion: 面向 VLA 模型的任务感知图像压缩是可行的，通过空间自适应比特率分配和针对性的训练目标，可以在大幅降低带宽需求的同时保持甚至提升机器人控制性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向 VLA 模型的图像压缩框架 SPARC，通过自适应比特率分配降低视觉通信带宽需求，属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal)。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with efficient inference and multimodal understanding priorities, but is not a core continuous or generative model paper. Signals: Learned compression for VLA, spatial adaptive bitrate, tilted rate loss, real-world deployment, bandwidth-constrained robotics

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly rely on high-frequency multi-camera observations, making visual communication a major bottleneck for real-time robotic control in bandwidth-constrained or distributed deployment settings. Existing image and video codecs, however, are designed to preserve generic visual fidelity rather than the control performance of downstream VLA policies. In this work, we introduce SPARC (SPatially Adaptive Rate Control), a learned image compression framework tailored for VLA-driven robots. Our key observation is that the importance of visual information varies substantially across both camera views and spatial regions within an image. Based on this observation, SPARC employs a lightweight temporal mask selector that adaptively allocates bitrate over latent representations according to task relevance while leveraging temporal context. We further introduce a tilted rate loss that stabilizes training by reducing the tendency of entropy-based objectives to over-suppress rare yet task-critical visual patterns. Experiments on diverse robotic benchmarks, including RoboCasa365, VLABench, and LIBERO, show that SPARC consistently achieves stronger control performance than conventional image/video codecs and recent learned compression methods under the same bitrate budget. We additionally demonstrate real-world deployment benefits in remote-control settings, where our method substantially improves the bitrate-success tradeoff.

</details>

### [192] [ReportQA: QA-Based Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15037)
*Yiming Shi, Shaoshuai Yang, Xi Chen, Haolin Li, Hengyu Zhang, Che Jiang, Kaiwen Wang, Xun Zhu, Dong Xie, Fei Wang, Dejing Dou, Miao Li, Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (73.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReportQA，一个基于问答的放射学报告评估框架。通过构建知识树、利用 LLM 提取结构化信息并生成 QA 对，以 QA 准确率作为 QAScore 指标来评估报告质量。实验表明 QAScore 与放射科医生判断的一致性优于现有指标，并揭示了当前报告生成模型在细粒度临床表征学习上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告评估指标存在两大问题：NLG 指标缺乏临床相关性；临床效能（CE）指标仅关注有限实体且依赖人工标注，难以扩展。需要一种更灵活、临床相关且可自动化的报告评估方法。

Method: 1) 收集覆盖多种成像模态和解剖区域的报告数据集；2) 在放射科医生指导下构建临床实体和属性的知识树；3) 使用 LLM 从原始报告中提取结构化信息；4) 从预定义模板生成 QA 对，并通过自过滤和报告过滤进行质量控制；5) 评估时将报告作为上下文，LLM 作为裁判模型回答 QA 对，基于 QA 准确率计算 QAScore。

Result: QAScore 与放射科医生判断的一致性优于现有指标。实验发现当前基于报告生成的推理范式难以学习细粒度临床表征，且存在强烈的负先验偏差；而问题驱动的推理（question-driven inference）提供了更有效的替代方案。

Conclusion: ReportQA 提供了一种临床相关且灵活的放射学报告评估框架，支持细粒度的定量分析。释放的知识树、结构化报告和 QA 对以及管道代码有助于可复现性和可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出放射学报告生成的评测框架 ReportQA，属于生成模型评测范畴。虽然放射学报告生成可归入多模态理解与生成方向，但论文核心贡献是评测指标（QAScore）和数据集，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on radiology report evaluation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Medical domain, Radiology report evaluation, QA-based metric, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Radiology report evaluation is essential for advancing automated report generation. Natural language generation metrics have limited clinical relevance. Clinical efficacy (CE) metrics evaluate important medical findings, but focus mainly on presence and cover only a limited set of entities. Due to heavy reliance on manual annotations, it is difficult for CE metrics to extend clinical entities or attributes. In clinical practice, radiology reports serve as a medium for information transfer. Clinicians use them to perform downstream diagnostic tasks without directly inspecting images. Based on this insight, we propose ReportQA, a clinical-related and flexible radiology report evaluation framework, supporting detailed quantitative analysis of radiology report generation systems. We first collect datasets covering multiple imaging modalities and anatomical regions. We then construct knowledge trees of clinical entities and attributes with radiologist guidance, and use large language models (LLMs) to extract structured information from raw reports. Next, we generate QA pairs from predefined templates and apply quality control through self-filtering and report-based filtering. During evaluation, the report is treated as context, and an LLM acts as a judge model to answer the QA pairs. Based on the resulting QA accuracy, we introduce QAScore metric. Compared with existing metrics, QAScore shows better alignment with radiologist judgments. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art vision-language models reveal that current report-based inference paradigms struggle to learn fine-grained clinical representations and exhibit strong negative prior biases. In contrast, question-driven inference provides a more effective alternative. For reproducibility and extensibility, we release the knowledge trees, structured reports, and QA pairs, along with the pipeline code for QA construction and evaluation.

</details>


### [193] [Decoupling Semantics from Distortions: Multi-Scale Two-Stream Vision-Language Alignment for AI-Generated Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16799)
*Zijie Meng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MST-CLIPIQA，一种多尺度双流视觉语言对齐框架，通过解耦语义与失真表征来实现AI生成图像质量评估。该方法使用双CLIP编码器分别处理粗粒度全局语义和细粒度纹理/伪影信息，并引入信息瓶颈门控融合机制进行自适应跨尺度蒸馏，在五个基准上取得SOTA结果，仅需0.8M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的AIGIQA方法存在根本性的语义-失真维度冲突：为语义判别优化的单一表征天然地将组合理解与低层感知敏感性纠缠在一起，导致对细粒度质量退化不敏感。

Method: 提出MST-CLIPIQA框架，包含：(1) 双CLIP编码器架构，粗粒度流捕捉全局语义一致性，细粒度流保留纹理特征和伪影模式；(2) 信息瓶颈启发的门控融合机制，实现自适应跨尺度蒸馏；(3) 可选交叉注意力机制，在生成提示可用时进行提示锚定的对应评估。

Result: 在五个基准上取得SOTA结果，质量预测SRCC平均提升1.11%，文本-图像对应预测SRCC平均提升2.35%，仅使用0.8M可训练参数。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦语义与失真表征，多尺度双流对齐框架能有效提升AI生成图像质量评估性能，且参数量极小，验证了表征解耦对细粒度质量感知的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出AI生成图像质量评估(AIGIQA)方法，本质上是对生成图像质量的评测方法，属于生成模型评测范畴。虽然使用了VLM架构，但核心任务是质量评估而非通用多模态理解，因此映射到multimodal_understanding_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on AIGIQA, which is a niche evaluation task, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: AIGIQA, multi-scale two-stream, CLIP, semantic-distortion decoupling, SOTA on benchmarks

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM)-based AI-generated image quality assessment (AIGIQA) methods suffer from a fundamental semantic-distortion dimensional conflict: monolithic representations optimized for semantic discrimination inherently entangle compositional understanding with low-level perceptual sensitivity, rendering them blind to fine-grained quality degradations. We introduce MST-CLIPIQA, a multi-scale two-stream framework that achieves hierarchical vision-language alignment through explicit representational decoupling. Our architecture leverages dual CLIP encoders with complementary patch granularities: coarse-grained streams capture global semantic coherence while fine-grained streams preserve textural signatures and artifact patterns. An information bottleneck-inspired gated fusion mechanism performs adaptive cross-scale distillation, with optional cross-attention enabling prompt-anchored correspondence evaluation when generation prompts are available. Extensive experiments across five benchmarks establish new state-of-the-art results, achieving average improvements of 1.11 percent SRCC on quality and 2.35 percent SRCC on text-image correspondence prediction, while maintaining efficiency with only 0.8M trainable parameters. Our project is available at .

</details>

### [194] [Binary Tracking for Spatial QA and Navigation with Open Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16902)
*Dongbin Na, Chanwoo Kim, Soonbin Rho, Giyun Choi, Gangbok Lee, Dooyoung Hong*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BinTrack，一个完全开源的基于二进制搜索的空间定位智能体，用于服务机器人的空间问答。它利用机器人轨迹的时间顺序，在查询识别的两个锚点地标之间进行二分搜索，在 SpaceLocQA 基准的全局类别上匹配了 GPT-4o 的闭源模型结果，并实现了 1.5 倍以上的推理加速。同时发布了 GangnamLoop 多行程户外基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间问答方法依赖 GPT-4o 等闭源模型进行检索增强路径探索，但机器人在实际部署中常因网络不稳定、通信延迟和成本问题无法可靠依赖在线闭源模型。因此需要一种能在机器人本体上运行的、完全开源的空间问答方法。

Method: BinTrack 利用机器人轨迹的时间顺序，将查询中识别的两个锚点地标之间的轨迹段进行二进制搜索。通过逐步缩小搜索范围，定位查询所指位置的度量坐标。该方法完全基于开源视觉语言模型，无需闭源模型支持。

Result: 在 SpaceLocQA 基准上，BinTrack 相比其他开源实现准确率提升高达 22.8%，在最具挑战性的全局类别上匹配了 GPT-4o 闭源模型的结果。推理速度相比之前方法提升 1.5 倍以上。同时发布了 GangnamLoop 多行程户外基准数据集。

Conclusion: BinTrack 证明了完全开源的空间问答方法可以达到与闭源模型相当的性能，同时具有更低的部署成本和更快的推理速度，为服务机器人在实际环境中的自主导航提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究服务机器人在长程第一视角路径上的空间问答（Spatial QA），将自然语言查询映射到度量坐标，属于空间推理与具身推理范畴。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on spatial QA for robotics with binary search, not aligning with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: open-source spatial QA, binary search trajectory, robotics navigation, outdoor benchmark, no continuous token or generation focus

Abstract: This work addresses spatial question answering for service robots traversing long egocentric routes. Given a query such as "where can I find a dry cleaner on the way back home?", the system returns a metric coordinate that downstream navigation components can act on. Prior Spatial Question Answering approaches leverage retrieval-augmented agents built on closed-source models such as GPT-4o for path exploration. However, robots operating in the real world often cannot reliably depend on online closed-source models due to network instability, communication latency, and deployment cost. It creates a need for open-source based Spatial Question Answering approaches that can run onboard the robot, yet prior research in this direction remains limited. This work proposes BinTrack, a simple yet effective, fully open-source spatial-localization agent that leverages the temporal ordering of a robot's trajectory. BinTrack performs a binary search over the trajectory segments between two anchor landmarks identified from a query. It improves overall accuracy by up to 22.8% over other open-source implementations and even matches the reported closed-source model result on the global category of the SpaceLocQA benchmark, the most challenging setting that has so far required strong reasoning agents such as GPT-4o. Furthermore, its optimized inference strategy consistently yields more than a 1.5x inference speedup over previous approaches. Finally, this work releases GangnamLoop, a novel and practical multi-trip outdoor benchmark collected by deploying a real quadruped robot on public streets with the anonymization policy. It revisits the same locations under different outdoor conditions and pairs the robot's low viewpoint with the human owner's. The source codes and datasets are publicly available at

</details>


### [195] [PROSE: Training-Free Egocentric Scene Registration with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16569)
*Zhiang Chen, Nahyuk Lee, Boyang Sun, Taein Kwon, Marc Pollefeys, Zuria Bauer, Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PROSE 提出一种无需训练的以自我中心场景配准方法，利用预训练 VLM 将 RGB 序列提升为对象级 3D 场景图，通过对象高度先验和相同/不同查询进行跨序列对象匹配，再基于几何一致性求解刚性变换，在 Aria 基准上超越几何和学习的场景图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人/AR 系统需要持久空间记忆，但以自我中心的 RGB 输入（头戴相机）存在模糊、快速运动、部分重叠等问题，传统几何配准依赖干净点云，而学习型场景图方法需要预构建/标注图且对以自我中心数据脆弱。因此需要一种无需深度传感器、无需训练、鲁棒的 RGB 配准方法。

Method: PROSE 使用现成的基础模型（几何、分割、语言）将每个 RGB 序列提升为对象级 3D 场景图；然后利用同一 VLM 通过对象高度先验和成对相同/不同查询来匹配两个序列中的对象实例；最后为每个匹配对象假设候选变换，选择几何一致性最强的刚性变换。

Result: 在 Aria Digital Twin 和 Aria Everyday Activities 基准上，PROSE 在配准精度上优于几何基线和学习型场景图基线，无论是在真实点云还是 RGB 重建点云上均表现更好，且生成的场景图可直接用于下游任务。

Conclusion: PROSE 证明了预训练 VLM 可以有效地用于以自我中心的场景配准，无需训练或深度传感器，为持久空间记忆提供了一种可扩展的 RGB-only 解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文利用预训练视觉语言模型进行以自我为中心的室内场景配准（3D场景图匹配与刚性变换求解），属于空间推理与3D视觉定位范畴，与[空间与3D多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on egocentric scene registration with VLM, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Training-free VLM registration, Egocentric RGB-only, Object-level 3D scene graph, Cross-sequence matching, No depth sensor needed

Abstract: Registering two captures of the same indoor space taken at different times underpins persistent spatial memory for robots and AR systems, yet the realistic version of this task is egocentric and its most scalable form is RGB-only. Head-mounted cameras yield blurry, fast-moving, partially overlapping views from which dense geometry is hard to recover. Classical registration leans on exactly the clean point clouds this setting lacks, while learned scene-graph methods require a pre-built or annotated graph and a trained matcher that we find brittle under egocentric data. We take a different route, using a pretrained vision-language model as the source of both scene understanding and cross-scan matching. Our method, PROSE (Prompted Scene rEgistration), lifts each RGB sequence into an object-level 3D scene graph using off-the-shelf foundation models for geometry, segmentation, and language, then prompts the same VLM to match object instances across the two RGB sequences. To make this matching tractable and reliable, we leverage object heights as a prior and verify each proposed match with a paired same/different query, then solve for the rigid transform by hypothesizing a candidate per matched object and selecting the one with the strongest geometric consensus. PROSE adds no learned parameters and requires no depth sensor, training, or annotated graph. On the egocentric Aria Digital Twin and Aria Everyday Activities benchmarks, it outperforms both geometric and learned scene-graph baselines in registration accuracy, on ground-truth and RGB-reconstructed point clouds alike, and the scene graph it produces transfers directly to downstream tasks.

</details>


### [196] [GraphBEV++: Multi-Modal Feature Alignment for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16354)
*Ziying Song, Caiyan Jia, Lin Liu, Shaoqing Xu, Lei Yang, Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GraphBEV++ 提出了一种鲁棒的多模态融合框架，通过 LocalAlign-v2 和 GlobalAlign-v2 两个模块系统性地缓解 BEV 感知中 LiDAR 与相机之间的投影特征错位问题，在 nuScenes、Waymo、Argoverse2 等数据集上取得了 SOTA 性能，并有效推广到 3D 占用预测和端到端自动驾驶任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV感知中，LiDAR与相机传感器之间的标定不确定性会导致投影特征错位，这一问题在现有工作中被严重忽视。作者旨在系统性地解决这一关键挑战，提升多模态BEV感知的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 GraphBEV++ 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) LocalAlign-v2：通过图匹配引入邻域感知深度特征，校正局部错位，兼容 LSS 和 query-based 两种 BEV 范式；(2) GlobalAlign-v2：包含 Deformable 和 Diffusion 两种变体，分别处理 LSS 和 query-based BEV 中的全局错位问题——Deformable 变体显式学习跨模态特征偏移，Diffusion 变体通过加噪模拟错位再通过去噪恢复对齐特征。

Result: 在 nuScenes 和 Waymo 子集上达到 SOTA 性能；在 Argoverse2 上提升了远距离检测精度；有效泛化到 3D 占用预测任务；在端到端自动驾驶中，相比 UniAD、VAD、FusionAD、MomAD、WoTE 五个基线，在开环（nuScenes）和闭环（Bench2Drive、NAVSIM）评测中均表现更优。

Conclusion: GraphBEV++ 系统性地解决了 BEV 感知中的特征错位问题，提出的局部与全局对齐模块在不同 BEV 范式下均有效，且能泛化到 3D 占用预测和端到端规划任务，为自动驾驶多模态融合提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注自动驾驶中多模态（LiDAR+相机）BEV感知的特征对齐问题，属于空间3D场景理解与多模态融合推理，与[空间与3D多模态推理]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving BEV fusion, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: BEV perception, multi-modal fusion, autonomous driving, graph matching, diffusion denoising

Abstract: Feature misalignment in BEV perception is a critical yet often overlooked challenge in autonomous driving, especially under calibration uncertainties between LiDAR and camera sensors. To address this issue, we propose a robust multi-modal fusion framework, GraphBEV++, which systematically mitigates projection-induced misalignment. The framework consists of two key modules: LocalAlign-v2 and GlobalAlign-v2. LocalAlign-v2 introduces neighborhood-aware depth features via graph matching to correct local misalignment. It supports both LSS-based and query-based BEV representations, making it compatible with BEVFusion and BEVFormer architectures for consistent cross-paradigm alignment. GlobalAlign-v2 encompasses two variants: Deformable and Diffusion. The Deformable variant addresses global misalignment in LSS-based multi-modal BEV by explicitly learning cross-modal feature offsets. In contrast, the Diffusion variant targets implicit misalignment in query-based BEV by injecting noise to simulate misalignment and employing a denoising process to recover aligned features. Experimental results show that GraphBEV++ achieves state-of-the-art performance under misalignment noise on nuScenes and Waymo subset, improves long-range detection on Argoverse2, and generalizes effectively to the 3D occupancy prediction task, consistently improving occupancy estimation accuracy and robustness under both clean and noisy settings. Furthermore, GraphBEV++ effectively alleviates misalignment issues in end-to-end autonomous driving. Compared with five baselines (UniAD, VAD, FusionAD, MomAD, and WoTE), it demonstrates superior performance in both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop (Bench2Drive and NAVSIM) evaluations across perception, prediction, and planning tasks.

</details>

### [197] [X-Tokenizer: A Multimodal Action Tokenizer for Vision-Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14752)
*Xirui Kang, Yanpei Shi, Lucy Liang, Roy Gan, Dongxiu Liu, Pushi Zhang, Danpeng Chen, Xiaoyi Qin, Yinan Zheng, Jinliang Zheng, Hao Wang, Xianyuan Zhan, Hang Su*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: X-Tokenizer 提出了一种面向机器人 VLA 预训练的语义动作 tokenizer，通过语义残差量化（SRQ）和掩码动作建模（MAM）将连续动作编码为离散动作语言，并与视觉-语言表征对比对齐，作为 VLA 骨干网络的表征塑形监督信号，显著提升了多模态接地和长程任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型的动作 tokenizer 仅以重建为目标对动作进行离散化，产生的编码保留了运动几何信息，但无法为骨干网络提供有效的语义监督。动作 tokenizer 不应仅仅是压缩工具，而应成为多模态推理与可执行控制之间的语义接口。

Method: 提出 X-Tokenizer，采用轻量级 encoder-SRQ-decoder 架构。核心组件 SRQ（语义残差量化）对残差向量量化施加非对称结构：第一级通过掩码动作建模（MAM）训练，形成捕获粗粒度运动意图的离散动作语言；更深层级保持面向重建的残差，保留细粒度细节。此外，通过对比学习与预训练基础模型的表征空间对齐，并预测下一帧视觉-语言特征，实现动作 token 与多模态语义的对齐。

Result: 在 240 万条轨迹（20 亿动作帧）上预训练后，单个冻结的 X-Tokenizer 可插入混合离散-连续 VLA 模型。在真实世界任务中取得最佳综合表现，在 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真中表现强劲。在多模态接地任务上超越 FAST 方法 13.5%，在长程任务上提升 8.25%。

Conclusion: 动作 tokenizer 可以作为 VLA 预训练的语义接口，而不仅仅是动作压缩工具。X-Tokenizer 通过语义残差量化和多模态对齐，有效弥合了视觉-语言推理与机器人连续控制之间的鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 X-Tokenizer，将机器人连续动作通过语义残差量化（SRQ）转化为离散动作 token，并与视觉-语言表征进行对比对齐，服务于 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 预训练。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的统一多模态模型（理解+生成+控制一体化），同时涉及连续语言/多模态方向中的连续 tokenizer 思想。由于 unified_multimodal_model 是 [CANONICAL] 且涵盖 VLA 统一模型，故优先选择该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal models with a novel semantic action tokenizer that aligns continuous control with vision-language reasoning, matching top priority subtopics. Signals: Semantic Residual Quantization (SRQ) for action tokenization, Masked Action Modeling (MAM) for coarse motion intent, Contrastive alignment with pretrained multimodal representations, Strong gains in multimodal grounding (+13.5%) and long-horizon tasks (+8.25%), Pretrained on 2.4M trajectories for diverse robotic embodiments

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models must bridge pretrained vision-language reasoning and precise continuous robot control. Existing action tokenizers discretize actions primarily for reconstruction, producing codes that preserve motion geometry but provide only weak semantic supervision to the backbone. We therefore formulate action tokenization not as mere compression, but as semantic interface learning between multimodal reasoning and executable control. To this end, we introduce X-Tokenizer, a lightweight encoder-Semantic Residual Quantization (SRQ)-decoder architecture that provides a shared action interface across diverse robotic arm embodiments. Its key component, SRQ, imposes an asymmetric structure on residual vector quantization: the first level is trained with Masked Action Modeling (MAM) to form a discrete action language that captures coarse motion intent, while deeper levels remain reconstruction-oriented residuals that preserve fine-grained details. To further align action tokens with multimodal semantics, X-Tokenizer is pretrained with contrastive alignment to the representation space of a pretrained foundation model and with next-frame vision-language feature prediction. Pretrained on 2.4M trajectories (2.0B action frames), a single frozen X-Tokenizer plugs into a mixed discrete-continuous VLA as a representation-shaping supervision signal. X-Tokenizer achieves top real-world aggregate and strong RoboTwin 2.0 simulation results. Outperforming FAST in multimodal grounding (+13.5%) and long-horizon tasks (+8.25), it shows that action tokenizers serve as semantic interfaces for VLA pretraining beyond mere action compression.

</details>


### [198] [Efficient Reinforcement for Visual-Textual Thinking with Discrete Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14792)
*Yoonjeon Kim, Yuhta Takida, Chieh-Hsin Lai, Eunho Yang, Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态离散扩散模型作为自回归模型的替代方案，用于统一多模态模型的强化学习后训练。离散扩散模型通过局部视觉编辑而非完整图像重生成实现高效 rollout，在 GRPO 中减少 26.9% 的计算量。同时提出分解奖励分配策略，将文本和视觉段独立奖励，缓解跨模态干扰，相比联合奖励提升 11.2%，相比基础模型提升 38.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归（AR）的统一多模态模型在进行交织图文推理时，每次视觉推理都需要完整重生成图像，计算开销巨大。本文旨在探索离散扩散模型作为 AR 的替代方案，利用其局部编辑能力实现更高效的视觉 rollout，并解决多模态 RL 中联合奖励分配导致的跨模态干扰问题。

Method: 采用多模态离散扩散模型作为基础架构，在 GRPO 框架下进行强化学习后训练。关键创新包括：(1) 利用离散扩散模型的局部视觉编辑能力（而非完整图像重生成）进行高效 rollout，降低计算量；(2) 提出分解奖励分配（Factorized Reward Assignment），将文本和视觉 token 序列的奖励独立分配，避免跨模态干扰。

Result: 相比 AR 基线，离散扩散模型的 rollout 计算量减少 26.9%，性能仅有微小下降。分解奖励分配相比联合奖励分配提升 11.2%，相比基础模型提升 38.04%。

Conclusion: 多模态离散扩散模型是 AR 模型在统一多模态 RL 后训练中的有效替代方案，兼具高效性和良好性能。分解奖励分配策略有效缓解了多模态 RL 中的跨模态干扰问题，为统一多模态模型的后训练对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究统一多模态模型（理解与生成一体化）的强化学习后训练，属于 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的"统一多模态模型"子主题（该子主题在两个方向下均有出现）。论文使用离散扩散模型实现图文交织推理，核心是统一多模态模型的后训练对齐，因此映射到该子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances unified multimodal model post-training with efficient discrete diffusion, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: unified multimodal model, discrete diffusion model, efficient rollout, factorized reward assignment, GRPO

Abstract: RL-based post-training has been widely adopted to enable interleaved visual and textual reasoning in unified multimodal models capable of both text and image generation. However, most existing approaches are built upon autoregressive (AR) unified models, which require full image regeneration during visual reasoning. In this work, we demonstrate that multimodal discrete diffusion models are effective alternatives to AR models for reinforcement learning in interleaved reasoning, owing to their ability to perform efficient visual rollouts via localized visual editing rather than full image-token regeneration. This reduces rollout computation during GRPO by 26.9\% compared to AR baselines, with minimal performance drop. Despite the improved efficiency, we find that joint reward assignment, which employs a shared reward signal across modalities, introduces cross-modal interference between unrelated image and text token sequences during RL updates. To address this issue, we propose factorized reward assignment, a strategy that assigns rewards independently to text and vision segments. With factorized reward assignment, our RL approach achieves an 11.2% improvement over joint reward assignment and a 38.04% improvement over the base model.

</details>


### [199] [UniDDT: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with Decoupled Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16255)
*Shuai Wang, Liang Li, Yang Chen, Ruopeng Gao, Yao Teng, Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniDDT 提出了一种解耦扩散 Transformer 架构，通过 Noisy ViT 编码器统一视觉理解与生成的语义表征，并采用独立的扩散解码器将扩散解码与文本解码解耦，同时构建了理解与生成数据的对偶结构，实现了多模态理解与生成的有效统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型面临三个挑战：(1) 视觉理解与生成任务之间存在固有的学习冲突，导致两者性能均不理想；(2) 理解与生成使用不同的视觉空间，阻碍了可扩展性；(3) 过度依赖任务特定数据，忽视了图文理解与生成之间的对偶性。本文旨在解决这些问题，实现理解与生成的有效统一。

Method: UniDDT 采用 Noisy ViT 编码器与 LLM 结合，统一视觉生成和理解任务的语义编码；使用独立的扩散解码器将扩散解码与文本解码解耦。Noisy ViT 编码器利用潜空间作为统一视觉表征，平衡生成任务的可扩展性与理解任务的语义表达能力。同时，从相同的图文对构建对偶数据结构，增强生成与理解数据之间的相互依赖关系，利用其内在对偶性。

Result: 在视觉生成任务上，UniDDT 达到 0.87 GenEval 分数和 86.9 DPG 总体分数；在多模态理解任务上，MME 基准达到 1699.5 分，SEEDbench 总体分数为 76.5。

Conclusion: UniDDT 通过解耦扩散 Transformer 架构有效统一了多模态理解与生成，在语义一致性和可扩展性方面均有提升，实验证明了其有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniDDT，统一多模态理解与生成，属于"统一多模态模型"这一现有 subtopic（在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已有该条目），且该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 方向下也有同名条目，因此同时匹配两个方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal understanding and generation with a novel decoupled diffusion transformer, aligning with high-priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: unified multimodal model, decoupled diffusion transformer, Noisy ViT encoder, dual data structures, GenEval 0.87

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have emerged as a critical direction for general-purpose multimodal intelligence, integrating understanding and generation into a single framework. However, existing UMMs face prominent challenges: (1) the inherent learning conflicts between visual understanding and generation tasks, leading to suboptimal modeling in both tasks; (2) different understanding and generation visual spaces impeding scalability; (3) over-reliance on task-specific data that neglects the duality of text-image understanding and generation. To address these challenges, we propose UniDDT, which leverages a Noisy ViT encoder along with an LLM to unify semantic encoding for visual generation and understanding tasks, while employing a separate diffusion decoder to decouple diffusion decoding from text decoding. With this Noisy ViT encoder, UniDDT is able to leverage the latent space as a unified visual representation, enabling seamless compatibility between understanding and generation tasks. Thus, the scalability within the generation tasks and the semantic expressiveness within understanding tasks can be balanced. Also, we construct dual data structures from the same image-text pairs, fostering interdependence between the generation and understanding data to exploit their inherent duality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniDDT achieves effective unification of multimodal understanding and generation with enhanced semantic consistency and scalability. For visual generation tasks, our UniDDT achieves 0.87 GenEval score and 86.9 DPG overall score. For multimodal understanding tasks, our UniDDT achieves 1699.5 score on MME benchmark and 76.5 overall score on SEEDbench.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [200] [Unified Multimodal Model for Brain MRI Imputation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16484)
*Zhiyun Song, Che Liu, Tian Xia, Avinash Kori, Wenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniBrain 是一个统一的医学多模态大语言模型，通过联合训练实现脑部 MRI 模态补全（imputation）与图像理解，采用交错描述增强数据流、自对齐策略学习细粒度解剖特征，以及动态隐藏状态机制缓解长上下文推理中的暴露偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学 MLLM 面临高质量训练数据稀缺和临床中模态缺失的挑战，现有方法难以同时处理模态补全与多模态理解。

Method: 提出 UniBrain 模型：(1) 统一训练策略联合进行 MRI 模态补全与图像理解；(2) 构建交错描述增强数据流进行自回归训练；(3) 自对齐策略利用密集图像嵌入学习细粒度解剖特征；(4) 动态隐藏状态机制缓解长上下文推理中的暴露偏差。

Result: 在多疾病脑部 MRI 数据集上，UniBrain 在模态不完整情况下实现了高水平的脑部图像补全、理解和疾病诊断性能。

Conclusion: UniBrain 证明了统一多模态模型在医学影像中同时处理模态缺失和理解的可行性，为临床实际应用提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniBrain，一个用于脑部 MRI 分析的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），属于通用 MLLM 架构在医学领域的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical MRI imputation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword-listed medical domain. Signals: Medical MLLM, MRI imputation, self-alignment, dynamic hidden state, modality incompleteness

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) hold great potential for medicine, as they inherit knowledge from LLM and allow multiple data modalities to be integrated, analysed and interpreted in natural language. However, the field of medical MLLMs is constrained by non-trivial challenges, notably the scarcity of high-quality training data and the frequent occurrence of missing data in the real-world clinical setting. Here, we propose a novel unified multimodal model, UniBrain, for brain magnetic resonance image (MRI) analysis. To address potential missing brain MRI modalities, we employ a unified training strategy to perform joint imaging modality imputation and brain image understanding. During training, an interleaved and description-enriched data flow is constructed to train the model in an autoregressive manner, enabling medical reasoning with generated multimodal data. A self-alignment strategy is introduced to leverage dense image embeddings to learn fine-grained anatomical features without requiring detailed image captions. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic hidden state mechanism to alleviate the exposure bias during long-context multimodal inference. Extensive experiments on multi-disease brain MRI dataset demonstrate that UniBrain achieves high performance for brain image imputation, understanding, and disease diagnosis under various extents of modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Self-Attention: Sub-Quadratic Vision Transformers for Fast Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14753)
*Chiradeep Ghosh, Dakshina Ranjan Kisku*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种次二次复杂度的Vision Transformer架构，用基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的软聚类机制替代标准自注意力，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(nK)，并配合GPT解码器用于图像描述任务，在Flickr30K上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Vision Transformer中的自注意力机制具有二次计算复杂度，在处理高分辨率图像时计算开销巨大；同时现有方法缺乏丰富的局部特征表示。本文旨在通过降低计算复杂度来提升图像描述模型的效率。

Method: 将Vision Transformer中的标准自注意力替换为基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的概率Transformer方法。通过期望最大化（EM）算法将相似图像块聚类到K个簇中（K << n），用聚类中心间的交互替代所有patch对的成对注意力，从而将复杂度从O(n²)降至O(nK)。解码器采用自回归GPT架构生成描述文本。

Result: 在Flickr30K数据集上评估，模型在BLEU-1/2/3/4、METEOR、ROUGE-L和CIDEr等指标上均取得了有竞争力的结果，显著优于现有方法，同时大幅降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 基于GMM聚类的次二次复杂度Vision Transformer能够有效替代标准自注意力机制，在保持图像描述生成质量的同时显著提升计算效率，为高效视觉语言模型提供了新的设计思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于GMM聚类的高效Vision Transformer用于图像描述（image captioning），属于视觉语言模型（VLM）架构改进。虽然侧重效率，但核心任务是图像描述生成，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Proposes sub-quadratic attention for vision transformers, but uses a standard GPT decoder and only evaluates on Flickr30K, lacking novelty in the core captioning task. Signals: Sub-quadratic attention, GMM clustering, Flickr30K only, standard GPT decoder, no continuous or multimodal latent focus

Abstract: Image captioning is a challenging and significant task that aims to generate coherent and semantically meaningful textual descriptions for given images. To accomplish this task, it requires a deep understanding of visual content along with the ability to express that understanding in natural language. Despite remarkable progress with transformer-based architectures, existing approaches often suffer from limitations, such as a lack of rich local feature representations and the high computational cost of quadratic self-attention. The proposed model focuses on improving computational efficiency by restructuring the vision transformer architecture. In designing this approach, the standard self-attention mechanism in Vision Transformers is replaced with a probabilistic transformer approach based on a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), a soft-clustering technique. Instead of computing pairwise attention among all image patches, the model groups similar patches into a fixed number of clusters using an Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. This clustering-based mechanism reduces the computational complexity from quadratic O(n^2) to linear O(nK), where K << n. The autoregressive GPT-based decoder is used for caption generation. The model is evaluated on the Flickr 30K dataset, demonstrating competitive and significant improvement over existing works.

</details>


### [202] [JoyAI-VL-Interaction: Real-Time Vision-Language Interaction Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14777)
*Dingyu Yao, Junhao Zhou, Chenxu Yang, Chuanyu Qin, Haowen Hou, Zheming Liang, Congcong Wang, Yuhang Cao, Shenglong Ye, Shuai Xie, Shuhuan Gu, Haoyang Huang, Qingyi Si, Nan Duan, Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (87.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: JoyAI-VL-Interaction 是一个8B规模的视觉优先实时交互模型，能够持续观看视频流、自主决定何时说话或保持沉默、实时交互，并委托后台模型处理困难问题。论文同时发布了完整的可部署系统，在六个真实场景中人类评估显著优于Doubao和Gemini的视频通话助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型大多是"轮询式"设计——只在被问到时才回答，即使是视频通话应用也仍然是问答系统。然而现实世界中的许多时刻（如监控中的火灾、视频通话中的表情变化、直播中的商品展示）不会等待用户提问。作者主张一种不同的范式：模型应该像人一样"在场"，持续观察当前发生的事，自主决定是否发言，并实时交互。

Method: 1) 提出 JoyAI-VL-Interaction，一个8B规模的视觉优先VL交互模型，模型内部自主决定每秒钟是保持沉默、回应还是委托给后台模型。2) 采用视觉优先（vision-first）设计，持续流式处理视频输入。3) 提供可迁移的训练配方，使模型涌现出未显式训练的能力（如引导用户切换应用界面、即兴讲解幻灯片）。4) 发布完整的可部署系统，包含ASR/TTS模块、记忆模块、可视化UI和可连接任意API/Agent的后台大脑。

Result: 在六个真实场景中，人类评估者以显著优势偏好JoyAI-VL-Interaction而非Doubao和Gemini的视频通话助手。这是首个开源的视觉驱动交互模型，同时发布了训练配方、数据和完整可部署系统。

Conclusion: 论文展示了从"被动问答"到"主动在场"的交互范式转变的可行性，通过开源模型和系统为实时视觉语言交互领域提供了基础性贡献，并证明了视觉触发响应和时间感知能力的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个8B规模的视觉优先的VL交互模型，能够持续观看视频流、自主决定何时回应、实时交互，并支持委托后台模型处理复杂问题。这属于通用视觉语言模型与MLLM架构的范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"（视觉语言模型与MLLM）。

Importance reason: Novel real-time proactive vision-language interaction paradigm with open-source model and system, highly relevant to multimodal understanding and generation. Signals: real-time vision-language interaction, proactive response model, open-source 8B model, continuous video streaming, human evaluation beats Doubao/Gemini

Abstract: Many moments in the real world do not wait for a user to ask. A fire starts on a security monitor, an expression flickers across a video call, or a product a viewer wants flashes by in a livestream. Yet today's large models remain mostly turn-based by design: they answer only when addressed, and even video-call apps that appear interactive still operate as question-answer systems, reacting only when polled or prompted. We argue for a different paradigm: a model that is present in the world like a person. It continuously watches what is happening now, decides on its own whether to speak or stay silent, interacts in real time, and delegates to a background model when the problem is hard. To advance interaction models and their adoption across domains, we make two fully open-sourced contributions. First, we release JoyAI-VL-Interaction, an 8B-scale, vision-first VL-interaction model. The model makes the response decision internally, choosing each second to stay silent, respond, or delegate to a background model, and it excels at vision-triggered responsiveness and time awareness. We pair it with a transferable training recipe, from which capabilities we never trained for emerge, such as guiding a shopper through changing app screens or improvising a lecture from a slide deck. Second, we release a complete, deployable system built around that model. The system streams any ongoing video into the model, making it genuinely present in the world. All other components are pluggable, including ASR/TTS modules, memory, visualization UI, and a background brain that can connect to any API or agent. Across six real-world scenarios, human raters prefer JoyAI-VL-Interaction over the in-app video-call assistants of Doubao and Gemini by a wide margin. To our knowledge, this is the first open, vision-driven interaction model released together with its training recipe, data, and complete deployable system.

</details>


### [203] [HSQ-VLM: A Novel Spatially-Constrained Quadrant Segmentation VLM Model for Explainability in Diabetic Retinopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14803)
*Shivum Telang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (62.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HSQ-VLM 提出了一种基于象限分割的视觉语言模型，利用地标锚定的笛卡尔交叉注意力机制和四象限拓扑潜空间划分，在眼底图像上实现解剖学精确的病理定位与自然语言报告生成，对出血点和微动脉瘤的检测灵敏度分别达到 99.6% 和 96.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）诊断中，深度学习模型虽然分类准确率高，但缺乏可解释性，无法详细说明导致临床决策的具体解剖标志和病灶分布，阻碍了临床落地。

Method: 提出 HSQ-VLM 管线，包含：(1) Landmark-Anchored Cartesian Cross-Attention 机制，将视觉特征提取与结构化临床推理统一；(2) 4-Quadrant Topological Latent Partitioning (TLP)，以中心凹为中心的坐标系动态对齐视网膜特征，实现解剖学精确的病理量化；(3) VLM 生成自然语言报告。

Result: 在 3,500 张高分辨率眼底图像数据集上，出血点检测灵敏度达 99.6%，微动脉瘤检测灵敏度达 96.4%，边界模糊错误相比标准分割基线显著减少。

Conclusion: HSQ-VLM 通过空间约束的象限分割与地标锚定注意力机制，有效提升了 DR 诊断的可解释性和病灶定位精度，为医学影像 VLM 的可信应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HSQ-VLM，一个用于糖尿病视网膜病变可解释性分析的视觉语言模型，属于通用 VLM 在医学影像领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 合理相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical explainability for diabetic retinopathy, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: medical diagnosis, diabetic retinopathy, explainability, quadrant segmentation, vision-language model

Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is an aggressive retinal disease and a leading cause of global blindness, yet its clinical management is currently hindered by the black-box nature of diagnostic AI. While deep learning models achieve high classification accuracy, there is a critical lack of explainability methods capable of detailing the exact anatomical landmarks and lesion distributions that lead to a clinical decision for DR. Therefore, we propose HSQ-VLM, a novel quadrant segmentation pipeline on fundus images that utilizes a Landmark-Anchored Cartesian Cross-Attention mechanism to unify visual feature extraction with structured clinical reasoning. Unlike traditional methods that rely on arbitrary image partitioning, our pipeline implements 4-quadrant Topological Latent Partitioning (TLP) to dynamically align retinal features with a fovea-centered coordinate system. This allows the Vision-Language Model to generate natural language reports that quantify pathology with anatomical precision. On a dataset of 3,500 high-resolution fundus images, this innovative methodology achieved a lesion detection sensitivity of 99.6% for hemorrhages and 96.4% for microaneurysms, while demonstrating a significant reduction in boundary-ambiguity errors compared to standard segmentation baselines.

</details>


### [204] [Understanding Cross-Modal Contributions in Continual Vision-Language Models: A Theoretical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14883)
*Salimeh Sekeh, Mary Wisell*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度分析了持续视觉语言模型中跨模态（视觉-语言）贡献的机制，揭示了顺序微调过程中不同模态对连续环境的贡献模式，并在大规模VLM上验证了理论发现的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法虽然研究了VLM中的灾难性遗忘问题，但对跨模态（视觉与语言）在连续环境序列中的具体贡献缺乏理论理解。

Method: 提出一个新的理论框架来分析跨模态贡献，通过数学建模刻画视觉和语言模态在连续任务环境中的贡献动态，并在大规模VLM上进行实证验证。

Result: 理论分析揭示了跨模态贡献对任务顺序和任务间相似性的鲁棒性，并展示了改进的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该理论视角为理解持续VLM中模态特定贡献提供了新见解，有助于设计更稳定的持续多模态学习算法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续学习（continual learning）下的视觉语言模型（VLM），关注跨模态贡献的理论理解，属于通用VLM/MLLM研究方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Theoretical analysis of cross-modal contributions in continual VLMs, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: continual learning, vision-language models, theoretical analysis, cross-modal contributions, catastrophic forgetting

Abstract: Continual vision-language models are commonly addressed through sequential fine-tuning; however, although this paradigm enables adaptation to new environments (tasks), it inherently emphasizes the contribution of previously learned environments (tasks) at the expense of the stability required to preserve previously acquired knowledge. While existing approaches have adequately studied continual learning and catastrophic forgetting in vision-language models (VLMs), the theoretical understanding of modality-specific contributions across a sequence of environments remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a new theoretical perspective to understand the cross-modal (vision-language) contributions to consecutive environments. We empirically evaluate our theoretical findings on large VLMs and demonstrate their effectiveness in capturing environment-level cross-modal contributions. Our analysis provides deeper insights into continual VLMs, highlighting their contribution robustness to varying task orders and inter-task similarities, and their improved generalization performance.

</details>


### [205] [Post-Launch Capability Expansion of Vision-Language Models via Prompting for On-Orbit Spacecraft Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15427)
*Nicholas A. Welsh, Lennon J. Shikhman, Monty Nehru Attazs, Seemanthini K. Putane, Van Minh Nguyen, Ryan T. White*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (65.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究在轨航天器检测场景下，利用提示驱动的视觉语言模型（SAM3）实现发射后语义能力扩展。在129张未见卫星图像上，零样本实例分割达到0.385 mAP@0.5，大结构（航天器主体、太阳翼）定位可靠，小部件（天线、推进器）效果有限。结构化提示相比短类别名提示可提升82%性能，模型可在嵌入式GPU上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天在轨检测系统通常在发射前部署感知模型，发射后更新模型权重或扩展固定标签集在操作上不可行。传统监督模型需要重新训练和上传参数，而航天器在轨期间可能遇到新的部件类型或异常情况，亟需一种无需修改权重的语义扩展机制。

Method: 采用SAM3（Segment Anything Model 3）作为基础视觉语言模型，在严格冻结权重、单次前向推理协议下，通过自然语言提示（prompt）指定新的航天器部件类别进行零样本实例分割。实验对比了短类别名提示与包含空间和几何描述的结构化提示对分割性能的影响。

Result: 在129张未见卫星图像上，SAM3在固定全局阈值且无后处理条件下达到0.385 mAP@0.5和0.267 mAP@0.5:0.95。大结构如航天器主体（0.639 AP@0.5）和太阳翼（0.598 AP@0.5）定位可靠，小部件如天线（0.221 AP@0.5）和推进器（0.081 AP@0.5）困难。结构化提示相比短类别名提示提升高达82%。模型可在当代嵌入式GPU上运行。

Conclusion: 提示驱动的视觉语言模型可以为在轨航天器检测提供实用的发射后语义扩展机制，尤其适用于主体结构等大尺度部件。但对于精细小部件和轨道域偏移下的零样本定位仍存在明显局限，需要进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用提示驱动的视觉语言模型（SAM3）在航天器在轨检测中实现零样本语义扩展，属于视觉语言模型在特定领域（航天）的应用。虽然应用场景特殊，但核心方法是VLM的零样本分割与提示工程，与[CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Niche application (spacecraft inspection) with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: zero-shot segmentation, domain-specific, no continuous language model, no video generation, no world model

Abstract: Spaceborne inspection systems often deploy perception models prior to launch, after which updating model weights or expanding fixed label sets becomes operationally impractical. While supervised models can be integrated pre-flight, adding new semantic capabilities in orbit requires retraining and re-uploading parameters. We investigate whether prompt-driven vision--language models can enable post-launch semantic expansion, allowing new spacecraft components to be specified via natural-language prompts without modifying onboard weights. We evaluate zero-shot instance segmentation of spacecraft components under a strictly frozen, single-pass inference protocol on a test set of $129$ images of previously unseen satellites. Under fixed global thresholds and no post-processing, SAM3 achieves $0.385$ mAP@$0.5$ and $0.267$ mAP@$0.5{:}0.95$. Performance is strongly scale-dependent: large structural elements like spacecraft bodies ($0.639$ AP@$0.50$) and solar arrays ($0.598$ AP@$0.5$) localize reliably, while relatively small appendages like antennas ($0.221$ AP@$0.5$) and thrusters ($0.081$ AP@$0.5$) remain difficult. Prompt formulation influences performance, with structured prompts incorporating spatial and geometric descriptors yielding up to $82%$ improvement over short category-name prompts. The model operates within the memory and compute envelope of contemporary embedded GPUs, suggesting prompt-driven grounding can provide a practical mechanism for post-launch semantic extension of dominant spacecraft structures while highlighting limitations of zero-shot localization for fine-scale components under orbital domain shift.

</details>


### [206] [VisualClaw: A Real-Time, Personalized Agent for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16295)
*Haoqin Tu, Jianwen Chen, Zijun Wang, Siwei Han, Juncheng Wu, Hardy Chen, Haonian Ji, Kaiwen Xiong, Jiaqi Liu, Peng Xia, Jieru Mei, Hongliang Fei, Jason Eshraghian, Zeyu Zheng, Yuyin Zhou, Huaxiu Yao, Cihang Xie*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (88.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisualClaw 是一个自进化的多模态智能体，通过混合编码（级联门控过滤视频帧 + 热/冷 top-k 注入压缩技能库）降低部署成本，并通过从失败中学习实现技能演化。在 4 个视频 QA 基准上，API 成本降低 98%，准确率平均提升 3.85%。同时构建了 VisualClawArena 基准（200 场景）用于评估工具使用工作区中的多模态智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLM 部署面临三个关键差距：处理密集视频帧和长提示时延迟和成本高；部署后智能体框架静态不变；标准视频 QA 基准无法测试智能体在工具使用工作区中利用视觉证据的能力。因此需要一种低成本、可自演化的多模态智能体系统。

Method: VisualClaw 基于两个核心原则：1) 混合编码——通过级联门控过滤信息量少的流式帧，并通过热/冷 top-k 注入压缩文本技能库；2) 技能演化——从失败中学习，检索到的记忆条件化一个演化器，作为直接拼接上下文或引导证据，产生技能库更新以帮助未来问题。同时构建了 VisualClawArena 基准，包含 200 个场景，要求模型在工作区内使用视频证据、文档、动态更新和可执行检查。

Result: 在 4 个视频 QA 基准上，VisualClaw 将每问题 API 成本平均降低 98%（对比全帧上传），比离线均匀 8 帧基线降低 25.9%，同时在多数设置中提升准确率（平均 +3.85%，在 EgoSchema 上最高 +15.80%）。在 VisualClawArena 上，使用计算机使用智能体后端时，宏观准确率提升 +2.9%（Codex）和 +3.2%（Claude Code），成本降低 9.5%。级联门控将 1 小时流媒体会话从约 3600 次 API 上传减少到仅 5-20 次调用。

Conclusion: VisualClaw 通过混合编码和技能演化有效解决了 VLM 部署中的成本、静态性和评测三大问题，在显著降低成本的同时提升性能，特别适合边缘设备应用场景，可作为个性化助手。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisualClaw，一个基于 VLM 的自进化多模态智能体系统，核心贡献在于 VLM 部署中的混合编码与技能演化机制，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 应用方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on efficient multimodal agents and continuous visual token processing, but focuses on agentic cost reduction rather than core continuous representation or generation. Signals: Hybrid encoding reduces API cost 98%, Self-evolving skill bank from failures, VisualClawArena benchmark for tool-using agents, Directly addresses user's efficient inference and MLLM subtopics, No direct contribution to continuous language/video generation models

Abstract: Vision language models are serving as general-purpose interfaces for complex multimodal tasks. However, deployment still faces three gaps: VLMs typically incur high latency and cost when processing dense video frames and long prompts, the agent scaffold remains static after deployment, and standard video-QA benchmarks do not test whether agents can use visual evidence inside tool-using workspaces. We present VisualClaw, a self-evolving multimodal agent built around two principles. First, hybrid encoding reduces deployment cost by filtering less informative streaming frames with a cascaded gate and compressing the text skill bank through hot/cold top-k injection. Second, skill evolution lets the agent learn from failures: retrieved memories condition an evolver as direct concatenated context or as guided evidence, producing skill-bank updates that help future questions. Across 4 video-QA benchmarks with 2 VLMs, VisualClaw cuts per-question API cost by an average -98% versus full-frame upload and by -25.9% over the offline uniform 8 frame baseline, while boosting accuracy in most settings, e.g., an average +3.85% and a peak +15.80% on EgoSchema with Gemini 3 Flash. To address the gap, we curate VisualClawArena, a 200-scenario multimodal agentic benchmark built through a strict five-stage pipeline; models must use video evidence, documents, dynamic updates, and executable checks inside a workspace. On VisualClawArena, the same framework with computer-use agent backends improves macro accuracy by +2.9% for Codex (GPT-5.5) and +3.2% for Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) over no-evolution baselines, with a -9.5% cost reduction compared to the uniform-sampled baseline. These properties make VisualClaw a natural fit for edge applications, where the cascade reduces a 1-hour streaming session from ~3,600 API uploads down to only 5-20 calls and the self-evolution makes it a perfect personalized assistant.

</details>


### [207] [ActiveSAM: Image-Conditional Class Pruning for Fast and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16996)
*Tran Dinh Tien, Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ActiveSAM 是一个无需训练、零样本的推理框架，通过图像条件类别剪枝策略，将 SAM 3 高效转化为开放词汇语义分割器，在 8 个基准上平均提升 1.4 mIoU 并实现最高 5.5 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 3 作为概念提示分割的强骨干网络，直接应用于开放词汇语义分割时效率极低——需要对整个数据集词汇表进行全分辨率解码，而每张图像实际只包含少量活跃类别。现有方法缺乏对图像条件类别激活的利用，导致大量冗余计算。

Method: ActiveSAM 包含三个核心步骤：(1) 类别提示规范化与扩展；(2) 通过低分辨率存在性预览估计图像条件活跃类别集；(3) 仅对保留类别使用桶式提示复用（bucketed prompt multiplexing）进行全分辨率解码，并采用边缘感知背景校准（margin-aware background calibration）抑制低置信度像素。整个过程无需训练、无需权重更新、无需 oracle 类别标签。

Result: 在 8 个开放词汇语义分割基准上，ActiveSAM 平均超越当前最优方法 SegEarth-OV3 约 1.4 mIoU，在大词汇量数据集上运行速度提升高达 5.5 倍。同时，在图像损坏模拟的真实分布偏移场景下展现出最强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ActiveSAM 通过图像条件类别剪枝策略，在不牺牲精度的情况下大幅提升 SAM 3 在开放词汇语义分割中的推理效率，为自动驾驶、具身 AI 等噪声输入场景提供了实用的部署方案。

Classification reason: 该论文基于 SAM 3 构建开放词汇语义分割框架，属于视觉语言模型的应用扩展。虽然聚焦分割任务，但核心是利用预训练视觉基础模型（SAM 3）进行零样本开放词汇分割，与 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）方向高度相关，且该 canonical 条目可覆盖此类视觉基础模型的应用工作。

Importance reason: Focuses on SAM 3 efficiency for OVSS, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Training-free zero-shot, SAM 3 acceleration, Open-vocabulary segmentation, Image-conditioned pruning, Robustness under corruption

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) provides a strong frozen backbone for concept-prompted segmentation, but applying it directly to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) is inefficient: full-resolution decoding is typically run over the entire dataset vocabulary, whereas each image contains only a small active subset of classes. We introduce ActiveSAM, a training-free, zero-shot inference framework that turns SAM 3 into an active-vocabulary segmenter. ActiveSAM first canonicalizes and expands class prompts, then estimates an image-conditioned active set from a low-resolution presence preview. Only the retained classes are decoded at full resolution, using bucketed prompt multiplexing with the frozen SAM 3 decoder. The preview stage uses only class-presence evidence and skips unnecessary segmentation-head computation, while the final stage applies margin-aware background calibration to suppress low-confidence pixels. ActiveSAM requires no target-dataset training, no weight updates, and no oracle class-presence labels. Across eight OVSS benchmarks, ActiveSAM improves the speed-accuracy tradeoff of training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, outperforming the current state-of-the-art SegEarth-OV3 by approximately +1.4 mIoU on average while running up to 5.5x faster on large-vocabulary datasets. ActiveSAM also demonstrates the strongest robustness under image corruption that simulates real-world distribution shift, making it well-suited for deployment in noisy-input domains such as autonomous driving and embodied AI. Code is available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

### [208] [Learning Directional Semantic Transitions for Longitudinal Chest X-ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15938)
*Zhangfeng Hu, Zefan Yang, Ge Wang, Tanveer Syeda-Mahmood, Anushree Burade, Mannudeep Kalra, Pingkun Yan*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出ProTrans框架，将胸部X光片的疾病进展建模为方向性语义转换，通过视觉-语言预训练学习配对影像间的语义变化，在纵向CXR分析任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X光片纵向分析方法主要依赖时序特征融合或差异建模，但难以捕捉细微的进展语义，且忽略了疾病轨迹固有的方向性（如好转vs恶化）。需要一种能显式建模方向性语义转换的预训练框架。

Method: ProTrans框架包含：(1) 利用放射学报告将每张CXR图像锚定到可解释的疾病状态；(2) 引入可学习的进展特征图（progression feature map）显式编码状态间的语义偏移，并与报告导出的进展描述对齐；(3) 反向时序建模过程，通过双向重建一致性约束（状态间和转换间）来解耦方向性语义，促进连贯的轨迹建模。

Result: 在疾病进展分类和进展描述生成两个纵向下游任务上，ProTrans一致优于现有方法，验证了其作为统一预训练框架的有效性。

Conclusion: ProTrans通过将疾病进展建模为方向性语义转换，有效提升了纵向CXR分析性能，为医学影像的时序理解提供了新的预训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注胸部X光片的纵向对比分析，涉及疾病进展分类和进展描述生成，属于医学视觉问答与文档理解范畴。虽然具体领域是医学影像，但核心任务是视觉理解+文本生成（进展描述），与[多模态评测与基准]或[视觉语言模型与MLLM]相比，[视觉问答与文档理解]更贴近其"基于影像进行问答/描述"的本质。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical imaging longitudinal analysis, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: Medical imaging, longitudinal analysis, vision-language pretraining, disease progression, chest X-ray

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation often requires longitudinal comparison to assess disease progression. Existing approaches typically rely on temporal feature fusion or inter-study discrepancy modeling, yet remain limited in capturing subtle progression semantics and overlook the inherently directional nature of disease trajectories. In this paper, we propose ProTrans, a novel vision-language pretraining framework that formulates disease progression as a directional semantic transition between paired CXR studies. ProTrans leverages radiology reports to anchor individual CXR representations within interpretable disease states, and introduces a learnable progression feature map to explicitly encode semantic shifts between states, aligned with report-derived progression descriptions. To enforce direction-aware perception, ProTrans incorporates a reversed temporal modeling process and imposes bidirectional reconstruction consistency across states and transitions, thereby disentangling directional semantics and promoting coherent trajectory modeling. Extensive experiments on longitudinal downstream tasks, including disease progression classification and progression captioning, demonstrate that ProTrans consistently outperforms existing methods, establishing a unified pretraining framework for longitudinal CXR understanding.

</details>


### [209] [Sub-Semantic Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14754)
*Aviad Cohen Zada, Nadav Orenstein, Shai Avidan, Gal Oren*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出子语义图像分割（Sub-Semantic Image Segmentation）这一新任务，介于纹理分割与语义分割之间，利用VLM+SAM将图像分割为语言可描述的稳定外观模式。作者识别了三种失败模式（语言泄漏、提示竞争、语义扭曲），并提出DETECTURE方法解决这些问题，同时构建了TextureADE数据集，实验表明DETECTURE在多个指标上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分割要么基于视觉线索（纹理分割），要么基于物体语义（语义分割），缺乏一种介于两者之间的分割方式——即根据语言可描述的稳定外观模式来分割图像，而非命名完整物体。作者希望利用VLM的语言理解能力与SAM的分割能力来实现这一目标。

Method: 提出DETECTURE方法，耦合VLM与SAM 3的分割骨干网络。具体解决三个失败模式：1）语言泄漏（texture region间的语言信息泄露）通过设计区域级语言约束缓解；2）提示竞争（分割骨干内部的prompt竞争）通过优化prompt分配机制解决；3）语义扭曲（语言到mask接口处的语义失真）通过改进语言到mask的映射接口处理。同时基于ADE20K构建了TextureADE数据集用于训练和评测。

Result: 在多个数据集上使用不同指标进行评测，DETECTURE相比多种基线方法取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: 子语义图像分割是一个有价值的新任务方向，DETECTURE有效解决了VLM+SAM耦合中的关键失败模式，为细粒度语言引导的图像分割提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出子语义图像分割（sub-semantic segmentation），利用VLM+SAM将图像分割为语言可描述的稳定外观模式，并构建了基于ADE20K的TextureADE数据集。这与视觉问答与文档理解中的细粒度视觉理解与分割任务相关，且涉及VLM驱动的图像解析，属于多模态理解范畴。

Importance reason: Novel task but tangential to user's focus on continuous/multimodal models and generation. Signals: New sub-semantic segmentation task, VLM+SAM coupling, failure mode analysis, TextureADE dataset, no direct link to generation or world models

Abstract: Images can be segmented based on visual cues (i.e., texture segmentation) or into objects (i.e., semantic segmentation). We propose a new category of sub-semantic image segmentation that blurs the line between the two. In sub-semantic image segmentation, language is not used to name whole objects. Instead, it is used to partition an image into stable appearance patterns that can be described by language. To do that, we couple a general-purpose vision-language model to SAM 3, a promptable segmentation backbone whose native text pathway can ground rich descriptions into masks. Simple coupling fails for a number of reasons that we identify in the paper, and we overcome them by introducing DETECTURE that resolves three concrete failure modes -- language leakage between texture regions, prompt competition inside the segmentation backbone, and semantic distortion at the language-to-mask interface. Since there is no dataset of sub-semantic image segmentation, we introduce one, termed TextureADE. The new dataset is derived from the ADE20K dataset using a system we designed. We compare DETECTURE to a number of baselines and find that it achieves the strongest performance on several datasets using different metrics. Code is available at .

</details>


### [210] [XMedFusion: A Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Perception and Reasoning Framework for Autonomous Medical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14766)
*Hamza Riaz, Arham Haroon, Maha Baig, Muhammad Dawood Rizwan, Muhammad Naseer Bajwa, Muhammad Moazam Fraz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MA, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (58.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: XMedFusion 是一个面向自主医疗系统的模块化多智能体框架，通过视觉感知、知识图谱构建、检索引导草稿和合成验证四个模块，将放射学报告生成分解为专家级分析流程，在胸部X光片数据集上显著优于基线VLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端多模态模型在放射学报告生成中视觉定位能力弱，导致不可靠的解释和细微临床发现的遗漏，亟需一种更结构化、可解释的推理框架来提升医学影像诊断的鲁棒性和透明度。

Method: XMedFusion 采用模块化多智能体架构：(1) 视觉感知智能体——提取图像级证据；(2) 知识图谱构建智能体——结构化组织临床相关发现；(3) 检索引导草稿过程——确保报告结构一致性；(4) 合成智能体——通过推理驱动的验证迭代整合视觉和结构化证据，生成可靠且可解释的诊断输出。

Result: 在公共胸部X光片数据集上，相比基线VLM，BLEU-1提升0.0493~0.3359，ROUGE-L提升0.0863~0.2440，METEOR提升0.0829~0.1708，语义指标Consistency提升2.38→7.80，Accuracy提升2.34→6.93。

Conclusion: 结构化多智能体感知与推理框架能有效增强医学影像报告的鲁棒性、透明度和自动化水平，可集成到自主医疗和机器人诊断工作流中。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于胸部X光片报告生成（医学影像报告生成），属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/文档理解）范畴下的医学影像理解与报告生成任务。虽然具体应用是医学领域，但核心问题是视觉理解+文本生成，与现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"、"视觉语言模型与MLLM"等相比，"视觉问答与文档理解"最贴切，因为放射学报告生成本质上是VQA/图像描述任务的医学特化形式。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: medical domain, negative keyword "clinical", no continuous language model or video generation, no autoregressive or diffusion generation, no world model

Abstract: Autonomous medical and robotic systems increasingly rely on intelligent perception and reasoning capabilities to interpret visual data and support clinical decision making. Radiology report generation represents a critical component of such automated diagnostic workflows, yet existing end-to-end multimodal models often suffer from weak visual grounding, resulting in unreliable interpretations and omission of subtle clinical findings. This paper presents XMedFusion, a modular AI framework designed as an intelligent perception and reasoning module for autonomous medical systems. The proposed framework decomposes visual information into coordinated functional components that emulate expert-driven analysis, including a visual perception agent that extracts image-grounded evidence, a knowledge graph construction agent that structures clinically relevant findings, and a retrieval-guided drafting process that ensures a consistent reporting structure. A synthesis agent iteratively integrates visual and structured evidence through reasoning-driven verification to produce reliable and interpretable diagnostic outputs. Experimental evaluation on a public chest radiograph dataset demonstrates significant improvements over baseline vision-language models, achieving gains from 0.0493 to 0.3359 in BLEU-1, 0.0863 to 0.2440 in ROUGE-L, and 0.0829 to 0.1708 in METEOR, along with substantial improvements in semantic evaluation metrics such as Consistency (2.38 to 7.80) and Accuracy (2.34 to 6.93). The results highlight the effectiveness of structured multi-agent perception and reasoning for enhancing robustness, transparency, and automation in intelligent medical imaging systems, enabling integration into autonomous healthcare and robotic diagnostic workflows.

</details>


### [211] [Decoupled Object-Centric Video Understanding for Generating Robotic Manipulation Commands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16470)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh, Thanh-Tuan Tran, Haolan Zhang, Ziyan Gao, Xiem HoangVan, Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦的物体中心视频理解框架，将动作识别与物体识别分离，结合TSM时序模块和物体选择算法，利用VLM生成精确的机器人操作指令。在修改后的Something-Something V2数据集上，动作分类准确率达86.79%，BLEU-4在新物体上达0.261，显著优于任务特定基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视频演示生成机器人命令的方法难以准确识别哪些物体与演示动作功能相关，导致生成的命令语言上合理但操作上模糊。需要一种能精确识别任务相关物体并生成无歧义操作指令的方法。

Method: 提出解耦的物体中心视频理解框架：(1) 使用Temporal Shift Modules (TSM)进行高效时空动作分类；(2) 提出Object Selection算法，通过轨迹角色分类、模糊检测和重叠最小化识别任务相关物体；(3) 利用VLM对选定物体进行类别识别和零样本泛化；(4) 生成无语法约束的操作命令。

Result: 在修改的Something-Something V2数据集上，动作分类准确率86.79%；BLEU-4在标准物体上0.337、新物体上0.261，分别比最强任务特定基线提升80.2%和143.9%；METEOR和CIDEr在新物体上提升达157.9%和171.7%。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦物体中心框架能有效从视频中识别动作和任务相关物体，生成精确的机器人操作指令。模块化设计使其在标准和新物体上均显著优于任务特定方法，且与大型通用VLM相比具有竞争力。

Classification reason: 论文提出面向机器人操作指令生成的物体中心视频理解框架，核心任务是从视频中识别动作和物体并生成命令，属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/指令生成）范畴。虽然涉及机器人领域，但方法核心是视频理解+VLM生成，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic manipulation command generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: Robotic manipulation, object-centric video understanding, action recognition, VLM for command generation, Something-Something V2 dataset

Abstract: Translating video demonstrations into executable robot commands remains challenging because existing methods often fail to identify which objects are functionally involved in the demonstrated action. As a result, they may generate commands that are linguistically plausible but operationally ambiguous. We propose an object-centric video understanding framework that decouples action recognition from object identification to generate precise, grammar-free manipulation commands. Our approach integrates Temporal Shift Modules (TSM) for efficient spatio-temporal action classification with a novel \textbf{Object Selection} algorithm that identifies task-relevant objects through trajectory-based role classification, blur detection, and overlap minimization. The selected objects are then processed by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for robust category recognition and zero-shot generalization. Evaluated on a modified Something-Something V2 dataset, our method achieves 86.79\% action classification accuracy and BLEU-4 scores of 0.337 on standard objects and 0.261 on novel objects. These results improve over the strongest task-specific baseline by 80.2\% and 143.9\%, respectively. Larger gains are observed in METEOR and CIDEr, reaching 157.9\% and 171.7\% on novel objects. Across all semantic metrics, our approach consistently outperforms task-specific methods and remains competitive with, or surpasses, large general-purpose VLMs while retaining a modular, object-centric design.

</details>


### [212] [Object Tokens as a Bridge Between Segmentation and Visual Question Answering in Robotic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15861)
*Yiping Li, Ronald de Jong, Romy van Jaarsveld, Franco Badaloni, Gino Kuiper, Jelle Ruurda, Josien Pluim, Marcel Breeuwer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将 VLM 与 SAM 解码器结合，通过 VLM 生成的对象 token 同时指导分割掩码预测和视觉问答，在手术 VQA 任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术 VQA 方法依赖粗粒度的边界框视觉定位，无法捕捉手术对象的精细空间结构，限制了视觉推理能力。

Method: 将 VLM 与基于 SAM 的解码器集成，VLM 生成场景元素的对象 token（object tokens），这些 token 同时用于答案预测和投影到 SAM 解码器生成分割掩码，通过分割和 VQA 双重目标联合优化对象 token 嵌入。

Result: 在私有 RAMIE 数据集和公开 EndoVis18 数据集上，该方法在手术 VQA 任务上一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将上下文感知的对象 token 融入视觉语言模型能够提升细粒度手术场景理解能力，分割与 VQA 的联合学习有助于获得空间上有依据的视觉表征。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于手术场景中的视觉问答（Surgical VQA），属于视觉问答（VQA）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on surgical VQA with segmentation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include medical diagnosis. Signals: surgical VQA, object tokens, SAM decoder, fine-grained segmentation, private dataset

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) in robotic surgery, referred to as surgical VQA, requires high-level understanding of complex surgical scenes and the integration of visual perception with language reasoning, with the potential to support surgical training and intraoperative decision-making. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown promising performance through parameter-efficient fine-tuning; however, most existing approaches rely on coarse visual grounding, typically limited to bounding boxes, which fails to capture the fine-grained spatial structure of surgical objects. In this work, we propose a unified framework that jointly performs pixel-level segmentation and visual question answering within a single framework. Our approach integrates a VLM with a Segment Anything Model (SAM)-based decoder and represents scene elements as object tokens generated by the VLM. These object tokens guide answer prediction and are further projected to the SAM-based decoder to produce segmentation masks. By optimizing the object token embeddings through both segmentation and question answering objectives, the model learns spatially grounded representations that enhance visual reasoning while providing explicit pixel-level grounding. We evaluate the proposed method on the private RAMIE (Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Esophagectomy) dataset and the public EndoVis18 dataset, where it consistently outperforms baseline methods for surgical VQA. These results demonstrate that incorporating context-aware object tokens into vision-language models improves fine-grained surgical scene understanding.

</details>


### [213] [VinQA: Visual Elements Interleaved Long-form Answer Generation for Real-World Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16092)
*Young Rok Jang, Hyesoo Kong, Kyunghwan An, Jae Sub Huh, Gyeonghun Kim, Stanley Jungkyu Choi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (72.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VinQA 是一个面向真实世界多模态文档的长文本答案生成数据集，要求模型在答案中显式穿插引用视觉元素（表格、图表、照片等）并关联到文档页面。论文研究了 Page Encoding 和 Modality Encoding 两种文档编码方式及对应的视觉元素引用机制，并提出了 M-GroSE 多模态评估框架和 Visual Source F1 指标。实验表明，在 Qwen2.5-VL 上微调可显著缩小与闭源模型的差距，且 Page Encoding 在微调后能达到与 Modality Encoding 相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档问答研究主要生成纯文本答案，未能充分利用文档中的表格、图表、照片等视觉元素。真实文档通常图文混排，需要模型在答案中引用相关视觉元素以提供更完整、可信的回答。

Method: 构建 VinQA 数据集，包含图文混排的长文本答案及视觉元素引用标注。提出两种编码方法：(1) Page Encoding：直接编码整页图像，用边界框标注视觉元素作为可引用单元；(2) Modality Encoding：分别提取文本和裁剪视觉元素，分别编码后作为可引用单元。提出 M-GroSE 评估框架，从完整性、答案相关性、忠实性和不可回答性四个维度评估，并引入 Visual Source F1 衡量视觉引用准确性。

Result: 闭源前沿模型在 VinQA 测试集上整体表现最佳；在 Qwen2.5-VL 上微调后性能显著提升，缩小了与闭源模型的差距。Modality Encoding 在复杂文档（长文本、多视觉元素、多样引用需求）上初始更鲁棒，但微调后 Page Encoding 可达到相当水平。Visual G-Eval 验证了微调模型能在语义恰当的位置插入视觉元素并配以忠实文本。

Conclusion: VinQA 推动了文档问答中视觉元素引用的研究，证明了微调开源 MLLM 可有效提升文档长文本答案生成质量，且 Page Encoding 在微调后无需显式解析即可达到与 Modality Encoding 竞争的水平。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态文档问答（Document QA），提出长文本答案生成数据集 VinQA，并研究文档页面编码方式与视觉元素引用机制。这属于视觉问答与文档理解范畴，与 canonical 子主题 multimodal_vqa_doc 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on document QA with visual citations, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Multimodal document QA, Long-form answer generation, Visual element citation, Page vs Modality Encoding, Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL

Abstract: Real-world documents combine text with tables, charts, photographs, and diagrams arranged in diverse layouts, yet existing research on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for document QA predominantly produces text-only responses, underutilizing these visual elements. We introduce VinQA, a dataset for long-form answer generation where cited visual elements are explicitly interleaved with their supporting text and grounded in relevant document pages. To support this task, we study two encoding methods for feeding raw document page images into an MLLM, along with their visual-element citation mechanisms: (1) Page Encoding, which directly encodes full-page images with bounding boxes of visual elements and treats these boxed regions as citable units; and (2) Modality Encoding, which parses each page to extract text and crop visual elements, encodes them separately, and uses these cropped elements as citable units. In our experiments, we propose M-GroSE, a multimodal evaluation framework extending GroUSE to assess answers along four dimensions: completeness, answer relevancy, faithfulness, and unanswerability. We additionally report Visual Source F1 to directly measure visual citation accuracy. Although proprietary frontier models still achieve the best overall scores on the VinQA test split, fine-tuning open Qwen2.5-VL models on the training split substantially improves their performance and narrows this gap. Modality Encoding is initially more robust for complex documents with long text, many visual elements, and diverse citation requirements. After training on VinQA, however, Page Encoding reaches a comparable level, competing effectively even without the explicit parsing used in Modality Encoding. Finally, Visual G-Eval, an MLLM-based judge, confirms that fine-tuned models insert visual elements at semantically appropriate positions with faithful supporting text.

</details>
